<body>
<div class="sidebar">
    <div class="sidebar-header">
        <button id="refresh-btn" class="btn-refresh" onclick="window.manualRefresh()" title="最新状態に更新">🔄</button>
        <h1 style="margin:0 0 5px 0;">競技カードチェック</h1>
        <div id="event-name" style="font-size:0.9em;">読み込み中...</div>
        <a href="admin_menu.html" id="menu-back" style="color:white; font-size:0.8em; display:block; margin-top:10px;">← 管理者メニューへ</a>
    </div>
    <div class="dot-legend">
        <div class="dot-legend-item"><div class="dot-legend-dot" style="background:#e0e0e0;border-color:#bdbdbd;"></div>未取得</div>
        <div class="dot-legend-item"><div class="dot-legend-dot" style="background:#ffcdd2;border-color:#ef9a9a;"></div>未提出</div>
        <div class="dot-legend-item"><div class="dot-legend-dot" style="background:#cfd8dc;border-color:#90a4ae;"></div>提出済</div>
        <div class="dot-legend-item"><div class="dot-legend-dot" style="background:#0288d1;border-color:#0288d1;"></div>確認中</div>
        <div class="dot-legend-item"><div class="dot-legend-dot" style="background:#388e3c;border-color:#388e3c;"></div>確認済</div>
    </div>
    <div class="final-toggle">
        <button class="final-toggle-btn active" id="btn-all-mode" onclick="window.setFinalMode(false)">全ラウンド</button>
        <button class="final-toggle-btn" id="btn-final-mode" onclick="window.setFinalMode(true)">🏆 決勝のみ</button>
    </div>
    <div id="sidebar-list" class="sidebar-list"></div>
</div>
<div class="main-viewer" id="main-viewer">
    <div id="viewer-placeholder">サイドバーから<br>チェックする演技を選択してください</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, collection, getDocs, getDoc, updateDoc, query, where, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { normalizeClassRules, calcBonus } from "./shared.js";
    import { getTournament, getAthletes, getSkills, getFinalOrders } from "./cache.js";
    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = (params.get('event') || '').trim() || null;

    // ─── State ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let athletes = [], submissions = new Map(), classRules = { individual: {}, synchro: {} }, skillMaster = [];
    let currentSelection = { athleteId: null, round: null };
    let finalOnlyMode = false;
    let indivClassOrder = [], syncClassOrder = [];

    // ─── Utilities ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function withTimeout(promise, ms = 10000) {
        let timer;
        return Promise.race([
            promise,
            new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('timeout')), ms); })
        ]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer));
    }

    // ドットの状態クラスとツールチップを返す
    function dotStatusInfo(subId) {
        if (!submissions.has(subId)) return { cls: '',              title: '未取得（クリックで読み込み）' };
        const sub = submissions.get(subId);
        if (!sub)                    return { cls: 'not-submitted', title: '未提出' };
        switch (sub.checkStatus) {
            case 0:  return { cls: 'pre-check', title: '提出済・未確認' };
            case 1:  return { cls: 'in-check',  title: '確認中' };
            case 2:  return { cls: 'approved',  title: '確認済' };
            default: return { cls: 'pre-check', title: '提出済' };
        }
    }

    function resolveSkill(raw) {
        if (!raw && raw !== 0) return { symbol: '-', dd: null, direction: '', isTriple: false };
        if (typeof raw === 'object' && raw !== null) {
            const found = raw.id
                ? (skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(raw.id)) || skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === raw.skill))
                : skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === raw.skill);
            return { symbol: raw.skill || '-', dd: raw.dd ?? null, direction: found?.direction || '', isTriple: found?.isTriple || false };
        }
        const found = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(raw));
        return found ? { symbol: found.symbol || '-', dd: found.dd ?? null, direction: found.direction || '', isTriple: found.isTriple || false }
                     : { symbol: '-', dd: null, direction: '', isTriple: false };
    }

    const DIR_LABEL = { '前方系': '前方', '後方系': '後方', '基礎技': '基礎' };

    // ─── Dot / Row（ピンポイント更新） ──────────────────────────────────
    // HTML生成（初回サイドバー構築時のみ使用）
    function createDot(athleteId, round, label) {
        const { cls, title } = dotStatusInfo(eventId + '_' + athleteId + '_' + round);
        const isActive = currentSelection.athleteId === athleteId && currentSelection.round === round;
        return '<div id="dot-' + athleteId + '-' + round + '" class="r-dot' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + (isActive ? ' active' : '') +
               '" title="' + title + '" onclick="window.showDetail(\'' + athleteId + '\',\'' + round + '\')">' + label + '</div>';
    }

    // 既存ドット要素のクラス・ツールチップだけ書き換える
    function updateDot(athleteId, round) {
        const el = document.getElementById('dot-' + athleteId + '-' + round);
        if (!el) return;
        const { cls, title } = dotStatusInfo(eventId + '_' + athleteId + '_' + round);
        const wasActive = el.classList.contains('active');
        el.className = 'r-dot' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + (wasActive ? ' active' : '');
        el.title = title;
    }

    // active クラスと行ハイライトを旧→新へ付け替える（currentSelection変更前に呼ぶ）
    function setActiveDot(newAthId, newRound) {
        if (currentSelection.athleteId) {
            const prevDot = document.getElementById('dot-' + currentSelection.athleteId + '-' + currentSelection.round);
            if (prevDot) prevDot.classList.remove('active');
            const prevRow = document.getElementById('row-' + currentSelection.athleteId);
            if (prevRow) prevRow.classList.remove('selected');
        }
        if (newAthId) {
            const nextDot = document.getElementById('dot-' + newAthId + '-' + newRound);
            if (nextDot) nextDot.classList.add('active');
            const nextRow = document.getElementById('row-' + newAthId);
            if (nextRow) nextRow.classList.add('selected');
        }
    }

    // ─── Sidebar（構造変化時のみ全体再構築） ────────────────────────────
    window.setFinalMode = function(isFinal) {
        finalOnlyMode = isFinal;
        document.getElementById('btn-all-mode').classList.toggle('active', !isFinal);
        document.getElementById('btn-final-mode').classList.toggle('active', isFinal);
        currentSelection = { athleteId: null, round: null };
        document.getElementById('main-viewer').innerHTML = '<div id="viewer-placeholder">サイドバーから<br>チェックする演技を選択してください</div>';
        renderSidebar();
    };

    function renderSidebar() {
        const listEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-list');

        if (finalOnlyMode) {
            const finalists = athletes.filter(a => a.finalOrder != null)
                                      .sort((a, b) => (a.finalOrder || 999) - (b.finalOrder || 999));
            let html = '';
            if (finalists.length === 0) {
                html = '<div style="padding:20px; color:#90a4ae; font-size:0.9rem;">決勝進出選手が設定されていません。<br>「決勝試技順処理」で設定してください。</div>';
            } else {
                let curType = null;
                finalists.forEach(ath => {
                    const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
                    if (athType !== curType) {
                        curType = athType;
                        html += '<div class="section-header ' + (athType === 'synchro' ? 'sync-header' : 'indiv-header') + '">🏆 決勝 — ' + (athType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人') + '</div>';
                    }
                    const athName  = ath.type === 'synchro' ? ath.name1 + ' / ' + ath.name2 : ath.name1;
                    const clubName = ath.type === 'synchro' ? (ath.club1 === ath.club2 ? ath.club1 : ath.club1 + ' / ' + ath.club2) : '(' + ath.club1 + ')';
                    const selCls   = ath.id === currentSelection.athleteId ? ' selected' : '';
                    html += '<div id="row-' + ath.id + '" class="athlete-item' + selCls + '">' +
                        '<div class="athlete-name"><strong>' + ath.finalOrder + '.</strong> ' + athName + ' <small>' + clubName + '</small></div>' +
                        '<div class="round-box">' + createDot(ath.id, 'final', 'F') + '</div></div>';
                });
            }
            listEl.innerHTML = html;
            return;
        }

        const grouped = athletes.reduce((acc, ath) => {
            const key = (ath.type || 'individual') + '-' + (ath.class || 'N/A') + '-' + (ath.gender || 'N/A');
            (acc[key] = acc[key] || []).push(ath);
            return acc;
        }, {});

        const sortedKeys = Object.keys(grouped).sort((a, b) => {
            const [tA, cA, gA] = a.split('-'), [tB, cB, gB] = b.split('-');
            if (tA !== tB) return tA.localeCompare(tB);
            const ord = tA === 'synchro' ? syncClassOrder : indivClassOrder;
            const iA = ord.indexOf(cA), iB = ord.indexOf(cB);
            const oA = iA >= 0 ? iA : 9999, oB = iB >= 0 ? iB : 9999;
            if (oA !== oB) return oA - oB;
            return gA.localeCompare(gB);
        });

        let html = '', curType = null;
        for (const key of sortedKeys) {
            const [type, className, gender] = key.split('-');
            if (type !== curType) {
                curType = type;
                html += '<div class="section-header ' + (type === 'synchro' ? 'sync-header' : 'indiv-header') + '">' + (type === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ競技' : '個人競技') + '</div>';
            }
            html += '<div class="class-header">' + className + ' ' + gender + '</div>';
            grouped[key].sort((a, b) => (a.order || 999) - (b.order || 999)).forEach(ath => {
                const athType  = ath.type || 'individual';
                const rules    = (classRules[athType] && classRules[athType][ath.class]) || { qualifyingRoutines: 1, hasFinal: false };
                const athName  = ath.type === 'synchro' ? ath.name1 + ' / ' + ath.name2 : ath.name1;
                const clubName = ath.type === 'synchro' ? (ath.club1 === ath.club2 ? ath.club1 : ath.club1 + ' / ' + ath.club2) : '(' + ath.club1 + ')';
                const selCls   = ath.id === currentSelection.athleteId ? ' selected' : '';
                let dots = '';
                for (let i = 1; i <= rules.qualifyingRoutines; i++) dots += createDot(ath.id, 'pre' + i, 'R' + i);
                if (rules.hasFinal) dots += createDot(ath.id, 'final', 'F');
                html += '<div id="row-' + ath.id + '" class="athlete-item' + selCls + '">' +
                    '<div class="athlete-name">' + ath.order + '. ' + athName + ' <small>' + clubName + '</small></div>' +
                    '<div class="round-box">' + dots + '</div></div>';
            });
        }
        listEl.innerHTML = html;
    }

    // ─── Data ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function refreshAllSubmissions() {
        athletes.forEach(ath => {
            const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
            const rules = (classRules[athType] && classRules[athType][ath.class]) || { qualifyingRoutines: 1, hasFinal: false };
            for (let i = 1; i <= (rules.qualifyingRoutines || 1); i++) submissions.set(eventId + '_' + ath.id + '_pre' + i, null);
            if (rules.hasFinal) submissions.set(eventId + '_' + ath.id + '_final', null);
        });
        const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, 'submissions'), where('eventId', '==', eventId)));
        snap.forEach(d => submissions.set(d.id, d.data()));
    }

    // 全件同期 → 全ドットをピンポイント更新
    async function refreshAndUpdateAllDots() {
        await withTimeout(refreshAllSubmissions(), 12000);
        athletes.forEach(ath => {
            const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
            const rules = (classRules[athType] && classRules[athType][ath.class]) || { qualifyingRoutines: 1, hasFinal: false };
            for (let i = 1; i <= (rules.qualifyingRoutines || 1); i++) updateDot(ath.id, 'pre' + i);
            if (rules.hasFinal) updateDot(ath.id, 'final');
        });
    }

    // 🔄 ボタン（手動更新）
    window.manualRefresh = async function() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('refresh-btn');
        if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '⟳'; }
        try { await refreshAndUpdateAllDots(); }
        catch(e) { console.warn('Manual refresh failed:', e.message); }
        finally { if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '🔄'; } }
    };

    // ─── Detail Rendering ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function renderDetail(viewerEl, athlete, athleteId, round, submission) {
        if (!submission) {
            const athName    = athlete ? (athlete.type === 'synchro' ? athlete.name1 + ' / ' + athlete.name2 : athlete.name1) : '—';
            const roundLabel = round.replace('pre1','予選1').replace('pre2','予選2').replace('final','決勝');
            viewerEl.innerHTML =
                '<div class="no-sub-view">' +
                    '<div class="no-sub-badge">未提出</div>' +
                    '<div class="no-sub-name">' + athName + '</div>' +
                    '<div class="no-sub-meta">' + (athlete ? athlete.class : '') + ' / ' + roundLabel + '</div>' +
                    '<div style="color:#90a4ae; font-size:0.9rem;">まだ競技カードが提出されていません。</div>' +
                    '<div style="margin-top:20px;"><button class="btn-next" onclick="window.goNext()">次の選手へ →</button></div>' +
                '</div>';
            return;
        }

        const athName  = athlete.type === 'synchro' ? athlete.name1 + ' / ' + athlete.name2 : athlete.name1;
        const athClub  = athlete.type === 'synchro' ? athlete.club1 + ' / ' + athlete.club2 : athlete.club1;
        const athType  = athlete.type || 'individual';
        const cr       = (classRules[athType] && classRules[athType][athlete.class]) || {};
        const rawDDCnt = (cr.designatedDDCount != null && cr.designatedDDCount >= 0) ? cr.designatedDDCount : -1;
        const isDesMod = rawDDCnt >= 0 && cr.qualifyingRoutines === 2 && cr.scoreMode === 'total' && round === 'pre1';
        const effDDCnt = isDesMod ? rawDDCnt : -1;
        const desSet   = new Set(submission.designatedDDIndices || []);
        const showDes  = effDDCnt >= 0;

        let tableRows = '', baseDD = 0, tripleCount = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const s = resolveSkill((submission.skills || [])[i]);
            const counts = effDDCnt < 0 ? true : (effDDCnt > 0 && desSet.has(i));
            if (s.dd != null && counts) baseDD += s.dd;
            if (s.isTriple && counts) tripleCount++;
            const desMark = showDes ? '<td class="skill-des">' + (counts && effDDCnt > 0 ? '<span class="skill-des-mark">★</span>' : '') + '</td>' : '';
            tableRows += '<tr><td class="skill-num">' + (i + 1) + '</td>' +
                '<td class="skill-dir">' + (DIR_LABEL[s.direction] || '-') + '</td>' +
                '<td class="skill-sym">' + s.symbol + '</td>' +
                '<td class="skill-dd">' + (s.dd != null ? s.dd.toFixed(1) : '-') + '</td>' + desMark + '</tr>';
        }

        const bonus  = calcBonus(athlete.gender || '', tripleCount);
        const total  = baseDD + bonus;
        const status = submission.checkStatus ?? 0;

        let desInfoHtml = '';
        if (effDDCnt === 0) {
            desInfoHtml = '<div class="des-info zero">指定難度：0本設定（このクラスのDD得点は0点）</div>';
        } else if (effDDCnt > 0) {
            const chosen = desSet.size, ok = chosen === effDDCnt;
            desInfoHtml = '<div class="des-info ' + (ok ? 'ok' : 'warn') + '">指定難度：' + chosen + ' / ' + effDDCnt + ' 本選択' + (ok ? '　✓' : '　⚠ 本数不一致') + '</div>';
        }

        const desHead = showDes ? '<th class="skill-des">指定</th>' : '';
        viewerEl.innerHTML =
            '<div class="card-preview">' +
                '<div style="font-size:0.9em; color:#5f6368;">' + athlete.class + ' / ' + round.replace('pre','予選').replace('final','決勝') + '</div>' +
                '<h2 style="margin:5px 0 10px 0;">' + athName + '</h2>' +
                '<div style="font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:20px;">' + (athClub || '') + '</div>' +
                '<div class="skill-table-wrap"><table class="skill-table">' +
                    '<thead><tr><th class="skill-num">#</th><th class="skill-dir">系統</th><th class="skill-sym" style="text-align:left;">Symbol</th><th class="skill-dd">DD</th>' + desHead + '</tr></thead>' +
                    '<tbody>' + tableRows + '</tbody>' +
                '</table></div>' +
                desInfoHtml +
                '<div class="dd-summary" style="margin-top:12px;">' +
                    '<div class="dd-sub">基礎点：<strong>' + baseDD.toFixed(1) + '</strong><br>ボーナス点：<strong>' + bonus.toFixed(1) + '</strong></div>' +
                    '<div class="dd-total-wrap"><div class="dd-total-label">Total DD</div><div class="dd-total-val">' + total.toFixed(1) + '</div></div>' +
                '</div>' +
                '<div class="actions-section"><div class="btn-group">' +
                    '<button class="btn-status ' + (status === 0 ? 'active-0' : '') + '" onclick="window.updateStatus(0)">確認前</button>' +
                    '<button class="btn-status ' + (status === 1 ? 'active-1' : '') + '" onclick="window.updateStatus(1)">確認中</button>' +
                    '<button class="btn-status ' + (status === 2 ? 'active-2' : '') + '" onclick="window.updateStatus(2)">確認済</button>' +
                '</div><button class="btn-next" onclick="window.goNext()">次の選手へ →</button></div>' +
            '</div>';
    }

    // ─── Show Detail（Stale-While-Revalidate） ───────────────────────────
    window.showDetail = async function(athleteId, round) {
        const athlete  = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
        const viewerEl = document.getElementById('main-viewer');
        const subId    = eventId + '_' + athleteId + '_' + round;

        // 1. アクティブ状態を更新（currentSelection変更前に呼ぶ）
        setActiveDot(athleteId, round);
        currentSelection = { athleteId, round };

        // 2. SWR: キャッシュがあれば 0ms で即時描画
        if (submissions.has(subId)) {
            renderDetail(viewerEl, athlete, athleteId, round, submissions.get(subId));
        } else {
            viewerEl.innerHTML = '<div id="viewer-placeholder">読み込み中...</div>';
        }

        // 3. バックグラウンドで最新データを取得
        try {
            const subSnap  = await withTimeout(getDoc(doc(db, 'submissions', subId)));
            const fresh    = subSnap.exists() ? subSnap.data() : null;
            const prev     = submissions.get(subId);
            const wasStale = !submissions.has(subId) || JSON.stringify(prev) !== JSON.stringify(fresh);
            submissions.set(subId, fresh);
            updateDot(athleteId, round);

            // 4. レースコンディション防止 + 差分がある時だけ再描画
            if (wasStale && currentSelection.athleteId === athleteId && currentSelection.round === round) {
                renderDetail(viewerEl, athlete, athleteId, round, fresh);
            }
        } catch(e) {
            console.error('fetch error:', e.message);
            // キャッシュ未取得かつ今も同じ選手を表示中の場合だけエラー表示
            if (!submissions.has(subId) && currentSelection.athleteId === athleteId && currentSelection.round === round) {
                viewerEl.innerHTML =
                    '<div style="text-align:center; margin-top:80px; padding:20px;">' +
                        '<div style="font-size:2rem; margin-bottom:12px;">⚠️</div>' +
                        '<div style="font-size:1rem; color:#c62828; margin-bottom:20px;">読み込みに失敗しました<br><small style="color:#888;">' + e.message + '</small></div>' +
                        '<button onclick="window.showDetail(\'' + athleteId + '\',\'' + round + '\')" style="padding:12px 28px; background:#1a73e8; color:white; border:none; border-radius:8px; font-size:1rem; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;">再試行</button>' +
                    '</div>';
            }
        }
    };

    // ─── Status Update（Optimistic） ─────────────────────────────────────
    window.updateStatus = async function(status) {
        if (!currentSelection.athleteId || !currentSelection.round) return;
        const { athleteId, round } = currentSelection;
        const subId = eventId + '_' + athleteId + '_' + round;
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-status').forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
        try {
            await updateDoc(doc(db, 'submissions', subId), { checkStatus: status, checkedAt: serverTimestamp() });
            // ローカルデータを直接更新（再fetchしない）
            const sub = submissions.get(subId);
            if (sub) sub.checkStatus = status;
            updateDot(athleteId, round);
            // ボタンのアクティブ状態を直接切り替え（showDetail再呼び出し不要）
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-status').forEach((b, i) => {
                b.disabled = false;
                b.classList.remove('active-0', 'active-1', 'active-2');
                if (i === status) b.classList.add('active-' + status);
            });
        } catch(e) {
            console.error('Update error:', e);
            alert('更新に失敗しました。');
            document.querySelectorAll('.btn-status').forEach(b => b.disabled = false);
        }
    };

    // ─── Navigation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    window.goNext = function() {
        const next = findNextTarget();
        if (next) {
            showDetail(next.athleteId, next.round);
            // バックグラウンドで全件同期（画面操作はブロックしない）
            refreshAndUpdateAllDots().catch(e => console.warn('bg refresh failed:', e.message));
        } else {
            setActiveDot(null, null);
            currentSelection = { athleteId: null, round: null };
            document.getElementById('main-viewer').innerHTML = '<div id="viewer-placeholder">全てのカードの確認が完了しました！</div>';
        }
    };

    function findNextTarget() {
        if (finalOnlyMode) {
            const sorted = athletes.filter(a => a.finalOrder != null).sort((a, b) => (a.finalOrder || 999) - (b.finalOrder || 999));
            const idx = sorted.findIndex(a => a.id === currentSelection.athleteId);
            for (const ath of [...sorted.slice(idx), ...sorted.slice(0, idx)]) {
                if (ath.id === currentSelection.athleteId && currentSelection.round === 'final') continue;
                const subId = eventId + '_' + ath.id + '_final';
                if (submissions.has(subId) && submissions.get(subId)?.checkStatus === 2) continue;
                return { athleteId: ath.id, round: 'final' };
            }
            return null;
        }

        const sorted = [...athletes].sort((a, b) => {
            const kA = (a.class || '') + '-' + (a.gender || '') + '-' + String(a.order || '999').padStart(4, '0');
            const kB = (b.class || '') + '-' + (b.gender || '') + '-' + String(b.order || '999').padStart(4, '0');
            return kA.localeCompare(kB);
        });
        const idx = sorted.findIndex(a => a.id === currentSelection.athleteId);
        for (const ath of [...sorted.slice(idx), ...sorted.slice(0, idx)]) {
            const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
            const rules   = (classRules[athType] && classRules[athType][ath.class]) || { qualifyingRoutines: 1, hasFinal: false };
            const rounds  = [];
            for (let i = 1; i <= rules.qualifyingRoutines; i++) rounds.push('pre' + i);
            if (rules.hasFinal) rounds.push('final');
            for (const r of rounds) {
                if (ath.id === currentSelection.athleteId && r === currentSelection.round) continue;
                const subId = eventId + '_' + ath.id + '_' + r;
                if (submissions.has(subId) && submissions.get(subId)?.checkStatus === 2) continue;
                return { athleteId: ath.id, round: r };
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ─── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) { document.body.innerHTML = '<h1>大会IDが見つかりません</h1><a href="admin_index.html">大会選択に戻る</a>'; return; }
        const fromJudge = params.get('from') === 'judge';
        const backEl = document.getElementById('menu-back');
        backEl.href = fromJudge ? `judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}` : `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;
        backEl.textContent = fromJudge ? '← 審判用メニューへ' : '← 管理者メニューへ';
        document.getElementById('sidebar-list').innerHTML = '<div style="padding:20px; color:#90a4ae; font-size:0.9rem;">読み込み中...</div>';

        const [tourData, athleteList, skillMasterData] = await Promise.all([getTournament(eventId), getAthletes(eventId), getSkills()]);
        skillMaster = skillMasterData;

        if (tourData) {
            document.getElementById('event-name').innerText = tourData.name;
            classRules = normalizeClassRules(tourData.classRules);
            const sortByOrder = arr => (arr || []).slice().sort((a, b) => (a.display_order ?? 0) - (b.display_order ?? 0));
            if (tourData.classes?.individual || tourData.classes?.synchro) {
                indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tourData.classes.individual).map(c => c.name);
                syncClassOrder  = sortByOrder(tourData.classes.synchro).map(c => c.name);
            } else if (tourData.classes) {
                indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tourData.classes).map(c => c.name);
            }
        }
        athletes = athleteList;
        const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
        athletes.forEach(a => { const fo = finalOrders[a.id]; if (fo) { a.finalOrder = fo.finalOrder; a.isInFinal = fo.isInFinal; } });

        try { await withTimeout(refreshAllSubmissions(), 12000); }
        catch(e) { console.warn('Initial submission load failed:', e.message); }

        renderSidebar();
        const first = findNextTarget();
        if (first) showDetail(first.athleteId, first.round);
    }

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

File: admin_card_edit.html

    <style>

<body>
<div class="list-container">
    <div class="header">
        <div>
            <h1 id="event-name">読み込み中...</h1>
            <div class="header-sub">競技カード管理（管理者）</div>
        </div>
        <a id="menu-back" href="admin_menu.html" class="back-link">← 管理メニューに戻る</a>
    </div>
    <div id="indiv-list"></div>
    <div id="sync-list"></div>
</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc, collection, getDocs, query, where } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) { document.body.innerHTML = '<h1>エラー: 大会IDが見つかりません。</h1><a href="admin_index.html">大会選択に戻る</a>'; return; }
        document.getElementById('menu-back').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

        const [tourSnap, athSnap, subSnap, resSnap] = await Promise.all([
            getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId)),
            getDocs(collection(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes")),
            getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
            getDocs(query(collection(db, "results"),     where("eventId", "==", eventId)))
        ]);

        const tour = tourSnap.data();
        document.getElementById('event-name').innerText = tour.name;

        const subDataMap = new Map(subSnap.docs.map(d => [d.id, d.data()]));
        const resDataMap = new Map(resSnap.docs.map(d => [d.id, d.data()]));
        const allAthletes = athSnap.docs.map(d => ({ ...d.data(), id: d.id }));

        const indivAthletes = allAthletes.filter(a => a.type !== 'synchro');
        const syncAthletes  = allAthletes.filter(a => a.type === 'synchro');

        const rawClassRules = tour.classRules || {};
        const hasNewFormat = 'individual' in rawClassRules || 'synchro' in rawClassRules;
        const classRules = hasNewFormat ? rawClassRules :
                           Object.keys(rawClassRules).length > 0 ? { individual: rawClassRules, synchro: {} } :
                           { individual: {}, synchro: {} };

        const sortByOrder = arr => (arr || []).slice().sort((a, b) => (a.display_order ?? 0) - (b.display_order ?? 0));
        let indivClassOrder = [], syncClassOrder = [];
        if (tour.classes && !Array.isArray(tour.classes)) {
            indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tour.classes.individual).map(c => c.name);
            syncClassOrder  = sortByOrder(tour.classes.synchro).map(c => c.name);
        } else if (Array.isArray(tour.classes)) {
            indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tour.classes).map(c => c.name);
        }

        if (indivAthletes.length > 0) {
            document.getElementById('indiv-list').innerHTML =
                '<h2 class="section-header indiv-header">個人競技</h2>' +
                renderAthleteTables(indivAthletes, classRules.individual, subDataMap, resDataMap, 'individual', rawClassRules, indivClassOrder);
        }
        if (syncAthletes.length > 0) {
            document.getElementById('sync-list').innerHTML =
                '<h2 class="section-header sync-header">シンクロ競技</h2>' +
                renderAthleteTables(syncAthletes, classRules.synchro, subDataMap, resDataMap, 'synchro', rawClassRules, syncClassOrder);
        }
    }

    function renderAthleteTables(athletes, rulesByType, subDataMap, resDataMap, type, fallbackRules = {}, classOrder = []) {
        const athletesByClass = athletes.reduce((acc, ath) => {
            const className = ath.class || 'N/A';
            if (!acc[className]) acc[className] = [];
            acc[className].push(ath);
            return acc;
        }, {});

        const ordered  = classOrder.filter(name => athletesByClass[name]);
        const leftover = Object.keys(athletesByClass).filter(name => !classOrder.includes(name)).sort();
        const sortedClasses = [...ordered, ...leftover];
        let finalHtml = '';

        for (const className of sortedClasses) {
            const classAthletes = athletesByClass[className];
            const rules = (rulesByType || {})[className] || fallbackRules[className] || { qualifyingRoutines: 2, hasFinal: false };

            classAthletes.sort((a, b) =>
                `${a.gender || ''}-${String(a.flightGroup || '').padStart(3,'0')}-${String(a.order || '').padStart(3,'0')}`
                .localeCompare(`${b.gender || ''}-${String(b.flightGroup || '').padStart(3,'0')}-${String(b.order || '').padStart(3,'0')}`)
            );

            finalHtml += `<div class="class-header ${type === 'synchro' ? 'sync-class-header' : 'indiv-class-header'}">${className}</div>`;
            let currentGender = "";

            classAthletes.forEach(ath => {
                if (currentGender !== ath.gender) {
                    if (currentGender !== "") finalHtml += '</tbody></table>';
                    currentGender = ath.gender;

                    const routineHeaders = Array.from({ length: rules.qualifyingRoutines }, (_, i) => `<th class="col-btn">予選${i+1}</th>`).join('');
                    const finalHeader = rules.hasFinal ? '<th class="col-btn">決勝</th>' : '';
                    const totalCols = 3 + rules.qualifyingRoutines + (rules.hasFinal ? 1 : 0);

                    finalHtml += `
                        <table>
                            <thead>
                                <tr class="category-row"><td colspan="${totalCols}" style="text-align:left;">${currentGender}</td></tr>
                                <tr>
                                    <th class="col-num">Gr</th>
                                    <th class="col-num">順</th>
                                    <th class="col-name">選手</th>
                                    ${routineHeaders}
                                    ${finalHeader}
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                    `;
                }

                const playerHtml = type === 'synchro'
                    ? `<span style="font-weight:bold;">${ath.name1 || ''} / ${ath.name2 || ''}</span><br><small style="color:#888;">${ath.club1 || ''} / ${ath.club2 || ''}</small>`
                    : `<span style="font-weight:bold;">${ath.name1}</span><br><small style="color:#888;">${ath.club1}</small>`;

                finalHtml += `<tr class="${ath.isWithdrawn ? 'withdrawn-row' : ''}"><td class="col-num">${ath.flightGroup || '-'}</td><td class="col-num">${ath.order || '-'}</td><td class="col-name">${playerHtml}</td>`;

                for (let i = 1; i <= rules.qualifyingRoutines; i++) {
                    finalHtml += `<td>${getStatusButton(ath, `pre${i}`, subDataMap, resDataMap)}</td>`;
                }
                if (rules.hasFinal) {
                    finalHtml += `<td>${getStatusButton(ath, 'final', subDataMap, resDataMap)}</td>`;
                }
                finalHtml += '</tr>';
            });

            if (classAthletes.length > 0) finalHtml += '</tbody></table>';
        }
        return finalHtml;
    }

    function getStatusButton(ath, round, subDataMap, resDataMap) {
        if (ath.isWithdrawn) return "<span style='font-size:0.8em; color:#d32f2f;'>棄権</span>";
        const subId = `${eventId}_${ath.id}_${round}`;
        const editUrl = `entry.html?event=${eventId}&athlete=${ath.id}&round=${round}&admin=1`;
        const sub = subDataMap.get(subId);
        if (!sub) return `<a href="${editUrl}" class="status-link status-none">未提出</a>`;
        const cs = sub.checkStatus ?? 0;
        if (cs === 2) return `<a href="${editUrl}" class="status-link status-ok">確認済</a>`;
        if (cs === 1) return `<a href="${editUrl}" class="status-link status-warning">要確認</a>`;
        return `<a href="${editUrl}" class="status-link status-done">提出済</a>`;
    }

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>



========================================
File: admin_final_order.html

<script type="module">
  import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
  import { doc, getDocs, collection, query, where, writeBatch } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
  import { ensureAdmin, logout } from "./auth.js";
  import { getTournament, getAthletes, getFinalOrders, invalidateCache } from "./cache.js";
  import { normalizeClassRules, calcQualifyingScore, calcQualifyingTb, compareRankEntries } from "./shared.js";

  const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const eventId = params.get('event');
  document.getElementById('back-link').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

  let athletes = [], tourData = null;
  let fType = 'individual', fGender = '', fClass = '';
  let pendingFinalOrder = null; // 確定前プレビュー

  const fmt = v => (v != null ? Number(v).toFixed(2) : '--');

  // ---- 認証 & 初期化 ----
  ensureAdmin(async user => {
    document.getElementById('auth-info').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = user.displayName || user.email;
    document.getElementById('logout-btn').onclick = logout;

    if (!eventId) { document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = 'URLにeventパラメータがありません'; return; }

    const [fetchedTour, fetchedAth] = await Promise.all([
      getTournament(eventId),
      getAthletes(eventId)
    ]);
    if (!fetchedTour) { document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = '大会が見つかりません'; return; }

    tourData = fetchedTour;
    document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = tourData.name || eventId;

    athletes = fetchedAth;
    // 決勝試技順はキャッシュ除外なので毎回Firestoreから取得してマージ
    const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
    athletes.forEach(a => {
      const fo = finalOrders[a.id];
      if (fo) { a.finalOrder = fo.finalOrder; a.isInFinal = fo.isInFinal; }
    });

    buildFilters();
    updateFinalCountFromRule();
    buildStartList();
  });

  // ---- フィルターUI構築 ----
  function buildFilters() {
    const types   = [...new Set(athletes.map(a => a.type || 'individual'))];
    const genders = [...new Set(athletes.map(a => a.gender || '').filter(Boolean))];

    const typeRow = document.getElementById('type-row');
    if (types.length > 1) {
      typeRow.style.display = '';
      [['individual','個人'],['synchro','シンクロ']].forEach(([v,l]) => {
        if (!types.includes(v)) return;
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.className = 'tab-btn' + (fType === v ? ' active' : '');
        b.textContent = l;
        b.onclick = () => { fType = v; fGender = ''; fClass = ''; renderFilters(); updateFinalCountFromRule(); };
        typeRow.appendChild(b);
      });
    }

    renderFilters();
  }

  function renderFilters() {
    const byType = athletes.filter(a => (a.type || 'individual') === fType);
    const genders = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.gender || '').filter(Boolean))];
    const tourClassDefs = (tourData?.classes?.[fType] || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
    const rawClasses = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.class || a.category || '').filter(Boolean))];
    const classes = [...tourClassDefs.filter(c=>rawClasses.includes(c)), ...rawClasses.filter(c=>!tourClassDefs.includes(c))];

    const genderRow = document.getElementById('gender-row');
    genderRow.innerHTML = '<span class="filter-label">性別</span>';
    if (genders.length > 1) {
      genderRow.style.display = '';
      const allBtn = mkTabBtn('全て', fGender === '', () => { fGender = ''; renderFilters(); updateFinalCountFromRule(); });
      genderRow.appendChild(allBtn);
      genders.forEach(g => {
        genderRow.appendChild(mkTabBtn(g, fGender === g, () => { fGender = g; renderFilters(); updateFinalCountFromRule(); }));
      });
    } else {
      genderRow.style.display = 'none';
      if (genders.length === 1) fGender = genders[0];
    }

    const classRow = document.getElementById('class-row');
    classRow.innerHTML = '<span class="filter-label">クラス</span>';
    if (classes.length > 1) {
      classRow.style.display = '';
      const allBtn = mkTabBtn('全て', fClass === '', () => { fClass = ''; renderFilters(); updateFinalCountFromRule(); });
      classRow.appendChild(allBtn);
      classes.forEach(c => {
        classRow.appendChild(mkTabBtn(c, fClass === c, () => { fClass = c; renderFilters(); updateFinalCountFromRule(); }));
      });
    } else {
      classRow.style.display = classes.length === 1 ? '' : 'none';
      if (classes.length === 1) { fClass = classes[0]; classRow.appendChild(mkTabBtn(classes[0], true, () => {})); }
    }

    // アクティブボタン更新
    document.querySelectorAll('#type-row .tab-btn').forEach(b => {
      b.classList.toggle('active', (b.textContent === '個人' && fType === 'individual') || (b.textContent === 'シンクロ' && fType === 'synchro'));
    });

    pendingFinalOrder = null;
    document.getElementById('standings-card').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('save-btn').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('warn-overwrite').style.display = 'none';
  }

  function mkTabBtn(label, active, fn) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'tab-btn' + (active ? ' active' : '');
    b.textContent = label;
    b.onclick = fn;
    return b;
  }

  // ---- クラスルールから決勝人数を自動入力 ----
  function updateFinalCountFromRule() {
    if (!tourData) return;
    const byType = normalizeClassRules(tourData.classRules);
    const rule = (byType[fType] || {})[fClass] || {};
    if (rule.finalCount) document.getElementById('final-count').value = rule.finalCount;
  }

  // ---- Fisher-Yates シャッフル ----
  function shuffleArray(arr) {
    const a = [...arr];
    for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
    }
    return a;
  }

  // ---- 確認・確定ボタン ----
  window.applyFinalCount = async function() {
    const finalCount  = parseInt(document.getElementById('final-count').value);
    if (!finalCount || finalCount < 1) { alert('決勝人数を入力してください'); return; }
    const shuffleMode = document.querySelector('input[name="shuffle"]:checked')?.value || 'none';

    // このクラスの全選手を取得
    const classAthletes = athletes.filter(a =>
      (a.type || 'individual') === fType &&
      (!fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender) &&
      (!fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
    );
    if (classAthletes.length === 0) { alert('該当する選手がいません'); return; }

    // publishedな予選結果を取得
    const resSnap = await getDocs(query(
      collection(db, 'results'),
      where('eventId', '==', eventId),
      where('published', '==', true)
    ));
    const resultsByAth = {};
    resSnap.docs.forEach(d => {
      const r = d.data();
      if (!resultsByAth[r.athleteId]) resultsByAth[r.athleteId] = {};
      resultsByAth[r.athleteId][r.round] = r;
    });

    // クラスルールからscoreMode取得
    const byType = normalizeClassRules(tourData.classRules);
    const rule = (byType[fType] || {})[fClass] || {};
    const numPre    = rule.qualifyingRoutines ?? tourData.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
    const scoreMode = rule.scoreMode || 'best';

    // 予選スコア計算・順位付け（shared.js 共通関数を使用）
    const sorted = classAthletes.map(a => {
      const rMap  = resultsByAth[a.id] || {};
      const pre1r = rMap['pre1'] || null;
      const pre2r = rMap['pre2'] || null;
      const pre1  = pre1r?.officialTotal ?? null;
      const pre2  = pre2r?.officialTotal ?? null;
      const qualScore       = calcQualifyingScore(pre1, pre2, numPre, scoreMode);
      const { tbSum, tb }   = calcQualifyingTb(pre1, pre2, pre1r, pre2r, numPre, scoreMode);
      return { a, pre1, pre2, qualScore, tbSum, tb };
    }).sort(compareRankEntries);

    const actual = Math.min(finalCount, sorted.filter(r => r.qualScore != null).length);

    // 予選順位を付与
    const withRanks = sorted.map((entry, idx) => ({
      ...entry,
      qualRank: idx + 1,
      inFinal:  idx < actual && entry.qualScore != null
    }));

    const finalists    = withRanks.filter(e => e.inFinal);
    const nonFinalists = withRanks.filter(e => !e.inFinal);

    // シャッフルモードに応じて試技順を決定
    let trialOrder;
    if (shuffleMode === 'full') {
      trialOrder = shuffleArray(finalists);
    } else if (shuffleMode === 'split4') {
      if (actual <= 4) {
        // 4人以下は完全シャッフルと同じ
        trialOrder = shuffleArray(finalists);
      } else {
        const top4 = shuffleArray(finalists.slice(0, 4)); // 予選上位4名
        const rest = shuffleArray(finalists.slice(4));    // それ以外
        trialOrder = [...rest, ...top4]; // 下位グループが先、上位4名が後
      }
    } else if (shuffleMode === 'qualifying') {
      // 予選の出番順（組番号 → 試技順）で昇順に並べる
      trialOrder = [...finalists].sort((x, y) => {
        const gx = Number(x.a.flightGroup) || 0;
        const gy = Number(y.a.flightGroup) || 0;
        if (gx !== gy) return gx - gy;
        return (Number(x.a.order) || 0) - (Number(y.a.order) || 0);
      });
    } else {
      // none: 予選逆順（下位から先に試技、1位が最後）
      trialOrder = [...finalists].reverse();
    }

    // finalOrder割り当て（1 = 最初に試技、actual = 最後に試技）
    trialOrder.forEach((entry, i) => { entry.finalOrder = i + 1; });

    // 予選順位順に並べて表示用データとする
    pendingFinalOrder = [...finalists, ...nonFinalists].sort((a, b) => a.qualRank - b.qualRank);

    renderStandings(pendingFinalOrder, numPre, actual, shuffleMode);
    document.getElementById('standings-card').style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('save-btn').style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('save-btn').disabled = false;
    document.getElementById('save-status').textContent = '';

    const hasExisting = classAthletes.some(a => a.finalOrder != null);
    document.getElementById('warn-overwrite').style.display = hasExisting ? '' : 'none';
  };

  // ---- 順位表描画 ----
  function renderStandings(entries, numPre, actual, shuffleMode = 'none') {
    const title = [fClass, fGender, fType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人'].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const shuffleLabel = { none:'逆順', full:'完全シャッフル', split4:'上位4名分割シャッフル', qualifying:'予選試技順維持' }[shuffleMode] || shuffleMode;
    document.getElementById('standings-title').textContent = `${title} 予選順位`;
    document.getElementById('standings-meta').textContent = `決勝進出: ${actual}人 ／ ${shuffleLabel}`;

    const r2col = numPre >= 2;
    const tbody = document.getElementById('standings-tbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = entries.map(e => {
      const badge = e.inFinal
        ? `<span class="final-order-badge">${e.finalOrder}</span>`
        : '<span style="color:#bbb;font-size:.8rem;">−</span>';
      const r2cell = r2col ? `<td class="num">${fmt(e.pre2)}</td>` : '';
      return `<tr class="${e.inFinal ? 'finalist' : 'non-finalist'}">
        <td class="num">${e.qualRank}</td>
        <td>
          <div style="font-weight:700">${e.a.name1 || '---'}</div>
          <div style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--text-sub)">${e.a.club1 || ''}</div>
        </td>
        <td class="num">${fmt(e.pre1)}</td>
        ${r2cell}
        <td class="num">${e.qualScore != null ? fmt(e.qualScore) : '<span class="no-score">未提出</span>'}</td>
        <td>${badge}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');

    // theadのR2列
    const thead = document.querySelector('table thead tr');
    const r2th = thead.querySelector('th:nth-child(4)');
    if (!r2col && r2th) r2th.style.display = 'none';
    else if (r2th) r2th.style.display = '';
  }

  // ---- スタートリスト ----
  function buildStartList() {
    const finalists = athletes.filter(a => a.isInFinal && a.finalOrder != null);
    const section   = document.getElementById('startlist-section');
    if (finalists.length === 0) { section.style.display = 'none'; return; }

    // 印刷タイトルに大会名をセット
    document.getElementById('sl-print-event').textContent = tourData?.name || '';

    // クラス別グループ化（種目 → 性別 → クラス順）
    const tourClassDefs = {};
    ['individual','synchro'].forEach(tp => {
      (tourData?.classes?.[tp] || [])
        .slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99))
        .forEach((c, i) => { tourClassDefs[`${tp}|${c.name}`] = i; });
    });

    const groupMap = {};
    finalists.forEach(a => {
      const type   = a.type || 'individual';
      const gender = a.gender || '';
      const cls    = a.class || a.category || '';
      const key    = `${type}|${gender}|${cls}`;
      if (!groupMap[key]) groupMap[key] = { type, gender, cls, athletes: [] };
      groupMap[key].athletes.push(a);
    });

    // グループを種目→クラス定義順→性別順でソート
    const groups = Object.entries(groupMap).sort(([ka,a],[kb,b]) => {
      if (a.type !== b.type) return a.type === 'individual' ? -1 : 1;
      const oa = tourClassDefs[`${a.type}|${a.cls}`] ?? 99;
      const ob = tourClassDefs[`${b.type}|${b.cls}`] ?? 99;
      if (oa !== ob) return oa - ob;
      return (a.gender||'').localeCompare(b.gender||'');
    }).map(([,g]) => g);

    const container = document.getElementById('startlist-container');
    container.innerHTML = '';

    groups.forEach(g => {
      g.athletes.sort((a,b) => (a.finalOrder||999) - (b.finalOrder||999));
      const typeLabel = g.type === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
      const title = [g.cls, g.gender, typeLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

      const rows = g.athletes.map(a => {
        if (a.type === 'synchro') {
          const kana = [a.kana1, a.kana2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
          const name = `${a.name1 || '---'} / ${a.name2 || '---'}`;
          const c1 = a.club1 || '', c2 = a.club2 || '';
          const ck1 = a.clubKana1 || '', ck2 = a.clubKana2 || '';
          let clubHtml;
          if (c1 && c1 === c2) {
            clubHtml = (ck1 ? `<div class="sl-kana">${ck1}</div>` : '') + c1;
          } else {
            const kanaRow = [ck1, ck2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
            const clubRow = [c1, c2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
            clubHtml = (kanaRow ? `<div class="sl-kana">${kanaRow}</div>` : '') + clubRow;
          }
          return `<tr>
            <td class="sl-no">${a.finalOrder}</td>
            <td>${kana ? `<div class="sl-kana">${kana}</div>` : ''}<div class="sl-name">${name}</div></td>
            <td class="sl-club">${clubHtml}</td>
          </tr>`;
        } else {
          return `<tr>
            <td class="sl-no">${a.finalOrder}</td>
            <td>${a.kana1 ? `<div class="sl-kana">${a.kana1}</div>` : ''}<div class="sl-name">${a.name1 || '---'}</div></td>
            <td class="sl-club">${a.clubKana1 ? `<div class="sl-kana">${a.clubKana1}</div>` : ''}${a.club1 || ''}</td>
          </tr>`;
        }
      }).join('');

      const acc = document.createElement('div');
      acc.className = 'sl-accordion';
      acc.dataset.type   = g.type;
      acc.dataset.gender = g.gender;
      acc.dataset.cls    = g.cls;
      acc.innerHTML = `
        <div class="sl-header" onclick="this.closest('.sl-accordion').classList.toggle('open')">
          <span class="sl-title">${title}</span>
          <span class="sl-count">${g.athletes.length}人</span>
          <button class="sl-btn-print-cls" onclick="event.stopPropagation();printClass(this,'${title}')">🖨️ 印刷</button>
          <span class="sl-chevron">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div class="sl-body">
          <table class="sl-table">
            <thead><tr>
              <th class="num-col">No.</th>
              <th>ふりがな / 氏名</th>
              <th>所属</th>
            </tr></thead>
            <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>`;
      container.appendChild(acc);
    });

    section.style.display = '';
  }

  window.printFiltered = function() {
    const accs = [...document.querySelectorAll('#startlist-container .sl-accordion')];
    const targets = accs.filter(acc =>
      acc.dataset.type === fType &&
      (!fGender || acc.dataset.gender === fGender) &&
      (!fClass  || acc.dataset.cls    === fClass)
    );
    const label = [fClass, fGender, fType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人'].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    document.getElementById('sl-print-event').textContent = (tourData?.name || '') + (label ? ` — ${label}` : '');
    targets.forEach(acc => acc.classList.add('print-target', 'open'));
    document.body.classList.add('printing-class');
    window.print();
    window.onafterprint = () => {
      targets.forEach(acc => acc.classList.remove('print-target'));
      document.body.classList.remove('printing-class');
      document.getElementById('sl-print-event').textContent = tourData?.name || '';
      window.onafterprint = null;
    };
  };

  window.printClass = function(btn, title) {
    const acc = btn.closest('.sl-accordion');
    // 印刷タイトルをクラス名に更新
    document.getElementById('sl-print-event').textContent = `${tourData?.name || ''} — ${title}`;
    acc.classList.add('print-target', 'open'); // 本文を確実に展開
    document.body.classList.add('printing-class');
    window.print();
    window.onafterprint = () => {
      acc.classList.remove('print-target');
      document.body.classList.remove('printing-class');
      document.getElementById('sl-print-event').textContent = tourData?.name || '';
      window.onafterprint = null;
    };
  };

  window.refreshStartList = async function() {
    const btn = document.querySelector('.btn-refresh');
    btn.textContent = '読込中...'; btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
      athletes.forEach(a => {
        const fo = finalOrders[a.id];
        a.finalOrder = fo?.finalOrder ?? null;
        a.isInFinal  = fo?.isInFinal  ?? false;
      });
      buildStartList();
    } finally {
      btn.textContent = '↻ 更新'; btn.disabled = false;
    }
  };

  // ---- 保存 ----
  document.getElementById('save-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (!pendingFinalOrder) return;
    const btn = document.getElementById('save-btn');
    const status = document.getElementById('save-status');
    btn.disabled = true;
    status.style.color = '#888';
    status.textContent = '保存中...';

    try {
      const batch = writeBatch(db);
      pendingFinalOrder.forEach(e => {
        const ref = doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'athletes', e.a.id);
        batch.update(ref, {
          finalOrder: e.inFinal ? e.finalOrder : null,
          isInFinal:  e.inFinal
        });
      });
      await batch.commit();
      invalidateCache(eventId);

      // ローカルのathletesデータも更新
      pendingFinalOrder.forEach(e => {
        const a = athletes.find(x => x.id === e.a.id);
        if (a) { a.finalOrder = e.inFinal ? e.finalOrder : null; a.isInFinal = e.inFinal; }
      });

      status.style.color = '#2e7d32';
      status.textContent = `✅ 保存しました。CJP・D審判の「決勝のみ」フィルターに反映されます。`;
      document.getElementById('warn-overwrite').style.display = 'none';
      buildStartList();
    } catch (err) {
      status.style.color = '#c62828';
      status.textContent = `❌ 保存に失敗しました: ${err.message}`;
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>管理者コンソール - Trampoline Scoring System</title>
    <style>
        :root { --primary: #263238; --accent: #1a73e8; --bg: #f0f2f5; --card: #ffffff; }
        body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; background: var(--bg); display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; color: var(--primary); }
        .card { background: var(--card); padding: 50px; border-radius: 24px; width: 440px; border-top: 8px solid var(--primary); text-align: center; box-shadow: 0 15px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); }
        h1 { font-size: 1.6rem; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 5px; }
        p { color: #78909c; font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 35px; }
        #welcome-message { font-size: 1rem; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 25px; color: var(--primary); }
        .form-group { text-align: left; margin-bottom: 25px; }
        label { display: block; font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: bold; color: #90a4ae; margin: 0 0 8px 5px; text-transform: uppercase; }
        select { width: 100%; padding: 15px; border-radius: 12px; border: 2px solid #e1e7eb; font-size: 1rem; background: #f8fbff; cursor: pointer; outline: none; transition: 0.3s; }
        select:focus { border-color: var(--accent); background: #fff; }
        .btn { display: block; width: 100%; padding: 18px; border-radius: 12px; border: none; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; transition: 0.2s; margin-bottom: 15px; text-decoration: none; }
        .btn-primary { background: var(--primary); color: white; box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(38, 50, 56, 0.3); }
        .btn-primary:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); background: #1a252b; }
        .btn-outline { background: transparent; color: #90a4ae; border: 2px solid #cfd8dc; }
        .btn-outline:hover { background: #f1f3f4; }
        .btn-create { background: #1e8e3e; color: white; box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(30,142,62,0.25); }
        .btn-create:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); background: #166b2e; }
        .divider { border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e1e7eb; margin: 5px 0 20px; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="card">
    <h1>ADMIN CONSOLE</h1>
    <p id="welcome-message">ようこそ</p>
    <div class="form-group">
        <label>Select Tournament</label>
        <select id="tour-select"><option value="">Loading events...</option></select>
    </div>
    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="goMenu()">管理メニュー（ポータル）を開く</button>
    <hr class="divider">
    <button class="btn btn-create" onclick="createTournament()">＋ 新規大会を作成</button>
</div>

<script type="module">
    import { doc, collection, setDoc, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { ensureAdmin, getTournaments } from './auth.js';
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";

    function isPastTournament(tour) {
        const end = tour.end_date || tour.endDate || tour.start_date || tour.startDate || '';
        if (!end) return false; // 日付未設定は「開催前・開催中」扱い
        const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        return end < today;
    }

    function makeOption(tour) {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = tour.id;
        opt.text = `[${tour.startDate || tour.start_date || '日付未設定'}] ${tour.name || '名称未設定'}`;
        return opt;
    }

    async function populateTournaments() {
        const tournaments = await getTournaments();
        const select = document.getElementById('tour-select');
        select.innerHTML = '';

        if (tournaments.length === 0) {
            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">登録された大会がありません</option>';
            return;
        }

        const upcoming = tournaments.filter(t => !isPastTournament(t));
        const past     = tournaments.filter(t => isPastTournament(t));

        // 開催前・開催中は開催日が近い順（昇順）に並べ替え
        upcoming.sort((a, b) => {
            const da = a.startDate || a.start_date || '';
            const db_ = b.startDate || b.start_date || '';
            return da < db_ ? -1 : da > db_ ? 1 : 0;
        });

        if (upcoming.length) {
            const grp = document.createElement('optgroup');
            grp.label = '開催前・開催中';
            upcoming.forEach(tour => grp.appendChild(makeOption(tour)));
            select.appendChild(grp);
        }
        if (past.length) {
            const grp = document.createElement('optgroup');
            grp.label = '過去大会';
            past.forEach(tour => grp.appendChild(makeOption(tour)));
            select.appendChild(grp);
        }
    }

    window.goMenu = () => {
        const id = document.getElementById('tour-select').value;
        if (!id) return alert("大会を選択してください");
        location.href = `admin_menu.html?event=${id}`;
    };

    window.createTournament = async () => {
        const name = prompt('新規大会名を入力してください:');
        if (!name?.trim()) return;
        try {
            const newRef = doc(collection(db, 'tournaments'));
            await setDoc(newRef, {
                name: name.trim(),
                startDate: '',
                createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
                updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
            });
            location.href = `settings.html?event=${newRef.id}`;
        } catch (e) {
            alert('大会の作成に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    ensureAdmin(user => {
        document.getElementById('welcome-message').textContent = `ようこそ、${user.displayName || user.email}さん`;
        populateTournaments();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<div class="container">
    <div class="header-bar">
        <a href="admin_index.html" class="btn-back">← 大会選択に戻る</a>

    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    async function initPage() {
        if (!eventId) { 
            location.href = 'admin_index.html'; 
            return; 
        }

    ensureAdmin(user => {

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) { location.href = 'admin_index.html'; return; }
        document.getElementById('link-back').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

        const docSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId));
        if (docSnap.exists()) {
            document.getElementById('display-event-name').innerText = docSnap.data().name;
        }

        const base = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname.split('/').slice(0, -1).join('/');
        setupLink('qr-judge-menu', 'url-judge-menu', `${base}/judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}`);
        setupLink('qr-venue',      'url-venue',      `${base}/display.html?event=${eventId}`);
        setupLink('qr-entry',      'url-entry',      `${base}/event.html?event=${eventId}`);
    }

    function setupLink(canvasId, boxId, url) {
        document.getElementById(boxId).innerHTML =
            `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:inherit;text-decoration:underline;word-break:break-all;">${url}</a>`;
        new QRious({ element: document.getElementById(canvasId), value: url, size: 180, level: 'H' });
    }

    function showToast(msg) {
        const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
        toast.textContent = msg;
        toast.style.display = 'block';
        setTimeout(() => { toast.style.display = 'none'; }, 2000);
    }

    window.copy = (elementId) => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(document.getElementById(elementId).innerText)
            .then(() => showToast('URLをコピーしました！'));
    };

    window.copyQR = (canvasId) => {
        document.getElementById(canvasId).toBlob(blob => {
            navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({ 'image/png': blob })])
                .then(() => showToast('QR画像をコピーしました！'))
                .catch(() => showToast('コピーに失敗しました（ブラウザ非対応の可能性）'));
        });
    };

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>
