  <!-- 表彰式画面 -->
  <div id="vs-ceremony" class="venue-screen">
    <div class="vs-cer-name" id="vs-cer-name">---</div>
    <div class="vs-ceremony-label">表彰式</div>
    <footer class="vs-footer">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</footer>
  </div>

  <!-- 集計中画面（内容はJS動的生成） -->
  <div id="vs-calculating" class="venue-screen"></div>

  <!-- 会場練習画面 -->
  <div id="vs-warmup" class="venue-screen">
    <div class="vs-warmup-top">
      <div class="vs-warmup-event" id="vs-warmup-event">---</div>
    </div>
    <!-- ラベル+クラス名+タイマーをまとめて上下中央 -->
    <div class="vs-warmup-main">
      <div class="vs-warmup-info">
        <div class="vs-warmup-label">会場練習</div>
        <div class="vs-class-names" id="vs-class-names">---</div>
      </div>
      <div class="vs-timer num done" id="vs-timer">--:--</div>
    </div>
    <footer class="vs-footer">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</footer>
  </div>

  <!-- 決勝スタートリスト画面 -->
  <div id="vs-final-startlist" class="venue-screen">
    <div class="fsl-layout" id="fsl-layout"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- 選手紹介画面 -->
  <div id="vs-intro" class="venue-screen">
    <div class="intro-layout" id="intro-layout">
      <div class="intro-top-bar">
        <div class="intro-event-lbl" id="intro-event-lbl"></div>
        <div class="intro-counter-lbl" id="intro-counter-lbl"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="intro-card" id="intro-card">
        <div class="intro-class-lbl" id="intro-class-lbl"></div>
        <div class="intro-order-lbl" id="intro-order-lbl"></div>
        <div class="intro-divider"></div>
        <div class="intro-name-lbl" id="intro-name-lbl"></div>
        <div class="intro-kana-lbl" id="intro-kana-lbl"></div>
        <div class="intro-club-lbl" id="intro-club-lbl"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="intro-progress-wrap">
        <div class="intro-progress-fill" id="intro-progress-fill"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

<script type="module">
  import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
  import { doc, getDoc, getDocs, collection, query, where, onSnapshot, orderBy, updateDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
  import { normalizeClassRules, calcBonus, extractTbScores, calcQualifyingScore, calcQualifyingTb, calcFinalScore, compareRankEntries } from "./shared.js";
  import { getTournament, getAthletes } from "./cache.js";

  const params    = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const eventId   = params.get('event')?.trim();
  let skillMaster = [], athleteMap = {}, tournamentName = '---';
  let lastResultId = null;
  let tournamentData = null;  // numExecJudges 等を保持

  // ---- ラベル ----
  function getRoundLabel(rid) {
    const labels = { pre1:'QUALIFYING ROUND 1', pre2:'QUALIFYING ROUND 2', final:'FINAL ROUND' };
    return labels[rid] || rid.toUpperCase();
  }

  // ---- 中間2名合計（個人用）: CJP の middle2 と同じ計算式 ----
  function mid2sum(vals) {
    const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
    if (filled.length < 2) return 0;
    filled.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const mid = Math.floor((filled.length - 2) / 2);
    return filled[mid] + filled[mid + 1];
  }

  // ---- 中央値1名（3人から真ん中） ----
  function median1(vals) {
    const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
    if (filled.length === 0) return null;
    filled.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (filled.length === 1) return filled[0];
    if (filled.length === 2) return (filled[0] + filled[1]) / 2;
    return filled[Math.floor(filled.length / 2)];
  }

  // ---- シンクロ用E減点（審判数に応じた平均） ----
  function synchroEAvg(vals, numE) {
    const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
    if (filled.length === 0) return 0;
    if (numE <= 4) {
      return filled.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / filled.length;
    }
    // 6人: E1,3,5の中間値 + E2,4,6の中間値 → 平均
    const oddVals  = [vals[0], vals[2], vals[4]];
    const evenVals = [vals[1], vals[3], vals[5]];
    const medOdd  = median1(oddVals);
    const medEven = median1(evenVals);
    if (medOdd === null && medEven === null) return 0;
    if (medOdd === null) return medEven;
    if (medEven === null) return medOdd;
    return (medOdd + medEven) / 2;
  }

  // ---- パネル別審判データ取得 ----
  function getPanelJudges(resData) {
    const allJudges = resData.execution?.judges || {};
    if (Object.keys(allJudges).length === 0) return null;
    const byPanel = {};
    Object.entries(allJudges).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      const p    = k.split('_')[0];
      const jKey = k.slice(p.length + 1);
      if (!byPanel[p]) byPanel[p] = {};
      byPanel[p][jKey] = v;
    });
    return Object.values(byPanel).reduce(
      (best, cur) => Object.keys(cur).length > Object.keys(best).length ? cur : best, {}
    );
  }

  // ---- 選手がシンクロかどうか ----
  function isSynchroAthlete(athleteId) {
    return (athleteMap[athleteId]?.type || 'individual') === 'synchro';
  }

  // ---- クラス別E審判人数を返す ----
  function getNumEForAthlete(athleteId) {
    const ath = athleteMap[athleteId];
    if (ath) {
      const type = ath.type || 'individual';
      const cls  = ath.class || ath.category || '';
      const n = parseInt(tournamentData?.classRules?.[type]?.[cls]?.eJudgeCount);
      if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
    }
    return tournamentData?.numExecJudges || 6;
  }

  // ---- 技ごとのE減点（データなし→null、表示は×10の整数） ----
  function getSkillEDed(panelJudges, si, synchro, numE) {
    if (!panelJudges) return null;
    const jKeys = Object.keys(panelJudges);
    if (jKeys.length < 2) return null;
    const vals   = jKeys.map(k => panelJudges[k]?.deductions?.[si] ?? null);
    const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null);
    if (filled.length === 0) return null;
    return synchro ? synchroEAvg(vals, numE) : mid2sum(vals);
  }

  // ---- 着地E減点 ----
  function getLandingDed(panelJudges, synchro, numE) {
    if (!panelJudges) return null;
    const jKeys = Object.keys(panelJudges);
    if (jKeys.length < 2) return null;
    const vals   = jKeys.map(k => panelJudges[k]?.landing ?? null);
    const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null);
    if (filled.length === 0) return null;
    return synchro ? synchroEAvg(vals, numE) : mid2sum(vals);
  }

  // ---- E合計スコア ----
  function calcMedE(resData) {
    if (resData.officialE !== undefined && resData.officialE !== null) return resData.officialE;
    const panelJudges = getPanelJudges(resData);
    if (!panelJudges) return null;
    const jKeys  = Object.keys(panelJudges);
    if (jKeys.length < 2) return null;
    const vLimit = resData.validCount ?? 10;
    const synchro = isSynchroAthlete(resData.athleteId);
    const numE = getNumEForAthlete(resData.athleteId);
    let totalDed = 0;
    for (let si = 0; si < vLimit; si++) {
      const vals = jKeys.map(k => panelJudges[k]?.deductions?.[si] ?? null);
      totalDed += synchro ? synchroEAvg(vals, numE) : mid2sum(vals);
    }
    if (vLimit === 10) {
      const lVals = jKeys.map(k => panelJudges[k]?.landing ?? null);
      totalDed += synchro ? synchroEAvg(lVals, numE) : mid2sum(lVals);
    }
    const basePerSkill = synchro ? 1.0 : 2.0;
    return vLimit * basePerSkill - totalDed;
  }

  // ---- 選手の他ラウンド結果取得 ----
  async function getAthleteResults(athleteId) {
    if (!eventId || !athleteId) return {};
    try {
      const q    = query(collection(db, "results"), where("eventId", "==", eventId), where("athleteId", "==", athleteId));
      const snap = await getDocs(q);
      const res  = {};
      snap.docs.forEach(d => {
        const data = d.data();
        if (data.officialTotal !== undefined) res[data.round] = data.officialTotal;
      });
      return res;
    } catch { return {}; }
  }

  // ---- 順位計算（タイブレーク対応）----
  // タイブレーク: 個人=T→H→D, シンクロ=S(=tof)→H→D
  // extractTbScores は shared.js の共通実装を使用

  async function calcRank(ath, round) {
    // fetchRankingEntriesFor の結果を流用（タイブレーク T→H→D 適用済みソート）
    // 同点時も正確な順位を返す。追加 Firestore クエリなし（_rankingCache 共用）。
    const classVal  = ath.class  || '';
    const genderVal = ath.gender || '';
    const typeVal   = ath.type   || 'individual';
    try {
      const entries = await fetchRankingEntriesFor(classVal, genderVal, typeVal, round);
      const idx = entries.findIndex(e => e.id === ath.id);
      if (idx < 0) return null;
      // ソート済みリストで「自分と真の同点でない上位選手」の数をカウント → rank
      const cur = entries[idx];
      function areTied(x) {
        if (Math.abs((x.total ?? -Infinity) - (cur.total ?? -Infinity)) > 1e-9) return false;
        // 予選2本ベスト採用時：2本合計も同じか
        if (Math.abs(((x.pre1 ?? 0) + (x.pre2 ?? 0)) - ((cur.pre1 ?? 0) + (cur.pre2 ?? 0))) > 1e-9) return false;
        // T（ToF/Syn）→ H → D
        for (const k of ['tof', 'hd', 'diff']) {
          const vx = x.tb?.[k], vy = cur.tb?.[k];
          if (vx != null && vy != null && Math.abs(vx - vy) > 1e-9) return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
      let rank = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < idx; i++) {
        if (!areTied(entries[i])) rank++;
      }
      return rank;
    } catch { return null; }
  }

  // ---- メインレンダリング ----
  async function renderFocusResult(resultDoc) {
    const isNew   = resultDoc.id !== lastResultId;
    lastResultId  = resultDoc.id;
    const resData = resultDoc.data();
    const ath     = athleteMap[resData.athleteId] || {};

    // 別コンテンツ→スコア切替時: 旧コンテンツが一瞬見えるのを防ぐためモニターを隠す
    const monitor = document.getElementById('monitor');
    if (currentMode === 'ranking' || rankingPageTimer || isNew) {
      monitor.style.opacity = '0';
    }

    // ---- DNS（棄権）表示 ----
    if (resData.dns === true) {
      const classGenderDns = [ath.class || ath.category, ath.gender].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
      const orderLabelDns  = `No.${ath.order || '?'}`;
      const nameDns = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2
        ? `${ath.name1 || '---'} / ${ath.name2}`
        : (ath.name1 || '---');
      monitor.innerHTML = `
        <div class="convention-header">${tournamentName}</div>
        <div class="athlete-section">
          <div class="class-no">${classGenderDns} / ${orderLabelDns}</div>
          <div class="session-info">${getRoundLabel(resData.round)}</div>
          <div class="athlete-identity-group">
            <h1 class="athlete-name">${nameDns}</h1>
            <div class="athlete-team">${ath.club1 || ''}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:12px;">
          <div style="font-size:16cqw;font-weight:900;color:#e040fb;letter-spacing:0.12em;line-height:1;">DNS</div>
          <div style="font-size:3cqw;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.45);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;">棄権 — DID NOT START</div>
        </div>
        <footer class="footer-credits">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</footer>`;
      monitor.style.opacity = '1';
      lastShownResData = resData; lastShownAth = ath;
      return;
    }

    // 提出データ取得
    const subId   = `${eventId}_${resData.athleteId}_${resData.round}`;
    const subSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "submissions", subId));
    const sub     = subSnap.exists() ? subSnap.data() : { skills: [] };

    const vLimit        = resData.validCount ?? 10;
    const officialSkills= resData.officialSkills || [];
    // officialE の有無に関わらず、技ごとの表示には審判ごとのデータを使う
    const panelJudges   = getPanelJudges(resData);
    const synchro       = isSynchroAthlete(resData.athleteId);
    const numE          = getNumEForAthlete(resData.athleteId);

    // ---- 指定難度 ----
    const _classRule  = getClassRule(ath);
    const _rawDesig   = _classRule.designatedDDCount ?? -1;
    const _validDesig = _rawDesig >= 0
        && _classRule.qualifyingRoutines === 2
        && _classRule.scoreMode === 'total'
        && resData.round === 'pre1';
    const desigCount   = _validDesig ? _rawDesig : -1;
    const desigIndices = resData.officialDesignatedDDIndices || sub?.designatedDDIndices || [];

    // ---- 技ごとデータ計算 ----
    const seenIds = new Set();
    let baseDD = 0, tripleCount = 0, hasAnyDiff = false;

    const hasManualDDValues = !!(resData.officialDDValues && resData.officialDDValues.length > 0);

    const skillCols = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      if (i >= vLimit) {
        skillCols.push(`
          <div class="skill-stack" style="opacity:0.25;">
            <div class="col-idx num">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</div>
            <div class="card e-card"><div class="e-val num" style="color:var(--text-muted);">--</div></div>
            <div class="card d-card">
              <div class="d-code">-</div>
              <div class="d-val num">-</div>
            </div>
          </div>`);
        continue;
      }

      const offRaw     = officialSkills[i];
      const skill      = offRaw
        ? (skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(offRaw)) || skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === String(offRaw)))
        : undefined;
      const subRaw     = sub.skills?.[i] || '';
      // subSkill: オブジェクト形式（entry.htmlが保存する{id,skill,dd}）はidでID検索、文字列は旧形式互換
      const subSkill   = (() => {
        if (!subRaw) return null;
        if (typeof subRaw === 'object' && subRaw !== null) {
          return subRaw.id
            ? skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(subRaw.id))
            : skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === (subRaw.skill || ''));
        }
        return isNaN(subRaw)
          ? skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === String(subRaw))
          : skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(subRaw));
      })();

      const isDiff    = !!(skill && subSkill && skill.symbol !== subSkill.symbol);
      // 重複判定はFirestoreドキュメントID基準（同名・同方向でもnoteが異なる別技を誤検知しない）
      const repeatKey = skill ? skill.id : String(offRaw);
      const isRepeat  = !!(skill && seenIds.has(repeatKey));
      if (isDiff) hasAnyDiff = true;

      const isDesigSkill = desigCount < 0
          || (desigCount > 0 && desigIndices.includes(i));

      let displayDD = 0, dCodeTxt, dValTxt;
      if (hasManualDDValues) {
        // 手動入力: officialDDValues から各技の DD 値を復元
        displayDD = Number(resData.officialDDValues[i]) || 0;
        if (!isRepeat && offRaw && isDesigSkill) baseDD += displayDD;
        if (skill && !isRepeat) seenIds.add(repeatKey); // 重複検出用キーを登録
        // 技マスターからの表示用シンボルを優先（シンボル優先・IDフォールバック）
        const skillM = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(offRaw))
            || skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === String(offRaw));
        dCodeTxt = skillM ? (skillM.displaySymbol || skillM.symbol) : (offRaw || '-');
        dValTxt  = !isDesigSkill ? '-' : displayDD.toFixed(1);
      } else {
        if (skill && !isRepeat) {
          seenIds.add(repeatKey);
          if (isDesigSkill) {
            displayDD = skill.dd;
            baseDD   += skill.dd;
            if (skill.isTriple) tripleCount++;
          }
        }
        dCodeTxt = skill ? (skill.displaySymbol || skill.symbol) : '-';
        dValTxt  = !skill        ? '-'
                 : !isDesigSkill ? '-'
                 : isRepeat      ? '0.0'
                 :                 displayDD.toFixed(1);
      }

      // officialEPerSkill があれば CJP が計算した値をそのまま使う（パネル不一致を回避）
      const eDedVal  = (resData.officialEPerSkill?.[i] != null)
          ? resData.officialEPerSkill[i]
          : getSkillEDed(panelJudges, i, synchro, numE);
      const eDisplay = eDedVal !== null
          ? (synchro ? (eDedVal * 10).toFixed(2) : Math.round(eDedVal * 10))
          : '--';
      const codeAlert = isDiff;             // 技名は変更時のみ赤
      const valAlert  = isDiff || isRepeat; // DD値は変更・重複どちらも赤
      const dimCode = !isDesigSkill && !codeAlert; // 非指定かつ差異なし→薄く
      const dimVal  = !isDesigSkill && !valAlert;

      skillCols.push(`
        <div class="skill-stack">
          <div class="col-idx num">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</div>
          <div class="card e-card"><div class="e-val num${synchro ? ' syn' : ''}">${eDisplay}</div></div>
          <div class="card d-card">
            <div class="d-code${codeAlert ? ' alert' : dimCode ? ' dim' : ''}">${dCodeTxt}</div>
            <div class="d-val num${valAlert ? ' alert' : dimVal ? ' dim' : ''}">${dValTxt}</div>
          </div>
        </div>`);
    }

    // ---- ボーナス計算 ----
    const bonus   = hasManualDDValues ? (resData.officialDDBonus || 0) : calcBonus(ath.gender, tripleCount);
    const finalDD = hasManualDDValues ? (resData.officialTotalDD || baseDD + bonus) : baseDD + bonus;

    // LAN列（officialELanding があれば CJP 値を優先）
    const landDed = (resData.officialELanding != null)
        ? resData.officialELanding
        : getLandingDed(panelJudges, synchro, numE);
    const isLanValid = vLimit === 10;
    const bonusCol = `
      <div class="skill-stack bonus-style" style="${isLanValid ? '' : 'opacity:0.25;'}">
        <div class="col-idx">LAN</div>
        <div class="card e-card"><div class="e-val num${synchro ? ' syn' : ''}">${isLanValid && landDed !== null ? (synchro ? (landDed * 10).toFixed(2) : Math.round(landDed * 10)) : '--'}</div></div>
        <div class="bonus-note-block" style="${bonus > 0 ? '' : 'opacity:0.25;'}">
          <span>D-SCORE</span>
          <span>BONUS</span>
        </div>
        <div class="bonus-dval" style="${bonus > 0 ? '' : 'opacity:0.25;'}">
          <span class="bonus-val num">+${bonus.toFixed(1)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    // ---- スコア集計 ----
    const dScore = resData.officialD     !== undefined ? resData.officialD     : finalDD;
    const eScore = calcMedE(resData);
    const tScore = resData.tof           !== undefined ? resData.tof           : null;
    const hdScore= resData.hd?.total     !== undefined ? resData.hd?.total     : null;
    const pScore = resData.penalty       !== undefined ? resData.penalty       : 0;

    let totalScore = null;
    if (resData.officialTotal !== undefined) {
      totalScore = resData.officialTotal;
    } else if (eScore !== null && tScore !== null && hdScore !== null) {
      totalScore = Math.max(0, dScore + eScore + tScore + hdScore - pScore);
    }

    const isPublished = resData.published === true;
    const fmt1 = v => (v !== null && v !== undefined) ? Number(v).toFixed(1) : '--';
    const fmt2 = v => (v !== null && v !== undefined) ? Number(v).toFixed(2) : '--';
    const fmt3 = v => (v !== null && v !== undefined) ? Number(v).toFixed(3) : '--';

    // ---- 他ラウンド結果 & 順位 ----
    const athResults = await getAthleteResults(resData.athleteId);
    // 予選合計（BEST/TOTAL 表示用）
    const rule2   = getClassRule(ath);
    const numPre2 = rule2.qualifyingRoutines ?? tournamentData?.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
    const sMode2  = rule2.scoreMode || 'best';
    let qualTotal = totalScore;
    if (numPre2 >= 2 && (resData.round === 'pre1' || resData.round === 'pre2')) {
      const p1 = resData.round === 'pre1' ? totalScore : (athResults['pre1'] ?? null);
      const p2 = resData.round === 'pre2' ? totalScore : (athResults['pre2'] ?? null);
      if (p1 != null && p2 != null) qualTotal = sMode2 === 'total' ? p1 + p2 : Math.max(p1, p2);
      else qualTotal = p1 ?? p2;
    }
    // 順位計算: fetchRankingEntriesFor の結果を流用（タイブレーク T→H→D 込み）
    const rank = await calcRank(ath, resData.round);

    // ---- ルーティン合計 ----
    let routineTotalsHTML = '';
    if (resData.round === 'pre1' || resData.round === 'pre2') {
      const r1 = athResults['pre1'];
      const r2 = athResults['pre2'];
      routineTotalsHTML = `
        <div class="routine-box${resData.round === 'pre1' ? ' active' : ''}">
          <label>ROUTINE 1</label>
          <div class="val num">${r1 !== undefined ? Number(r1).toFixed(2) : '--'}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="routine-box${resData.round === 'pre2' ? ' active' : ''}">
          <label>ROUTINE 2</label>
          <div class="val num">${r2 !== undefined ? Number(r2).toFixed(2) : '--'}</div>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      routineTotalsHTML = `
        <div class="routine-box active">
          <label>FINAL</label>
          <div class="val num">${fmt2(totalScore)}</div>
        </div>`;
    }

    // ---- セッション情報 ----
    const dateStr    = new Date().toLocaleDateString('ja-JP').replace(/\//g, '.');
    const groupStr   = ath.flightGroup ? `PANEL ${ath.flightGroup}` : '';
    const sessionLine2 = [groupStr, dateStr].filter(Boolean).join(' | ');
    const classGender  = [ath.class, ath.gender].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const orderLabel   = (resData.round === 'final' && ath.finalOrder != null)
      ? `No.${ath.finalOrder}`
      : `No.${ath.order || '?'}`;

    // ---- フルHTML生成 ----
    const html = `
      <div class="convention-header">${tournamentName}</div>

      <div class="athlete-section">
        <div class="class-no">${classGender} / ${orderLabel}</div>
        <div class="session-info">
          ${getRoundLabel(resData.round)}<br>
          ${sessionLine2}
        </div>
        <div class="athlete-identity-group">
          ${ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2
            ? `<h1 class="athlete-name" style="font-size:5cqw">${ath.name1 || '---'} <span style="opacity:0.5;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:0">/</span> ${ath.name2}</h1>
               <div class="athlete-team">${ath.club1 || '---'}</div>`
            : `<h1 class="athlete-name">${ath.name1 || '---'}</h1>
               <div class="athlete-team">${ath.club1 || '---'}</div>`
          }
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="ribbon-wrapper">
        <div class="row-labels">
          <div class="row-title idx-label">Skill No</div>
          <div class="row-title e-label">E-Deduction</div>
          <div class="row-title d-sub-label">Skill Code</div>
          <div class="row-title d-sub-label">D-Score</div>
        </div>
        ${skillCols.join('')}
        ${bonusCol}
      </div>

      <div class="breakdown-row">
        <div class="stat-item"><label>Exec.</label><div class="val num">${fmt3(eScore)}</div></div>
        <div class="stat-item"><label>Diff.</label><div class="val num${hasAnyDiff ? ' alert':''}">${fmt1(dScore)}</div></div>
        <div class="stat-item"><label>${synchro ? 'Syn' : 'ToF'}</label><div class="val num">${fmt3(tScore)}</div></div>
        <div class="stat-item"><label>HD</label><div class="val num">${fmt2(hdScore)}</div></div>
        <div class="stat-item"><label>Penalty</label><div class="val num${pScore > 0 ? ' alert' : ''}">${fmt1(pScore)}</div></div>
      </div>

      <div class="result-section">
        <div class="routine-totals">${routineTotalsHTML}</div>
        <div class="final-results">
          <div class="score-unit">
            ${(() => {
              // 2本制の場合は best/total ともに qualTotal（累計またはベスト）を表示
              const isMultiRound = numPre2 >= 2 && (resData.round === 'pre1' || resData.round === 'pre2');
              const lbl  = (isMultiRound && sMode2 === 'best') ? 'BEST' : 'TOTAL';
              const disp = isMultiRound ? qualTotal : totalScore;
              return `<label>${isPublished ? lbl : lbl + '*'}</label><div class="total-val num">${fmt2(disp)}</div>`;
            })()}
          </div>
          <div class="score-unit">
            <label>RANK</label>
            <div class="rank-val num">${rank !== null ? rank : '--'}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <footer class="footer-credits">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</footer>`;

    // 順位表用にデータを保持
    lastShownResData = resData;
    lastShownAth     = ath;

    // 既存タイマー・オーバーレイをリセット
    if (rankingTimer) { clearTimeout(rankingTimer); rankingTimer = null; }
    stopRankingLoop();
    let overlay = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('visible');

    if (isNew) {
      monitor.classList.remove('no-anim');
    } else {
      monitor.classList.add('no-anim');
    }
    monitor.innerHTML = html;
    monitor.style.opacity = '';

    // オーバーレイを再アタッチ（innerHTML で消えるため）
    if (!overlay) { overlay = document.createElement('div'); overlay.id = 'ranking-overlay'; }
    // carousel-mode クラスを保持しつつリセット（巡回中にスコアが割り込んだ場合も carousel-mode を維持）
    overlay.className = carouselActive ? 'carousel-mode' : '';
    overlay.innerHTML = '';
    monitor.appendChild(overlay);

    // スコア表示後 → キュー処理 or 順位表ループへ
    const displayDelay = (displaySettings.animTotalSec + displaySettings.holdSec) * 1000;
    rankingTimer = setTimeout(() => onScoreDisplayDone(), displayDelay);
  }

  // ==== 表示設定（Firestoreから読み込み、デフォルト値付き） ====
  let displaySettings = {
    animTotalSec:  10,   // スコアアニメーションが完了するまでの秒数
    holdSec:        3,   // アニメーション完了後に保持する秒数
    rankingRows:   12,   // 順位表の1ページあたりの表示人数
    rankingPageSec: 8,   // 順位表ページ送りの間隔（秒）
  };

  let rankingTimer     = null;   // スコア表示→順位表遷移タイマー
  let rankingPageTimer = null;   // 順位表ページ切替タイマー
  let currentMode      = 'idle'; // 'idle' | 'score' | 'ranking' | 'carousel'
  let displayQueue     = [];     // 表示待ちの resultDoc キュー
  let lastShownResData = null;   // 最後に表示したスコアのデータ（順位表用）
  let lastShownAth     = null;   // 最後に表示した選手情報
  let currentRankingPage = 0;
  let allRankingEntries  = [];
  let savedVenueScreen = null;  // スコア表示中に退避した会場画面（warmup等）

  // ---- 順位表 巡回モード ----
  let carouselActive   = false;
  let carouselItems    = [];   // [{classVal, gender, type}]
  let carouselIndex    = 0;
  let carouselShowLogo = false;
  let carouselPageIdx  = 0;
  let carouselEntries  = [];
  let carouselTimer    = null;

  function enqueueResult(resultDoc) {
    invalidateRankingCache();
    // 同一ドキュメントIDがキューに既存なら最新版で上書き（修正再確定時に旧スコアを表示しない）
    const idx = displayQueue.findIndex(d => d.id === resultDoc.id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      displayQueue[idx] = resultDoc;
    } else {
      displayQueue.push(resultDoc);
    }
    if (currentMode !== 'score') processQueue();
  }

  async function processQueue() {
    if (displayQueue.length === 0) {
      if (lastShownResData) startRankingLoop();
      return;
    }
    // currentMode を変える前に判定する（変えた後だと ranking チェックが常に false になる）
    if (currentMode === 'ranking' || rankingPageTimer) {
      const monitor = document.getElementById('monitor');
      if (monitor) monitor.style.opacity = '0';
    }
    currentMode = 'score';
    stopRankingLoop();
    const resultDoc = displayQueue.shift();
    await renderFocusResult(resultDoc);
  }

  function onScoreDisplayDone() {
    // スコア表示完了 → キューに次があれば続行、なければ順位表へ
    // 順位表も終わったら退避していた会場画面（warmup等）を復元
    processQueue();
  }

  function restoreSavedVenueScreen() {
    if (savedVenueScreen === 'warmup') {
      document.getElementById('vs-warmup')?.classList.add('active');
      // タイマーはintervalが残っていれば継続中、止まっていれば再起動
      if (!warmupTimerInterval && tournamentData?.warmupSettings) {
        startWarmupDisplay(tournamentData.warmupSettings);
      }
    } else if (savedVenueScreen === 'intro') {
      document.getElementById('vs-intro')?.classList.add('active');
      if (tournamentData?.introSettings) applyIntroSlide(tournamentData.introSettings);
    }
    savedVenueScreen = null;
  }

  // ---- 順位表ループ ----
  async function startRankingLoop() {
    currentMode = 'ranking';
    allRankingEntries = await fetchRankingEntries();
    currentRankingPage = 0;
    showRankingPage();
  }

  function stopRankingLoop() {
    if (rankingPageTimer) { clearTimeout(rankingPageTimer); rankingPageTimer = null; }
    const overlay = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('visible');
  }

  function getClassRule(ath) {
    const classRules = normalizeClassRules(tournamentData?.classRules);
    const selType = (ath.type || 'individual');
    const classVal = ath.class || ath.category || '';
    return (classRules[selType] || {})[classVal] || {};
  }

  // ---- 順位表クエリキャッシュ（巡回中の重複Firestoreクエリ防止）----
  let _rankingCache = null;   // { docs: [], time: number }
  const RANKING_CACHE_MS = 60_000;
  function invalidateRankingCache() { _rankingCache = null; }
  async function _getPublishedResultDocs() {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (_rankingCache && now - _rankingCache.time < RANKING_CACHE_MS) return _rankingCache.docs;
    const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, "results"),
      where("eventId", "==", eventId), where("published", "==", true)));
    _rankingCache = { docs: snap.docs, time: now };
    return snap.docs;
  }

  // クラス指定でランキングエントリを取得（巡回モード・通常モード共用）
  async function fetchRankingEntriesFor(classVal, genderVal, selType, round) {
    const rule      = getClassRule({ class: classVal, type: selType });
    const numPre    = rule.qualifyingRoutines ?? tournamentData?.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
    const scoreMode = rule.scoreMode || 'best';
    try {
      const resultDocs = await _getPublishedResultDocs();
      const byAth = {};
      resultDocs.forEach(d => {
        const r = d.data();
        const a = athleteMap[r.athleteId];
        if (!a) return;
        if ((a.class || a.category || '') !== classVal) return;
        if ((a.gender || '') !== genderVal) return;
        if ((a.type || 'individual') !== selType) return;
        if (!byAth[r.athleteId]) byAth[r.athleteId] = { a, pre1: null, pre2: null, final: null, pre1r: null, pre2r: null, finr: null };
        if (r.round === 'pre1')  { byAth[r.athleteId].pre1 = r.officialTotal ?? null; byAth[r.athleteId].pre1r = r; }
        if (r.round === 'pre2')  { byAth[r.athleteId].pre2 = r.officialTotal ?? null; byAth[r.athleteId].pre2r = r; }
        if (r.round === 'final') { byAth[r.athleteId].final = r.officialTotal ?? null; byAth[r.athleteId].finr  = r; }
      });

      // 決勝判定: round='final'明示 or 自動検出（finalスコアあり＆finalOrder設定済みの選手がいる）
      const hasFinalResults = Object.values(byAth).some(s => s.final !== null);
      const hasFinalOrder   = Object.keys(byAth).some(id => athleteMap[id]?.finalOrder != null);
      const isFinal = round === 'final' || (round == null && hasFinalResults && hasFinalOrder);

      // 決勝モード: finalOrder確定済み選手 or 決勝スコアが存在する選手のみ
      // （表示ページがfinalOrder保存前にロードされてキャッシュが古い場合のフォールバック）
      let entries = Object.entries(byAth);
      if (isFinal) {
        entries = entries.filter(([id, s]) =>
          athleteMap[id]?.finalOrder != null || s.final !== null
        );
      }

      return entries
        .map(([id, s]) => {
          // 予選スコア計算（shared.js 共通関数）
          const qualPre = calcQualifyingScore(s.pre1, s.pre2, numPre, scoreMode);
          // タイブレーク用データ
          let tbSum, tb;
          if (isFinal) {
            tbSum = null;
            tb = extractTbScores(s.finr); // 決勝: 決勝ラウンドの T→H→D
          } else {
            ({ tbSum, tb } = calcQualifyingTb(s.pre1, s.pre2, s.pre1r, s.pre2r, numPre, scoreMode));
          }
          // 合計スコア（表示・ランキング共用）
          const finalScoreMode = rule.finalScoreMode ?? 'zero';
          const total = isFinal ? calcFinalScore(qualPre, s.final, finalScoreMode) : qualPre;
          return { id, ...s, qualScore: total, total, tbSum, tb, _isFinal: isFinal };
        })
        .sort(compareRankEntries);
    } catch(e) { console.error(e); return []; }
  }

  async function fetchRankingEntries() {
    if (!lastShownResData) return [];
    const ath = lastShownAth || {};
    const round = lastShownResData.round === 'final' ? 'final' : null;
    return fetchRankingEntriesFor(
      ath.class || ath.category || '',
      ath.gender || '',
      ath.type || 'individual',
      round
    );
  }

  function showRankingPage() {
    if (allRankingEntries.length === 0) return;
    const ath = lastShownAth || {};
    const rowsPerPage = displaySettings.rankingRows;
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(allRankingEntries.length / rowsPerPage);
    const page  = currentRankingPage % totalPages;
    const start = page * rowsPerPage;
    let pageEntries = allRankingEntries.slice(start, start + rowsPerPage);
    while (pageEntries.length < rowsPerPage) pageEntries.push(null);
    renderRankingOverlay(pageEntries, start, totalPages, page,
      ath.class || ath.category || '', ath.gender || '', ath.type || 'individual');
    rankingPageTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      currentRankingPage++;
      // 1周完了: 1位からの画面に戻って静止（オーバーレイは出したまま）
      if (currentRankingPage >= totalPages) {
        rankingPageTimer = null;
        currentMode = 'idle';
        currentRankingPage = 0;
        // 1ページ目を再描画してタイマーなしで静止
        let p1entries = allRankingEntries.slice(0, rowsPerPage);
        while (p1entries.length < rowsPerPage) p1entries.push(null);
        renderRankingOverlay(p1entries, 0, totalPages, 0,
          ath.class || ath.category || '', ath.gender || '', ath.type || 'individual');
        if (savedVenueScreen) restoreSavedVenueScreen();
        if (carouselActive) carouselNext();
      } else {
        showRankingPage();
      }
    }, displaySettings.rankingPageSec * 1000);
  }

  function renderRankingOverlay(entries, startOffset, totalPages, currentPage, classVal, genderVal, selType) {
    const overlay = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (!overlay) return;
    // 引数未指定時は lastShownAth からフォールバック（通常の確定表示）
    if (classVal === undefined) {
      const ath = lastShownAth || {};
      classVal  = ath.class || ath.category || '';
      genderVal = ath.gender || '';
      selType   = ath.type || 'individual';
    }
    const typeLabel = selType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロナイズド' : '個人';
    const heading   = [typeLabel, classVal, genderVal].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const f = v => v !== null && v !== undefined ? Number(v).toFixed(2) : '--';
    const curAthId = currentMode === 'carousel' ? null : lastShownResData?.athleteId;

    const rule           = getClassRule({ class: classVal, type: selType });
    const numPre         = rule.qualifyingRoutines ?? tournamentData?.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
    const scoreMode      = rule.scoreMode || 'best';
    const finalScoreMode = rule.finalScoreMode ?? 'zero';
    const scoreModeLabel = scoreMode === 'total' ? 'TOTAL' : 'BEST';
    const show2          = numPre >= 2;

    // 決勝モード判定（entriesの_isFinalフラグから取得）
    const isFinal = entries.find(e => e !== null)?._isFinal || false;

    // 表示する列を決定
    //   show2 (予選2本): R1 | R2 | BEST/TOTAL  (5列)
    //   isFinal + combined: FINAL | TOTAL       (4列)
    //   それ以外 (予選1本 / 決勝ゼロ): SCORE のみ (3列)
    const showR1 = show2 || (isFinal && finalScoreMode === 'combined');
    const showR2 = show2 && !isFinal;
    const colLayout = showR2
      ? '4cqw 1fr 12cqw 12cqw 16cqw'
      : showR1
        ? '4cqw 1fr 12cqw 16cqw'
        : '4cqw 1fr 16cqw';

    // 表示人数に応じて文字サイズをスケール（基準: 6行）
    const numRows = entries.length || displaySettings.rankingRows || 6;
    const rkScale = Math.max(0.65, Math.min(1.4, Math.sqrt(6 / numRows)));
    overlay.style.setProperty('--rk-cols',  colLayout);
    overlay.style.setProperty('--rk-scale', rkScale.toFixed(3));

    const rowsHTML = entries.map((e, i) => {
      if (!e) return `<div class="rk-row" style="visibility:hidden;"></div>`;
      const rank   = startOffset + i + 1;
      const cur    = e.id === curAthId;
      const hasAny = isFinal ? (e.final !== null || e.pre1 !== null) : (e.pre1 !== null || e.pre2 !== null);
      const r1val  = isFinal ? e.final : e.pre1;
      const r1col  = showR1 ? `<div class="rk-sc num">${f(r1val)}</div>` : '';
      const r2col  = showR2 ? `<div class="rk-sc num">${f(e.pre2)}</div>` : '';
      return `<div class="rk-row${cur ? ' rk-current' : ''}">
        <div class="rk-rank num">${rank}</div>
        <div class="rk-name-group">
          ${e.a.type === 'synchro' && e.a.name2
            ? `<div class="rk-name">${e.a.name1} <span style="opacity:0.45;font-weight:400">/</span> ${e.a.name2}</div>`
            : `<div class="rk-name">${e.a.name1 || '---'}</div>
               <div class="rk-team">${e.a.club1 || ''}</div>`
          }
        </div>
        ${r1col}${r2col}
        <div class="rk-tot num">${hasAny ? f(e.total) : '--'}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const pageIndicator = totalPages > 1
      ? `<span class="rk-page-info">${currentPage + 1} / ${totalPages}</span>`
      : '';

    const r1head    = showR1 ? `<span>${isFinal ? 'FINAL' : 'R1'}</span>` : '';
    const r2head    = showR2 ? '<span>R2</span>' : '';
    const totalHead = show2
      ? (isFinal ? 'TOTAL' : scoreModeLabel)
      : (isFinal && finalScoreMode === 'combined' ? 'TOTAL' : 'SCORE');

    overlay.innerHTML = `
      <div class="rk-event-name">${tournamentName}</div>
      <div class="rk-title">${heading} — ${isFinal ? 'FINAL ' : ''}STANDINGS ${pageIndicator}</div>
      <div class="rk-col-head">
        <span>#</span><span>NAME</span>${r1head}${r2head}<span>${totalHead}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="rk-table">${rowsHTML}</div>
      <div class="rk-footer">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</div>`;
    overlay.classList.add('visible');
  }

  // ---- 順位表 巡回モード ----
  function carouselStop() {
    if (carouselTimer) { clearTimeout(carouselTimer); carouselTimer = null; }
    const overlay = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('carousel-mode');
    stopRankingLoop();
    document.getElementById('vs-logo')?.classList.remove('active');
    currentMode = 'idle';
  }

  async function carouselLoadItem() {
    if (!carouselActive || carouselItems.length === 0) return;
    // 表示すべきエントリがあるクラスを探す（スキップ対応）
    let tries = 0;
    while (tries < carouselItems.length) {
      const item = carouselItems[carouselIndex % carouselItems.length];
      carouselEntries = await fetchRankingEntriesFor(item.classVal, item.gender, item.type);
      if (carouselEntries.length > 0) break;
      carouselIndex++;
      tries++;
    }
    if (carouselEntries.length === 0) { carouselStop(); return; }
    // carousel-mode が消えていた場合（スコア割り込み後など）に再適用
    const _ov = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (_ov) _ov.classList.add('carousel-mode');
    carouselPageIdx = 0;
    currentMode = 'carousel';
    carouselShowPage();
  }

  function carouselShowPage() {
    if (!carouselActive || currentMode === 'score' || currentMode === 'ranking') return;
    const item = carouselItems[carouselIndex % carouselItems.length];
    const rowsPerPage = displaySettings.rankingRows;
    const totalPages  = Math.ceil(carouselEntries.length / rowsPerPage);
    const start = carouselPageIdx * rowsPerPage;
    let pageEntries = carouselEntries.slice(start, start + rowsPerPage);
    while (pageEntries.length < rowsPerPage) pageEntries.push(null);
    renderRankingOverlay(pageEntries, start, totalPages, carouselPageIdx,
      item.classVal, item.gender, item.type);
    carouselTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      carouselPageIdx++;
      if (carouselPageIdx >= totalPages) {
        await carouselNext();
      } else {
        carouselShowPage();
      }
    }, displaySettings.rankingPageSec * 1000);
  }

  async function carouselNext() {
    if (carouselTimer) { clearTimeout(carouselTimer); carouselTimer = null; }
    if (!carouselActive) return;
    carouselIndex++;
    if (carouselIndex >= carouselItems.length) {
      // 全クラス終了
      carouselIndex = 0;
      if (carouselShowLogo) {
        // ロゴ画面を一定時間表示してからループ
        stopRankingLoop();
        document.getElementById('vs-logo')?.classList.add('active');
        carouselTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
          document.getElementById('vs-logo')?.classList.remove('active');
          await carouselLoadItem();
        }, displaySettings.rankingPageSec * 2 * 1000);
        return;
      }
    }
    await carouselLoadItem();
  }

  async function carouselStart() {
    if (!carouselActive || carouselItems.length === 0) return;
    if (currentMode === 'score' || currentMode === 'ranking') return;
    // overlay を全画面固定・即表示（venue screens / score 画面を隠す）
    const overlay = document.getElementById('ranking-overlay');
    if (overlay) { overlay.classList.add('carousel-mode', 'visible'); overlay.innerHTML = ''; }
    carouselIndex = 0;
    await carouselLoadItem();
  }

  // ---- アニメーション設定をCSS変数で適用 ----
  // animTotalSec から duration / stagger を比率（元の 2600:1400 = 13:7）で自動計算
  // 5要素分の stagger + duration = animTotal なので:
  //   stagger = animTotal * 7/48、duration = animTotal * 13/48
  function applyAnimSettings() {
    const total    = displaySettings.animTotalSec * 1000;
    const stagger  = Math.round(total * 7 / 48);
    const duration = Math.round(total * 13 / 48);
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--anim-duration', duration + 'ms');
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--anim-stagger',  stagger  + 'ms');
  }

  // ---- 会場画面オーバーレイ管理 ----
  let warmupTimerInterval = null;

  function applyVenueScreen(tourData) {
    const screen = tourData.venueScreen || 'score';
    const warmup = tourData.warmupSettings  || {};
    const ts     = tourData.titleSettings   || {};
    const cs     = tourData.ceremonySettings|| {};
    const name   = tournamentName || '---';

    // フォントサイズ CSS変数を適用
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vs-title-fs',    (ts.fontSize      || 6.5) + 'vw');
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vs-cer-name-fs', (cs.nameFontSize  || 3.5) + 'vw');
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vs-cer-label-fs',(cs.labelFontSize || 10)  + 'vw');
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vs-class-fs',    (warmup.classFontSize || 4.5) + 'vw');
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--vs-timer-fs',    (warmup.timerFontSize || 24)  + 'vw');

    // 大会名を各画面に反映
    // タイトル3行表示（line1未設定なら大会名をフォールバック）
    const lines = [
        ts.line1 || name,
        ts.line2 || '',
        ts.line3 || ''
    ];
    ['vs-title-line1','vs-title-line2','vs-title-line3'].forEach((id, i) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!el) return;
        el.textContent = lines[i];
        el.style.display = lines[i] ? '' : 'none';
    });
    document.getElementById('vs-cer-name').textContent   = name;

    // クラス名（2クラスまで）
    const classes = [warmup.class1, warmup.class2].filter(Boolean);
    document.getElementById('vs-class-names').textContent = classes.join('\n');
    document.getElementById('vs-warmup-event').textContent = tournamentName || '';

    // 全画面を一旦非表示
    ['vs-logo','vs-title','vs-ceremony','vs-warmup','vs-intro','vs-calculating','vs-final-startlist'].forEach(id => {
      document.getElementById(id)?.classList.remove('active');
    });
    // スコア表示中にwarmupを退避している場合はタイマーを止めない
    if (!savedVenueScreen) {
      clearInterval(warmupTimerInterval);
      warmupTimerInterval = null;
    }

    // 集計中は最優先（他の画面より前面）
    if (tourData.calculating) {
      const calcEl = document.getElementById('vs-calculating');
      const ath = athleteMap[tourData.calculatingAthleteId || ''] || {};
      const classGender = [ath.class, ath.gender].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
      const orderPart   = ath.order ? ` / No.${ath.order}` : '';
      const roundLbl    = tourData.calculatingRound ? getRoundLabel(tourData.calculatingRound) : '';
      const nameHtml = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2
        ? `<div class="calc-name" style="font-size:4.8cqw">${ath.name1||'---'} <span style="opacity:0.45;font-weight:400">/</span> ${ath.name2}</div>`
        : `<div class="calc-name">${ath.name1||'---'}</div>`;
      calcEl.innerHTML = `
        <div class="calc-layout">
          <div class="calc-conv">${tournamentName}</div>
          <div class="calc-meta">
            <div class="calc-class">${classGender}${orderPart}</div>
            <div class="calc-round">${roundLbl}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="calc-name-block">
            ${nameHtml}
            <div class="calc-team">${ath.club1||''}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="calc-status">得点集計中</div>
          <div class="calc-credit">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</div>
        </div>`;
      calcEl.classList.add('active');
      return;
    }

    // ---- 巡回モード制御 ----
    const carousel = tourData.rankingCarousel || {};
    const wasCarousel = carouselActive;
    carouselActive   = !!carousel.active;
    carouselItems    = carousel.items    || [];
    carouselShowLogo = !!carousel.showLogo;

    if (carouselActive && !wasCarousel) {
      // 巡回開始
      carouselStart();
      return; // 通常の venue screen 表示はしない
    }
    if (!carouselActive && wasCarousel) {
      // 巡回停止 → 通常画面を再適用（fall through）
      carouselStop();
    }
    if (carouselActive) return; // 巡回中は venueScreen 表示に干渉しない（stopされるまで）

    // スコア表示中・キュー処理中は会場画面を上書きしない
    // （CJP の publish により calculating:false と results の2つの書き込みが走るため
    //   onSnapshot の発火順序によっては applyVenueScreen が後から venue 画面を再表示してしまう）
    if (currentMode === 'score' || displayQueue.length > 0) return;

    if (screen === 'logo') {
      document.getElementById('vs-logo').classList.add('active');
    } else if (screen === 'title') {
      document.getElementById('vs-title').classList.add('active');
    } else if (screen === 'ceremony') {
      document.getElementById('vs-ceremony').classList.add('active');
    } else if (screen === 'warmup') {
      document.getElementById('vs-warmup').classList.add('active');
      startWarmupDisplay(warmup);
    } else if (screen === 'intro') {
      document.getElementById('vs-intro').classList.add('active');
      applyIntroSlide(tourData.introSettings || {});
    } else if (screen === 'final_startlist') {
      document.getElementById('vs-final-startlist').classList.add('active');
      renderFinalStartlist(tourData.finalStartlistTarget || {});
    }
    // 'score' の場合はオーバーレイなし（通常のスコア表示）
  }

  function startWarmupDisplay(warmup) {
    // timerEndMs（開始時に Date.now()+残り時間 で確定した絶対終了時刻）を使用。
    // 旧データ互換: timerEndMs がない場合は timerRef + timerRemainingMs で終了時刻を推定。
    clearInterval(warmupTimerInterval);
    warmupTimerInterval = null;
    const timerEndMs = warmup.timerEndMs != null ? warmup.timerEndMs
      : (warmup.timerRef?.toMillis ? warmup.timerRef.toMillis() + (warmup.timerRemainingMs ?? 0) : null);
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('vs-timer');
    function tick() {
      if (!timerEl) return;
      const running = !warmup.timerPaused && timerEndMs != null;
      const rem = running
        ? Math.max(0, timerEndMs - Date.now())
        : (warmup.timerRemainingMs ?? 0);
      showTimerDisplay(timerEl, rem);
      if (rem === 0 && running) { clearInterval(warmupTimerInterval); warmupTimerInterval = null; }
    }
    tick();
    if (!warmup.timerPaused && timerEndMs != null) {
      warmupTimerInterval = setInterval(tick, 500);
    }
  }

  function showTimerDisplay(el, ms) {
    const totalSec = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
    const min = Math.floor(totalSec / 60);
    const sec = totalSec % 60;
    el.textContent = `${String(min).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    el.className = 'vs-timer num' +
      (ms === 0 ? ' done' : ms < 60000 ? ' warning' : '');
  }

  function applyIntroSlide(settings) {
    const athletes  = settings.athletes  || [];
    const idx       = settings.currentIndex ?? 0;
    const total     = athletes.length;
    const durationMs= settings.durationMs || 5000;
    if (total === 0) return;
    const ath = athletes[Math.min(idx, total - 1)];

    document.getElementById('intro-event-lbl').textContent   = tournamentName;
    document.getElementById('intro-counter-lbl').textContent = `${idx + 1} / ${total}`;

    const classGender = [ath.class, ath.gender].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
    const orderStr    = ath.order ? `No. ${ath.order}` : '';
    const name  = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2
                  ? `${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2}` : (ath.name1 || '');
    const kana  = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.kana2
                  ? `${ath.kana1 || ''} / ${ath.kana2 || ''}` : (ath.kana1 || '');
    const club  = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.club2 && ath.club1 !== ath.club2
                  ? `${ath.club1} / ${ath.club2}` : (ath.club1 || '');

    document.getElementById('intro-class-lbl').textContent = classGender;
    document.getElementById('intro-order-lbl').textContent = orderStr;
    document.getElementById('intro-name-lbl').textContent  = name;
    document.getElementById('intro-kana-lbl').textContent  = kana;
    document.getElementById('intro-club-lbl').textContent  = club;

    // カードアニメーション（フラッシュ＋子要素個別アニメ）
    const layout = document.getElementById('intro-layout');
    const card   = document.getElementById('intro-card');
    layout.classList.remove('intro-flash');
    card.classList.remove('intro-anim');
    void card.offsetWidth;
    layout.classList.add('intro-flash');
    card.classList.add('intro-anim');

    // プログレスバー（durationMs かけて 0→100% で残り時間を表示）
    const fill = document.getElementById('intro-progress-fill');
    fill.style.transition = 'none';
    fill.style.width = '0%';
    void fill.offsetWidth;
    fill.style.transition = `width ${durationMs}ms linear`;
    fill.style.width = '100%';
  }

  // ---- 決勝スタートリスト描画 ----
  function renderFinalStartlist(target) {
    const cls     = target.class  || '';
    const gender  = target.gender || '';
    const selType = target.type   || 'individual';
    const heading = [cls, gender, selType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人'].filter(Boolean).join(' ');

    // finalOrder が設定済みの選手を抽出してソート
    const finalists = Object.values(athleteMap)
      .filter(a =>
        (a.class || '') === cls &&
        (a.gender || '') === gender &&
        (a.type || 'individual') === selType &&
        a.finalOrder != null
      )
      .sort((a, b) => (a.finalOrder || 0) - (b.finalOrder || 0));

    // 人数に応じてフォントサイズをスケール
    const n = finalists.length || 1;
    const nameFsVw  = n <= 4 ? 5.0 : n <= 8 ? 3.8 : 2.8;
    const noFsVw    = nameFsVw * 0.85;
    const clubFsVw  = nameFsVw * 0.48;

    const rows = finalists.length > 0
      ? finalists.map(a => {
          const nameHtml = a.type === 'synchro' && a.name2
            ? `${a.name1 || '---'} / ${a.name2 || '---'}`
            : (a.name1 || '---');
          const clubHtml = a.type === 'synchro' && a.club2 && a.club1 !== a.club2
            ? `${a.club1 || ''} / ${a.club2 || ''}` : (a.club1 || '');
          return `<div class="fsl-row">
            <div class="fsl-no num">${a.finalOrder}</div>
            <div class="fsl-athlete">
              <div class="fsl-name">${nameHtml}</div>
              ${clubHtml ? `<div class="fsl-club">${clubHtml}</div>` : ''}
            </div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')
      : `<div style="text-align:center;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.28);font-size:1.5cqw;padding:4cqh;">
           スタートリストが登録されていません
         </div>`;

    document.getElementById('fsl-layout').innerHTML = `
      <div class="fsl-header">${tournamentName}</div>
      <div class="fsl-meta">
        <div class="fsl-final-badge">Final Round</div>
        <div class="fsl-class">${heading}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="fsl-table"
           style="--fsl-name-fs:${nameFsVw}cqw;--fsl-no-fs:${noFsVw}cqw;--fsl-club-fs:${clubFsVw}cqw;">
        ${rows}
      </div>
      <div class="fsl-footer">SYSTEM DESIGN &amp; DEVELOPMENT BY HIROTAKA.H</div>`;
  }

  // ---- 初期化 ----
  async function init() {
    if (!eventId) return;
    try {
      const [smSnap, athleteList, fetchedTour] = await Promise.all([
        getDocs(collection(db, "skillMaster")),
        getAthletes(eventId),
        getTournament(eventId)
      ]);

      skillMaster = smSnap.docs
        .sort((a, b) => (a.data().displayOrder ?? parseInt(a.id)) - (b.data().displayOrder ?? parseInt(b.id)))
        .map(d => ({
          id            : d.id,
          symbol        : d.data().symbol,
          displaySymbol : d.data().displaySymbol || d.data().symbol,
          dd            : parseFloat(d.data().dd) || 0,
          isTriple      : d.data().isTriple === true,
          direction     : d.data().direction || '',
          displayOrder  : d.data().displayOrder ?? parseInt(d.id)
        }));

      athleteList.forEach(a => athleteMap[a.id] = a);

      // 試技順をリアルタイム監視（管理者が決勝試技順を変更しても即反映）
      // display.html は朝から終日リロードしないため onSnapshot で常に最新を保持する
      onSnapshot(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'athletes'), snap => {
        snap.docChanges().forEach(change => {
          if (change.type === 'modified' || change.type === 'added') {
            const id = change.doc.id;
            const d  = change.doc.data();
            if (athleteMap[id]) {
              athleteMap[id].finalOrder = d.finalOrder ?? null;
              athleteMap[id].isInFinal  = d.isInFinal  ?? false;
            }
          }
        });
      });

      if (fetchedTour) {
        const tourData = fetchedTour;
        tournamentData = tourData;
        tournamentName = tourData.name || '---';
        document.getElementById('event-name-el').textContent = tournamentName;
        // displaySettings を読み込み
        if (tourData.displaySettings) {
          Object.assign(displaySettings, tourData.displaySettings);
          applyAnimSettings();
        }
      }

      // ---- 初期ページ表示でのスコア復元 ----
      // venueScreen='score' の状態でページを開いた場合、最新スコアを遡って表示する
      // tournament / results 両 onSnapshot の初回発火が揃った時点で判定する
      let _tourInitDone    = false;
      let _initialResultDoc = undefined; // undefined=未確認, null=結果なし, Doc=復元対象
      let _restoreTriggered = false;
      function tryRestoreLastScore() {
        if (_restoreTriggered || !_tourInitDone || _initialResultDoc === undefined) return;
        _restoreTriggered = true;
        const screen = tournamentData?.venueScreen ?? 'score';
        if (_initialResultDoc && screen === 'score' && currentMode === 'idle') {
          enqueueResult(_initialResultDoc);
        }
      }

      // 大会ドキュメントをリアルタイム監視（displaySettings + 会場画面切り替え）
      onSnapshot(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), snap => {
        if (!snap.exists()) return;
        const data = snap.data();
        tournamentData = data;
        if (data.displaySettings) {
          Object.assign(displaySettings, data.displaySettings);
          applyAnimSettings();
        }
        applyVenueScreen(data);
        if (!_tourInitDone) { _tourInitDone = true; tryRestoreLastScore(); }
      });

      // 全published結果を監視（デュアルパネル対応）
      const q = query(
        collection(db, "results"),
        where("eventId", "==", eventId),
        where("published", "==", true),
        orderBy("publishedAt", "desc")
      );
      let initialLoad = true;
      onSnapshot(q, snap => {
        if (initialLoad) {
          initialLoad = false;
          // displaySkip でない最新結果を保持（venueScreen='score' 時に復元するため）
          _initialResultDoc = snap.docs.find(d => !d.data().displaySkip) ?? null;
          tryRestoreLastScore();
          return;
        }
        // 以後: 新規追加分のみキューに入れる
        snap.docChanges().forEach(change => {
          // 新規公開(added)と再公開(modified)の両方を表示
          if (change.type === 'added' || change.type === 'modified') {
            // 「確定のみ」で登録された結果は会場ディスプレイをスキップ
            if (change.doc.data().displaySkip === true) return;
            // 集計中画面が出ていたらローカルで即消し＋Firestoreも更新＆キューリセット
            // calculating が実際に active のときだけ書き込む（余分な snapshot 発火を防ぐ）
            const calcEl = document.getElementById('vs-calculating');
            if (calcEl?.classList.contains('active')) {
              calcEl.classList.remove('active');
              displayQueue.length = 0;
              // calculating:false はCJPのpublish()側で書き込む（venue_displayは非ログインのため不可）
            }
            // スコア確定時：全会場画面を即時非表示にし、復元しない
            ['vs-logo','vs-title','vs-ceremony','vs-warmup','vs-intro','vs-final-startlist'].forEach(id => {
              document.getElementById(id)?.classList.remove('active');
            });
            // warmupタイマーも停止
            if (warmupTimerInterval) { clearInterval(warmupTimerInterval); warmupTimerInterval = null; }
            // 退避をクリア（順位表・スコア表示後に会場画面を復元しない）
            savedVenueScreen = null;
            // ※ venueScreen:'score' への切替は judge_cjp.html の publish() 側で書き込む
            // （venue_displayは非ログインのためFirestore書き込み不可）
            enqueueResult(change.doc);
          }
        });
      });

    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  }

  init();
</script>
</body>
</html>
