<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDocs, collection, updateDoc, onSnapshot, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { ensureAdmin, logout } from "./auth.js";
    import { getTournament } from "./cache.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    document.getElementById('back-link').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;
    const venueUrl = `${location.origin}/display.html?event=${eventId}`;
    const venueLink = document.getElementById('venue-link');
    venueLink.href = venueUrl;
    venueLink.textContent = venueUrl;

    // ---- タブ切り替え ----
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
            btn.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('tab-' + btn.dataset.tab).classList.add('active');
        });
    });

    // ---- タブ1: 画面表示設定 ----
    const DISP_KEYS = ['animTotalSec','holdSec','rankingRows','rankingPageSec'];
    const DISP_DEF  = { animTotalSec:10, holdSec:3, rankingRows:12, rankingPageSec:8 };

    function updateTimeline() {
        const a = Number(document.getElementById('val-animTotalSec').value);
        const h = Number(document.getElementById('val-holdSec').value);
        document.getElementById('timeline-preview').innerHTML =
            `スコア表示 → <strong>${a}秒</strong>でアニメーション完了 → さらに<strong>${h}秒</strong>保持 → 順位表へ（合計 <strong>${a+h}秒</strong>）`;
    }
    DISP_KEYS.forEach(key => {
        const r = document.getElementById(`range-${key}`);
        const v = document.getElementById(`val-${key}`);
        if (!r || !v) return;
        r.addEventListener('input', () => { v.value = r.value; updateTimeline(); });
        v.addEventListener('input', () => { r.value = v.value; updateTimeline(); });
    });
    updateTimeline();

    function applyDispToUI(s) {
        DISP_KEYS.forEach(k => {
            const v = s[k] ?? DISP_DEF[k];
            const r = document.getElementById(`range-${k}`);
            const i = document.getElementById(`val-${k}`);
            if (r) r.value = v; if (i) i.value = v;
        });
        updateTimeline();
    }
    function readDispSettings() {
        const o = {};
        DISP_KEYS.forEach(k => { o[k] = Number(document.getElementById(`val-${k}`).value); });
        return o;
    }

    document.getElementById('save-settings-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const btn = document.getElementById('save-settings-btn');
        const st  = document.getElementById('save-settings-status');
        btn.disabled = true; st.textContent = '保存中...'; st.style.color = '#888';
        try {
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), { displaySettings: readDispSettings() });
            st.textContent = '✅ 保存しました'; st.style.color = '#2e7d32';
        } catch(e) { st.textContent = `❌ ${e.message}`; st.style.color = '#c62828'; }
        finally { btn.disabled = false; }
    });

    // ---- スライダー↔数値ボックス 汎用連動 ----
    function linkSlider(rangeId, valId) {
        const r = document.getElementById(rangeId);
        const v = document.getElementById(valId);
        if (!r || !v) return;
        r.addEventListener('input', () => { v.value = r.value; });
        v.addEventListener('input', () => { r.value = v.value; });
    }
    linkSlider('title-fs','title-fs-val');
    linkSlider('cer-name-fs','cer-name-fs-val');
    linkSlider('cer-label-fs','cer-label-fs-val');
    linkSlider('wu-class-fs','wu-class-fs-val');
    linkSlider('wu-timer-fs','wu-timer-fs-val');
    linkSlider('intro-duration','intro-duration-val');

    // ---- タブ2: 画面切り替え ----
    let currentScreen  = 'score';
    let warmupData     = {};
    let classCombos    = [];  // 既存クラス一覧
    let ctrlTimerInterval = null;

    const SCREEN_LABELS = {
        score:'📊 スコア表示中', logo:'💠 システムロゴ画面',
        title:'🏟️ 大会タイトル画面',
        ceremony:'🏅 表彰式画面', warmup:'🏃 会場練習画面',
        carousel:'🔄 巡回表示中',
        intro:'👤 選手紹介中',
        final_startlist:'📋 決勝スタートリスト表示中'
    };

    // クラス一覧をselect要素に追加
    function buildClassOptions(combos) {
        ['wu-class1','wu-class2'].forEach(id => {
            const sel = document.getElementById(id);
            const cur = sel.value;
            // 既存オプション（選択なし以外）を削除
            while (sel.options.length > 1) sel.remove(1);
            combos.forEach(c => {
                const o = document.createElement('option');
                o.value = c; o.textContent = c;
                sel.appendChild(o);
            });
            if (cur) sel.value = cur;
        });
    }

    function applyScreenState(screen, warmup, ts, cs, carousel) {
        currentScreen = screen;
        warmupData    = warmup || {};

        // 巡回が有効な場合はcarouselボタンをアクティブとして扱う
        const effectiveScreen = carousel?.active ? 'carousel' : screen;
        document.getElementById('current-screen-badge').textContent = SCREEN_LABELS[effectiveScreen] || effectiveScreen;
        // active = 現在会場に表示中（緑）
        document.querySelectorAll('.screen-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.screen === effectiveScreen));
        // 選択中（pending）でないときのみパネルをスナップショット側で切り替える
        if (!pendingScreen) showScreenPanel(effectiveScreen);

        // 各画面の設定値を反映
        if (ts) {
            const r = document.getElementById('title-fs');
            const v = document.getElementById('title-fs-val');
            if (r) r.value = ts.fontSize || 6.5;
            if (v) v.value = ts.fontSize || 6.5;
            const l1 = document.getElementById('title-line1');
            const l2 = document.getElementById('title-line2');
            const l3 = document.getElementById('title-line3');
            if (l1) l1.value = ts.line1 || '';
            if (l2) l2.value = ts.line2 || '';
            if (l3) l3.value = ts.line3 || '';
        }
        if (cs) {
            setSlider('cer-name-fs', cs.nameFontSize  || 3.5);
            setSlider('cer-label-fs', cs.labelFontSize || 10);
        }
        if (warmup) {
            document.getElementById('wu-class1').value = warmup.class1 || '';
            document.getElementById('wu-class2').value = warmup.class2 || '';
            setSlider('wu-class-fs', warmup.classFontSize || 4.5);
            setSlider('wu-timer-fs', warmup.timerFontSize || 24);
            document.getElementById('timer-repeat').checked = !!warmup.timerRepeat;
        }

        // 巡回チェックボックス復元（巡回停止中のみ上書き）
        if (carousel && !carousel.active && carousel.items) {
            document.querySelectorAll('#carousel-class-list input').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);
            (carousel.items || []).forEach(item => {
                const idx = carouselCombos.findIndex(
                    c => c.classVal === item.classVal && c.gender === item.gender && c.type === item.type);
                if (idx >= 0) {
                    const cb = document.querySelector(`#carousel-class-list input[data-idx="${idx}"]`);
                    if (cb) cb.checked = true;
                }
            });
        }
        if (carousel) {
            document.getElementById('carousel-show-logo').checked = !!carousel.showLogo;
        }

        syncCtrlTimer();
    }

    function setSlider(rangeId, val) {
        const r = document.getElementById(rangeId);
        const v = document.getElementById(rangeId + '-val');
        if (r) r.value = val; if (v) v.value = val;
    }

    // サブパネルだけ切り替える（Firestoreへの書き込みなし）
    function showScreenPanel(screen) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.screen-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
        const panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + screen);
        if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
    }

    // 画面切り替え: 選択→「表示する」で確定の2ステップ
    let pendingScreen = null;

    document.querySelectorAll('.screen-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            pendingScreen = btn.dataset.screen;
            // selected クラスを付け替え（active=現在表示中 は触らない）
            document.querySelectorAll('.screen-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
            btn.classList.add('selected');
            document.getElementById('btn-show-screen').disabled = false;
            // 選択に応じてサブパネルを開く（既存ロジック流用）
            showScreenPanel(pendingScreen);
        });
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-show-screen').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (!pendingScreen) return;
        try {
            if (pendingScreen === 'carousel') {
                // 巡回開始: 選択中のクラスと設定を書き込む
                const checked = carouselCombos.filter((_, i) => {
                    const cb = document.querySelector(`#carousel-class-list input[data-idx="${i}"]`);
                    return cb?.checked;
                });
                if (checked.length === 0) { alert('表示するクラスを選択してください'); return; }
                const showLogo = document.getElementById('carousel-show-logo').checked;
                await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                    'rankingCarousel.active':   true,
                    'rankingCarousel.items':    checked.map(c => ({ classVal: c.classVal, gender: c.gender, type: c.type })),
                    'rankingCarousel.showLogo': showLogo
                });
            } else if (pendingScreen === 'intro') {
                const sel = getSelectedIntroAthletes();
                if (sel.length === 0) { alert('表示する選手を選択してください'); return; }
                const durMs = Number(document.getElementById('intro-duration-val').value) * 1000;
                await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                    venueScreen: 'intro',
                    'rankingCarousel.active': false,
                    'introSettings.athletes': sel,
                    'introSettings.durationMs': durMs,
                    'introSettings.currentIndex': 0,
                    'introSettings.isRunning': true
                });
                startIntroSequence(sel, durMs);
            } else if (pendingScreen === 'final_startlist') {
                const target = document.getElementById('fsl-target')?.value || '';
                if (!target) {
                    document.getElementById('show-screen-err').textContent = 'クラスを選択してください';
                    setTimeout(() => { document.getElementById('show-screen-err').textContent = ''; }, 3000);
                    return;
                }
                const [cls, gender, type] = target.split('|');
                await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                    venueScreen: 'final_startlist',
                    'rankingCarousel.active': false,
                    'finalStartlistTarget': { class: cls, gender, type }
                });
            } else {
                // warmupSettings のタイマーフィールドには触れない
                // → 他画面表示中もバックグラウンドでカウントダウン継続
                // → warmup に戻った際は timerEndTime から残り時間を再計算して表示
                await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                    venueScreen: pendingScreen,
                    'rankingCarousel.active': false   // 巡回中でも画面切り替えで停止
                });
            }
            // 確定後は selected を外す（active がスナップショット経由で更新される）
            document.querySelectorAll('.screen-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
            document.getElementById('btn-show-screen').disabled = true;
            pendingScreen = null;
        } catch(e) {
            pendingScreen = null;
            document.querySelectorAll('.screen-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('selected'));
            document.getElementById('btn-show-screen').disabled = true;
            const errEl = document.getElementById('show-screen-err');
            errEl.textContent = '切り替え失敗: ' + e.message;
            setTimeout(() => { errEl.textContent = ''; }, 5000);
        }
    });

    // ---- タイトル保存 ----
    document.getElementById('save-title-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const st = document.getElementById('save-title-status');
        st.textContent = '保存中...'; st.style.color = '#888';
        try {
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                'titleSettings.fontSize': Number(document.getElementById('title-fs-val').value),
                'titleSettings.line1': document.getElementById('title-line1').value.trim(),
                'titleSettings.line2': document.getElementById('title-line2').value.trim(),
                'titleSettings.line3': document.getElementById('title-line3').value.trim()
            });
            st.textContent = '✅ 保存しました'; st.style.color = '#2e7d32';
        } catch(e) { st.textContent = `❌ ${e.message}`; st.style.color = '#c62828'; }
    });

    // ---- 表彰式保存 ----
    document.getElementById('save-ceremony-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const st = document.getElementById('save-ceremony-status');
        st.textContent = '保存中...'; st.style.color = '#888';
        try {
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                'ceremonySettings.nameFontSize':  Number(document.getElementById('cer-name-fs-val').value),
                'ceremonySettings.labelFontSize': Number(document.getElementById('cer-label-fs-val').value),
            });
            st.textContent = '✅ 保存しました'; st.style.color = '#2e7d32';
        } catch(e) { st.textContent = `❌ ${e.message}`; st.style.color = '#c62828'; }
    });

    // ---- 会場練習保存（クラス名＋文字サイズ） ----
    document.getElementById('save-warmup-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const st = document.getElementById('save-warmup-status');
        st.textContent = '保存中...'; st.style.color = '#888';
        try {
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                'warmupSettings.class1':       document.getElementById('wu-class1').value,
                'warmupSettings.class2':       document.getElementById('wu-class2').value,
                'warmupSettings.classFontSize':Number(document.getElementById('wu-class-fs-val').value),
                'warmupSettings.timerFontSize':Number(document.getElementById('wu-timer-fs-val').value),
                'warmupSettings.timerRepeat':  document.getElementById('timer-repeat').checked,
            });
            st.textContent = '✅ 保存しました'; st.style.color = '#2e7d32';
        } catch(e) { st.textContent = `❌ ${e.message}`; st.style.color = '#c62828'; }
    });

    // ---- 順位表 巡回モード ----
    let carouselCombos = []; // { classVal, gender, type, label }

    function buildCarouselList(combos) {
        const list = document.getElementById('carousel-class-list');
        if (!list) return;
        list.innerHTML = '';
        combos.forEach((c, i) => {
            const lbl = document.createElement('label');
            lbl.className = 'carousel-item';
            lbl.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" data-idx="${i}"><span>${c.label}</span>`;
            list.appendChild(lbl);
        });
    }

    // （巡回の開始・停止は btn-show-screen / applyScreenState で統合管理）

    // ---- 選手紹介 ----
    let introAthletes   = [];
    let introTimerItv   = null;
    let introCurIdx     = 0;
    let introAthList    = [];
    let introClassDefs  = [];
    let introFilterKey  = '';          // '' = 全員, 'class|gender|type' = 絞り込み
    let introCheckedIds = new Set();   // フィルターをまたいでチェック状態を保持
    let introSortMode   = 'qualifying'; // 'qualifying' | 'final'

    function getIntroSorted() {
        const gScore = g => g.includes('女') ? 0 : g.includes('男') ? 1 : 2;
        const base = introSortMode === 'final'
            ? introAthletes.filter(a => a.finalOrder != null)  // 決勝: finalOrder 設定済みのみ
            : introAthletes.slice();
        const orderField = introSortMode === 'final' ? 'finalOrder' : 'order';
        return base.sort((a, b) => {
            const ia = introClassDefs.indexOf(a.class);
            const ib = introClassDefs.indexOf(b.class);
            const ca = ia >= 0 ? ia : 99, cb = ib >= 0 ? ib : 99;
            if (ca !== cb) return ca - cb;
            const gd = gScore(a.gender || '') - gScore(b.gender || '');
            if (gd !== 0) return gd;
            return (parseInt(a[orderField])||0) - (parseInt(b[orderField])||0);
        });
    }

    function renderIntroAthleteList(athletes) {
        const list = document.getElementById('intro-athlete-list');
        if (!list) return;
        list.innerHTML = '';
        let lastGroup = null;
        athletes.forEach(a => {
            const group = `${a.class} ${a.gender}`.trim();
            if (group !== lastGroup) {
                const h = document.createElement('div');
                h.className = 'intro-class-header';
                h.textContent = group;
                list.appendChild(h);
                lastGroup = group;
            }
            const lbl = document.createElement('label');
            lbl.className = 'intro-athlete-item';
            const name = a.type === 'synchro' && a.name2 ? `${a.name1} / ${a.name2}` : a.name1;
            const orderNum = introSortMode === 'final' ? a.finalOrder : a.order;
            lbl.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" data-id="${a.id}"><span>${orderNum ? 'No.' + orderNum + '&nbsp;' : ''}${name}${a.club1 ? ' (' + a.club1 + ')' : ''}</span>`;
            list.appendChild(lbl);
        });
        list.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
            cb.checked = introCheckedIds.has(cb.dataset.id);
        });
    }

    function saveCheckedState() {
        document.querySelectorAll('#intro-athlete-list input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
            if (cb.checked) introCheckedIds.add(cb.dataset.id);
            else introCheckedIds.delete(cb.dataset.id);
        });
    }

    function applyIntroFilter() {
        saveCheckedState();
        const all = getIntroSorted();
        const filtered = introFilterKey
            ? all.filter(a => `${a.class}|${a.gender}|${a.type}` === introFilterKey)
            : all;
        renderIntroAthleteList(filtered);
    }

    function buildIntroFilterBtns() {
        const wrap = document.getElementById('intro-filter-wrap');
        if (!wrap) return;
        wrap.innerHTML = '';
        const allBtn = document.createElement('button');
        allBtn.className = 'intro-filter-btn active';
        allBtn.textContent = '全員';
        allBtn.dataset.key = '';
        wrap.appendChild(allBtn);
        const seen = new Set();
        getIntroSorted().forEach(a => {
            const key = `${a.class}|${a.gender}|${a.type}`;
            if (!seen.has(key)) {
                seen.add(key);
                const btn = document.createElement('button');
                btn.className = 'intro-filter-btn';
                const typeLabel = a.type === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
                btn.textContent = [a.class, a.gender, typeLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
                btn.dataset.key = key;
                wrap.appendChild(btn);
            }
        });
        wrap.querySelectorAll('.intro-filter-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                wrap.querySelectorAll('.intro-filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
                btn.classList.add('active');
                introFilterKey = btn.dataset.key;
                applyIntroFilter();
            });
        });
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.intro-mode-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            document.querySelectorAll('.intro-mode-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            btn.classList.add('active');
            introSortMode = btn.dataset.mode;
            introFilterKey = '';          // モード切替時はフィルターをリセット
            buildIntroFilterBtns();
            applyIntroFilter();
        });
    });

    document.getElementById('intro-select-all').addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('#intro-athlete-list input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
            cb.checked = true; introCheckedIds.add(cb.dataset.id);
        });
    });
    document.getElementById('intro-clear-all').addEventListener('click', () => {
        introCheckedIds.clear(); // 非表示の選手も含めて選択記憶を完全リセット
        document.querySelectorAll('#intro-athlete-list input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
            cb.checked = false;
        });
    });

    function getSelectedIntroAthletes() {
        saveCheckedState();
        return getIntroSorted().filter(a => introCheckedIds.has(a.id));
    }

    function updateIntroStatus(idx, total) {
        const st = document.getElementById('intro-run-status');
        if (!st) return;
        st.textContent = `紹介中: ${idx + 1} / ${total} 人目`;
        st.className = 'intro-run-status running';
    }

    function stopIntroSequence() {
        if (introTimerItv) { clearInterval(introTimerItv); introTimerItv = null; }
        const st = document.getElementById('intro-run-status');
        if (st) { st.textContent = ''; st.className = 'intro-run-status'; }
        const stopBtn = document.getElementById('intro-stop-btn');
        if (stopBtn) stopBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function startIntroSequence(athletes, durationMs) {
        stopIntroSequence();
        introAthList = athletes;
        introCurIdx  = 0;
        updateIntroStatus(0, athletes.length);
        document.getElementById('intro-stop-btn').style.display = '';
        introTimerItv = setInterval(async () => {
            introCurIdx++;
            if (introCurIdx >= introAthList.length) {
                introCurIdx = 0; // ループ: 先頭に戻る
            }
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                'introSettings.currentIndex': introCurIdx
            }).catch(() => {});
            updateIntroStatus(introCurIdx, introAthList.length);
        }, durationMs);
    }

    document.getElementById('intro-stop-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        stopIntroSequence();
        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
            'introSettings.isRunning': false,
            venueScreen: 'score'
        }).catch(e => alert('失敗: ' + e.message));
    });

    // ---- タイマー制御 ----
    // timerRef（Firestore serverTimestamp）+ timerRemainingMs から終了時刻を計算。
    // クライアント側の _serverOffset を使わないため、どのデバイス・どのタイミングでも一致する。
    function syncCtrlTimer() {
        clearInterval(ctrlTimerInterval); ctrlTimerInterval = null;
        const timerEl  = document.getElementById('ctrl-timer');
        const startBtn = document.getElementById('timer-start-btn');
        const pauseBtn = document.getElementById('timer-pause-btn');

        function tick() {
            const wu = warmupData;
            // timerEndMs（開始時に Date.now()+残り時間 で確定した絶対終了時刻）を使用。
            // 旧データ互換: timerEndMs がない場合は timerRef + timerRemainingMs で終了時刻を推定。
            const timerEndMs = wu.timerEndMs != null ? wu.timerEndMs
                : (wu.timerRef?.toMillis ? wu.timerRef.toMillis() + (wu.timerRemainingMs ?? 0) : null);
            const running = !wu.timerPaused && timerEndMs != null;
            let rem;
            if (running) {
                rem = Math.max(0, timerEndMs - Date.now());
            } else {
                rem = wu.timerRemainingMs ?? 0;
            }
            renderCtrlTimer(timerEl, rem, running);
            startBtn.textContent = '▶ 開始';
            startBtn.disabled = running || rem === 0;
            pauseBtn.disabled = !running;

            if (rem === 0 && running) {
                clearInterval(ctrlTimerInterval); ctrlTimerInterval = null;
                // 繰り返し設定: 自動で再スタート
                if (wu.timerRepeat) {
                    const defaultMs = wu.timerDefaultMs ?? 5 * 60 * 1000;
                    setTimeout(async () => {
                        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                            'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':      Date.now() + defaultMs,
                            'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs':defaultMs,
                            'warmupSettings.timerPaused':     false,
                        }).catch(() => {});
                    }, 1500); // 1.5秒後に再スタート
                }
            }
        }
        tick();
        const wu = warmupData;
        const _timerEndMs = wu.timerEndMs != null ? wu.timerEndMs
            : (wu.timerRef?.toMillis ? wu.timerRef.toMillis() + (wu.timerRemainingMs ?? 0) : null);
        if (!wu.timerPaused && _timerEndMs != null) {
            ctrlTimerInterval = setInterval(tick, 500);
        }
    }

    function renderCtrlTimer(el, ms, running) {
        const s = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
        el.textContent = `${String(Math.floor(s/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s%60).padStart(2,'0')}`;
        el.className = 'timer-display num' + (ms===0?' done':ms<60000?' warning':running?' running':'');
    }

    document.getElementById('timer-start-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const rem = warmupData.timerRemainingMs ?? 0;
        if (rem <= 0) return;
        // Date.now() + 残り時間 = 絶対終了時刻をその場で計算して書き込む。
        // serverTimestamp を待たないので開始と同時に正確な残り時間を表示できる。
        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
            'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':      Date.now() + rem,
            'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs':rem,
            'warmupSettings.timerPaused':     false,
        }).catch(e => alert('失敗: '+e.message));
    });
    document.getElementById('timer-pause-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const wu = warmupData;
        if (wu.timerEndMs == null) return;
        const rem = Math.max(0, wu.timerEndMs - Date.now());
        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
            'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs': rem,
            'warmupSettings.timerPaused':      true,
            'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':       null,
        }).catch(e => alert('失敗: '+e.message));
    });
    document.getElementById('timer-reset-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const rem = warmupData.timerDefaultMs ?? 5*60*1000;
        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
            'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs': rem,
            'warmupSettings.timerPaused':      true,
            'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':       null,
        }).catch(e => alert('失敗: '+e.message));
    });
    // ---- タイマー手入力：onSnapshot で上書きされないよう「更新」ボタンでのみ反映 ----
    document.getElementById('timer-manual-set-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const minEl = document.getElementById('timer-manual-min');
        const secEl = document.getElementById('timer-manual-sec');
        const minVal = parseInt(minEl.value, 10);
        const secVal = parseInt(secEl.value, 10);
        if (isNaN(minVal) || isNaN(secVal) || minVal < 0 || secVal < 0 || secVal > 59) {
            minEl.focus(); return;
        }
        const ms = (minVal * 60 + secVal) * 1000;
        if (ms <= 0) { minEl.focus(); return; }
        await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
            'warmupSettings.timerDefaultMs':   ms,
            'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs': ms,
            'warmupSettings.timerPaused':      true,
            'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':       null,
        }).catch(e => alert('失敗: '+e.message));
        // 入力欄をクリア
        minEl.value = ''; secEl.value = '';
    });
    // 秒フィールドで Enter → 更新ボタンと同じ動作
    document.getElementById('timer-manual-sec').addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') document.getElementById('timer-manual-set-btn').click();
    });
    // 秒を2桁に自動補正（59以上は59に丸める）
    document.getElementById('timer-manual-sec').addEventListener('blur', e => {
        const v = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
        if (!isNaN(v)) e.target.value = Math.min(59, Math.max(0, v));
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.preset-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            const ms = Number(btn.dataset.min) * 60 * 1000;
            await updateDoc(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), {
                'warmupSettings.timerDefaultMs':   ms,
                'warmupSettings.timerRemainingMs': ms,
                'warmupSettings.timerPaused':      true,
                'warmupSettings.timerEndMs':       null,
            }).catch(e => alert('失敗: '+e.message));
        });
    });

    // ---- 認証 & ロード ----
    ensureAdmin(async user => {
        document.getElementById('auth-info').style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = user.displayName || user.email;
        document.getElementById('logout-btn').onclick = logout;

        if (!eventId) { document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = 'URLにeventパラメータがありません'; return; }

        const tourData = await getTournament(eventId);
        if (!tourData) { document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = '大会が見つかりません'; return; }
        const tournamentName = tourData.name || '';
        document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = tournamentName || eventId;
        document.getElementById('btn-autofill-name').addEventListener('click', () => {
            document.getElementById('title-line1').value = tournamentName;
        });
        if (tourData.displaySettings) applyDispToUI(tourData.displaySettings);
        document.getElementById('save-settings-btn').disabled = false;

        // 選手紹介: クラス表示順を確定
        const _iDefs = (tourData.classes?.individual || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
        const _sDefs = (tourData.classes?.synchro    || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
        introClassDefs = [...new Set([..._iDefs,..._sDefs])];

        // 選手データからクラス一覧を取得
        const athSnap = await getDocs(collection(db,'tournaments',eventId,'athletes'));
        const comboSet = new Set();
        const carouselSet = new Set();
        athSnap.forEach(d => {
            const a = d.data();
            const typeRaw = a.type || 'individual';
            const typeLabel = typeRaw === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
            const gender = a.gender || '';
            const cls    = a.class  || '';
            if (cls) {
                comboSet.add(`${cls} ${gender}（${typeLabel}）`);
                const key = `${cls}|${gender}|${typeRaw}`;
                if (!carouselSet.has(key)) {
                    carouselSet.add(key);
                    carouselCombos.push({ classVal: cls, gender, type: typeRaw,
                        label: `${cls} ${gender}（${typeLabel}）` });
                }
            }
        });
        classCombos = [...comboSet].sort();
        carouselCombos.sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, 'ja'));
        buildClassOptions(classCombos);
        buildCarouselList(carouselCombos);

        // 決勝スタートリスト: isInFinal の選手がいるクラス組み合わせを列挙
        const fslSel = document.getElementById('fsl-target');
        const fslMap = new Map(); // key → label
        athSnap.forEach(d => {
            const a = d.data();
            if (!a.isInFinal || !a.class) return;
            const typeRaw   = a.type || 'individual';
            const typeLabel = typeRaw === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
            const key = `${a.class}|${a.gender||''}|${typeRaw}`;
            if (!fslMap.has(key))
                fslMap.set(key, `${a.class} ${a.gender||''}（${typeLabel}）`.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim());
        });
        [...fslMap.entries()].sort((a,b) => a[1].localeCompare(b[1],'ja')).forEach(([val, label]) => {
            const o = document.createElement('option');
            o.value = val; o.textContent = label;
            fslSel.appendChild(o);
        });

        // 選手紹介用リスト
        athSnap.forEach(d => {
            const a = d.data();
            if (a.name1) introAthletes.push({
                id: d.id, name1: a.name1||'', name2: a.name2||'',
                kana1: a.kana1||'', kana2: a.kana2||'',
                club1: a.club1||'', club2: a.club2||'',
                type: a.type||'individual', class: a.class||'',
                gender: a.gender||'', order: a.order||''
            });
        });
        introAthletes.forEach(a => introCheckedIds.add(a.id));
        buildIntroFilterBtns();
        applyIntroFilter();

        // 初期画面状態を即座に反映（onSnapshot 到着前にタイマーを表示する）
        applyScreenState(
            tourData.venueScreen      || 'score',
            tourData.warmupSettings   || {},
            tourData.titleSettings    || {},
            tourData.ceremonySettings || {},
            tourData.rankingCarousel  || {}
        );

        // リアルタイム監視
        onSnapshot(doc(db,'tournaments',eventId), snap => {
            if (!snap.exists()) return;
            const d = snap.data();
            if (d.displaySettings) applyDispToUI(d.displaySettings);
            applyScreenState(
                d.venueScreen      || 'score',
                d.warmupSettings   || {},
                d.titleSettings    || {},
                d.ceremonySettings || {},
                d.rankingCarousel  || {}
            );
        });
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc, setDoc, updateDoc, deleteDoc, serverTimestamp, increment, deleteField, collection, getDocs, writeBatch, query, where } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { getSkills } from "./cache.js";

    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event')?.trim(), athleteId = params.get('athlete')?.trim(), round = params.get('round');
    const isAdmin = params.get('admin') === '1';

    let skillMaster = [];
    let currentAthlete = null;
    let tourData = {};
    let subDocRef = null;   // submissionDoc の存在確認用
    let designatedDDCount = -1; // 指定難度本数（-1=無効、0=0本指定、1-9=N本指定）

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId || !athleteId || !round) return;
        document.getElementById('back-link').href = `event.html?event=${eventId}`;
        if (isAdmin) document.getElementById('admin-clear-btn').style.display = "block";
        document.getElementById('submit-btn').disabled = true;

        const roundMap = {'pre1': '予選1', 'pre2': '予選2', 'final': '決勝'};
        document.getElementById('round-display').textContent = roundMap[round] || round;

        skillMaster = await getSkills();

        const [tourDoc, athDoc, subDocResult] = await Promise.all([
            getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId)),
            getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes", athleteId)),
            getDoc(doc(db, "submissions", `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`))
        ]);
        tourData = tourDoc.exists() ? tourDoc.data() : {};
        subDocRef = subDocResult;

        if (athDoc.exists()) {
            currentAthlete = athDoc.data();
            const athName = currentAthlete.type === 'synchro' ? `${currentAthlete.name1} / ${currentAthlete.name2}` : currentAthlete.name1;
            const athClub = currentAthlete.type === 'synchro' ? `${currentAthlete.club1} / ${currentAthlete.club2}` : currentAthlete.club1;

            document.getElementById('ath-name').innerText = athName;
            document.getElementById('ath-gender').value = currentAthlete.gender || "男子";
            document.getElementById('ath-class').textContent = currentAthlete.class || '-';
            document.getElementById('ath-flight-group').textContent = currentAthlete.flightGroup ? `G${currentAthlete.flightGroup}` : '-';

            if (currentAthlete.type === 'synchro') {
                document.getElementById('indiv-kana-area').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('synchro-kana-area').style.display = '';
                document.getElementById('synchro-name1').textContent = currentAthlete.name1 || '';
                document.getElementById('synchro-name2').textContent = currentAthlete.name2 || '';
                document.getElementById('ath-kana1').value = currentAthlete.kana1 || '';
                document.getElementById('ath-kana2').value = currentAthlete.kana2 || '';
                // チーム名が同一か自動判定
                const isDiffClub = (currentAthlete.club1 || '') !== (currentAthlete.club2 || '');
                const diffChk = document.getElementById('club-diff-chk');
                diffChk.checked = isDiffClub;
                if (isDiffClub) {
                    document.getElementById('club-same-area').style.display = 'none';
                    document.getElementById('club-diff-area').style.display = '';
                    document.getElementById('synchro-club1').textContent = currentAthlete.club1 || '';
                    document.getElementById('synchro-club2').textContent = currentAthlete.club2 || '';
                    document.getElementById('ath-club-kana1').value = currentAthlete.clubKana1 || '';
                    document.getElementById('ath-club-kana2').value = currentAthlete.clubKana2 || '';
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('synchro-club-shared').textContent = currentAthlete.club1 || '';
                    document.getElementById('ath-club-kana-shared').value = currentAthlete.clubKana1 || '';
                }
                // チェック変更時：エリア切替＋値を引き継ぎ
                diffChk.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    const diff = diffChk.checked;
                    document.getElementById('club-same-area').style.display = diff ? 'none' : '';
                    document.getElementById('club-diff-area').style.display = diff ? '' : 'none';
                    if (diff) {
                        const v = document.getElementById('ath-club-kana-shared').value;
                        document.getElementById('ath-club-kana1').value = v;
                        document.getElementById('ath-club-kana2').value = v;
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('ath-club-kana-shared').value = document.getElementById('ath-club-kana1').value;
                    }
                });
            } else {
                document.getElementById('ath-club').innerText = athClub;
                document.getElementById('ath-kana').value = currentAthlete.kana1 || "";
                document.getElementById('ath-club-kana').value = currentAthlete.clubKana1 || "";
            }

            // 指定難度本数をクラスルールから取得（2本合計時のみ有効）
            const athType = currentAthlete.type || 'individual';
            const athClass = currentAthlete.class || currentAthlete.category || '';
            const cr = (tourData.classRules?.[athType] || {})[athClass] || {};
            // -1=設定なし, 0=0本指定(DD全0), 1-9=N本指定
            const rawDDCount = (cr.designatedDDCount != null && cr.designatedDDCount >= 0) ? cr.designatedDDCount : -1;
            designatedDDCount = (rawDDCount >= 0 && cr.qualifyingRoutines === 2 && cr.scoreMode === 'total' && round === 'pre1')
                ? rawDDCount : -1;
            if (designatedDDCount > 0) {
                // 1本以上指定: チェックボックス列を表示
                document.getElementById('check-th').style.display = '';
                document.getElementById('designation-info').style.display = '';
            } else if (designatedDDCount === 0) {
                // 0本指定: チェックボックス不要、インジケーターのみ表示
                document.getElementById('designation-info').style.display = '';
            }
        }

        const skillsData = subDocResult.exists() ? subDocResult.data().skills : [];
        const savedSkillIds = skillsData.map(s => {
            if (s === null || s === undefined) return "";
            if (typeof s === 'object') {
                // id フィールドがあれば ID で直接照合（同Symbol異注釈の技に対応）
                if (s.id) {
                    const byId = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(s.id));
                    if (byId) return byId.id;
                }
                // 旧フォーマット互換: Symbol で検索
                const found = skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === s.skill);
                return found ? found.id : "";
            }
            // 一括登録フォーマット: IDの文字列または数値
            const sStr = String(s);
            return skillMaster.find(m => m.id === sStr) ? sStr : "";
        });

        let rows = "";
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const skillId = savedSkillIds[i-1] || "";
            const skill = skillMaster.find(s => s.id === skillId) || {};
            const hasSoma = skill.direction && skill.direction !== '基礎技';
            // 2回宙は doubleSomaGroup で細分化したオプション値にマップ
            let somaVal = '';
            if (hasSoma) {
                if (skill.somersaultType === '2回宙') {
                    somaVal = skill.doubleSomaGroup === 'high' ? '2回宙（1.6以上）' : '2回宙（1.5以下）';
                } else {
                    somaVal = skill.somersaultType || '';
                }
            }
            const checkStyle = designatedDDCount > 0 ? 'text-align:center' : 'display:none';
            rows += `<tr id="row-${i}"><td>${i}</td>
                <td><select id="dir-${i}" onchange="updateOptions(${i})"><option value="">--</option>
                    <option value="前方系" ${skill.direction==='前方系'?'selected':''}>前方系</option>
                    <option value="後方系" ${skill.direction==='後方系'?'selected':''}>後方系</option>
                    <option value="基礎技" ${skill.direction==='基礎技'?'selected':''}>基礎技</option></select></td>
                <td><select id="soma-${i}" onchange="updateOptions(${i})" style="${hasSoma ? '' : 'visibility:hidden'}">
                    <option value="">--</option>
                    <option value="1回宙"       ${somaVal==='1回宙'       ?'selected':''}>1回宙系</option>
                    <option value="2回宙（1.5以下）" ${somaVal==='2回宙（1.5以下）'?'selected':''}>2回宙系（1.5以下）</option>
                    <option value="2回宙（1.6以上）" ${somaVal==='2回宙（1.6以上）'?'selected':''}>2回宙系（1.6以上）</option>
                    <option value="3回宙"       ${somaVal==='3回宙'       ?'selected':''}>3回宙系</option>
                    <option value="4回宙"       ${somaVal==='4回宙'       ?'selected':''}>4回宙系</option>
                </select></td>
                <td><select id="skill-${i}" onchange="calcTotal()"></select></td>
                <td><span id="dd-${i}">0.0</span></td>
                <td class="check-cell" style="${checkStyle}"><input type="checkbox" id="chk-${i}" onchange="onCheckChange(${i})"></td></tr>`;
        }
        document.getElementById('skill-rows').innerHTML = rows;
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
             updateOptions(i, savedSkillIds[i-1], false)
        };
        // 指定難度インデックスを復元（calcTotal前に設定する）
        if (designatedDDCount > 0 && subDocResult.exists()) {
            const savedIdx = subDocResult.data().designatedDDIndices || [];
            savedIdx.forEach(idx => {
                const chk = document.getElementById(`chk-${idx + 1}`);
                if (chk) chk.checked = true;
            });
        }
        calcTotal();

        // 予選引用ボタンのラベル・onclickは提出状況に関わらず設定する
        // （提出済みでも「内容を修正する」で表示されるため、ここで未設定だと空ボタンになる）
        if (round === 'pre2' || round === 'final') {
            const cpBtn = document.getElementById('copy-prev-btn');
            cpBtn.textContent = round === 'final' ? '📋 予選演技をコピーする' : '📋 予選１の構成をコピーする';
            cpBtn.onclick = copyPrevRound;
        }

        if (subDocResult.exists()) {
            lockInputs(true);
            document.getElementById('submit-btn').style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById('modify-btn').style.display = "block";
        } else {
            document.getElementById('submit-btn').disabled = false;
            if (round === 'pre2' || round === 'final') {
                document.getElementById('copy-prev-area').style.display = '';
            }
        }

        // 同じチームの引用メニューを準備（提出済み構成がある場合のみ、編集可能時に表示）
        const teamRefCount = await setupTeamReference();
        if (teamRefCount > 0 && !subDocResult.exists()) {
            document.getElementById('team-ref-area').style.display = '';
        }
    }

    window.updateOptions = (idx, selectedId = "", doCalc = true) => {
        const dir    = document.getElementById(`dir-${idx}`).value;
        const somaEl = document.getElementById(`soma-${idx}`);
        const sel    = document.getElementById(`skill-${idx}`);
        const currentVal = sel.value;
        // 基礎技は宙系不要
        if (somaEl) {
            const showSoma = dir && dir !== '基礎技';
            somaEl.style.visibility = showSoma ? '' : 'hidden';
            if (!showSoma) somaEl.value = '';
        }
        const soma = somaEl?.value || '';
        // '2回宙（1.5以下）' / '2回宙（1.6以上）' を somersaultType + doubleSomaGroup にマップ
        let somaType  = soma;
        let somaGroup = '';
        if (soma === '2回宙（1.5以下）') { somaType = '2回宙'; somaGroup = 'low'; }
        else if (soma === '2回宙（1.6以上）') { somaType = '2回宙'; somaGroup = 'high'; }
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">選択してください</option>';
        if (dir) {
            skillMaster.filter(s => {
                if (s.direction !== dir) return false;
                if (somaType && s.somersaultType !== somaType) return false;
                if (somaGroup && s.doubleSomaGroup !== somaGroup) return false;
                return true;
            }).forEach(s => {
                const opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = s.id;
                opt.textContent = s.note ? `${s.symbol}（${s.note}）` : s.symbol;
                if (s.id === selectedId) opt.selected = true;
                sel.appendChild(opt);
            });
        }
        if (!selectedId && currentVal) sel.value = currentVal;
        if (doCalc) calcTotal();
    };

    window.calcTotal = () => {
        let baseDD = 0, tripleCount = 0;
        const seenIds = new Set(), idCounts = {};
        for(let i=1; i<=10; i++) {
            const id = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`).value;
            if(id) idCounts[id] = (idCounts[id] || 0) + 1;
        }

        let designatedChecked = 0;
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const id = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`).value;
            const row = document.getElementById(`row-${i}`);
            const ddSpan = document.getElementById(`dd-${i}`);
            if (!id) {
                ddSpan.textContent = "0.0";
                ddSpan.classList.remove('dd-zero', 'dd-not-designated');
                row.classList.remove('duplicate-row');
                continue;
            }

            const skill = skillMaster.find(s => s.id === id);
            row.classList.toggle('duplicate-row', idCounts[id] > 1);

            // 指定難度チェック（-1=全技有効、0=全技無効、1以上=チェック済みのみ有効）
            const isDesignated = designatedDDCount < 0
                || (designatedDDCount > 0 && document.getElementById(`chk-${i}`)?.checked === true);
            if (isDesignated && designatedDDCount > 0) designatedChecked++;

            if (seenIds.has(id)) {
                ddSpan.textContent = "0.0";
                ddSpan.classList.add('dd-zero');
                ddSpan.classList.remove('dd-not-designated');
            } else {
                seenIds.add(id);
                ddSpan.textContent = skill.dd.toFixed(1);
                ddSpan.classList.remove('dd-zero');
                if (isDesignated) {
                    baseDD += skill.dd;
                    if (skill.isTriple) tripleCount++;
                    ddSpan.classList.remove('dd-not-designated');
                } else {
                    ddSpan.classList.add('dd-not-designated'); // 非指定はグレー表示
                }
            }
        }

        const gender = document.getElementById('ath-gender').value;
        let bonus = 0;
        if (gender === '男子' && tripleCount > 5) bonus = (tripleCount - 5) * 0.3;
        if ((gender === '女子' || gender === '混合') && tripleCount >= 3) bonus = (tripleCount - 2) * 0.3; // 混合は女子ルール適用

        document.getElementById('dd-base').textContent  = baseDD.toFixed(1);
        document.getElementById('dd-bonus').textContent = bonus.toFixed(1);
        document.getElementById('total-dd').textContent = (baseDD + bonus).toFixed(1);

        // 指定難度インジケーター更新
        if (designatedDDCount === 0) {
            const infoEl = document.getElementById('designation-info');
            if (infoEl) {
                infoEl.textContent = '指定難度：0本設定（このクラスのDD得点は0点）';
                infoEl.style.background = '#fce4ec';
                infoEl.style.color      = '#c62828';
            }
        } else if (designatedDDCount > 0) {
            const infoEl = document.getElementById('designation-info');
            if (infoEl) {
                const ok = designatedChecked === designatedDDCount;
                infoEl.textContent = `指定難度：${designatedChecked} / ${designatedDDCount} 本 選択中`;
                infoEl.style.background = ok ? '#e8f5e9' : '#fff3e0';
                infoEl.style.color      = ok ? '#1e8e3e' : '#e65100';
            }
        }
    };

    window.onCheckChange = (idx) => {
        const chkEl  = document.getElementById(`chk-${idx}`);
        const skillEl = document.getElementById(`skill-${idx}`);
        // スキル未選択の場合はチェック不可
        if (!skillEl?.value) {
            if (chkEl) chkEl.checked = false;
            return;
        }
        // チェック数の上限確認
        if (chkEl?.checked && designatedDDCount > 0) {
            const total = Array.from({length: 10}, (_, i) => document.getElementById(`chk-${i+1}`)?.checked ? 1 : 0)
                              .reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
            if (total > designatedDDCount) {
                chkEl.checked = false;
                return;
            }
        }
        calcTotal();
    };

    function lockInputs(v) {
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            document.getElementById(`dir-${i}`).disabled = v;
            document.getElementById(`soma-${i}`).disabled = v;
            document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`).disabled = v;
            const chk = document.getElementById(`chk-${i}`);
            if (chk) chk.disabled = v;
        }
        document.getElementById('ath-kana').disabled = v;
        document.getElementById('ath-club-kana').disabled = v;
        ['ath-kana1','ath-kana2','ath-club-kana1','ath-club-kana2','ath-club-kana-shared','club-diff-chk'].forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el) el.disabled = v;
        });
    }

    window.enableEditing = () => {
        lockInputs(false);
        document.getElementById('modify-btn').style.display = "none";
        const submitBtn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
        submitBtn.style.display = "block";
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.textContent = 'この内容で提出する';
        if (round === 'pre2' || round === 'final') {
            document.getElementById('copy-prev-area').style.display = '';
        }
        // 同じチームの引用メニュー（選択肢があれば表示）
        if (document.getElementById('team-ref-select').options.length > 1) {
            document.getElementById('team-ref-area').style.display = '';
        }
    };

    // 提出データの skills 配列を技ID配列に変換（複数フォーマット互換）
    function submissionToSkillIds(srcSkills) {
        return (srcSkills || []).map(s => {
            if (s === null || s === undefined) return '';
            if (typeof s === 'object') {
                if (s.id) { const byId = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(s.id)); if (byId) return byId.id; }
                const found = skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === s.skill);
                return found ? found.id : '';
            }
            const sStr = String(s);
            return skillMaster.find(m => m.id === sStr) ? sStr : '';
        });
    }

    // 技ID配列を入力フォーム（系統・宙系・技名）に反映
    function applySkillIdsToForm(skillIds) {
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const skillId = skillIds[i - 1] || '';
            const skill   = skillMaster.find(s => s.id === skillId);
            const dirEl   = document.getElementById(`dir-${i}`);
            const somaEl  = document.getElementById(`soma-${i}`);
            dirEl.value = skill?.direction || '';
            // 2回宙は doubleSomaGroup で細分化した値にマップ
            let somaVal = '';
            if (skill?.direction && skill.direction !== '基礎技') {
                if (skill.somersaultType === '2回宙') {
                    somaVal = skill.doubleSomaGroup === 'high' ? '2回宙（1.6以上）' : '2回宙（1.5以下）';
                } else {
                    somaVal = skill.somersaultType || '';
                }
            }
            somaEl.value = somaVal;
            updateOptions(i, skillId, false);
        }
        calcTotal();
    }

    // 同じチームの提出済み構成を引用メニューに並べる
    // 提出有無は submissions コレクション（提出データ本体）を正として判定する
    async function setupTeamReference() {
        if (!currentAthlete) return 0;
        const myClubs = new Set();
        if (currentAthlete.club1) myClubs.add(currentAthlete.club1);
        if (currentAthlete.club2) myClubs.add(currentAthlete.club2);
        if (myClubs.size === 0) return 0;

        // 全選手（所属・氏名照合用）と、この大会の全提出を取得
        const [athSnap, subSnap] = await Promise.all([
            getDocs(collection(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes")),
            getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId)))
        ]);

        // 同じ所属の選手（自分以外）を athleteId → データ で保持
        const teammates = new Map();
        athSnap.docs.forEach(d => {
            if (d.id === athleteId) return; // 自分は除外
            const a = d.data();
            const inClub = (a.club1 && myClubs.has(a.club1)) || (a.club2 && myClubs.has(a.club2));
            if (inClub) teammates.set(d.id, a);
        });
        if (teammates.size === 0) return 0;

        const roundLabels = { pre1: '予選1', pre2: '予選2', final: '決勝' };
        const roundOrder  = { pre1: 0, pre2: 1, final: 2 };

        const entries = [];
        subSnap.docs.forEach(d => {
            const s = d.data();
            const a = teammates.get(s.athleteId);
            if (!a) return;                                                // 同じチームでなければ除外
            if (!roundLabels[s.round]) return;                             // 想定外ラウンドは除外
            if (!Array.isArray(s.skills) || s.skills.length === 0) return; // 空提出は除外
            const nm  = a.type === 'synchro' ? `${a.name1 || ''} / ${a.name2 || ''}` : (a.name1 || '名称未設定');
            const cls = a.class ? `（${a.class}）` : '';
            entries.push({ value: `${s.athleteId}|${s.round}`, label: `${nm}${cls} ー ${roundLabels[s.round]}`, sortKey: `${nm}_${roundOrder[s.round]}` });
        });

        entries.sort((x, y) => x.sortKey.localeCompare(y.sortKey, 'ja'));
        const sel = document.getElementById('team-ref-select');
        entries.forEach(e => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = e.value;
            opt.textContent = e.label;
            sel.appendChild(opt);
        });
        return entries.length;
    }

    // 選択した構成を読み込んでフォームへ反映
    window.loadTeamComposition = async () => {
        const val = document.getElementById('team-ref-select').value;
        if (!val) { alert('引用する選手・ラウンドを選択してください。'); return; }
        const [srcAthleteId, srcRound] = val.split('|');
        if (!confirm('選択した構成をこの画面に読み込みます。現在の入力内容は上書きされます。よろしいですか？')) return;
        const btn = document.getElementById('team-ref-btn');
        btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '読込中...';
        try {
            const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'submissions', `${eventId}_${srcAthleteId}_${srcRound}`));
            if (!snap.exists()) { alert('選択した構成のデータが見つかりませんでした。'); return; }
            applySkillIdsToForm(submissionToSkillIds(snap.data().skills || []));
        } catch (e) {
            alert('読込に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '読込';
        }
    };

    window.copyPrevRound = async () => {
        const isFinal  = round === 'final';
        // 予選本数をクラスルールから取得（1本制の場合は pre1 から直接コピー）
        const _athType  = currentAthlete?.type || 'individual';
        const _athClass = currentAthlete?.class || currentAthlete?.category || '';
        const _cr       = (tourData.classRules?.[_athType] || {})[_athClass] || {};
        const numPre    = _cr.qualifyingRoutines ?? 2;

        let srcRound, fallback, srcLabel;
        if (!isFinal) {
            srcRound = 'pre1'; fallback = null;  srcLabel = '予選１の構成';
        } else if (numPre === 1) {
            srcRound = 'pre1'; fallback = null;  srcLabel = '予選演技';
        } else {
            srcRound = 'pre2'; fallback = 'pre1'; srcLabel = '予選演技';
        }
        if (!confirm(`${srcLabel}をこの画面にコピーします。現在の入力内容は上書きされます。よろしいですか？`)) return;
        const btn = document.getElementById('copy-prev-btn');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = '読み込み中...';
        try {
            let snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'submissions', `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${srcRound}`));
            if (!snap.exists() && fallback) {
                snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'submissions', `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${fallback}`));
            }
            if (!snap.exists()) {
                alert('予選の提出データが見つかりませんでした。先に予選を提出してください。');
                return;
            }
            applySkillIdsToForm(submissionToSkillIds(snap.data().skills || []));
        } catch (e) {
            alert('コピーに失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.textContent = `📋 ${srcLabel}をコピーする`;
        }
    };

    window.clearEntry = async () => {
        if (!confirm("本当にこのラウンドの登録をクリアしますか？")) return;
        try {
            await deleteDoc(doc(db, "submissions", `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`));
            await updateDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes", athleteId), {
                [`submissions.${round}`]: deleteField()
            });
            alert("登録をクリアしました。");
            location.reload();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(e);
            alert("クリア中にエラーが発生しました。");
        }
    };

    window.submitRoutine = async () => {
        // 指定難度バリデーション（1本以上指定時のみ）
        if (designatedDDCount > 0) {
            const checked = Array.from({length: 10}, (_, i) => document.getElementById(`chk-${i+1}`)?.checked ? 1 : 0)
                                .reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
            if (checked !== designatedDDCount) {
                alert(`指定難度を正確に ${designatedDDCount} 本選択してください（現在 ${checked} 本）`);
                return;
            }
        }

        // 期限チェック
        const now = new Date();
        const isFinal = round === 'final';
        const deadlineKey = isFinal ? 'finalSubmissionDeadline'
                          : subDocRef?.exists?.() ? 'revisionDeadline' : 'submissionDeadline';
        const deadlineStr = tourData?.[deadlineKey];
        if (deadlineStr) {
            const deadline = new Date(deadlineStr);
            if (now > deadline) {
                const labels = { submissionDeadline:'提出締切', revisionDeadline:'修正締切', finalSubmissionDeadline:'決勝締切' };
                alert(`${labels[deadlineKey] || '締切'}を過ぎているため送信できません。`);
                return;
            }
        }
        const skills = [];
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const skillId = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`).value;
            if (!skillId) {
                alert(`技 ${i} が選択されていません。`);
                return;
            }
            const skill = skillMaster.find(s => s.id === skillId);
            skills.push({ id: skillId, skill: skill.symbol, dd: skill.dd });
        }
        const isSynchro = currentAthlete.type === 'synchro';
        const kana     = document.getElementById('ath-kana').value.trim();
        const clubKana = document.getElementById('ath-club-kana').value.trim();
        if (!isSynchro && (!kana || !clubKana)) {
            alert("選手と所属のふりがなを入力してください。");
            return;
        }
        if (isSynchro) {
            const k1     = document.getElementById('ath-kana1').value.trim();
            const k2     = document.getElementById('ath-kana2').value.trim();
            const isDiff = document.getElementById('club-diff-chk')?.checked;
            const ck1    = isDiff ? document.getElementById('ath-club-kana1').value.trim() : document.getElementById('ath-club-kana-shared').value.trim();
            const ck2    = isDiff ? document.getElementById('ath-club-kana2').value.trim() : ck1;
            if (!k1 || !k2 || !ck1 || (isDiff && !ck2)) {
                alert("選手2名のふりがなと所属よみをすべて入力してください。");
                return;
            }
        }

        const btn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = '送信中...';

        try {
            const subId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
            const totalDD = parseFloat(document.getElementById('total-dd').textContent);

            // 指定難度インデックスを収集
            const designatedDDIndices = [];
            if (designatedDDCount > 0) {
                for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
                    const chk = document.getElementById(`chk-${i}`);
                    if (chk?.checked) designatedDDIndices.push(i - 1); // 0-indexed
                }
            }

            // 1. Update the detailed submission document
            await setDoc(doc(db, "submissions", subId), {
                eventId, athleteId, round, skills, totalDD,
                ...(designatedDDCount > 0
                    ? { designatedDDIndices }
                    : { designatedDDIndices: deleteField() }),
                checkStatus: 0, // 0: Submitted, not yet checked
                updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
                revisionCount: increment(1)
            }, { merge: true });

            // 2. Update the athlete document with a summary
            const athleteUpdateData = {
                [`submissions.${round}`]: 0 // 0: Submitted
            };
            // 所属名→所属ふりがな のマップ（同じ所属の他選手へ反映するため）
            const clubKanaMap = {};
            if (!isSynchro) {
                athleteUpdateData.kana1 = kana;
                athleteUpdateData.clubKana1 = clubKana;
                if (currentAthlete.club1) clubKanaMap[currentAthlete.club1] = clubKana;
            } else {
                const isDiff = document.getElementById('club-diff-chk')?.checked;
                const ck1    = isDiff ? document.getElementById('ath-club-kana1').value.trim() : document.getElementById('ath-club-kana-shared').value.trim();
                const ck2    = isDiff ? document.getElementById('ath-club-kana2').value.trim() : ck1;
                athleteUpdateData.kana1     = document.getElementById('ath-kana1').value.trim();
                athleteUpdateData.kana2     = document.getElementById('ath-kana2').value.trim();
                athleteUpdateData.clubKana1 = ck1;
                athleteUpdateData.clubKana2 = ck2;
                if (currentAthlete.club1) clubKanaMap[currentAthlete.club1] = ck1;
                if (currentAthlete.club2) clubKanaMap[currentAthlete.club2] = ck2;
            }
            await updateDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes", athleteId), athleteUpdateData);

            // 同じ所属名の他選手にも所属ふりがなを反映（失敗しても提出自体は成立させる）
            try {
                await propagateClubKana(clubKanaMap);
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('所属ふりがなの一括反映に失敗しました:', e);
            }

            alert("競技カードを提出しました。");
            location.href = `event.html?event=${eventId}`;
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(e);
            alert("エラーが発生しました: " + e.message);
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.textContent = 'この内容で提出する';
        }
    };

    // 同じ所属名を持つ全選手の所属ふりがなを、今回入力した値で揃える
    async function propagateClubKana(clubKanaMap) {
        const clubNames = Object.keys(clubKanaMap).filter(name => name && clubKanaMap[name]);
        if (clubNames.length === 0) return;

        const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes"));
        let batch = writeBatch(db);
        let ops = 0;
        for (const d of snap.docs) {
            if (d.id === athleteId) continue; // 自分は更新済み
            const a = d.data();
            const upd = {};
            if (a.club1 && clubKanaMap[a.club1] != null && a.clubKana1 !== clubKanaMap[a.club1]) {
                upd.clubKana1 = clubKanaMap[a.club1];
            }
            if (a.club2 && clubKanaMap[a.club2] != null && a.clubKana2 !== clubKanaMap[a.club2]) {
                upd.clubKana2 = clubKanaMap[a.club2];
            }
            if (Object.keys(upd).length) {
                batch.update(d.ref, upd);
                if (++ops >= 450) { await batch.commit(); batch = writeBatch(db); ops = 0; }
            }
        }
        if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
    }

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ja">

<body>
<div class="list-container">
    <div id="admin-back" class="admin-back"><a href="#">← 管理メニューに戻る</a></div>
    <div class="header-section">
        <h1 id="event-title" style="font-size:2em; margin-bottom:5px;">読み込み中...</h1>
        <div id="event-date" style="font-size: 1em; color: #888;"></div>
        <div id="event-venue" style="font-size: 0.95em; color: #888;"></div>
    </div>
    <div id="countdown-area" class="countdown-container"></div>
    <div id="indiv-list"></div>
    <div id="sync-list"></div>
</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc, collection, getDocs, query, where } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    // ── localStorage キャッシュ ──────────────────────────────
    const CACHE_KEY = `ev_${eventId}`;
    function loadCache() {
        try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY) || 'null'); }
        catch { return null; }
    }
    function saveCache(tour, athletes, subMap) {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
                tour, athletes, submissions: Object.fromEntries(subMap)
            }));
        } catch {}
    }

    // ── 大会情報をDOMに反映 ─────────────────────────────────
    function applyTourInfo(tour) {
        document.getElementById('event-title').innerText = tour.name;
        document.getElementById('event-date').innerText =
            `${tour.startDate || tour.start_date || ''} ～ ${tour.endDate || tour.end_date || ''}`;
        if (tour.venue) document.getElementById('event-venue').innerText = `📍 ${tour.venue}`;
    }

    // ── バッジのピンポイント更新 ────────────────────────────
    function updateStatusBadge(athId, round, sub) {
        const el = document.getElementById(`status-${athId}-${round}`);
        if (!el) return;
        el.className = `status-link ${sub ? 'status-done' : 'status-none'}`;
        el.textContent = sub ? '提出済' : '入力';
    }

    // ── 選手リスト全体描画（初回のみ呼ぶ）────────────────────
    function renderList(tour, athletes, subMap) {
        const rawClassRules = tour.classRules || {};
        const hasNewFormat  = 'individual' in rawClassRules || 'synchro' in rawClassRules;
        const classRules    = hasNewFormat ? rawClassRules :
                              Object.keys(rawClassRules).length > 0 ? { individual: rawClassRules, synchro: {} } :
                              { individual: {}, synchro: {} };
        const sortByOrder = arr => (arr || []).slice().sort((a, b) => (a.display_order ?? 0) - (b.display_order ?? 0));
        let indivClassOrder = [], syncClassOrder = [];
        if (tour.classes && !Array.isArray(tour.classes)) {
            indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tour.classes.individual).map(c => c.name);
            syncClassOrder  = sortByOrder(tour.classes.synchro).map(c => c.name);
        } else if (Array.isArray(tour.classes)) {
            indivClassOrder = sortByOrder(tour.classes).map(c => c.name);
        }
        const indivAthletes = athletes.filter(a => a.type !== 'synchro');
        const syncAthletes  = athletes.filter(a => a.type === 'synchro');
        const indivContainer = document.getElementById('indiv-list');
        if (indivAthletes.length > 0) {
            indivContainer.innerHTML = '<h2 class="section-header indiv-header">個人競技</h2>' +
                buildTablesHtml(indivAthletes, classRules.individual, subMap, 'individual', rawClassRules, indivClassOrder);
        }
        const syncContainer = document.getElementById('sync-list');
        if (syncAthletes.length > 0) {
            syncContainer.innerHTML = '<h2 class="section-header sync-header">シンクロ競技</h2>' +
                buildTablesHtml(syncAthletes, classRules.synchro, subMap, 'synchro', rawClassRules, syncClassOrder);
        }
    }

    function buildTablesHtml(athletes, rulesByType, subMap, type, fallbackRules = {}, classOrder = []) {
        const byClass = athletes.reduce((acc, ath) => {
            const cn = ath.class || 'N/A';
            (acc[cn] = acc[cn] || []).push(ath);
            return acc;
        }, {});
        const ordered  = classOrder.filter(n => byClass[n]);
        const leftover = Object.keys(byClass).filter(n => !classOrder.includes(n)).sort();
        let html = '';
        for (const cn of [...ordered, ...leftover]) {
            const cls   = byClass[cn];
            const rules = (rulesByType || {})[cn] || fallbackRules[cn] || { qualifyingRoutines: 2, hasFinal: false };
            cls.sort((a, b) =>
                `${a.gender||''}-${String(a.flightGroup||'').padStart(3,'0')}-${String(a.order||'').padStart(3,'0')}`.localeCompare(
                `${b.gender||''}-${String(b.flightGroup||'').padStart(3,'0')}-${String(b.order||'').padStart(3,'0')}`));
            html += `<div class="class-header ${type==='synchro'?'sync-class-header':'indiv-class-header'}">${cn}</div>`;
            let curGender = '';
            cls.forEach(ath => {
                if (curGender !== ath.gender) {
                    if (curGender !== '') html += '</tbody></table></div>';
                    curGender = ath.gender;
                    const rh = Array.from({length: rules.qualifyingRoutines}, (_, i) => `<th class="col-btn">予選${i+1}</th>`).join('');
                    const fh = rules.hasFinal ? '<th class="col-btn">決勝</th>' : '';
                    html += `<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead>
                        <tr class="category-row"><td colspan="${3+rules.qualifyingRoutines+(rules.hasFinal?1:0)}" style="text-align:left;">${curGender}</td></tr>
                        <tr><th class="col-num">Gr</th><th class="col-num">順</th><th class="col-name">選手</th>${rh}${fh}</tr>
                        </thead><tbody>`;
                }
                const pHtml = type === 'synchro'
                    ? `<span style="font-weight:bold;">${ath.name1||''} / ${ath.name2||''}</span><br><small style="color:#888;">${ath.club1||''} / ${ath.club2||''}</small>`
                    : `<span style="font-weight:bold;">${ath.name1}</span><br><small style="color:#888;">${ath.club1}</small>`;
                html += `<tr class="${ath.isWithdrawn?'withdrawn-row':''}"><td class="col-num">${ath.flightGroup||'-'}</td><td class="col-num">${ath.order||'-'}</td><td class="col-name">${pHtml}</td>`;
                for (let i = 1; i <= rules.qualifyingRoutines; i++) html += `<td>${statusButton(ath, `pre${i}`, subMap)}</td>`;
                if (rules.hasFinal) html += `<td>${statusButton(ath, 'final', subMap)}</td>`;
                html += '</tr>';
            });
            if (cls.length > 0) html += '</tbody></table></div>';
        }
        return html;
    }

    function statusButton(ath, round, subMap) {
        if (ath.isWithdrawn) return `<span style="font-size:0.8em;color:#d32f2f;">棄権</span>`;
        const sub = subMap.get(`${eventId}_${ath.id}_${round}`);
        return `<a id="status-${ath.id}-${round}" href="entry.html?event=${eventId}&athlete=${ath.id}&round=${round}" class="status-link ${sub?'status-done':'status-none'}">${sub?'提出済':'入力'}</a>`;
    }

    // ── カウントダウンタイマー ────────────────────────────────
    let _timerIds = []; // 起動中のインターバルIDを管理（再呼び出し時にクリアするため）
    function startGlobalTimers(tour) {
        // 既存タイマーをすべてクリア（キャッシュ→Firestore更新時に正しく再描画するため）
        _timerIds.forEach(id => clearInterval(id));
        _timerIds = [];

        const area = document.getElementById('countdown-area');
        const deadlines = [
            { id: 'sub', label: '提出締切', time: tour.submissionDeadline },
            { id: 'rev', label: '修正締切', time: tour.revisionDeadline },
            { id: 'fin', label: '決勝締切', time: tour.finalSubmissionDeadline }
        ].filter(d => d.time).map(d => {
            const dt = new Date(d.time);
            return { ...d,
                timeStr: `${dt.getMonth()+1}/${dt.getDate()} ${String(dt.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(dt.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}`,
                ms: dt.getTime()
            };
        });
        if (deadlines.length === 0) { area.style.display = 'none'; return; }
        area.style.display = 'flex';
        area.innerHTML = deadlines.map(d =>
            `<div class="timer-card"><div class="timer-label">${d.label}</div><div class="timer-time">${d.timeStr}</div><div id="clock-${d.id}" class="timer-clock urgent"></div></div>`
        ).join('');
        const tick = () => {
            const now = Date.now();
            let allDone = true;
            deadlines.forEach(d => {
                const el = document.getElementById(`clock-${d.id}`);
                if (!el) return;
                const diff = d.ms - now;
                if (diff > 0) {
                    allDone = false;
                    el.className = 'timer-clock urgent';
                    el.textContent = `${Math.floor(diff/3600000)}h ${Math.floor(diff%3600000/60000)}m ${Math.floor(diff%60000/1000)}s`;
                } else {
                    el.className = 'timer-clock expired';
                    el.textContent = '終了';
                }
            });
            if (allDone) { _timerIds.forEach(id => clearInterval(id)); _timerIds = []; }
        };
        tick();
        _timerIds.push(setInterval(tick, 1000));
    }

    // ── バックグラウンド差分同期 ─────────────────────────────
    let liveTour     = null;
    let liveAthletes = [];

    async function backgroundSync() {
        if (!eventId || !liveAthletes.length) return;
        try {
            const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId)));
            const freshMap = new Map(snap.docs.map(d => [d.id, d.data()]));
            liveAthletes.forEach(ath => {
                ['pre1','pre2','pre3','final'].forEach(round => {
                    const el = document.getElementById(`status-${ath.id}-${round}`);
                    if (!el) return;
                    const sub = freshMap.get(`${eventId}_${ath.id}_${round}`) || null;
                    if (el.classList.contains('status-done') !== !!sub) updateStatusBadge(ath.id, round, sub);
                });
            });
            if (liveTour) saveCache(liveTour, liveAthletes, freshMap);
        } catch(e) { console.warn('BG sync failed:', e.message); }
    }

    // ── 初期化 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) return;

        if (params.get('from') === 'admin') {
            const backDiv = document.getElementById('admin-back');
            backDiv.style.display = 'block';
            backDiv.querySelector('a').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;
        }

        // ① キャッシュがあれば即座に描画（0ms）
        const cache = loadCache();
        if (cache?.tour && cache?.athletes) {
            liveTour     = cache.tour;
            liveAthletes = cache.athletes;
            applyTourInfo(cache.tour);
            startGlobalTimers(cache.tour);
            renderList(cache.tour, cache.athletes, new Map(Object.entries(cache.submissions || {})));
        }

        // ② 最新データを常時フェッチ
        try {
            const tourSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId));
            if (!tourSnap.exists()) return;
            const tour = tourSnap.data();
            liveTour = tour;
            applyTourInfo(tour);
            startGlobalTimers(tour); // timerStarted フラグで二重起動ガード

            const [subSnap, athSnap] = await Promise.all([
                getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
                getDocs(collection(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes"))
            ]);
            const freshSubMap = new Map(subSnap.docs.map(d => [d.id, d.data()]));
            const allAthletes = athSnap.docs.map(d => ({ ...d.data(), id: d.id }));
            liveAthletes = allAthletes;

            if (cache?.athletes) {
                // キャッシュ描画済み → 差分バッジ更新のみ
                allAthletes.forEach(ath => {
                    ['pre1','pre2','pre3','final'].forEach(round => {
                        const el = document.getElementById(`status-${ath.id}-${round}`);
                        if (!el) return;
                        const sub = freshSubMap.get(`${eventId}_${ath.id}_${round}`) || null;
                        if (el.classList.contains('status-done') !== !!sub) updateStatusBadge(ath.id, round, sub);
                    });
                });
            } else {
                // 初回：全体描画
                renderList(tour, allAthletes, freshSubMap);
            }
            saveCache(tour, allAthletes, freshSubMap);
        } catch(e) {
            console.error('Fetch failed:', e.message);
            if (!cache) document.getElementById('event-title').innerText = '読み込みに失敗しました';
        }
    }

    // ③ タブ復帰時にバックグラウンド差分同期
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
        if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') backgroundSync();
    });

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-app.js";
import { initializeFirestore, memoryLocalCache } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
import { getAuth } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-auth.js";

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: "AIzaSyBhSJsTmnzBCFrHuXuB8zGUTiQauOS96m4",
  authDomain: "trampoline-dd-test.firebaseapp.com",
  projectId: "trampoline-dd-test",
  storageBucket: "trampoline-dd-test.firebasestorage.app",
  messagingSenderId: "722487617244",
  appId: "1:722487617244:web:9ae10218c36c82763af619",
  measurementId: "G-47B0W81CKZ"
};

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
// memoryLocalCache: IndexedDB を一切使わずメモリのみでキャッシュ
// → iOS Safari プライベートモードで IndexedDB が制限される環境でもクラッシュしない
const db = initializeFirestore(app, { localCache: memoryLocalCache() });
const auth = getAuth(app);

const isLocalDev = location.hostname === 'localhost' || location.hostname === '127.0.0.1';

export { app, db, auth, isLocalDev };

File: hdts_test.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ja">
