<script>
  const EP = {
    health: { proxy: '/api/qira/health', direct: '/health',        label: 'health' },
    state:  { proxy: '/api/qira/state',  direct: '/state',         label: 'state'  },
    result: { proxy: '/api/qira/result', direct: '/routineresult', label: 'result' },
  };

  function getUrl(key) {
    const mode = document.getElementById('mode').value;
    const base = mode === 'direct'
      ? document.getElementById('direct-url').value.replace(/\/$/, '')
      : document.getElementById('base-url').value.replace(/\/$/, '');
    return base + EP[key][mode === 'direct' ? 'direct' : 'proxy'].replace(/.*\//, '/');
  }

  function fullUrl(key) {
    const mode = document.getElementById('mode').value;
    if (mode === 'direct') {
      return document.getElementById('direct-url').value.replace(/\/$/, '') + EP[key].direct;
    }
    return EP[key].proxy;
  }

  function updateEpLabels() {
    ['health','state','result'].forEach(k => {
      document.getElementById('ep-' + k).textContent = fullUrl(k);
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('mode').addEventListener('change', updateEpLabels);
  document.getElementById('base-url').addEventListener('input', updateEpLabels);
  document.getElementById('direct-url').addEventListener('input', updateEpLabels);
  updateEpLabels();

  // ---- CJP連携設定 ----
  const QIRA_KEY = 'qiraBaseUrl';

  function loadCjpConfig() {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(QIRA_KEY);
    const curEl = document.getElementById('cjp-current');
    if (saved) {
      curEl.textContent = '保存済み: ' + saved;
      const isRemote = !saved.includes('localhost') && !saved.includes('127.0.0.1');
      document.getElementById('cjp-mode').value = isRemote ? 'remote' : 'local';
      if (isRemote) {
        const m = saved.match(/http:\/\/([^:]+):(\d+)/);
        if (m) { document.getElementById('cjp-ip').value = m[1]; document.getElementById('cjp-port').value = m[2]; }
        document.getElementById('cjp-ip-row').style.display = '';
        // テスト用直接接続URLにも反映
        document.getElementById('direct-url').value = saved;
        updateEpLabels();
      }
    } else {
      curEl.textContent = '未設定（CJP画面はlocalhost:8080を使用）';
    }
  }

  async function saveCjpConfig() {
    const mode = document.getElementById('cjp-mode').value;
    const ip   = mode === 'remote' ? document.getElementById('cjp-ip').value.trim()   : 'localhost';
    const port = mode === 'remote' ? document.getElementById('cjp-port').value.trim() : '8080';
    const baseUrl = 'http://' + ip + ':' + port;

    // localStorage に保存（CJP画面の直接接続用）
    localStorage.setItem(QIRA_KEY, baseUrl);
    document.getElementById('direct-url').value = baseUrl;
    updateEpLabels();

    const st  = document.getElementById('cjp-save-status');
    const cur = document.getElementById('cjp-current');
    cur.textContent = '保存済み: ' + baseUrl;

    // ローカルサーバー（node server.js）が起動中なら /api/config にも送信
    let serverMsg = '';
    try {
      const r = await fetch('/api/config', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ host: ip, port: parseInt(port) }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1000)
      });
      if (r.ok) serverMsg = ' ＋ サーバー設定も更新';
    } catch { /* サーバー未起動の場合は無視 */ }

    st.style.color = '#3fb950';
    st.textContent = '✅ 保存しました' + serverMsg;
    setTimeout(() => { st.textContent = ''; }, 4000);
  }

  document.getElementById('cjp-mode').addEventListener('change', e => {
    document.getElementById('cjp-ip-row').style.display = e.target.value === 'remote' ? '' : 'none';
  });

  loadCjpConfig();

  function ts() { return new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ja-JP', {hour12:false}); }

  function syntaxHL(json) {
    return json
      .replace(/(".*?")\s*:/g, '<span style="color:#79c0ff">$1</span>:')
      .replace(/:\s*(".*?")/g, ': <span style="color:#a5d6ff">$1</span>')
      .replace(/:\s*(true|false)/g, ': <span style="color:#ff7b72">$1</span>')
      .replace(/:\s*(-?\d+\.?\d*)/g, ': <span style="color:#f0a742">$1</span>');
  }

  async function fetchEndpoint(key) {
    const url = fullUrl(key);
    const outEl = document.getElementById('out-' + key);
    const stEl  = document.getElementById('status-' + key);
    const tsEl  = document.getElementById('ts-' + key);
    const dot   = document.getElementById('dot-' + key);
    const btn   = key === 'result' ? document.getElementById('btn-result') : null;

    if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    dot.className = 'dot pending';
    stEl.innerHTML = '<span class="tag-warn">取得中...</span>';
    outEl.textContent = '取得中...';

    try {
      const t0  = performance.now();
      const res = await fetch(url);
      const ms  = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
      const raw = await res.text();
      const nowTs = ts();

      let pretty;
      try {
        const obj = JSON.parse(raw);
        pretty = JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2);
        outEl.innerHTML = syntaxHL(pretty);

        // /routineresult のパース
        if (key === 'result') {
          parsedResult(obj);
        }
      } catch {
        outEl.textContent = raw || '（空レスポンス）';
      }

      if (res.ok) {
        dot.className = 'dot ok';
        stEl.innerHTML = `<span class="tag-ok">✅ ${res.status} ${res.statusText}</span> <span style="color:#484f58">${ms}ms</span>`;
      } else {
        dot.className = 'dot err';
        stEl.innerHTML = `<span class="tag-err">❌ ${res.status} ${res.statusText}</span>`;
      }
      tsEl.textContent = `取得時刻: ${nowTs}`;
    } catch(e) {
      dot.className = 'dot err';
      stEl.innerHTML = `<span class="tag-err">❌ 接続失敗</span>`;
      outEl.textContent = e.message;
      tsEl.textContent = `取得時刻: ${ts()}`;
    } finally {
      if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
    }
  }

  function parsedResult(data) {
    const card = document.getElementById('card-parsed');
    const out  = document.getElementById('out-parsed');
    card.style.display = '';

    const type = data.type || '不明';
    const mat  = data.maturity || '不明';
    const r    = type === 'synchronized' ? data.synchronizedResult : data.individualResult;

    if (!r) {
      out.innerHTML = '<span class="tag-err">individualResult / synchronizedResult が見つかりません</span>';
      return;
    }

    const tof = r.timeOfFlightInSeconds;
    const hd  = r.horizontalDisplacement;
    const cnt = r.elementCount;

    let lines = [];
    lines.push(`<span class="tag-ok">type     :</span> ${type}`);
    lines.push(`<span class="tag-ok">maturity :</span> ${mat}`);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push(`<span class="tag-ok">ToF (秒)  :</span> ${tof != null ? tof.toFixed(3) : '<span class="tag-err">null</span>'}`);
    lines.push(`<span class="tag-ok">HD (点)   :</span> ${hd  != null ? hd.toFixed(1)  : '<span class="tag-err">null</span>'}`);
    lines.push(`<span class="tag-ok">技数      :</span> ${cnt != null ? cnt            : '<span class="tag-err">null</span>'}`);

    if (r.elements && r.elements.length > 0) {
      lines.push('');
      lines.push('<span style="color:#8b949e">--- 技別 ToF ---</span>');
      r.elements.forEach((el, i) => {
        lines.push(`  #${String(i+1).padStart(2,' ')} : ToF=${el.timeOfFlightInSeconds?.toFixed(3) ?? '-'}s  HD=${el.horizontalDisplacement?.toFixed(1) ?? '-'}`);
      });
    }

    out.innerHTML = lines.join('\n');
  }

  async function checkAll() {
    await Promise.all(['health','state','result'].map(k => fetchEndpoint(k)));
  }
</script>
</body>
</html>

                    <th style="width:70px;">申請</th>
                    <th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th>
                    <th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th><th class="d-declared">-</th>
                    <th class="cell-blank"></th><th class="cell-blank"></th><th class="toggle-col"></th>
                </tr>
                <!-- 実施行 -->
                <tr class="d-info-row" id="skill-symbols-row">
                    <th style="width:70px;white-space:normal;line-height:1.4;">実施<br><span id="d-confirmed-badge"></span></th>
                    <th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th>
                    <th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th><th class="skill-header">-</th>
                    <th>Bonus</th><th>SUM</th><th class="toggle-col"></th>
                </tr>
                <!-- DD行 -->
                <tr class="d-info-row" id="dd-row">
                    <th style="width:70px;">DD</th>
                    <th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th>
                    <th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th><th class="d-dd">-</th>
                    <th id="dd-bonus-cell">-</th><th id="dd-total-cell">-</th><th class="toggle-col"><label class="toggle-switch-sm"><input type="checkbox" id="d-mode-toggle" onchange="toggleDMode(this.checked)"><span class="track-sm"></span></label></th>
                </tr>
                <!-- 指定難度行 -->
                <tr class="d-info-row" id="designated-row">
                    <th style="width:70px;white-space:normal;line-height:1.4;">指定<br><span id="dsg-count" style="font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:normal;color:#90a4ae;">0/0</span></th>
                    <th class="d-designated" id="dsg-0" onclick="toggleDesignated(0)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-1" onclick="toggleDesignated(1)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-2" onclick="toggleDesignated(2)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-3" onclick="toggleDesignated(3)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-4" onclick="toggleDesignated(4)">·</th>
                    <th class="d-designated" id="dsg-5" onclick="toggleDesignated(5)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-6" onclick="toggleDesignated(6)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-7" onclick="toggleDesignated(7)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-8" onclick="toggleDesignated(8)">·</th><th class="d-designated" id="dsg-9" onclick="toggleDesignated(9)">·</th>
                    <th>-</th><th>-</th><th class="toggle-col"></th>
                </tr>
                <tr class="section-head hdts-section-head">
                    <th colspan="14">HDTS</th>
                </tr>
                <!-- HDTS 技別 ToF 行 -->
                <tr class="d-info-row hdts-row" id="hdts-tof-row">
                    <th class="hdts-label">HDTS<br>ToF(s)</th>
                    <th id="hdts-tof-1">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-2">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-3">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-4">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-5">-</th>
                    <th id="hdts-tof-6">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-7">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-8">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-9">-</th><th id="hdts-tof-10">-</th>
                    <th>-</th><th class="hdts-sum" id="hdts-tof-sum">-</th><th class="toggle-col"></th>
                </tr>
                <!-- HDTS 技別 HD 行 -->
                <tr class="d-info-row hdts-row" id="hdts-hd-row">
                    <th class="hdts-label">HDTS<br>HD(減)</th>
                    <th id="hdts-hd-1">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-2">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-3">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-4">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-5">-</th>
                    <th id="hdts-hd-6">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-7">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-8">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-9">-</th><th id="hdts-hd-10">-</th>
                    <th>-</th><th class="hdts-sum" id="hdts-hd-sum">-</th><th class="toggle-col"></th>
                </tr>
            </tfoot>
        </table>
        </div>
        </div>

        <div class="input-grid">
            <div class="input-card">
                <label>VALID SKILLS</label>
                <select id="valid-count" onchange="updateValid(this.value)">
                    <option value="10">10本</option>
                    <option value="9">9本</option><option value="8">8本</option><option value="7">7本</option><option value="6">6本</option><option value="5">5本</option>
                    <option value="4">4本</option><option value="3">3本</option><option value="2">2本</option><option value="1">1本</option><option value="0">0本</option>
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="input-card">
                <label><span id="tof-input-label">ToF (T)</span> <button class="btn-th-mode" id="tof-mode-btn" onclick="toggleTofMode()">読込</button></label>
                <input type="number" id="tof" step="0.001" onchange="calc()">
            </div>
            <div class="input-card">
                <label>HD <button class="btn-th-mode" id="hd-mode-btn" onclick="toggleHdMode()">読込</button></label>
                <input type="number" id="hd" step="0.1" onchange="calc()">
            </div>
            <div class="input-card">
                <label>PENALTY (P) <button class="btn-th-mode" id="pen-mode-btn" onclick="togglePenMode()">読込</button></label>
                <input type="number" id="pen" step="0.1" value="0.0" onchange="calc()">
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="score-summary">
            <div class="score-box"><label>Difficulty</label><div id="res-d">0.0</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>Execution</label><div id="res-e">0.0</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label id="res-t-label">T</label><div id="res-t">0.000</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>HD</label><div id="res-hd">0.000</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>Total</label><div id="res-total" style="color:var(--success)">0.00</div></div>
        </div>

        <div class="action-row">
            <!-- 1行目 -->
            <button class="btn-action btn-confirm"        id="btn-confirm"        onclick="publish()">確定&amp;表示</button>
            <button class="btn-action btn-unlock"         id="btn-unlock"         onclick="unlock()">修正</button>
            <button class="btn-action btn-republish"      id="btn-republish"      onclick="republish()">再表示</button>
            <button class="btn-action btn-calculating"    id="btn-calculating"    onclick="showCalculating()">集計中</button>
            <button class="btn-action btn-hdts"           id="btn-hdts"           onclick="getFromQira()">HDTS</button>
            <!-- 2行目（今後ここに追加） -->
            <button class="btn-action btn-confirm-silent" id="btn-confirm-silent" onclick="publishSilent()">確定のみ</button>
        </div>
        <div id="qira-status" class="qira-status-bar"></div>
    </div>
    </div><!-- /.console-scroll -->
</div><!-- /.console-wrapper -->

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, onSnapshot, getDocs, getDocFromServer, collection, query, where, setDoc, deleteDoc, serverTimestamp, deleteField } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { ensureAdmin } from "./auth.js";
    import { getTournament, getAthletes, getFinalOrders } from "./cache.js";

    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event'), panel = params.get('panel'), role = params.get('role');
    let athleteId = params.get('athlete'), round = params.get('round') || "pre1";

    let athletes = [], submissions = {}, results = {}, currentTour = null;
    let fType = 'individual', fClass = '', fGender = '', fFinalOnly = false;
    let manualRows = new Set(), pendingEdits = {}, latestData = {}, unsubscribeScores = null, unsubscribeSub = null;
    let tofMode = 'auto', hdMode = 'auto', penMode = 'auto'; // 'auto'=Firestore読込, 'manual'=手動入力
    let isLocked = false;
    let lockedOfficialSkills = null; // 確定時にスナップショットした実施技（D審判の更新を遮断）
    let _preciseE = 0; // 表示は第1位だが保存用に全精度を保持（シンクロ4人時の第2位など）
    let _lastEc   = null; // 最後に計算した calcExecution 結果（publish 時に per-skill E を保存するため）

    function roundLabel(r) {
        if (r === 'pre1')  return 'Routine 1';
        if (r === 'pre2')  return 'Routine 2';
        if (r === 'final') return 'Final';
        return r.toUpperCase();
    }

    window.openRanking = function() {
        const base = `ranking.html?event=${eventId}`;
        const ath  = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
        if (!ath) { window.open(base, '_blank'); return; }
        const cls      = encodeURIComponent(ath.class || ath.category || '');
        const gender   = encodeURIComponent(ath.gender || '');
        const type     = encodeURIComponent(ath.type   || 'individual');
        const rkRound  = round === 'final' ? 'final' : 'qualifying'; // pre1/pre2 → qualifying
        window.open(`${base}&class=${cls}&gender=${gender}&type=${type}&round=${rkRound}`, '_blank');
    };

    // 現在の選手クラスに応じた E審判人数を返す
    function getNumE() {
        const ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
        if (ath) {
            const type = ath.type || 'individual';
            const cls  = ath.class || ath.category || '';
            const n = parseInt(currentTour?.classRules?.[type]?.[cls]?.eJudgeCount);
            if (!isNaN(n) && n > 0) return n;
        }
        return currentTour?.numExecJudges || 6;
    }
    let skillMasterMap = {};  // skillId → { symbol, dd, ... }
    let dMode = 'auto';           // 'auto' | 'manual'
    let dModeAutoExplicit = false; // ユーザーが明示的にautoを選択した場合true（手動復元を抑制）
    // 指定難度（-1=無効、0=0本指定(全DD=0)、1-9=N本指定）
    let designatedDDCount = -1;
    let designatedDDIndices = []; // 指定中の技インデックス（0-based）
    let designatedDDManualChanged = false; // 審判が手動で変更したフラグ

    function withTimeout(promise, ms) {
        return Promise.race([
            promise,
            new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('TIMEOUT')), ms))
        ]);
    }

    function showLoadState(msg, isError) {
        document.getElementById('sidebar-list').innerHTML = `
            <div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:${isError ? '#e53935' : '#90a4ae'};">
                <div style="font-size:2em;margin-bottom:10px;">${isError ? '⚠️' : '⏳'}</div>
                <div style="font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:${isError ? '14px' : '0'};">${msg}</div>
                ${isError ? '<button onclick="retryInit()" style="padding:10px 24px;background:#1a73e8;color:white;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:0.9em;cursor:pointer;font-weight:bold;">🔄 再読み込み</button>' : ''}
            </div>`;
        if (isError) document.getElementById('event-name-sidebar').innerText = '読み込みエラー';
    }

    window.retryInit = () => init();
    let _initRunning = false;

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId || _initRunning) return;
        _initRunning = true;
        document.getElementById('menu-back').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;
        document.getElementById('exit-link').href = `judge_role_select.html?event=${eventId}`;
        document.getElementById('panel-role-label').innerText = `PANEL ${panel} | ROLE ${role}`;

        // 初期化完了前はアクションボタンを無効化
        ['btn-confirm','btn-confirm-silent','btn-unlock','btn-republish','btn-calculating','btn-hdts'].forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.disabled = true;
        });

        showLoadState('データを読み込んでいます...', false);
        try {
        const [tourData, athleteList, subSnap, resSnap, smSnap] = await withTimeout(
            Promise.all([
                getTournament(eventId),
                getAthletes(eventId),
                getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
                getDocs(query(collection(db, "results"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
                getDocs(collection(db, "skillMaster"))
            ]),
            20000
        );
        // Firestore skillMaster → skillMasterMap（ドキュメントID = 数値文字列）
        smSnap.forEach(d => {
            skillMasterMap[d.id] = {
                id: d.id,
                symbol: d.data().symbol,
                note: d.data().note || '',
                dd: parseFloat(d.data().dd) || 0,
                isTriple: d.data().isTriple === true,
                direction: d.data().direction || '',
                displayOrder: d.data().displayOrder ?? parseInt(d.id)
            };
        });

        if (!tourData) throw new Error('大会データが見つかりません');
        currentTour = tourData;
        document.getElementById('event-name-sidebar').innerText = currentTour.name;
        athletes = athleteList;
        // 決勝試技順はキャッシュ除外なので毎回Firestoreから取得してマージ
        const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
        athletes.forEach(a => {
            const fo = finalOrders[a.id];
            if (fo) { a.finalOrder = fo.finalOrder; a.isInFinal = fo.isInFinal; }
        });
        subSnap.forEach(d => submissions[d.id] = d.data());
        resSnap.forEach(d => results[d.id] = d.data());

        renderSidebar();
        renderScores({}); // 審判行を常に表示（numExecJudges 人分の空行）

        if (athleteId) {
            const ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
            if (ath) {
                if (ath.type) fType = ath.type; // fType をURLロード時の選手typeに合わせる
                applySynchroUI(fType === 'synchro');
                const dispName = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2 ? `${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2}` : ath.name1;
                const clubDisp = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.club2 && ath.club1 !== ath.club2
                    ? `${ath.club1} / ${ath.club2}` : (ath.club1 || '');
                const displayOrder1 = (round === 'final' && ath.finalOrder != null) ? ath.finalOrder : ath.order;
                document.getElementById('ath-name').innerText = `No.${displayOrder1} ${dispName}`;
                document.getElementById('ath-club').textContent = clubDisp;
                document.getElementById('ath-meta').innerText = `${ath.class || ath.category || '-'} / ${roundLabel(round)}`;
                updateDesignatedForAthlete(ath, round);

                // E審判が自動追従するために liveState を更新（パネルごとに分離）
                await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                    liveState: {
                        [panel]: {
                            athleteId,
                            athleteName: `No.${displayOrder1} ${dispName}`,
                            athleteClass: ath.class || ath.category || '',
                            athleteGender: ath.gender || '',
                            athleteType: ath.type || 'individual',
                            round,
                            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
                        }
                    }
                }, { merge: true });
            }
            watchScores();
            // クラスルールのToF/HDデフォルトモードを適用
            const ath2 = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
            if (ath2) {
                const athType  = ath2.type || 'individual';
                const athClass = ath2.class || ath2.category || '';
                const cRule    = currentTour.classRules?.[athType]?.[athClass] || {};
                // 新形式(tofDefaultMode/hdDefaultMode)、旧形式(tofHdDefaultMode)両対応
                if ((cRule.tofDefaultMode || cRule.tofHdDefaultMode) === 'manual') toggleTofMode();
                if ((cRule.hdDefaultMode  || cRule.tofHdDefaultMode) === 'manual') toggleHdMode();
                applyHdtsVisibility(ath2);
            }
        }
        } catch(e) {
            console.error('初期化エラー:', e);
            const msg = e.message === 'TIMEOUT' ? 'ネットワーク接続に時間がかかっています' : 'データの読み込みに失敗しました';
            showLoadState(msg, true);
            document.getElementById('ath-name').innerText = msg;
            document.getElementById('ath-meta').innerText = e.message === 'TIMEOUT' ? '' : (e.message || 'ネットワークエラー');
        } finally {
            _initRunning = false;
            // 選手選択に依存しないボタンは初期化完了後に常に有効化
            ['btn-calculating','btn-republish','btn-hdts'].forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.disabled = false;
            });
        }
    }

    function renderSidebar() {
        const isSynchro = fType === 'synchro';

        // 種目でまず分ける
        const byType = athletes.filter(a => (a.type || 'individual') === fType);

        // クラス一覧（表示順を維持）
        const tourClassDefs = (currentTour?.classes?.[fType] || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
        const rawClasses = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.class || a.category || ''))];
        const classes = [...tourClassDefs.filter(c=>rawClasses.includes(c)), ...rawClasses.filter(c=>!tourClassDefs.includes(c))].filter(Boolean);

        // 性別一覧（個人・シンクロ共通）
        const genders = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.gender || ''))].filter(Boolean);

        // タブ描画
        const hasType = athletes.some(a => (a.type || 'individual') === 'synchro'); // シンクロ選手が存在するか
        const filterEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-filters');
        let filHtml = '';

        if (hasType) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row">
                <span class="filter-label">種目</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='individual'?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('type','individual')">個人</button>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='synchro'?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('type','synchro')">シンクロ</button>
            </div>`;
        }

        if (classes.length >= 1) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">クラス</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fClass===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','')">全て</button>
                ${classes.map(c => `<button class="tab-btn ${fClass===c?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','${c}')">${c}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }

        if (genders.length > 1) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">性別</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fGender===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','')">全て</button>
                ${genders.map(g => `<button class="tab-btn ${fGender===g?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','${g}')">${g}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }

        // 決勝フィルター行（常時表示）
        filHtml += `<div class="filter-row">
            <span class="filter-label">ラウンド</span>
            <button class="tab-btn ${!fFinalOnly?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',false)">予選</button>
            <button class="tab-btn ${fFinalOnly?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',true)">🏆 決勝</button>
        </div>`;

        filterEl.style.display = filHtml ? '' : 'none';
        filterEl.innerHTML = filHtml;

        // 絞り込み＆ソート
        let filtered;
        if (fFinalOnly) {
            filtered = byType
                .filter(a => a.finalOrder != null)                           // 試技順確定済みのみ
                .filter(a => !fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
                .filter(a => !fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender)
                .sort((a, b) => a.finalOrder - b.finalOrder);               // 試技順（昇順）
        } else {
            filtered = byType
                .filter(a => !fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
                .filter(a => !fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender)
                .sort((a, b) => {
                    const ca = (a.class || a.category || ''), cb = (b.class || b.category || '');
                    if (ca !== cb) return ca.localeCompare(cb);
                    const ga = a.gender || '', gb = b.gender || '';
                    if (ga !== gb) return ga.localeCompare(gb);
                    return (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999);
                });
        }

        const listEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-list');
        const parts = [];
        let lastCat = "", lastGender = "";

        filtered.forEach(ath => {
            const cls = ath.class || ath.category || '';
            if (cls !== lastCat) {
                parts.push(`<div class="cat-group">${cls}</div>`);
                lastCat = cls; lastGender = "";
            }
            if (!fFinalOnly && (ath.gender || '') !== lastGender) {
                parts.push(`<div class="gender-group">${ath.gender || ''}</div>`);
                lastGender = ath.gender || '';
            }

            // クラスごとの予選本数を classRules から取得（なければトップレベル値にフォールバック）
            const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
            const classRule = currentTour.classRules?.[athType]?.[cls] || {};
            const numPre = classRule.qualifyingRoutines ?? currentTour.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
            const hasFinalClass = classRule.hasFinal ?? false;

            let dotsHtml = "";
            if (fFinalOnly) {
                // 決勝モード: Fドットのみ（番号列に試技順を表示）
                dotsHtml = getDotHtml(ath.id, 'final', 'F');
            } else {
                for (let i = 1; i <= numPre; i++) dotsHtml += getDotHtml(ath.id, `pre${i}`, `R${i}`);
                if (hasFinalClass) dotsHtml += getDotHtml(ath.id, 'final', 'F');
            }

            const isActive = ath.id === athleteId;
            const orderNum = fFinalOnly ? ath.finalOrder : ath.order;
            const nameHtml = isSynchro
                ? `<span class="ath-order">${orderNum}.</span>${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2 || ''}`
                : `<span class="ath-order">${orderNum}.</span>${ath.name1}`;

            parts.push(`
                <div class="athlete-row ${isActive ? 'active-athlete' : ''}">
                    <div class="ath-name-text">${nameHtml}</div>
                    <div class="round-box">${dotsHtml}</div>
                </div>`);
        });
        listEl.innerHTML = parts.join('');
    }

    window.setFilter = (key, val) => {
        if (key === 'type')      { fType = val; fClass = ''; fGender = ''; fFinalOnly = false; }
        if (key === 'class')     fClass  = val;
        if (key === 'gender')    fGender = val;
        if (key === 'finalOnly') fFinalOnly = (val === true);
        renderSidebar();
    };

    function getDotHtml(aid, rid, label) {
        const id = `${eventId}_${aid}_${rid}`;
        const cls = (results[id]?.published ? 'has-res' : (submissions[id] ? 'has-sub' : '')) +
                    (aid === athleteId && rid === round ? ' current' : '');
        return `<a href="#" class="r-dot ${cls}" onclick="switchAthlete('${aid}','${rid}');return false;">${label}</a>`;
    }

    // ---- 選手切り替え（ページリロードなし） ----
    window.switchAthlete = async function(aid, rid) {
        if (aid === athleteId && rid === round) return;

        // 旧リスナー解除
        if (unsubscribeScores) { unsubscribeScores(); unsubscribeScores = null; }
        if (unsubscribeSub)    { unsubscribeSub();    unsubscribeSub    = null; }

        // 変数更新
        athleteId = aid;
        round = rid;

        // 指定難度リセット
        designatedDDCount = -1; designatedDDIndices = []; designatedDDManualChanged = false;
        renderDesignatedRow();

        // 手動入力状態リセット
        manualRows = new Set();
        pendingEdits = {};
        latestData = {};

        // D mode リセット
        dMode = 'auto';
        dModeAutoExplicit = false;
        const dToggle = document.getElementById('d-mode-toggle');
        if (dToggle) dToggle.checked = false;
        document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
        document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
        const dBonusCell = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
        if (dBonusCell) { dBonusCell.contentEditable = 'false'; dBonusCell.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }
        const dTotalCell0 = document.getElementById('dd-total-cell');
        if (dTotalCell0) { dTotalCell0.contentEditable = 'false'; dTotalCell0.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }

        // classRules が更新されている場合を考慮して大会データをサーバーから最新取得
        try {
            const _freshSnap = await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId));
            if (_freshSnap.exists()) currentTour = _freshSnap.data();
        } catch(e) { /* ネットワーク不良時は既存の currentTour を使用 */ }

        // ToF/HD/Pen mode リセット → classRules の初期値を直接適用（'auto' ハードコード不使用）
        isLocked = false;
        const _resetMode = (btnId, inpId, defaultVal = '') => {
            const btn = document.getElementById(btnId);
            const inp = document.getElementById(inpId);
            if (btn) { btn.textContent = '読込'; btn.classList.remove('manual'); btn.disabled = false; }
            if (inp) { inp.style.borderColor = ''; inp.style.color = ''; inp.style.background = '';
                       inp.value = defaultVal; inp.disabled = false; }
        };
        {
            const _sa    = athletes.find(a => a.id === aid);
            const _saT   = _sa?.type || 'individual';
            const _saC   = _sa?.class || _sa?.category || '';
            const _saR   = currentTour?.classRules?.[_saT]?.[_saC] || {};
            tofMode = 'auto'; _resetMode('tof-mode-btn', 'tof');
            hdMode  = 'auto'; _resetMode('hd-mode-btn',  'hd');
            penMode = 'auto'; _resetMode('pen-mode-btn', 'pen', '0');
            // classRulesの初期モードをリセット直後に適用
            if ((_saR.tofDefaultMode || _saR.tofHdDefaultMode) === 'manual') toggleTofMode();
            if ((_saR.hdDefaultMode  || _saR.tofHdDefaultMode) === 'manual') toggleHdMode();
        }

        // HDTS 技別行リセット
        resetHdtsRows();

        // バナー更新
        const ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === aid);
        if (ath) {
            if (ath.type && ath.type !== fType) { fType = ath.type; fClass = ''; fGender = ''; } // 種目切替時はクラス・性別フィルタもリセット
            else if (ath.type) fType = ath.type;
            applySynchroUI(fType === 'synchro');
            const dispName2 = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2 ? `${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2}` : ath.name1;
            const clubDisp2 = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.club2 && ath.club1 !== ath.club2
                ? `${ath.club1} / ${ath.club2}` : (ath.club1 || '');
            const displayOrder2 = (rid === 'final' && ath.finalOrder != null) ? ath.finalOrder : ath.order;
            document.getElementById('ath-name').innerText = `No.${displayOrder2} ${dispName2}`;
            document.getElementById('ath-club').textContent = clubDisp2;
            document.getElementById('ath-meta').innerText = `${ath.class || ath.category || '-'} / ${roundLabel(rid)}`;

            // liveState 更新（選手切り替え時は必ずロック解除）
            await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                liveState: { [panel]: {
                    athleteId: aid,
                    athleteName: `No.${displayOrder2} ${dispName2}`,
                    athleteClass: ath.class || ath.category || '',
                    athleteGender: ath.gender || '',
                    athleteType: ath.type || 'individual',
                    round: rid, updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
                    locked: false
                }}
            }, { merge: true });

            // 指定難度ロード
            updateDesignatedForAthlete(ath, rid);

            applyHdtsVisibility(ath);
        }

        // URL 更新（ブラウザ履歴に残す）
        history.pushState({}, '', `judge_cjp.html?event=${eventId}&panel=${panel}&role=${role}&athlete=${aid}&round=${rid}`);

        // サイドバー更新 → スコア監視開始
        renderSidebar();
        watchScores();
        watchSubmission();
    };

    // ---- 指定難度 関数群 ----
    function renderDesignatedRow() {
        const row = document.getElementById('designated-row');
        if (!row) return;
        // 1本以上指定時のみ行を表示（0本指定=全DD0なのでチェック操作不要）
        row.style.display = designatedDDCount > 0 ? '' : 'none';
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const cell = document.getElementById(`dsg-${i}`);
            if (!cell) continue;
            const sel = designatedDDIndices.includes(i);
            cell.textContent = sel ? '✓' : '·';
            cell.classList.toggle('dsg-on', sel);
        }
        const countEl = document.getElementById('dsg-count');
        if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `${designatedDDIndices.length}/${designatedDDCount}`;
    }

    window.toggleDesignated = function(idx) {
        if (designatedDDCount <= 0) return; // 無効または0本指定時は操作不可
        const pos = designatedDDIndices.indexOf(idx);
        if (pos >= 0) {
            designatedDDIndices.splice(pos, 1);
        } else {
            if (designatedDDIndices.length >= designatedDDCount) return; // 上限
            designatedDDIndices.push(idx);
        }
        designatedDDManualChanged = true;
        renderDesignatedRow();
        renderScores(latestData);
    };

    function updateDesignatedForAthlete(ath, rid) {
        if (!ath) {
            designatedDDCount = -1; designatedDDIndices = []; designatedDDManualChanged = false;
            renderDesignatedRow(); return;
        }
        const athType  = ath.type || 'individual';
        const athClass = ath.class || ath.category || '';
        const classRule = currentTour?.classRules?.[athType]?.[athClass] || {};
        // 指定難度は2本合計・予選1本目のみ有効（-1=無効, 0=全DD0, 1-9=N本指定）
        const rawDDCount = (classRule.designatedDDCount != null && classRule.designatedDDCount >= 0) ? classRule.designatedDDCount : -1;
        designatedDDCount = (rawDDCount >= 0 && classRule.qualifyingRoutines === 2 && classRule.scoreMode === 'total' && rid === 'pre1')
            ? rawDDCount : -1;

        const resId = `${eventId}_${ath.id}_${rid}`;
        const officialIdx    = results[resId]?.officialDesignatedDDIndices;
        const submissionIdx  = submissions[resId]?.designatedDDIndices;
        designatedDDIndices  = [...(officialIdx || submissionIdx || [])];
        designatedDDManualChanged = false;
        renderDesignatedRow();
    }

    function watchSubmission() {
        if (unsubscribeSub) unsubscribeSub();
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        unsubscribeSub = onSnapshot(doc(db, "submissions", resId), snap => {
            submissions[resId] = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
            // 審判が未変更の場合、選手の申告した指定インデックスを反映
            if (!designatedDDManualChanged && designatedDDCount > 0) {
                const idx = submissions[resId]?.designatedDDIndices;
                if (idx) { designatedDDIndices = [...idx]; renderDesignatedRow(); }
            }
            renderScores(latestData);
        });
    }

    function watchScores() {
        if (unsubscribeScores) { unsubscribeScores(); unsubscribeScores = null; }
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        unsubscribeScores = onSnapshot(doc(db, "results", resId), async (snap) => {
            latestData = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : {};
            // ローカルキャッシュ更新（getDotHtml のドット判定に使用）
            if (snap.exists()) { results[resId] = latestData; }
            else               { delete results[resId]; }
            const newLocked = latestData.published === true;
            const lockChanged = newLocked !== isLocked;
            renderScores(latestData);
            renderSidebar(); // ドット色をリアルタイムに更新
            // E審判のロック状態をFirestoreに同期（変化があった場合のみ）
            if (lockChanged) {
                setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                    liveState: { [panel]: { locked: newLocked } }
                }, { merge: true }).catch(() => {});
            }
        });
    }

    function applyLock(locked) {
        isLocked = locked;
        document.getElementById('btn-confirm').disabled        =  locked;  // 確定済みは再確定不可
        document.getElementById('btn-confirm-silent').disabled =  locked;  // 同上
        document.getElementById('btn-unlock').disabled         = !locked;  // 未確定時は修正不要
        document.getElementById('btn-republish').disabled   = false;    // 選手選択後は常に有効
        document.getElementById('btn-calculating').disabled = false;    // 選手選択後は常に有効
        document.getElementById('btn-hdts').disabled        = locked;   // 確定後は操作不可
        // 確定後: 手動切替・有効本数・入力欄をロック
        document.getElementById('tof-mode-btn').disabled = locked;
        document.getElementById('hd-mode-btn').disabled  = locked;
        document.getElementById('pen-mode-btn').disabled = locked;
        document.getElementById('valid-count').disabled  = locked;
        document.getElementById('tof').disabled          = locked;
        document.getElementById('hd').disabled           = locked;
        document.getElementById('pen').disabled          = locked;
        document.querySelectorAll('.sn-th').forEach(th => {
            th.style.pointerEvents = locked ? 'none' : '';
            th.style.cursor        = locked ? 'default' : '';
            th.style.opacity       = locked ? '0.5' : '';
        });
        // E審判 行ごとの手動トグルをロック
        document.querySelectorAll('.toggle-col input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(cb => {
            cb.disabled = locked;
        });
        // 得点削除ボタン: 選手選択後は常に表示
        const delBtn = document.getElementById('btn-delete-result');
        if (delBtn) delBtn.style.display = athleteId ? '' : 'none';
        // 棄権ボタン: DNS でなく、未確定で選手選択済みのときのみ表示
        const isDns = latestData?.dns === true;
        const dnsBtn = document.getElementById('btn-dns');
        if (dnsBtn) dnsBtn.style.display = (athleteId && !locked && !isDns) ? '' : 'none';
        // DNSバナー
        const dnsBanner = document.getElementById('dns-banner');
        if (dnsBanner) dnsBanner.classList.toggle('show', isDns);
    }

    window.unlock = async function() {
        applyLock(false);
        lockedOfficialSkills = null;
        // 指定難度を申告値にリセット
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        const submissionIdx = submissions[resId]?.designatedDDIndices;
        designatedDDIndices = [...(submissionIdx || [])];
        designatedDDManualChanged = false;
        renderDesignatedRow();
        await Promise.all([
            setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                liveState: { [panel]: { locked: false } }
            }, { merge: true }),
            athleteId ? setDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId), {
                verifiedAt: deleteField(),
                dConfirmedAt: deleteField(),
                published: false   // D審判の修正を許可
            }, { merge: true }) : Promise.resolve()
        ]);
    };

    // 得点削除: results ドキュメントを完全削除してゼロ状態に戻す
    window.deleteResult = async function() {
        if (!athleteId) return;
        if (!confirm('この選手のラウンドの得点データをすべて削除しますか？\nD審判・E審判の入力内容も含めて完全に削除されます。')) return;
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        try {
            await Promise.all([
                deleteDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId)),
                setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                    liveState: { [panel]: { locked: false } },
                    calculating: false
                }, { merge: true })
            ]);

            // ローカル状態リセット（switchAthlete と同等）
            delete results[resId];
            latestData = {};
            manualRows = new Set();
            pendingEdits = {};
            dMode = 'auto';
            dModeAutoExplicit = false;
            lockedOfficialSkills = null;
            const dToggle = document.getElementById('d-mode-toggle');
            if (dToggle) dToggle.checked = false;
            document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
            document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
            const dBonusCell = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
            if (dBonusCell) { dBonusCell.contentEditable = 'false'; dBonusCell.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }
            const dTotalCell1 = document.getElementById('dd-total-cell');
            if (dTotalCell1) { dTotalCell1.contentEditable = 'false'; dTotalCell1.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }

            // 指定難度を申告値にリセット
            const submissionIdx = submissions[resId]?.designatedDDIndices;
            designatedDDIndices = [...(submissionIdx || [])];
            designatedDDManualChanged = false;
            renderDesignatedRow();

            // ロック解除・申告技をデフォルトとして再表示
            applyLock(false);
            const subData = submissions[resId];
            const initSkills = (subData?.skills || []).map(s => {
                if (!s) return '';
                if (typeof s === 'object' && s !== null) {
                    if (s.id) return String(s.id);
                    // idなしオブジェクト（{skill,dd}形式）→ symbolからID逆引き
                    const found = s.skill ? Object.values(skillMasterMap).find(v => v.symbol === s.skill) : null;
                    return found ? found.id : (s.skill || '');
                }
                return String(s);
            });
            renderScores(initSkills.length > 0 ? { officialSkills: initSkills } : {});
            renderSidebar();
        } catch(e) {
            alert('削除に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    // ---- 棄権（DNS）----
    window.declareDNS = async function() {
        if (!athleteId) return;
        if (!confirm(`No.${athletes.find(a=>a.id===athleteId)?.order || ''} を棄権（DNS）に設定しますか？`)) return;
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        try {
            await Promise.all([
                setDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId), {
                    eventId, athleteId, round,
                    dns: true, published: true, dnsAt: serverTimestamp()
                }, { merge: true }),
                setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                    liveState: { [panel]: { locked: true } }
                }, { merge: true })
            ]);
        } catch(e) { alert('エラー: ' + e.message); }
    };

    // 棄権取り消し: results を完全削除してゼロ状態に戻す
    window.cancelDNS = async function() {
        if (!athleteId) return;
        if (!confirm('棄権を取り消しますか？\n得点データも削除されます。')) return;
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        try {
            await Promise.all([
                deleteDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId)),
                setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                    liveState: { [panel]: { locked: false } }, calculating: false
                }, { merge: true })
            ]);
            delete results[resId];
            latestData = {};
            manualRows = new Set(); pendingEdits = {};
            dMode = 'auto'; dModeAutoExplicit = false; lockedOfficialSkills = null;
            const dToggle = document.getElementById('d-mode-toggle');
            if (dToggle) dToggle.checked = false;
            document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable='false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
            document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable='false'; el.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); });
            const dBc = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
            if (dBc) { dBc.contentEditable='false'; dBc.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }
            const dTotalCell2 = document.getElementById('dd-total-cell');
            if (dTotalCell2) { dTotalCell2.contentEditable='false'; dTotalCell2.classList.remove('d-manual-cell'); }
            const submissionIdx = submissions[resId]?.designatedDDIndices;
            designatedDDIndices = [...(submissionIdx || [])];
            designatedDDManualChanged = false;
            renderDesignatedRow();
            applyLock(false);
            renderScores({});
            renderSidebar();
        } catch(e) { alert('取り消しに失敗しました: ' + e.message); }
    };

    // 再表示: publishedAtを更新して会場ディスプレイに再アニメーション
    window.republish = async function() {
        if (!athleteId) return;
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        await Promise.all([
            setDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId), { publishedAt: serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true }),
            // 再表示で別の画面が出るので集計中表示を解除
            setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), { calculating: false }, { merge: true })
        ]);
    };

    // 集計中表示: 会場ディスプレイに選手情報付きの「得点集計中」画面を出す（審判側は変更なし）
    window.showCalculating = async function() {
        await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
            calculating: true,
            calculatingAthleteId: athleteId || null,
            calculatingRound:     round     || null
        }, { merge: true });
    };


    // ---- セル色クラス決定（2段階）----
    // v: 0.0-1.0 形式の減点値, sr: skillResult, jIdx: 審判インデックス（jKeys中の位置）
    // i: 技番号, vLimit: 有効本数
    function _resolveRange(sr, jIdx) {
        // シンクロ: jIdx偶数=奇数グループ(E1,E3,E5), jIdx奇数=偶数グループ(E2,E4,E6)
        if (sr.groupData) {
            const gr = jIdx % 2 === 0 ? sr.groupData.oddResult : sr.groupData.evenResult;
            return { midMin: gr.midMin, midMax: gr.midMax, isMiddle: gr.indices.has(jIdx) };
        }
        return { midMin: sr.midMin, midMax: sr.midMax, isMiddle: sr.indices.has(jIdx) };
    }
    function cellColorCls(v, sr, jIdx, i, vLimit) {
        if (i >= vLimit || v === null) return v !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        const { midMin, midMax, isMiddle } = _resolveRange(sr, jIdx);
        if (midMin === null) return v !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        if (isMiddle) return 'val-filled'; // 採用審判は無色
        if (v < midMin - 1e-9) {
            const diff = Math.round((midMin - v) * 10);
            return diff >= 2 ? 'low-cell' : 'low-cell-mild';
        }
        if (v > midMax + 1e-9) {
            const diff = Math.round((v - midMax) * 10);
            return diff >= 2 ? 'high-cell' : 'high-cell-mild';
        }
        return 'val-filled';
    }
    function landingColorCls(lv, lr, jIdx, vLimit) {
        if (vLimit < 10 || lv === null) return lv !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        const { midMin, midMax, isMiddle } = _resolveRange(lr, jIdx);
        if (midMin === null) return lv !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        if (isMiddle) return 'val-filled';
        if (lv < midMin - 1e-9) {
            const diff = Math.round((midMin - lv) * 10);
            return diff >= 2 ? 'low-cell' : 'low-cell-mild';
        }
        if (lv > midMax + 1e-9) {
            const diff = Math.round((lv - midMax) * 10);
            return diff >= 2 ? 'high-cell' : 'high-cell-mild';
        }
        return 'val-filled';
    }

    // ---- 中間2名ルール（グループインデックス付き） ----
    // groupIdxs: 例 [0,2,4] → 全審判配列中のインデックス
    function middle2Synchro(allVals, groupIdxs) {
        const groupVals = groupIdxs.map(gi => allVals[gi]);
        const filled = groupVals.map((v, li) => ({ v, gi: groupIdxs[li] }))
                                .filter(x => x.v !== null && x.v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length < 2) return { sum: 0, indices: new Set(), midMin: null, midMax: null };
        const sorted = [...filled].sort((a, b) => a.v - b.v);
        const n = sorted.length;
        const mid = Math.floor((n - 2) / 2);
        const v0 = sorted[mid].v, v1 = sorted[mid + 1].v;
        return {
            sum: v0 + v1,
            indices: new Set([sorted[mid].gi, sorted[mid + 1].gi]),
            midMin: Math.min(v0, v1),
            midMax: Math.max(v0, v1)
        };
    }

    // ---- 中間2名ルール（個人用） ----
    // values: 各E審判の減点値（null=未入力）
    // 返り値: { sum: 中間2名の和, indices: 中間2名のインデックスSet }
    function middle2(values) {
        const filled = values.map((v, i) => ({ v, i })).filter(x => x.v !== null && x.v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length < 2) return { sum: 0, indices: new Set(), midMin: null, midMax: null };
        const sorted = [...filled].sort((a, b) => a.v - b.v);
        const n = sorted.length;
        const mid = Math.floor((n - 2) / 2);
        const v0 = sorted[mid].v, v1 = sorted[mid + 1].v;
        return {
            sum:    v0 + v1,
            indices: new Set([sorted[mid].i, sorted[mid + 1].i]),
            midMin: Math.min(v0, v1),   // 採用値の下限
            midMax: Math.max(v0, v1)    // 採用値の上限
        };
    }

    // ---- 中央値1名（3人から真ん中を取得） ----
    function median1(values) {
        const filled = values.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length === 0) return null;
        filled.sort((a, b) => a - b);
        if (filled.length === 1) return filled[0];
        if (filled.length === 2) return (filled[0] + filled[1]) / 2;
        return filled[Math.floor(filled.length / 2)]; // 3人→真ん中
    }

    // ---- シンクロ用E減点計算 ----
    // numE=2: 2人の平均, numE=4: 4人の平均, numE=6: E1,3,5の中間値 + E2,4,6の中間値 の平均
    function synchroEDed(values, numE) {
        const filled = values.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length === 0) return { avg: 0, hasData: false };
        if (numE <= 4) {
            // 2人 or 4人: 全員の平均
            const avg = filled.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / filled.length;
            return { avg, hasData: true };
        }
        // 6人: E1,3,5(奇数)の中間値 + E2,4,6(偶数)の中間値 → 平均
        const oddVals  = [values[0], values[2], values[4]]; // E1, E3, E5
        const evenVals = [values[1], values[3], values[5]]; // E2, E4, E6
        const medOdd  = median1(oddVals);
        const medEven = median1(evenVals);
        if (medOdd === null && medEven === null) return { avg: 0, hasData: false };
        if (medOdd === null) return { avg: medEven, hasData: true };
        if (medEven === null) return { avg: medOdd, hasData: true };
        return { avg: (medOdd + medEven) / 2, hasData: true };
    }

    // ---- 演技点計算 ----
    function calcExecution(data) {
        const isSynchro = fType === 'synchro';
        // パネルプレフィックスでフィルタリングし、表示用にプレフィックスを除去
        const allJudges   = data?.execution?.judges || {};
        const panelPrefix = `${panel}_`;
        const judges      = Object.fromEntries(
            Object.entries(allJudges)
                .filter(([k]) => k.startsWith(panelPrefix))
                .map(([k, v]) => [k.slice(panelPrefix.length), v])
        );
        const vLimit = data?.validCount ?? 10;
        const numE   = getNumE();
        const jKeys  = Array.from({ length: numE }, (_, i) => `E${i + 1}`);

        if (!isSynchro) {
            // ---- 個人: 各技の中間2名の合計 ----
            const skillResults = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, si) => {
                const vals = jKeys.map(k => (judges[k]?.deductions || [])[si] ?? null);
                return middle2(vals);
            });
            const landingResult = vLimit === 10
                ? middle2(jKeys.map(k => judges[k]?.landing ?? null))
                : { sum: 0, indices: new Set() };
            const totalDed  = skillResults.slice(0, vLimit).reduce((a, r) => a + r.sum, 0)
                            + landingResult.sum;
            const execution = (vLimit * 2.0) - totalDed;
            return { skillResults, landingResult, totalDed, execution, vLimit, numE, jKeys, judges, isSynchro };
        } else {
            // ---- シンクロ: 審判数に応じた平均計算 ----
            // numE===6 のとき奇数審判(E1,E3,E5)と偶数審判(E2,E4,E6)でグループ分け
            const _synchroGroup = numE >= 6
                ? (vals) => {
                    const odd  = middle2Synchro(vals, [0, 2, 4]);
                    const even = middle2Synchro(vals, [1, 3, 5]);
                    const allIdx = new Set([...odd.indices, ...even.indices]);
                    return { groupData: { oddResult: odd, evenResult: even }, indices: allIdx };
                }
                : () => ({ groupData: null, indices: new Set() });

            const skillResults = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, si) => {
                const vals = jKeys.map(k => (judges[k]?.deductions || [])[si] ?? null);
                const r = synchroEDed(vals, numE);
                const g = _synchroGroup(vals);
                return { sum: r.avg, indices: g.indices, midMin: null, midMax: null, hasData: r.hasData, groupData: g.groupData };
            });
            const landingResult = vLimit === 10
                ? (() => {
                    const lVals = jKeys.map(k => judges[k]?.landing ?? null);
                    const r = synchroEDed(lVals, numE);
                    const g = _synchroGroup(lVals);
                    return { sum: r.avg, indices: g.indices, midMin: null, midMax: null, hasData: r.hasData, groupData: g.groupData };
                  })()
                : { sum: 0, indices: new Set(), hasData: false, groupData: null };
            const totalDed  = skillResults.slice(0, vLimit).reduce((a, r) => a + r.sum, 0)
                            + landingResult.sum;
            const execution = (vLimit * 1.0) - totalDed;  // シンクロ: 有効技数 × 1.0
            return { skillResults, landingResult, totalDed, execution, vLimit, numE, jKeys, judges, isSynchro };
        }
    }

    // ---- スコア表示 ----
    function renderScores(data) {
        latestData = data;
        const _vc = data.validCount ?? 10;
        document.getElementById('valid-count').value = _vc;
        updateValidHeaders(_vc);
        // 手動入力中は Firestore の値で上書きしない
        if (dMode === 'auto') {
            document.getElementById('res-d').innerText   = data.officialTotalDD?.toFixed(1) || "0.0";
        }

        // ---- D情報行 ----
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        const subSkills = submissions[resId]?.skills || [];
        const execSkills = data.officialSkills || [];
        const vLimitD = data.validCount ?? 10;

        // 申請行（重複は黄色、実施との差異は申請行では赤くしない→実施行で表示）
        let hasDeclaredMismatch = false;
        const declaredSymbols = []; // シンボルベースで比較するため格納
        {
            const seenKeys = new Set();
            document.querySelectorAll('.d-declared').forEach((el, i) => {
                const s = subSkills[i];
                el.style.color = '';
                el.style.fontWeight = '';
                el.classList.remove('d-dup');
                if (!s) { el.textContent = '-'; declaredSymbols.push(null); return; }
                let sym;
                if (typeof s === 'object' && s !== null) {
                    // オブジェクト形式（entry.html が保存する {id, skill, dd}）
                    // s.skill はシンボル文字列なので直接使わず、s.id でマスター照合する
                    const found = s.id ? skillMasterMap[String(s.id)] : null;
                    sym = found ? found.symbol : (s.skill || '');
                } else {
                    // 文字列形式（旧データ互換）
                    const rawVal = String(s);
                    if (!isNaN(rawVal) && rawVal !== '' && skillMasterMap[rawVal]) {
                        sym = skillMasterMap[rawVal].symbol;
                    } else {
                        sym = rawVal;
                    }
                }
                if (!sym) { el.textContent = '-'; declaredSymbols.push(null); return; }
                el.textContent = sym;
                declaredSymbols.push(sym);
                // 申請内での重複判定: IDがあればID、なければシンボルをキーに使う
                const dupKey = (typeof s === 'object' && s.id) ? String(s.id) : sym;
                if (dupKey && seenKeys.has(dupKey)) {
                    el.classList.add('d-dup');
                } else if (dupKey) {
                    seenKeys.add(dupKey);
                }
            });
        }

        // 実施行（officialSkills → skillMasterMap でシンボル取得、重複は黄色・申請差異は赤）
        if (dMode === 'auto') {
            const ddCells = [...document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd')];
            // Pass1: シンボル・ID収集 & クラスリセット（skill-header と d-dd 両方）
            const execSymbols = [];
            const execIds = [];
            document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach((el, i) => {
                el.style.color = '';
                el.classList.remove('d-dup', 'd-mismatch');
                if (ddCells[i]) ddCells[i].classList.remove('d-dup', 'd-mismatch');
                const id = execSkills[i];
                if (i >= vLimitD || !id) { el.textContent = '-'; execSymbols.push(null); execIds.push(null); return; }
                const skill = skillMasterMap[id]
                    || Object.values(skillMasterMap).find(v => v.symbol === id);
                const sym = skill ? skill.symbol : String(id);
                el.textContent = sym;
                execSymbols.push(sym);
                execIds.push(skill ? skill.id : String(id));
            });
            // 重複判定はIDベース
            const idCount = {};
            execIds.forEach(id => { if (id) idCount[id] = (idCount[id] || 0) + 1; });
            // Pass2: 重複（両方）と申告差異をハイライト（skill-header と d-dd 両方に適用）
            document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach((el, i) => {
                const sym = execSymbols[i];
                if (!sym) return;
                if (execIds[i] && idCount[execIds[i]] > 1) {
                    el.classList.add('d-dup'); // 重複は黄色（初回も含む全て）
                    if (ddCells[i]) ddCells[i].classList.add('d-dup');
                }
                const declSym = declaredSymbols[i];
                if (i < vLimitD && declSym && sym !== declSym) {
                    el.classList.add('d-mismatch');
                    if (ddCells[i]) ddCells[i].classList.add('d-mismatch');
                    hasDeclaredMismatch = true;
                }
            });
        }

        // D確定バッジ・緑枠（クリックで切り替え）
        const badge = document.getElementById('d-confirmed-badge');
        const dInfoRows = document.querySelectorAll('.d-info-row');
        if (data.dConfirmedAt) {
            badge.textContent = '確定';
            badge.className = 'd-badge';
            dInfoRows.forEach(r => r.classList.add('d-confirmed'));
        } else {
            badge.textContent = '未確定';
            badge.style.cssText = 'font-size:0.68rem;color:#90a4ae;font-weight:normal;margin-left:4px;';
            badge.className = '';
            dInfoRows.forEach(r => r.classList.remove('d-confirmed'));
        }

        // DD行（重複=0.0赤、ボーナス計算）
        if (dMode === 'auto') {
            if (data.officialDDValues && data.officialDDValues.length > 0) {
                // 手動入力で保存されたDD値を復元
                document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach((el, i) => {
                    el.style.color = '';
                    el.style.fontWeight = '';
                    const val = data.officialDDValues[i];
                    el.textContent = (val !== undefined && val !== null) ? Number(val).toFixed(1) : '-';
                });
                const bonus = data.officialDDBonus || 0;
                const bonusCell = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
                if (bonusCell) bonusCell.textContent = bonus > 0 ? `+${bonus.toFixed(1)}` : '-';
                const totalDD = data.officialTotalDD || 0;
                document.getElementById('dd-total-cell').textContent = totalDD > 0 ? totalDD.toFixed(1) : '-';
                document.getElementById('res-d').innerText = totalDD > 0 ? totalDD.toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                // 手動モードに切り替え（再読み込み後の復元。ユーザーがautoを明示選択中は除く）
                if (!dModeAutoExplicit) {
                    dMode = 'manual';
                    const dToggleM = document.getElementById('d-mode-toggle');
                    if (dToggleM) dToggleM.checked = true;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'true'; el.classList.add('d-manual-cell'); });
                    document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header').forEach(el => { el.contentEditable = 'true'; el.classList.add('d-manual-cell'); });
                    const dBonusCellM = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
                    if (dBonusCellM) { dBonusCellM.contentEditable = 'true'; dBonusCellM.classList.add('d-manual-cell'); }
                    const dTotalCellM = document.getElementById('dd-total-cell');
                    if (dTotalCellM) { dTotalCellM.contentEditable = 'true'; dTotalCellM.classList.add('d-manual-cell'); }
                }
            } else {
                const ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
                const gender = ath?.gender || '';
                const seenIds = new Set();
                let baseDD = 0, tripleCount = 0;

                document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd').forEach((el, i) => {
                    el.style.color = '';
                    el.style.fontWeight = '';
                    const id = execSkills[i];
                    // 指定難度マスキング（-1=無効=全技有効、0=全DD0、1以上=指定のみ有効）
                    const isDesignated = designatedDDCount < 0
                        || (designatedDDCount > 0 && designatedDDIndices.includes(i));

                    if (i >= vLimitD || !id) {
                        // 中断以降：非指定はハイフン(グレー)、指定スロットは空欄
                        if (i >= vLimitD) {
                            if (!isDesignated) { el.textContent = '-'; el.style.color = '#90a4ae'; }
                            else { el.textContent = ''; }
                        } else {
                            el.textContent = '-';
                        }
                        return;
                    }
                    const skill = skillMasterMap[id]
                        || Object.values(skillMasterMap).find(v => v.symbol === id);
                    if (!skill) { el.textContent = '-'; return; }

                    if (seenIds.has(id)) {
                        el.textContent = '0.0';
                        el.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
                        el.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
                    } else {
                        seenIds.add(id);
                        if (isDesignated) {
                            el.textContent = skill.dd.toFixed(1);
                            baseDD += skill.dd;
                            if (skill.isTriple) tripleCount++;
                        } else {
                            el.textContent = '-';          // 非指定はハイフン
                            el.style.color = '#90a4ae';    // グレー
                        }
                    }
                });

                // ボーナス計算（男子:6本目以降のトリプル×0.3、女子・混合:3本目以降のトリプル×0.3）
                let bonus = 0;
                if (gender === '男子' && tripleCount > 5) bonus = (tripleCount - 5) * 0.3;
                else if ((gender === '女子' || gender === '混合') && tripleCount >= 3) bonus = (tripleCount - 2) * 0.3;

                const totalDD = baseDD + bonus;
                document.getElementById('dd-total-cell').textContent = (baseDD + bonus) > 0 ? (baseDD + bonus).toFixed(1) : '-';
                const bonusCell = document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell');
                if (bonusCell) bonusCell.textContent = bonus > 0 ? `+${bonus.toFixed(1)}` : '-';

                const resDEl = document.getElementById('res-d');
                resDEl.innerText = totalDD > 0 ? totalDD.toFixed(1) : "0.0";
                resDEl.style.color = hasDeclaredMismatch ? 'var(--danger)' : '';
            }
        }

        // pendingEdits を Firestore フォーマットに変換してマージ（中間2名計算用）
        let mergedData = data;
        if (manualRows.size > 0) {
            const existingJudges = data?.execution?.judges || {};
            const mergedJudges = { ...existingJudges };
            manualRows.forEach(jKey => {
                const pe = pendingEdits[jKey];
                if (!pe) return;
                const deductions = Array.from({length: 10}, (_, i) => {
                    const d = pe.deductions?.[i] ?? null;
                    return d !== null ? d / 10 : null;
                });
                const landing = pe.landing !== null ? pe.landing / 10 : null;
                mergedJudges[`${panel}_${jKey}`] = { deductions, landing };
            });
            mergedData = { ...data, execution: { ...(data.execution || {}), judges: mergedJudges } };
        }

        const ec = calcExecution(mergedData);
        _lastEc = ec; // publish 時に per-skill E 保存用
        const { skillResults, landingResult, totalDed, vLimit, numE, jKeys, judges, isSynchro } = ec;

        // フォーカス位置を記憶（re-render後に復元するため）
        const activeEl = document.activeElement;
        const focusJKey = activeEl?.dataset?.jkey;
        const focusIdx  = activeEl?.dataset?.idx;
        const focusIsLanding = activeEl?.classList?.contains('manual-landing-cell');

        const tbody = document.getElementById('judge-rows');
        tbody.innerHTML = "";

        // 各E審判の行
        jKeys.forEach((jKey, jIdx) => {
            const jData = judges[jKey] || { deductions: Array(10).fill(null), landing: null };
            const isManual = manualRows.has(jKey);
            const pe = pendingEdits[jKey];

            const rowStyle = isManual ? ' style="background:rgba(255,152,0,0.05)"' : '';
            let row = `<tr${rowStyle}><td>${jKey}</td>`;

            for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                const v        = jData.deductions?.[i] ?? null;
                const dimStyle = i >= vLimit ? 'opacity:0.15;' : '';
                const sr       = skillResults[i];
                if (isManual) {
                    const cur    = pe?.deductions?.[i] ?? null;
                    const valStr = cur !== null ? cur : '0';
                    const vFloat = cur !== null ? cur / 10 : null;
                    const nullCls = cur === null ? ' null-val' : '';
                    const colorCls = cellColorCls(vFloat, sr, jIdx, i, vLimit);
                    row += `<td class="manual-cell${nullCls} ${colorCls}" tabindex="0" data-jkey="${jKey}" data-idx="${i}" style="${dimStyle}">${valStr}</td>`;
                } else {
                    const colorCls = cellColorCls(v, sr, jIdx, i, vLimit);
                    row += `<td class="${colorCls}" style="${dimStyle}">${v !== null ? Math.round(v * 10) : '-'}</td>`;
                }
            }
            // 着地(Ln)列
            const lv = jData.landing ?? null;
            if (isManual) {
                const curLv    = pe?.landing ?? null;
                const valLv    = curLv !== null ? curLv : '0';
                const vFloatLv = curLv !== null ? curLv / 10 : null;
                const nullLvCls = curLv === null ? ' null-val' : '';
                const colorLvCls = landingColorCls(vFloatLv, landingResult, jIdx, vLimit);
                const disAttrs = vLimit < 10 ? ' tabindex="-1" style="opacity:0.3;"' : ` tabindex="0"`;
                row += `<td class="manual-cell manual-landing-cell${nullLvCls} ${colorLvCls}" data-jkey="${jKey}"${disAttrs}>${vLimit < 10 ? '-' : valLv}</td>`;
                const manualDedSum = Array.from({length: vLimit}, (_, i) => {
                    const d = pe?.deductions?.[i] ?? null;
                    return d !== null ? d / 10 : 0;
                }).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) + (vLimit === 10 && curLv !== null ? curLv / 10 : 0);
                row += `<td style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05);color:#ff9800;font-weight:bold">${(vLimit - manualDedSum).toFixed(1)}</td>`;
            } else {
                const lnCls = landingColorCls(lv, landingResult, jIdx, vLimit);
                row += `<td class="${lnCls}" style="${vLimit < 10 ? 'opacity:0.15;' : ''}">${lv !== null ? Math.round(lv * 10) : '-'}</td>`;
                row += `<td style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05)">${(vLimit - (jData.totalDeduction || 0)).toFixed(1)}</td>`;
            }
            // 行ごとのトグルスイッチ（Total右）
            row += `<td class="toggle-col"><label class="toggle-switch-sm"><input type="checkbox" ${isManual ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleRowManual('${jKey}', this.checked)"><span class="track-sm"></span></label></td>`;
            row += `</tr>`;
            tbody.innerHTML += row;
        });

        // 中間2名合計行（データがない技は "-"）
        const hasJudgeData = Object.keys(judges).length > 0;
        const _medFmt = (v) => isSynchro ? (v * 10).toFixed(2) : Math.round(v * 10);
        let m2Row = `<tr class="middle-row"><td>Med</td>`;
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const hasVal = hasJudgeData && (skillResults[i].hasData ?? skillResults[i].indices.size > 0);
            m2Row += i < vLimit
                ? `<td>${hasVal ? _medFmt(skillResults[i].sum) : '-'}</td>`
                : `<td style="opacity:0.15">-</td>`;
        }
        const hasLanding = hasJudgeData && (landingResult.hasData ?? landingResult.indices.size > 0);
        m2Row += `<td>${vLimit === 10 && hasLanding ? _medFmt(landingResult.sum) : '-'}</td>`;
        m2Row += `<td class="ded-total">${hasJudgeData ? (isSynchro ? ec.execution.toFixed(3) : ec.execution.toFixed(1)) : '-'}</td><td></td></tr>`;
        tbody.innerHTML += m2Row;

        // フォーカスを復元（手動入力行の再描画後）
        if (focusJKey && manualRows.has(focusJKey)) {
            const toFocus = focusIsLanding
                ? document.querySelector(`.manual-landing-cell[data-jkey="${focusJKey}"]`)
                : document.querySelector(`.manual-cell[data-jkey="${focusJKey}"][data-idx="${focusIdx}"]`);
            if (toFocus) toFocus.focus();
        }

        // 確定済み(published)の場合はモードに関わらず保存値を表示する
        if (tofMode === 'auto' || data.published === true) {
            const rawTof = data.tofByPanel?.[panel] ?? data.tof ?? "";
            document.getElementById('tof').value = (fType === 'synchro' && rawTof !== "")
                ? (parseFloat(rawTof) / 2).toFixed(3) : rawTof;
        }
        if (hdMode === 'auto' || data.published === true) {
            document.getElementById('hd').value  = data.hdByPanel?.[panel] ?? data.hd?.total ?? "";
        }
        if (penMode === 'auto') document.getElementById('pen').value = data.penalty ?? 0;
        updateScoreSummary(ec, hasJudgeData);

        // 確定済みロック状態を反映
        applyLock(data.published === true);
    }

    window.updateValid = async (val) => {
        if (isLocked) return;
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        await setDoc(doc(db, "results", resId), { eventId, athleteId, round, validCount: parseInt(val) }, { merge: true });
    };

    function updateValidHeaders(n) {
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const th = document.getElementById(`sn-th-${i}`);
            if (!th) continue;
            th.classList.toggle('sn-valid',   i < n);
            th.classList.toggle('sn-cutoff',  i === n);
            th.classList.toggle('sn-invalid', i > n);
        }
    }

    window.setValidCount = (n) => {
        if (isLocked) return;
        document.getElementById('valid-count').value = n;
        updateValidHeaders(n);
        updateValid(n);
    };

    window.calc = function(data) { updateScoreSummary(data ? calcExecution(data) : null); };

    function applySynchroUI(isSyn) {
        const tofLbl = document.getElementById('tof-input-label');
        if (tofLbl) tofLbl.textContent = isSyn ? 'SYN（1/2）' : 'ToF (T)';
        const resLbl = document.getElementById('res-t-label');
        if (resLbl) resLbl.textContent = isSyn ? 'Syn' : 'T';
    }

    function updateScoreSummary(ec, hasJudgeData = true) {
        const d    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-d').innerText) || 0;
        const e    = ec ? (hasJudgeData ? ec.execution : 0)
                       : (parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-e').innerText) || 0);
        const tRaw = parseFloat(document.getElementById('tof').value) || 0;
        const t    = (fType === 'synchro') ? tRaw * 2 : tRaw; // シンクロは×2表示
        const h    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('hd').value) || 0;
        const p    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pen').value) || 0;

        _preciseE = e; // 全精度を保持（保存時に使用）
        document.getElementById('res-e').innerText     = (fType === 'synchro') ? e.toFixed(3) : e.toFixed(1);
        document.getElementById('res-t').innerText     = t.toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('res-hd').innerText    = h.toFixed(3);
        document.getElementById('res-total').innerText = Math.max(0, d + e + t + h - p).toFixed(2);
    }

    window.toggleRowManual = function(jKey, on) {
        if (isLocked) return;
        if (on) {
            manualRows.add(jKey);
            const allJudges = latestData?.execution?.judges || {};
            const jd = allJudges[`${panel}_${jKey}`] || {};
            pendingEdits[jKey] = {
                deductions: Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) =>
                    jd.deductions?.[i] != null ? Math.round(jd.deductions[i] * 10) : null),
                landing: jd.landing != null ? Math.round(jd.landing * 10) : null
            };
        } else {
            manualRows.delete(jKey);
            delete pendingEdits[jKey];
        }
        renderScores(latestData);
        if (on) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                const first = document.querySelector(`.manual-cell[data-jkey="${jKey}"][data-idx="0"]`);
                if (first) first.focus();
            }, 30);
        }
    };

    // ---- HDTS 技別行 表示切替（useHDTS 設定に従い section を show/hide） ----
    function applyHdtsVisibility(ath) {
        const athType  = ath?.type  || 'individual';
        const athClass = ath?.class || ath?.category || '';
        const cRule    = currentTour?.classRules?.[athType]?.[athClass] || {};
        const show     = cRule.useHDTS !== false; // default true
        document.querySelectorAll('.hdts-section-head, #hdts-tof-row, #hdts-hd-row').forEach(el => {
            el.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
        });
    }

    // ---- HDTS 技別行リセット ----
    function resetHdtsRows() {
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const t = document.getElementById(`hdts-tof-${i}`);
            const h = document.getElementById(`hdts-hd-${i}`);
            if (t) t.textContent = '-';
            if (h) h.textContent = '-';
        }
        ['hdts-tof-sum','hdts-hd-sum'].forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.textContent = '-';
        });
        const st = document.getElementById('qira-status');
        if (st) { st.textContent = ''; st.style.color = '#90a4ae'; }
    }

    // ---- QIRA 取得 ----
    // 優先順位:
    //   1. localhost で動作中 → /api/qira/result プロキシ（server.js が QIRA ホストを解決）
    //   2. localStorage に別PC IPが設定されている → 直接接続
    //   3. フォールバック → localhost:8080 直接
    async function tryFetchQira() {
        if (location.hostname === 'localhost' || location.hostname === '127.0.0.1') {
            try {
                const r = await fetch('/api/qira/result', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2000) });
                const ct = r.headers.get('content-type') || '';
                if (ct.includes('json')) return r;
            } catch {}
        }
        const savedBase = localStorage.getItem('qiraBaseUrl');
        const isRemote  = savedBase && !savedBase.includes('localhost') && !savedBase.includes('127.0.0.1');
        if (isRemote) {
            return fetch(savedBase + '/routineresult', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
        }
        return fetch('http://localhost:8080/routineresult', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
    }

    window.getFromQira = async function() {
        const statusEl = document.getElementById('qira-status');
        const btn      = document.getElementById('btn-hdts');
        btn.disabled   = true;
        statusEl.style.color = '#90a4ae';
        statusEl.textContent = '取得中...';
        try {
            const res  = await tryFetchQira();
            if (res.status === 404) { statusEl.style.color = '#e57373'; statusEl.textContent = 'QIRAに確定データがありません'; return; }
            if (!res.ok)            { statusEl.style.color = '#e57373'; statusEl.textContent = `エラー: ${res.status}`; return; }
            const data = await res.json();
            const r    = data.type === 'synchronized' ? data.synchronizedResult : data.individualResult;
            if (!r) { statusEl.style.color = '#e57373'; statusEl.textContent = 'データ形式不明'; return; }

            const tofVal = r.timeOfFlightInSeconds;
            const hdVal  = r.horizontalDisplacement;
            const vcVal  = r.elementCount;
            const label  = data.maturity === 'confirmed' ? '確定' : '暫定';

            // 有効本数をセット
            if (vcVal != null) {
                const vcSelect = document.getElementById('valid-count');
                if (vcSelect) {
                    vcSelect.value = String(vcVal);
