<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc, collection, getDocs, query, where, setDoc, serverTimestamp, increment, onSnapshot } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    
    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event')?.trim();
    const panel   = params.get('panel')?.trim() || '';
    const role    = params.get('role')?.trim()  || '';
    let athleteId = params.get('athlete')?.trim(), round = params.get('round') || "pre1";

    let skillMaster = [], athletes = [], submissions = {}, results = {}, currentTour = null;
    let cjpConfirmed = false; // CJPが公式スコアを確定済（published:true）かどうか
    let unsubValidCount = null;
    let fType = 'individual', fClass = '', fGender = '', fFinalOnly = false;
    let designatedDDCount = -1;   // -1=無効, 0=全DD0, 1以上=N本指定
    let designatedDDIndices = []; // 指定技インデックス（0-based）

    // Firestore doc ID (文字列 or 数値) でスキルを引く。位置アクセスは使わない
    function findSkillById(id) {
        if (id === null || id === undefined || id === '') return null;
        return skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(id)) || null;
    }

    function resolveSubSkill(s) {
        if (!s) return null;
        if (typeof s === 'object') {
            if (s.id) return findSkillById(s.id);
            if (s.skill) return skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === s.skill) || null;
            return null;
        }
        // 数値文字列 → ID として検索（旧形式互換）
        if (!isNaN(s) && s !== '') return findSkillById(s) || skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === String(s)) || null;
        return skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === s) || null;
    }

    function withTimeout(promise, ms) {
        return Promise.race([
            promise,
            new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('TIMEOUT')), ms))
        ]);
    }

    function showLoadState(msg, isError) {
        document.getElementById('sidebar-list').innerHTML = `
            <div style="padding:24px;text-align:center;color:${isError ? '#e53935' : '#90a4ae'};">
                <div style="font-size:2em;margin-bottom:10px;">${isError ? '⚠️' : '⏳'}</div>
                <div style="font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:${isError ? '14px' : '0'};">${msg}</div>
                ${isError ? '<button onclick="retryInit()" style="padding:10px 24px;background:#1a73e8;color:white;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:0.9em;cursor:pointer;font-weight:bold;">🔄 再読み込み</button>' : ''}
            </div>`;
        if (isError) document.getElementById('event-name').innerText = '読み込みエラー';
    }

    window.retryInit = () => init();
    let _initRunning = false;

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId || _initRunning) return;
        _initRunning = true;
        document.getElementById('menu-back').href = `judge_role_select.html?event=${eventId}`;
        if (panel || role) {
            const parts = [panel && `PANEL ${panel}`, role && `ROLE ${role}`].filter(Boolean);
            document.getElementById('panel-role-label').textContent = parts.join(' | ');
        }
        showLoadState('データを読み込んでいます...', false);
        try {
            const [tourSnap, athSnap, subSnap, resSnap, smSnap] = await withTimeout(
                Promise.all([
                    getDoc(doc(db, "tournaments", eventId)),
                    getDocs(collection(db, "tournaments", eventId, "athletes")),
                    getDocs(query(collection(db, "submissions"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
                    getDocs(query(collection(db, "results"), where("eventId", "==", eventId))),
                    getDocs(collection(db, "skillMaster"))
                ]),
                20000
            );

            // skillMaster: ドキュメントID（数値文字列）でソートして配列化（ID=インデックスでアクセスされる）
            skillMaster = smSnap.docs
                .sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.id) - parseInt(b.id))
                .map(d => ({
                    id: d.id,
                    symbol: d.data().symbol,
                    dd: parseFloat(d.data().dd) || 0,
                    isTriple: d.data().isTriple === true,
                    direction: d.data().direction || '',
                    somersaultType: d.data().somersaultType || '',
                    displayOrder: d.data().displayOrder ?? parseInt(d.id)
                }));

            currentTour = tourSnap.data();
            document.getElementById('event-name').innerText = currentTour.name;
            athletes = athSnap.docs.map(d => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }));
            submissions = {}; subSnap.forEach(d => { submissions[d.id] = d.data(); });
            results = {}; resSnap.forEach(d => { results[d.id] = d.data(); });

            renderSidebar();
            if (athleteId) renderScoringArea();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Init Error:", e);
            const msg = e.message === 'TIMEOUT' ? 'ネットワーク接続に時間がかかっています' : 'データの読み込みに失敗しました';
            showLoadState(msg, true);
        } finally {
            _initRunning = false;
        }
    }

    function checkHasDiff(aid, rid) {
        const subId = `${eventId}_${aid}_${rid}`;
        const sub = submissions[subId], res = results[subId];
        if (!sub || !res || !res.officialSkills) return false;
        const validLimit = res.validCount !== undefined ? res.validCount : 10;
        for (let i = 0; i < validLimit; i++) {
            const skill = findSkillById(res.officialSkills[i]);
                const subSkill = resolveSubSkill(sub.skills[i]);
            if (skill && subSkill && skill.symbol !== subSkill.symbol) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    function renderSidebar() {
        if (!currentTour) return;
        const isSynchro = fType === 'synchro';

        // 種目でまず分ける
        const byType = athletes.filter(a => (a.type || 'individual') === fType);

        // クラス一覧
        const tourClassDefs = (currentTour?.classes?.[fType] || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
        const rawClasses = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.class || a.category || ''))];
        const classes = [...tourClassDefs.filter(c=>rawClasses.includes(c)), ...rawClasses.filter(c=>!tourClassDefs.includes(c))];

        // 性別一覧
        const genders = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.gender || ''))].filter(Boolean);

        // フィルタータブ描画
        const hasType = athletes.some(a => (a.type || 'individual') === 'synchro');
        const filterEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-filters');
        let filHtml = '';

        if (hasType) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row">
                <span class="filter-label">種目</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='individual'?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('type','individual')">個人</button>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='synchro'?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('type','synchro')">シンクロ</button>
            </div>`;
        }

        if (classes.length > 1) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">クラス</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fClass===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','')">全て</button>
                ${classes.map(c => `<button class="tab-btn ${fClass===c?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','${c}')">${c}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }

        if (genders.length > 1) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">性別</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fGender===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','')">全て</button>
                ${genders.map(g => `<button class="tab-btn ${fGender===g?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','${g}')">${g}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }

        // 決勝フィルター行を追加
        const hasAnyFinals = athletes.some(a => a.finalOrder != null);
        if (hasAnyFinals) {
            filHtml += `<div class="filter-row">
                <span class="filter-label">決勝</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${!fFinalOnly?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',false)">全て</button>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fFinalOnly?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',true)">🏆 決勝のみ</button>
            </div>`;
        }

        filterEl.style.display = filHtml ? '' : 'none';
        filterEl.innerHTML = filHtml;

        // 絞り込み＆ソート
        let filtered;
        if (fFinalOnly) {
            filtered = byType  // athletes ではなく種目フィルタ済みの byType を使う
                .filter(a => a.finalOrder != null)
                .filter(a => !fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
                .filter(a => !fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender)
                .sort((a, b) => (a.finalOrder ?? 999) - (b.finalOrder ?? 999));
        } else {
            filtered = byType
                .filter(a => !fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
                .filter(a => !fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender)
                .sort((a, b) => {
                    const ca = (a.class || a.category || ''), cb = (b.class || b.category || '');
                    if (ca !== cb) return ca.localeCompare(cb);
                    const ga = a.gender || '', gb = b.gender || '';
                    if (ga !== gb) return ga.localeCompare(gb);
                    return (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999);
                });
        }

        const listEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-list');
        listEl.innerHTML = "";
        let lastCat = "", lastGender = "";

        filtered.forEach(ath => {
            const cls = ath.class || ath.category || '';
            if (cls !== lastCat) {
                listEl.innerHTML += `<div class="cat-group">${cls}</div>`;
                lastCat = cls; lastGender = "";
            }
            if (!fFinalOnly && (ath.gender || '') !== lastGender) {
                listEl.innerHTML += `<div class="gender-group">${ath.gender || ''}</div>`;
                lastGender = ath.gender || '';
            }

            // クラスごとの予選本数を classRules から取得
            const athType = ath.type || 'individual';
            const classRule = currentTour.classRules?.[athType]?.[cls] || {};
            const numPre = classRule.qualifyingRoutines ?? currentTour.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
            const hasFinalClass = classRule.hasFinal ?? false;

            let dotsHtml = "";
            if (fFinalOnly) {
                dotsHtml = getDotHtml(ath.id, 'final', 'F');
            } else {
                for (let i = 1; i <= numPre; i++) dotsHtml += getDotHtml(ath.id, `pre${i}`, `R${i}`);
                if (hasFinalClass) dotsHtml += getDotHtml(ath.id, 'final', 'F');
            }

            const orderNum = fFinalOnly ? ath.finalOrder : ath.order;
            const nameLine1 = isSynchro
                ? `<span class="ath-order">${orderNum}.</span>${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2 || ''}`
                : `<span class="ath-order">${orderNum}.</span>${ath.name1}`;
            const nameLine2 = isSynchro
                ? (ath.kana1 || ath.kana2 ? `${ath.kana1 || ''} / ${ath.kana2 || ''}` : '')
                : (ath.kana1 || '');
            const titleAttr = `${ath.name1 || ''}${ath.kana1 ? ' (' + ath.kana1 + ')' : ''}`;

            listEl.innerHTML += `
                <div class="athlete-row ${ath.id === athleteId ? 'active-athlete' : ''}" title="${titleAttr}">
                    <div class="ath-name-text">
                        <div class="ath-name-main">${nameLine1}</div>
                        ${nameLine2 ? `<div class="ath-name-sub">${nameLine2}</div>` : ''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="round-box">${dotsHtml}</div>
                </div>`;
        });
    }

    window.setFilter = (key, val) => {
        if (key === 'type')      { fType = val; fClass = ''; fGender = ''; fFinalOnly = false; }
        if (key === 'class')     fClass  = val;
        if (key === 'gender')    fGender = val;
        if (key === 'finalOnly') fFinalOnly = (val === true);
        renderSidebar();
    };

    function getDotHtml(aid, rid, label) {
        const id = `${eventId}_${aid}_${rid}`;
        const cls = (results[id] ? 'has-res' : (submissions[id] ? 'has-sub' : '')) +
                    (aid === athleteId && rid === round ? ' current' : '') +
                    (checkHasDiff(aid, rid) ? ' diff-dot' : '');
        return `<a href="#" class="r-dot ${cls}" onclick="selectRound('${aid}','${rid}');return false;">${label}</a>`;
    }

    window.selectRound = (aid, rid) => {
        if (unsubValidCount) { unsubValidCount(); unsubValidCount = null; }
        athleteId = aid;
        round = rid;
        history.replaceState(null, '', `?event=${eventId}&athlete=${aid}&round=${rid}`);
        renderSidebar();
        renderScoringArea();
        document.getElementById('main-content').scrollTop = 0;
    };

    function watchValidCount(resId) {
        if (unsubValidCount) { unsubValidCount(); unsubValidCount = null; }
        let prevExists = !!results[resId];
        unsubValidCount = onSnapshot(doc(db, 'results', resId), snap => {
            const nowExists = snap.exists();
            // 結果ドキュメントが削除された → 申告技をデフォルトで再ロード
            if (!nowExists && prevExists) {
                delete results[resId];
                cjpConfirmed = false;
                prevExists = false;
                renderScoringArea();
                return;
            }
            prevExists = nowExists;
            const data = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
            const vc = (data?.validCount) ?? 10;
            const el = document.getElementById('valid-count');
            const disp = document.getElementById('valid-count-display');
            if (el) el.value = vc;
            if (disp) disp.innerText = `${vc}本`;

            // CJP確定状態のリアルタイム反映
            const newCjpConf = data?.published === true;
            if (newCjpConf !== cjpConfirmed) {
                cjpConfirmed = newCjpConf;
                if (data) results[resId] = data;
                const actionArea = document.getElementById('action-area');
                if (actionArea) {
                    if (cjpConfirmed) {
                        document.querySelectorAll('select').forEach(s => s.disabled = true);
                        actionArea.innerHTML = `<div class="cjp-lock-msg">🔒 CJPが確定済のため修正できません</div>`;
                    } else {
                        // CJP解除 → 修正するボタンを表示（セレクトはまだ無効のまま）
                        const dSaved = !!data;
                        actionArea.innerHTML = dSaved
                            ? `<button id="btn-save" class="btn btn-modify" onclick="enableEditing()">修正する</button>`
                            : `<button id="btn-save" class="btn btn-submit" onclick="saveOfficialScore()">確定・保存</button>`;
                    }
                }
            }

            calcTotal();
        });
    }

    function renderScoringArea() {
        const subId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`, sub = submissions[subId], resData = results[subId], ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
        if (!ath) { document.getElementById('main-content').innerHTML = `<div style="margin-top:150px;text-align:center;color:var(--danger);">選手データが見つかりません</div>`; return; }
        const noSub = !sub;
        const dSaved = !!resData;
        cjpConfirmed = resData?.published === true;
        const isLocked = dSaved; // D審判側のソフトロック（修正するで解除可）
        const initialData = (dSaved ? resData.officialSkills : sub?.skills) || [];
        const validCount = (isLocked && resData.validCount !== undefined) ? resData.validCount : 10;

        // 指定難度ロード
        const athType2 = ath.type || 'individual';
        const athClass2 = ath.class || ath.category || '';
        const classRule = currentTour?.classRules?.[athType2]?.[athClass2] || {};
        const rawDDCount = classRule.designatedDDCount ?? -1;
        const validDesig = rawDDCount >= 0
            && classRule.qualifyingRoutines === 2
            && classRule.scoreMode === 'total'
            && round === 'pre1';
        designatedDDCount   = validDesig ? rawDDCount : -1;
        designatedDDIndices = [...(resData?.officialDesignatedDDIndices || sub?.designatedDDIndices || [])];

        const initialIds = initialData.map(s => {
            if (!s && s !== 0) return "";
            if (typeof s === 'object') {
                if (s.id) { const b = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(s.id)); if (b) return b.id; }
                if (s.skill) { const b = skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === s.skill); if (b) return b.id; }
                return "";
            }
            // 数値文字列 → Firestore ID として検索（配列インデックスではなくIDで検索）
            if (s !== "" && !isNaN(s)) {
                const byId = skillMaster.find(m => m.id === String(s));
                if (byId) return byId.id;
            }
            // シンボル文字列として検索
            const found = skillMaster.find(m => m.symbol === String(s));
            return found ? found.id : "";
        });

        // ヘッダー表示用: 名前・ふりがな・チーム名
        const isSynchroCard = (ath.type === 'synchro');
        const headerName = isSynchroCard
            ? `${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2 || ''}`
            : ath.name1;
        const headerKana = isSynchroCard
            ? [ath.kana1, ath.kana2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')
            : (ath.kana1 || '');
        let headerClub = '';
        if (isSynchroCard) {
            const c1 = ath.club1 || '', c2 = ath.club2 || '';
            const ck1 = ath.clubKana1 || '', ck2 = ath.clubKana2 || '';
            if (c1 && c1 === c2) {
                // 同じチーム名 → 一つだけ表示
                headerClub = c1 + (ck1 ? `（${ck1}）` : '');
            } else {
                const p1 = c1 ? c1 + (ck1 ? `（${ck1}）` : '') : '';
                const p2 = c2 ? c2 + (ck2 ? `（${ck2}）` : '') : '';
                headerClub = [p1, p2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
            }
        } else {
            const c1 = ath.club1 || '', ck1 = ath.clubKana1 || '';
            headerClub = c1 ? c1 + (ck1 ? `（${ck1}）` : '') : '';
        }

        document.getElementById('main-content').innerHTML = `
            <div class="score-card">
                <div class="header-info">
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between;">
                        <div style="font-size:0.9em; color:var(--accent); font-weight:bold;">OFFICIAL SCORING</div>
                        ${cjpConfirmed ? '<span class="status-badge badge-cjp-locked">🔒 CJP確定済</span>' : dSaved ? '<span class="status-badge badge-locked">✓ D確定済</span>' : ''}
                        ${noSub ? '<span style="font-size:0.85em; font-weight:bold; color:var(--warn-orange); background:#fff3e0; padding:4px 10px; border-radius:6px; border:1px solid #ffcc80;">⚠ 選手未申告</span>' : ''}
                    </div>
                    <h2>No.${ath.order} ${headerName} <span style="font-size:0.5em; color:#666;">${headerKana ? `(${headerKana})` : ''}</span></h2>
                    ${headerClub ? `<div style="font-size:0.9em; color:#78909c; margin-top:2px;">${headerClub}</div>` : ''}
                </div>
                <table class="comparison-table">
                    <thead><tr><th class="col-num">#</th><th class="col-sub">選手申告</th><th class="col-sub-dd">DD</th><th>系統</th><th>宙系</th><th>記号</th><th class="col-dd">DD</th></tr></thead>
                    <tbody id="skill-rows"></tbody>
                </table>
                <div class="valid-count-area">
                    <label style="font-weight:bold;">有効本数:</label>
                    <span id="valid-count-display" style="font-size:1.2em; font-weight:900; color:var(--ok-green); padding:6px 14px; background:#f1f8e9; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #c8e6c9;">${validCount}本</span>
                    <input type="hidden" id="valid-count" value="${validCount}">
                    <span style="font-size:0.75em; color:#90a4ae;">(CJP設定)</span>
                </div>
                <div class="footer-stats"><span id="total-dd" class="total-official">0.0</span></div>
                <div class="action-area" id="action-area">
                    ${cjpConfirmed
                        ? `<div class="cjp-lock-msg">🔒 CJPが確定済のため修正できません</div>`
                        : `<button id="btn-save" class="btn ${dSaved?'btn-modify':'btn-submit'}" onclick="${dSaved?'enableEditing()':'saveOfficialScore()'}">
                            ${dSaved?'修正する':'確定・保存'}</button>`
                    }
                </div>
            </div>`;

        const tbody = document.getElementById('skill-rows');
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const currentId = initialIds[i-1] || "";
            const skill = findSkillById(currentId) || {};
            const subSkill = resolveSubSkill((sub?.skills || [])[i-1]);

            const tr = document.createElement('tr'); tr.id = `row-${i}`;
            const hasSoma = skill.direction && skill.direction !== '基礎技';
            tr.innerHTML = `<td class="col-num">${i}</td><td class="col-sub">${subSkill?.symbol || "-"}</td><td class="col-sub-dd">${subSkill?.dd?.toFixed(1) || '0.0'}</td>
                <td><select id="dir-${i}" onchange="updateOptions(${i})" ${(isLocked || cjpConfirmed) ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                    <option value="">--</option><option value="前方系" ${skill.direction==='前方系'?'selected':''}>前方</option>
                    <option value="後方系" ${skill.direction==='後方系'?'selected':''}>後方</option><option value="基礎技" ${skill.direction==='基礎技'?'selected':''}>基礎</option>
                </select></td>
                <td><select id="soma-${i}" onchange="updateOptions(${i})" ${(isLocked || cjpConfirmed) ? 'disabled' : ''} style="${hasSoma ? '' : 'visibility:hidden'}">
                    <option value="">--</option><option value="1回宙" ${skill.somersaultType==='1回宙'?'selected':''}>1回宙</option>
                    <option value="2回宙" ${skill.somersaultType==='2回宙'?'selected':''}>2回宙</option>
                    <option value="3回宙" ${skill.somersaultType==='3回宙'?'selected':''}>3回宙</option>
                    <option value="4回宙" ${skill.somersaultType==='4回宙'?'selected':''}>4回宙</option>
                </select></td>
                <td><select id="skill-${i}" onchange="calcTotal()" ${(isLocked || cjpConfirmed) ? 'disabled' : ''}></select></td>
                <td><span id="dd-${i}">0.0</span></td>`;
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
            updateOptions(i, currentId, false);
        }
        calcTotal();
        watchValidCount(subId);
    }

    window.updateOptions = (idx, selectedId = "", doCalc = true) => {
        const dir = document.getElementById(`dir-${idx}`).value;
        const somaEl = document.getElementById(`soma-${idx}`);
        const sel = document.getElementById(`skill-${idx}`);
        if (!sel) return;
        // 基礎技は宙系不要
        if (somaEl) {
            const showSoma = dir && dir !== '基礎技';
            somaEl.style.visibility = showSoma ? '' : 'hidden';
            if (!showSoma) somaEl.value = '';
        }
        const soma = somaEl?.value || '';
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">選択</option>';
        if (dir) skillMaster.filter(s => s.direction === dir && (!soma || s.somersaultType === soma))
            .sort((a, b) => a.displayOrder - b.displayOrder).forEach(s => {
                const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = s.id; opt.innerText = s.symbol;
                if (s.id === selectedId) opt.selected = true;
                sel.appendChild(opt);
            });
        if (doCalc) calcTotal();
    };

    window.calcTotal = () => {
        const subId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`, sub = submissions[subId];
        const validCountEl = document.getElementById('valid-count'), totalDDContainer = document.getElementById('total-dd');
        if (!validCountEl) return;

        const validLimit = parseInt(validCountEl.value);
        let baseDD = 0, tripleCount = 0, hasAnyDiff = false;
        const seenIds = new Set(), idCounts = {};

        for(let i=1; i<=validLimit; i++) {
            const id = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`)?.value;
            if(id) idCounts[id] = (idCounts[id] || 0) + 1;
        }

        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const id = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`)?.value, row = document.getElementById(`row-${i}`), ddSpan = document.getElementById(`dd-${i}`), selSkill = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`), selDir = document.getElementById(`dir-${i}`);
            const subSkill = resolveSubSkill((sub?.skills || [])[i-1]);

            // 指定難度の星マーク（中断以降も含めて全行対象）
            const isDesigIdx = designatedDDCount > 0 && designatedDDIndices.includes(i - 1);
            const numCell = row?.querySelector('td.col-num');
            if (numCell) numCell.classList.toggle('designated', isDesigIdx);

            if (i > validLimit) {
                row.classList.add('invalid-row');
                ddSpan.style.color = '';
                // 非指定はハイフン、指定スロットは0.0
                if (!isDesigIdx && designatedDDCount > 0) {
                    ddSpan.innerText = '-'; ddSpan.style.color = '#90a4ae';
                } else {
                    ddSpan.innerText = '0.0';
                }
                continue;
            }
            row.classList.remove('invalid-row');

            if (!id) { ddSpan.innerText = "0.0"; row.classList.remove('duplicate-row'); selSkill.classList.remove('diff-skill'); selDir.classList.remove('diff-skill'); ddSpan.classList.remove('diff-skill'); continue; }

            const skill = findSkillById(id);
            if (!skill) continue;
            if (idCounts[id] > 1) row.classList.add('duplicate-row'); else row.classList.remove('duplicate-row');
            
            if (subSkill && skill.symbol !== subSkill.symbol) {
                selSkill.classList.add('diff-skill'); selDir.classList.add('diff-skill'); ddSpan.classList.add('diff-skill'); hasAnyDiff = true;
            } else {
                selSkill.classList.remove('diff-skill'); selDir.classList.remove('diff-skill'); ddSpan.classList.remove('diff-skill');
            }

            ddSpan.style.color = ''; // 色リセット
            if (seenIds.has(id)) {
                ddSpan.innerText = "0.0"; ddSpan.classList.add('dd-zero');
            } else {
                seenIds.add(id); ddSpan.classList.remove('dd-zero');
                const isDesig = designatedDDCount < 0
                    || (designatedDDCount > 0 && designatedDDIndices.includes(i - 1));
                if (isDesig) {
                    baseDD += skill.dd; ddSpan.innerText = skill.dd.toFixed(1); if (skill.isTriple) tripleCount++;
                } else {
                    ddSpan.innerText = '-';
                    if (!ddSpan.classList.contains('diff-skill')) ddSpan.style.color = '#90a4ae';
                }
            }
        }

        const ath = athletes.find(a => a.id === athleteId);
        let bonus = 0;
        if (ath?.gender === '男子' && tripleCount > 5) bonus = (tripleCount - 5) * 0.3;
        else if ((ath?.gender === '女子' || ath?.gender === '混合') && tripleCount >= 3) bonus = (tripleCount - 2) * 0.3;

        if (hasAnyDiff) totalDDContainer.classList.add('diff-skill'); else totalDDContainer.classList.remove('diff-skill');
        totalDDContainer.innerHTML = `${(baseDD + bonus).toFixed(1)} <span style="font-size:0.4em; color:#999; font-weight:normal;">(Base ${baseDD.toFixed(1)} + Bonus ${bonus.toFixed(1)})</span>`;
    };

    window.enableEditing = () => {
        if (cjpConfirmed) return; // CJP確定済のため修正不可
        document.querySelectorAll('select').forEach(s => s.disabled = false);
        document.getElementById('action-area').innerHTML = `<button id="btn-save" class="btn btn-submit" onclick="saveOfficialScore()">修正を保存</button>`;
    };

    window.saveOfficialScore = async () => {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save'); if (!btn) return;
        const validCount = parseInt(document.getElementById('valid-count').value);
        const skills = [];
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const val = document.getElementById(`skill-${i}`).value;
            if (i <= validCount && !val) {
                alert(`${i}本目の技が未選択です。すべての技を選択してから保存してください。`);
                return;
            }
            skills.push(val);
        }
        btn.disabled = true;
        try {
            const totalVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('total-dd').innerText.split(' ')[0]);
            await setDoc(doc(db, "results", `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`), { eventId, athleteId, round, officialSkills: skills, validCount, officialTotalDD: totalVal, dConfirmedAt: serverTimestamp(), revisionCount: increment(1) }, { merge: true });
            init(); 
        } catch (e) { console.error(e); btn.disabled = false; }
    };
    window.addEventListener('pageshow', e => {
        if (e.persisted) { _initRunning = false; init(); }
    });
    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="module">
    import { getTournament } from "./cache.js";
    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search), eventId = params.get('event');
    document.getElementById('btn-back').href = eventId ? `judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}` : 'judge_menu.html';

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) { document.body.innerHTML = '<h1>エラー: 大会IDが見つかりません。</h1><a href="admin_index.html">大会選択に戻る</a>'; return; }
        const data = await getTournament(eventId);
        if (data) {
            document.getElementById('event-name').innerText = data.name;
            const numE = data.numExecJudges || 6;
            ['a', 'b'].forEach(p => {
                const container = document.getElementById(`exec-${p}`);
                for (let i = 1; i <= numE; i++) {
                    const a = document.createElement('a');
                    a.className = "btn btn-exec"; a.innerText = `E${i}`;
                    a.onclick = () => go(p.toUpperCase(), `E${i}`);
                    container.appendChild(a);
                }
            });
        }
    }
    window.go = (p, r) => {
        let url;
        if (r.startsWith('E'))       url = "judge_e.html";
        else if (r === 'D')          url = "judge_panel.html";
        else if (r === 'T' || r === 'HD') url = "judge_tof_hd.html";
        else                         url = "judge_cjp.html";
        location.href = `${url}?event=${eventId}&panel=${p}&role=${r}`;
    };
    init();
</script>

<!DOCTYPE html>

    <title>審判構成 - Trampoline System</title>
    <style>
        :root { --accent: #1a73e8; --bg: #f0f2f5; --card: #fff; --text-main: #263238; --text-sub: #546e7a; }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; background: var(--bg); margin: 0; color: var(--text-main); }
        .header { background: #1e3a5f; padding: 16px 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; }

        .header-title { color: #fff; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 900; margin: 0; flex: 1; text-align: center; }

<body>
<div class="header">
    <a href="#" id="btn-back" class="btn-back">← 戻る</a>
    <h1 class="header-title">審判構成</h1>
    <div style="width:40px"></div>
</div>

<div class="container">
    <!-- 大会スタッフ -->
    <div class="card" id="staff-card">
        <div class="card-title">大会スタッフ</div>
        <div class="staff-grid" id="staff-grid">
            <div class="staff-field" id="sw-chief"><span class="staff-label">審判長</span><span class="staff-value empty" id="s-chief">—</span></div>
            <div class="staff-field" id="sw-recorder"><span class="staff-label">記録主任</span><span class="staff-value empty" id="s-recorder">—</span></div>
            <div class="staff-field" id="sw-senior1"><span class="staff-label">上級審判 1</span><span class="staff-value empty" id="s-senior1">—</span></div>
            <div class="staff-field" id="sw-senior2"><span class="staff-label">上級審判 2</span><span class="staff-value empty" id="s-senior2">—</span></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- クラス別 -->
    <div id="classes-container"></div>
</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDocFromServer, getDocsFromServer, collection } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');
    document.getElementById('btn-back').href = `judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) return;

        // 大会データと選手リストを並列取得（両方ともキャッシュ回避でサーバー直取得）
        const [snap, athSnap] = await Promise.all([
            getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId)),
            getDocsFromServer(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'athletes'))
        ]);

        if (!snap.exists()) return;
        const d = snap.data();

    document.title = `${d.name || '大会'} — 審判構成`;

    // スタッフ
    const staff = d.judgeConfig?.staff || {};
    const set = (id, val) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (val) { el.textContent = val; el.classList.remove('empty'); }
        else { el.textContent = '—'; }
    };
    // 「設定しない」フラグがあれば非表示、なければ値を表示
    if (staff.chiefJudgeDisabled)    { document.getElementById('sw-chief').style.display    = 'none'; }
    else { set('s-chief',    staff.chiefJudge); }
    if (staff.chiefRecorderDisabled) { document.getElementById('sw-recorder').style.display = 'none'; }
    else { set('s-recorder', staff.chiefRecorder); }
    if (staff.seniorJudge1Disabled)  { document.getElementById('sw-senior1').style.display  = 'none'; }
    else { set('s-senior1',  staff.seniorJudge1); }
    if (staff.seniorJudge2Disabled)  { document.getElementById('sw-senior2').style.display  = 'none'; }
    else { set('s-senior2',  staff.seniorJudge2); }
    // 全員「設定しない」ならスタッフカードごと非表示
    if (staff.chiefJudgeDisabled && staff.chiefRecorderDisabled && staff.seniorJudge1Disabled && staff.seniorJudge2Disabled) {
        document.getElementById('staff-card').style.display = 'none';
    }

    // 選手登録済みクラスのみ対象（サーバーから取得済みのデータを使用）
    const athClasses = new Set(athSnap.docs.map(ad => {
        const a = ad.data();
        return `${a.type || 'individual'}:${a.class || a.category || ''}`;
    }));

    // クラス別（選手が1人以上いるクラスのみ）
    const cls          = d.classes || {};
    const individual   = (cls.individual || [])
        .filter(c => athClasses.has(`individual:${c.name}`))
        .map(c => ({ name: c.name, type: 'individual' }));
    const synchro      = (cls.synchro    || [])
        .filter(c => athClasses.has(`synchro:${c.name}`))
        .map(c => ({ name: c.name, type: 'synchro' }));
    const judgeConfig  = d.judgeConfig?.classes || {};
    const classRules   = d.classRules || {};
    const container    = document.getElementById('classes-container');

    // クラス別のロール配列を返す（E審判人数・H5H6の有無を反映）
    function getRoles(name, type) {
        const rule   = classRules[type]?.[name] || {};
        const numE   = parseInt(rule.eJudgeCount) || 4;
        const hasH56 = rule.hasH5H6 === true;
        const roles  = [['cjp','CJP']];
        for (let i = 1; i <= numE; i++) roles.push([`e${i}`, `E${i}`]);
        if (hasH56) roles.push(['h5','H5'], ['h6','H6']);
        roles.push(['d7','D7'], ['d8','D8']);
        return roles;
    }

    function escHtml(s) {
        return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    }

    // ロール配列から審判テーブルを生成
    function buildJudgeTable(data, ROLES) {
        const table = document.createElement('table');
        table.className = 'judge-table';
        const hasAny = ROLES.some(([k]) => data[k]);
        let html = '';
        if (hasAny) {
            ROLES.forEach(([key, lbl]) => {
                const val  = data[key]           || '';
                const kind = data[key + '_kind'] || '';
                html += `<tr>
                    <td class="role">${lbl}</td>
                    <td class="name${val ? '' : ' empty'}">${escHtml(val || '—')}${val && kind ? `<span class="judge-kind">${escHtml(kind)}</span>` : ''}</td>
                </tr>`;
            });
        } else {
            html = `<tr><td colspan="2" class="name empty">未設定</td></tr>`;
        }
        table.innerHTML = `<tbody>${html}</tbody>`;
        return table;
    }

    // クラスをまたいで同じ審判構成 (fingerprint) ごとにまとめてカードを生成
    // fingerprint にはロール種別も含めるため、E審判数が異なるクラスは別グループになる
    function buildSection(list, type) {
        if (list.length === 0) return;
        const GENDERS = ['男子', '女子'];
        const ROUNDS  = ['予選', '決勝'];

        // fingerprint → { sections:[{className, gender, round}], data, ROLES }
        const groupMap = new Map();

        list.forEach(({ name }) => {
            const classData   = judgeConfig[type]?.[name] || judgeConfig[name] || {};
            const ROLES       = getRoles(name, type);
            // 旧形式検出（トップレベルキーに cjp/e1 等が含まれる）
            const isOldFormat = Object.keys(classData).some(k => ['cjp','e1','e2','e3','e4','d7','d8'].includes(k));

            if (isOldFormat) {
                const hasAny = ROLES.some(([k]) => classData[k]);
                if (!hasAny) return;
                // ロールキー込みのフィンガープリントで識別
                const fp = JSON.stringify(ROLES.map(([k]) => [k, classData[k]||'', classData[k+'_kind']||'']));
                if (!groupMap.has(fp)) groupMap.set(fp, { sections: [], data: classData, ROLES });
                groupMap.get(fp).sections.push({ className: name, gender: '', round: '' });
            } else {
                for (const g of GENDERS) {
                    for (const r of ROUNDS) {
                        const sec = classData[g]?.[r];
                        if (!sec) continue;
                        const hasAny = ROLES.some(([k]) => sec[k]);
                        if (!hasAny) continue;
                        const fp = JSON.stringify(ROLES.map(([k]) => [k, sec[k]||'', sec[k+'_kind']||'']));
                        if (!groupMap.has(fp)) groupMap.set(fp, { sections: [], data: sec, ROLES });
                        groupMap.get(fp).sections.push({ className: name, gender: g, round: r });
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        if (groupMap.size === 0) return;

        const typeLabel = type === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
        const heading = document.createElement('div');
        heading.className = 'section-heading';
        heading.textContent = typeLabel;
        container.appendChild(heading);

        groupMap.forEach(({ sections, data, ROLES }) => {
            const classes = [...new Set(sections.map(s => s.className))];
            const genders = [...new Set(sections.map(s => s.gender).filter(Boolean))];
            const rounds  = [...new Set(sections.map(s => s.round).filter(Boolean))];
            const subLabel = genders.length
                ? genders.join('・') + ' / ' + rounds.join('・')
                : '';

            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'cls-card';
            let html = `<div class="cls-header">
                <span class="cls-badge ${type}">${typeLabel}</span>
                <span class="cls-name">${escHtml(classes.join('・'))}</span>
            </div>`;
            if (subLabel) html += `<div class="cls-sublabel">${escHtml(subLabel)}</div>`;
            html += '<div class="cls-body"></div>';
            card.innerHTML = html;
            card.querySelector('.cls-body').appendChild(buildJudgeTable(data, ROLES));
            container.appendChild(card);
        });
    }

        buildSection(individual, 'individual');
        buildSection(synchro,    'synchro');

        if (individual.length === 0 && synchro.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<p class="empty-msg">クラスが設定されていません。</p>';
        }
    }
    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

File: judge_tof_hd.html

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ja">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>ToF/HD Input - Trampoline System</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #0b0e11; --card: #1e2227;
            --accent: #1a73e8; --success: #00e676; --danger: #ff1744;
            --text: #ffffff; --text-dim: #90a4ae;
        }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
        body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; min-height: 100vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }

        /* トップバー */
        .top-bar { background: #000; padding: 12px 20px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; flex-shrink: 0; }
        .role-badge { font-size: 1.6rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.03em; }
        .panel-badge { font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-dim); margin-left: 10px; }
        .btn-exit { color: var(--danger); text-decoration: none; border: 1px solid var(--danger); padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: bold; }

        /* 選手バー */
        .athlete-bar { background: var(--card); padding: 16px 24px; border-bottom: 1px solid #333; flex-shrink: 0; }
        .athlete-name { font-size: 1.5rem; font-weight: 900; }
        .athlete-meta { font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-dim); margin-top: 4px; }
        .no-athlete { color: var(--text-dim); font-style: italic; }

        /* メイン */
        .main { flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 30px 20px; gap: 24px; }

        /* 保存済み値 / 有効本数 */
        .saved-row { width: 100%; max-width: 360px; background: var(--card); border-radius: 10px; padding: 14px 20px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #333; }
        .saved-label { font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-dim); font-weight: bold; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.04em; }
        .saved-val { font-size: 1.4rem; font-weight: 900; font-family: monospace; color: var(--text-dim); }
        .saved-val.has-val { color: var(--success); }
        .vc-val { font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: 900; font-family: monospace; color: var(--text-dim); }
        .vc-val.has-val { color: #ffd740; }

        /* 入力エリア */

        .input-label { font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-dim); font-weight: bold; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.05em; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        .big-input {
            width: 100%; background: #111; border: 2px solid #444; color: #fff;
            padding: 18px; font-size: 3.5rem; font-weight: 900; border-radius: 12px;
            text-align: center; font-family: monospace;
            -moz-appearance: textfield;
        }
        .big-input::-webkit-outer-spin-button,
        .big-input::-webkit-inner-spin-button { -webkit-appearance: none; margin: 0; }
        .big-input:focus { border-color: var(--accent); outline: none; background: #161a1f; }

        .actual-wrap { margin-top: 12px; }
        .actual-value { font-size: 2rem; color: var(--success); font-weight: 900; font-family: monospace; }
        .actual-label { font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-dim); margin-top: 2px; }

        /* 送信ボタン */

            width: 100%; max-width: 360px;
            background: var(--accent); color: white; border: none;
            padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 1.3rem; font-weight: 900;

<div class="top-bar">
    <div>
        <span class="role-badge" id="role-label">-</span>
        <span class="panel-badge" id="panel-label">PANEL -</span>
    </div>
    <a href="#" id="exit-link" class="btn-exit">✕ 終了</a>

<div class="athlete-bar">
    <div class="athlete-name" id="ath-name"><span class="no-athlete">選手を待機中…</span></div>
    <div class="athlete-meta" id="ath-meta">-</div>
</div>

<div class="main">

    <div class="saved-row">
        <span class="saved-label">有効本数</span>
        <span class="vc-val" id="vc-val">-</span>
    </div>

    <div class="saved-row">
        <span class="saved-label">保存済み値</span>
        <span class="saved-val" id="saved-val">-</span>
    </div>

    <div class="input-section">
        <div class="input-label" id="input-label">入力値（整数）</div>
        <input type="number" class="big-input" id="val-input"
               placeholder="0" min="0" max="999999" inputmode="numeric"
               oninput="onInput(this.value)">
        <div class="actual-wrap">
            <div class="actual-value" id="actual-display">-</div>
            <div class="actual-label" id="actual-label">実際の値</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <button class="btn-send" id="btn-send" onclick="send()" disabled>送信</button>
    <div class="status-msg" id="status-msg"></div>

</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, onSnapshot, getDoc, setDoc, serverTimestamp }
        from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params    = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId   = params.get('event');
    const panel     = params.get('panel') || 'A';
    const role      = params.get('role')  || 'T'; // 'T' or 'HD'

    // ToF は × 1000（例: 16.200 → 16200）、HD は × 100（例: 8.50 → 850）
    const divisor = role === 'T' ? 1000 : 100;

    let currentAthleteId = null;
    let currentRound     = null;
    let unsubResults     = null;

    // ---- UI 初期化 ----
    document.getElementById('role-label').textContent  = role === 'T' ? 'ToF (T)' : 'HD';
    document.getElementById('panel-label').textContent = `PANEL ${panel}`;
    document.getElementById('exit-link').href = `judge_role_select.html?event=${eventId}`;
    document.getElementById('input-label').textContent  = `入力値（実際の値 × ${divisor} の整数）`;
    document.getElementById('actual-label').textContent = `実際の値（入力値 ÷ ${divisor}）`;

    // ---- results を onSnapshot で監視して有効本数をリアルタイム表示 ----
    function watchResults() {
        if (unsubResults) { unsubResults(); unsubResults = null; }
        const vcEl = document.getElementById('vc-val');
        if (!currentAthleteId || !currentRound) {
            vcEl.textContent = '-'; vcEl.classList.remove('has-val'); return;
        }
        const resId = `${eventId}_${currentAthleteId}_${currentRound}`;
        unsubResults = onSnapshot(doc(db, 'results', resId), snap => {
            const vc = snap.exists() ? snap.data().validCount : null;
            if (vc != null) {
                vcEl.textContent = `${vc}本`; vcEl.classList.add('has-val');
            } else {
                vcEl.textContent = '-'; vcEl.classList.remove('has-val');
            }
        });
    }

    // ---- 選手切替時に入力欄・ステータスをクリア ----
    function clearInput() {
        document.getElementById('val-input').value = '';
        document.getElementById('actual-display').textContent = '-';
        document.getElementById('btn-send').disabled = true;
        const statusEl = document.getElementById('status-msg');
        statusEl.textContent = '';
        statusEl.className = 'status-msg';
    }

    // ---- liveState を監視して現在の選手を追従 ----
    if (eventId) {
        onSnapshot(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), snap => {
            const data = snap.data() || {};
            const live = data.liveState?.[panel] || {};

            const newAthleteId = live.athleteId || null;
            const newRound     = live.round     || 'pre1';

            // 選手またはラウンドが切り替わったら入力値・ステータスをリセット
            if (newAthleteId !== currentAthleteId || newRound !== currentRound) {
                clearInput();
            }

            currentAthleteId = newAthleteId;
            currentRound     = newRound;

            const nameEl = document.getElementById('ath-name');
            const metaEl = document.getElementById('ath-meta');
            if (live.athleteName) {
                nameEl.innerHTML = '';
                nameEl.textContent = live.athleteName;
            } else {
                nameEl.innerHTML = '<span class="no-athlete">選手を待機中…</span>';
            }
            metaEl.textContent = live.athleteClass
                ? `${live.athleteClass} / ${currentRound.toUpperCase()}`
                : '-';

            // 送信ボタンの活性制御（入力済みのとき）
            const v = parseInt(document.getElementById('val-input').value, 10);
            document.getElementById('btn-send').disabled = !(currentAthleteId && !isNaN(v) && v >= 0);

            // 有効本数のリアルタイム監視を開始（選手切替時はリスナー張り直し）
            watchResults();
            // 保存済み値を取得・表示
            loadSavedVal();
        });
    }

    // ---- 保存済み値を読み込んで表示 ----
    async function loadSavedVal() {
        const savedEl = document.getElementById('saved-val');
        if (!currentAthleteId || !currentRound) {
            savedEl.textContent = '-';
            savedEl.className = 'saved-val';
            return;
        }
        const resId = `${eventId}_${currentAthleteId}_${currentRound}`;
        const snap  = await getDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId));
        if (snap.exists()) {
            const d   = snap.data();
            const val = role === 'T'
                ? (d.tofByPanel?.[panel] ?? d.tof)
                : (d.hdByPanel?.[panel] ?? d.hd?.total);
            if (val !== undefined && val !== null) {
                savedEl.textContent = Number(val).toFixed(3);
                savedEl.className   = 'saved-val has-val';
                return;
            }
        }
        savedEl.textContent = '-';
        savedEl.className   = 'saved-val';
    }

    // ---- 入力値変化時 ----
    window.onInput = function(raw) {
        const v   = parseInt(raw, 10);
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-send');
        if (!isNaN(v) && v >= 0) {
            document.getElementById('actual-display').textContent = (v / divisor).toFixed(3);
            btn.disabled = !currentAthleteId;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('actual-display').textContent = '-';
            btn.disabled = true;
        }
    };

    // ---- 送信 ----
    window.send = async function() {
        if (!currentAthleteId || !currentRound) {
            showStatus('選手が選択されていません', true);
            return;
        }
        const raw = parseInt(document.getElementById('val-input').value, 10);
        if (isNaN(raw) || raw < 0) {
            showStatus('値を入力してください', true);
            return;
        }

        const actual = raw / divisor;
        const resId  = `${eventId}_${currentAthleteId}_${currentRound}`;
        const btn    = document.getElementById('btn-send');
        btn.disabled = true;

        try {
            const saveData = role === 'T'
                ? { tofByPanel: { [panel]: actual }, updatedAt: serverTimestamp() }
                : { hdByPanel: { [panel]: actual }, updatedAt: serverTimestamp() };

            await setDoc(doc(db, 'results', resId), saveData, { merge: true });

            // 保存済み値を更新
            const savedEl = document.getElementById('saved-val');
            savedEl.textContent = actual.toFixed(3);
            savedEl.className   = 'saved-val has-val';

            showStatus('✓ 送信完了', false, true); // 選手切替まで表示を保持
        } catch (e) {
            showStatus('エラー: ' + e.message, true);
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    // persistent=true のとき自動消去しない（選手切替時に clearInput() で消す）
    function showStatus(msg, isError = false, persistent = false) {
        const el = document.getElementById('status-msg');
        el.textContent = msg;
        el.className   = 'status-msg ' + (isError ? 'err' : 'ok');
        if (!persistent) {
            setTimeout(() => { el.textContent = ''; el.className = 'status-msg'; }, 3000);
        }
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script type="module">
  import { getDocs, collection, query, where } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
  import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
  import { normalizeClassRules, extractTbScores, calcQualifyingScore, calcQualifyingTb, calcFinalScore, compareRankEntries, assignRanks } from "./shared.js";
  import { getTournament, getAthletes, getFinalOrders } from "./cache.js";

  const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  const eventId = params.get('event');
  let athletes = [], tourData = null;

  async function init() {
    if (!eventId) { document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = '<div class="no-data">URLにeventパラメータがありません</div>'; return; }
    const [fetchedTour, fetchedAth] = await Promise.all([
      getTournament(eventId),
      getAthletes(eventId)
    ]);
    if (!fetchedTour) { document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = '<div class="no-data">大会が見つかりません</div>'; return; }
    tourData = fetchedTour;
    athletes = fetchedAth;
    // 決勝試技順はキャッシュ除外なので毎回Firestoreから取得してマージ
    const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
    athletes.forEach(a => {
      const fo = finalOrders[a.id];
      if (fo) { a.finalOrder = fo.finalOrder; a.isInFinal = fo.isInFinal; }
    });
    buildSelectors();
  }

  function buildSelectors() {
    const urlType   = params.get('type');
    const urlGender = params.get('gender');
    const urlClass  = params.get('class');
    const urlRound  = params.get('round');

    // 選手登録のある (type, class, gender) 組み合わせを列挙
    const hasBothTypes = athletes.some(a => (a.type || 'individual') === 'synchro');
    const combos = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    ['individual','synchro'].forEach(type => {
      const tourClassDefs = (tourData?.classes?.[type] || [])
        .slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c=>c.name);
      const byType = athletes.filter(a => (a.type || 'individual') === type);
      const rawClasses = [...new Set(byType.map(a => a.class || a.category || '').filter(Boolean))];
      const classes = [...tourClassDefs.filter(c=>rawClasses.includes(c)), ...rawClasses.filter(c=>!tourClassDefs.includes(c))];
      classes.forEach(cls => {
        const genders = [...new Set(
          byType.filter(a => (a.class || a.category || '') === cls).map(a => a.gender || '').filter(Boolean)
        )];
        // 性別が登録されていない場合は空文字で1件
        (genders.length ? genders : ['']).forEach(gender => {
          const key = `${type}::${cls}::${gender}`;
          if (seen.has(key)) return;
          seen.add(key);
          let label = `${cls}${gender ? ' ' + gender : ''}`;
          if (hasBothTypes) label = (type === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ ' : '個人 ') + label;
          combos.push({ key, label });
        });
      });
    });

    const selGroup = document.getElementById('sel-group');
    selGroup.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- 選択 --</option>'
      + combos.map(c => `<option value="${c.key}">${c.label}</option>`).join('');

    // URLパラメータで初期選択
    if (urlType || urlClass || urlGender) {
      const matchKey = `${urlType||'individual'}::${urlClass||''}::${urlGender||''}`;
      // 完全一致を優先、なければ type+class のみで探す
      const exact = combos.find(c => c.key === matchKey);
      const loose = exact || combos.find(c => c.key.startsWith(`${urlType||'individual'}::${urlClass||''}`));
      if (loose) selGroup.value = loose.key;
    }

    if (urlRound) document.getElementById('sel-round').value = urlRound;

    // 変更で自動再読み込み
    ['sel-group','sel-round','sel-qual','sel-resv'].forEach(id =>
      document.getElementById(id).addEventListener('change', () => loadRanking())
    );

    // URLパラメータあれば即表示
    if (selGroup.value) loadRanking();
  }

  // S1-S10: 全審判の各技減点を合計（stored値×10 = タップ単位に戻す）
  function calcSkillTotals(result) {
    const judges = Object.values(result.execution?.judges || {});
    if (judges.length === 0) return { skills: Array(10).fill(null), land: null };

    // CJP の middle2 と同じ：中間2名の合計を ×10 して表示
    function mid2sum(vals) {
      const filled = vals.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
      if (filled.length < 2) return filled.length === 1 ? Math.round(filled[0] * 10) : null;
      filled.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const mid = Math.floor((filled.length - 2) / 2);
      // 中間2名の合計（×10整数）
      return Math.round((filled[mid] + filled[mid + 1]) * 10);
    }

    // 各技の有効減点合計を ×10 して整数表示（CJP の middle2sum と同じ値）
    const skills = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => {
      const vals = judges.map(j => (j.deductions || [])[i] ?? null);
      return mid2sum(vals);
    });

    const land = mid2sum(judges.map(j => j.landing ?? null));

    return { skills, land };
  }

  const fmt3 = v => v != null ? Number(v).toFixed(3) : null;
  const fmt2 = v => v != null ? Number(v).toFixed(2) : null;
  const fmt1 = v => v != null ? Number(v).toFixed(1) : null;

  window.loadRanking = async function() {
    const selGroup = document.getElementById('sel-group').value;
    if (!selGroup) { document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = '<div class="no-data">グループを選択してください</div>'; return; }
    const [fType, fClass, fGender] = selGroup.split('::');
    const fRound  = document.getElementById('sel-round').value;

    document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = '<div class="loading">読み込み中...</div>';

    const classRules = normalizeClassRules(tourData.classRules);
    let filtered = athletes.filter(a =>
      (a.type || 'individual') === fType &&
      (!fGender || (a.gender || '') === fGender) &&
      (!fClass  || (a.class || a.category || '') === fClass)
    );
    // 決勝は試技順が確定した選手のみ表示
    if (fRound === 'final') filtered = filtered.filter(a => a.finalOrder != null);
    if (!filtered.length) { document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = '<div class="no-data">該当する選手がいません</div>'; return; }

    // published な結果取得
    const resSnap = await getDocs(query(
      collection(db, 'results'),
      where('eventId', '==', eventId),
      where('published', '==', true)
    ));
    const byAth = {};
    resSnap.docs.forEach(d => {
      const r = d.data();
      if (!byAth[r.athleteId]) byAth[r.athleteId] = {};
      byAth[r.athleteId][r.round] = r;
    });

    // 各選手のスコア計算 ─ shared.js の共通関数を使用
    const rows = filtered.map(a => {
      const rMap = byAth[a.id] || {};
      const rule = (classRules[fType] || {})[a.class || a.category || ''] || {};
      const numPre         = rule.qualifyingRoutines ?? tourData.qualifyingRoutines ?? 1;
      const scoreMode      = rule.scoreMode || 'best';
      const finalScoreMode = rule.finalScoreMode ?? 'zero';
      const pre1r = rMap['pre1'] || null;
      const pre2r = rMap['pre2'] || null;
      const finr  = rMap['final'] || null;
      const pre1  = pre1r?.officialTotal ?? null;
      const pre2  = pre2r?.officialTotal ?? null;
      const final = finr?.officialTotal ?? null;

      let qualScore, tbSum, tb;
      if (fRound === 'qualifying') {
        qualScore = calcQualifyingScore(pre1, pre2, numPre, scoreMode);
        ({ tbSum, tb } = calcQualifyingTb(pre1, pre2, pre1r, pre2r, numPre, scoreMode));
      } else if (fRound === 'pre1') {
        qualScore = pre1; tbSum = null; tb = extractTbScores(pre1r);
      } else if (fRound === 'pre2') {
        qualScore = pre2; tbSum = null; tb = extractTbScores(pre2r);
      } else if (fRound === 'final') {
        const qualPart = calcQualifyingScore(pre1, pre2, numPre, scoreMode);
        qualScore = calcFinalScore(qualPart, final, finalScoreMode);
        tbSum = null; tb = extractTbScores(finr);
      }

      // DNS（棄権）チェック: 対象ラウンドの result に dns:true があれば最下位扱い
      const isDns = (() => {
        if (fRound === 'qualifying') return pre1r?.dns === true || pre2r?.dns === true;
        if (fRound === 'pre1')  return pre1r?.dns === true;
        if (fRound === 'pre2')  return pre2r?.dns === true;
        if (fRound === 'final') return finr?.dns  === true;
        return false;
      })();
      if (isDns) qualScore = null; // null にすることで compareRankEntries が最下位へ
      return { a, pre1r, pre2r, finr, pre1, pre2, final, qualScore, isDns, numPre, scoreMode, finalScoreMode, tbSum, tb };
    }).sort(compareRankEntries);

    assignRanks(rows);

    // タイトル
    const labelType = fType === 'synchro' ? 'シンクロ' : '個人';
    const roundLabels = { qualifying:'予選', pre1:'予選 R1', pre2:'予選 R2', final:'決勝' };
    const sheetTitle = ['TRA', labelType, fGender, fClass, roundLabels[fRound]].filter(Boolean).join('_');

    // 表示するラウンドリスト
    const showRounds = fRound === 'qualifying'
      ? (rows[0]?.numPre >= 2 ? ['pre1','pre2'] : ['pre1'])
      : [fRound];

    const isFinal = fRound === 'final';
    const _sectionRule    = (classRules[fType] || {})[fClass] || {};
    const scoreMode       = _sectionRule.scoreMode || 'best';
    const finalScoreMode  = _sectionRule.finalScoreMode ?? 'zero';
    const totalLabel = isFinal
      ? (finalScoreMode === 'combined' ? 'Total' : 'No.')
      : (scoreMode === 'total' ? 'Total' : 'Best');
    // 決勝: Round列は不要、No.列（zero mode）も不要
    const showRoundCol = !isFinal;
    const showTotalCol = !isFinal || finalScoreMode === 'combined';

    // thead
    const thead = `<thead><tr>
      <th class="col-rank" rowspan="2">Rank</th>
      <th class="col-name" rowspan="2">氏名</th>
      ${showRoundCol ? '<th class="col-round" rowspan="2">Round</th>' : ''}
      <th class="col-s">S1</th><th class="col-s">S2</th><th class="col-s">S3</th>
      <th class="col-s">S4</th><th class="col-s">S5</th><th class="col-s">S6</th>
      <th class="col-s">S7</th><th class="col-s">S8</th><th class="col-s">S9</th>
      <th class="col-s">S10</th>
      <th class="col-land">L</th>
      <th class="col-exe">Exe</th>
      <th class="col-diff">Diff</th>
      <th class="col-tof">${fType === 'synchro' ? 'Syn' : 'ToF'}</th>
      <th class="col-hd">HD</th>
      <th class="col-pen">Pen</th>
      <th class="col-score">Score</th>
      ${showTotalCol ? `<th class="col-total">${totalLabel}</th>` : ''}
    </tr></thead>`;

    // 列幅colgroup
    const colgroup = `<colgroup>
      <col class="col-rank"><col class="col-name">
      ${showRoundCol ? '<col class="col-round">' : ''}
      ${Array(10).fill('<col class="col-s">').join('')}
      <col class="col-land"><col class="col-exe"><col class="col-diff">
      <col class="col-tof"><col class="col-hd"><col class="col-pen">
      <col class="col-score">
      ${showTotalCol ? '<col class="col-total">' : ''}
    </colgroup>`;

    const nQual = parseInt(document.getElementById('sel-qual').value) || 0;
    const nResv = parseInt(document.getElementById('sel-resv').value) || 0;

    // tbody
    let tbodyHtml = '';
    rows.forEach((r, idx) => {
      const rank = r.rank;
      const isLast = idx === rows.length - 1;
      const isQual = nQual > 0 && rank <= nQual;
      const isResv = nResv > 0 && rank > nQual && rank <= nQual + nResv;
      const rankCls = r.isDns ? ' dns-row' : isQual ? ' is-qual' : isResv ? ' is-resv' : '';
      const qmark = r.isDns ? '' : isQual ? '<span class="q-mark">Q</span>' : isResv ? '<span class="r-mark">R</span>' : '';
      const medal = '';

      // rowspanセルは最終選手かつ複数ラウンド時のみ底線を直接付与（CSSのlast-childでは届かない）
      const rsStyle = (isLast && showRounds.length > 1) ? ' style="border-bottom:2px solid #aaa"' : '';

      const nameHtml = r.a.type === 'synchro'
        ? `<div class="ath-name">${r.a.name1 || '−'} / ${r.a.name2 || '−'}</div>
           <div class="ath-club">${[r.a.club1, r.a.club2].filter(Boolean).join(' / ')}</div>`
        : `<div class="ath-name">${r.a.name1 || '−'}</div>
           <div class="ath-club">${r.a.club1 || ''}</div>`;

      const totalCell = isFinal
        ? (r.finalScoreMode === 'combined'
            ? `<td class="total-val" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}>${r.qualScore != null ? fmt2(r.qualScore) : '<span class="score-na">−</span>'}</td>`
            : `<td class="total-val" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}>${r.a.finalOrder != null ? `No.${r.a.finalOrder}` : '−'}</td>`)
        : `<td class="total-val" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}>${r.qualScore != null ? fmt2(r.qualScore) : '<span class="score-na">−</span>'}</td>`;

      showRounds.forEach((rnd, ri) => {
        const res = rnd === 'pre1' ? r.pre1r : rnd === 'pre2' ? r.pre2r : r.finr;
        const rndLabel = rnd === 'pre1' ? '予選1' : rnd === 'pre2' ? '予選2' : '決勝';
        const isFirst = ri === 0;
        const rowCls = `ath-row-${ri+1}${rankCls}`;

        // DNS行: 全セルを空にして DNS バッジのみ表示
        if (r.isDns) {
          const dnsTotalCell = isFirst && showTotalCol
            ? `<td class="total-val" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}><span class="dns-badge">DNS</span></td>`
            : '';
          const emptySkills = Array(11).fill('<td></td>').join('');
          tbodyHtml += `<tr class="${rowCls}">
            ${isFirst ? `<td class="col-rank" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}><span class="dns-badge">DNS</span></td>` : ''}
            ${isFirst ? `<td rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}>${nameHtml}</td>` : ''}
            ${showRoundCol ? `<td class="round-label">${rndLabel}</td>` : ''}
            ${emptySkills}
            <td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td>
            ${dnsTotalCell}
          </tr>`;
          return;
        }

        // S1-S10, L
        let skillCells = '';
        if (res) {
          const { skills, land } = calcSkillTotals(res);
          const vLimit = res.validCount ?? 10;  // 中断時の有効本数
          for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            if (i >= vLimit) {
              // 中断後の技：空欄
              skillCells += `<td class="score-na"></td>`;
            } else {
              const v = skills[i];
              skillCells += `<td class="s-val">${v != null ? (Number.isInteger(v) ? v : v.toFixed(1)) : '−'}</td>`;
            }
          }
          // 着地：validCount=10のときのみ表示
          if (vLimit < 10) {
            skillCells += `<td class="score-na"></td>`;
          } else {
            skillCells += `<td class="s-val">${land != null ? (Number.isInteger(land) ? land : land.toFixed(1)) : '−'}</td>`;
          }
        } else {
          skillCells = Array(11).fill('<td class="score-na">−</td>').join('');
        }

        const exe  = res?.officialE    != null ? fmt1(res.officialE)    : null;
        const diff = res?.officialD    != null ? fmt1(res.officialD)    : null;
        const tof  = res?.tof          != null ? fmt3(res.tof)          : null;
        const hd   = res?.hd?.total    != null ? fmt2(res.hd.total)     : null;
        const pen  = (res?.penalty != null && res.penalty !== 0) ? fmt1(res.penalty) : null;
        const sc   = res?.officialTotal!= null ? fmt2(res.officialTotal): null;

        const cell = v => v != null ? v : '<span class="score-na">−</span>';

        tbodyHtml += `<tr class="${rowCls}">
          ${isFirst ? `<td class="col-rank" rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}><span class="rank-num">${rank}</span>${qmark}</td>` : ''}
          ${isFirst ? `<td rowspan="${showRounds.length}"${rsStyle}>${nameHtml}</td>` : ''}
          ${showRoundCol ? `<td class="round-label">${rndLabel}</td>` : ''}
          ${skillCells}
          <td class="score-val">${cell(exe)}</td>
          <td class="score-val">${cell(diff)}</td>
          <td class="score-val">${cell(tof)}</td>
          <td class="score-val">${cell(hd)}</td>
          <td>${cell(pen)}</td>
          <td class="score-val">${cell(sc)}</td>
          ${isFirst && showTotalCol ? totalCell : ''}
        </tr>`;
      });
    });

    function toJpDate(ds) {
      if (!ds) return '';
      const d = new Date(ds + 'T00:00:00');
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ja-JP-u-ca-japanese', {
        era:'long', year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'
      }).format(d);
    }
    const venue = tourData.venue || '';
    const s = tourData.start_date || tourData.startDate || '';
    const e = tourData.end_date   || tourData.endDate   || '';
    const jpS = toJpDate(s), jpE = toJpDate(e);
    const dateStr = jpS && jpE && s !== e ? `${jpS} 〜 ${jpE}` : (jpS || jpE);

    const groupParts = [];
    if (fType === 'synchro') groupParts.push('シンクロ'); else groupParts.push('個人');
    if (fGender) groupParts.push(fGender);
    if (fClass)  groupParts.push(fClass);
    groupParts.push(roundLabels[fRound] || fRound);
    const groupText = groupParts.join(' / ');

    document.getElementById('output').innerHTML = `
      <div class="sheet-header">
        <div class="header-top">
          <div class="header-main">
            <div class="event-name">${tourData.name || ''}</div>
            ${venue  ? `<div class="sheet-meta">${venue}</div>`   : ''}
            ${dateStr ? `<div class="sheet-meta">${dateStr}</div>` : ''}
          </div>
          <div class="sheet-credit">
            <div class="sys-text">
              <span class="sys-dev">Dev. Hirotaka.H</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <hr class="sheet-divider">
      </div>
      <div class="group-label">${groupText}</div>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto">
        <table class="score-table">${colgroup}${thead}<tbody>${tbodyHtml}</tbody></table>
      </div>`;
  };

  init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<main class="main" id="main">
    <nav class="top-nav">
        <a href="admin_index.html" class="back-link">← 管理画面へ</a>
    </nav>

    <h1 class="page-title">📘 Readme</h1>
    <p class="page-sub">Trampoline Scoring System — 運用マニュアル（管理者向け）</p>


    <!-- ====================================================== -->
    <!-- Section: Googleアカウントの追加                         -->
    <!-- ====================================================== -->
    <section class="section" id="google-account">
        <h2>Googleアカウントの追加（管理者の追加）</h2>

        <h3 id="google-account-how">仕組み</h3>
        <p>
            管理者ログインには Google アカウントを使用します。
            ログイン時に Firestore の <code>admins</code> コレクションへ
            そのアカウントの <strong>UID</strong> が登録されているかを確認し、
            登録済みの場合のみ管理者として認可されます。
        </p>
        <div class="callout callout-info">
            ℹ️　<strong>UID</strong> とは Firebase が各 Google アカウントに割り当てる固有IDです。
            追加される人には専用ページでUID取得・送付を依頼します（次の手順）。
        </div>

        <h3 id="google-account-share">① 追加したい人に専用URLを送る</h3>
        <p>
            以下の URL を LINEやメールなどで相手に送ってください。<br>
            相手はそのページを開き、Googleでログインするだけで自分のUIDを確認・コピーできます。
        </p>
        <div class="path-block" id="share-url">https://travaults-system.web.app/request_access.html</div>
        <button class="copy-btn" onclick="copyShareUrl()">📋 URLをコピー</button>
        <p style="margin-top:14px;">
            相手が操作を完了すると、<strong>メールアドレス</strong> と <strong>UID</strong> を送ってくれます。<br>
            それを受け取ったら次のステップへ進みます。
        </p>

        <h3 id="google-account-add">② 受け取った UID を Firestore に追加する</h3>
        <ol class="steps">
            <li>
                <span class="step-num">1</span>
                <div class="step-body">
                    <strong>Firebase Console を開く</strong><br>
                    <a href="https://console.firebase.google.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">console.firebase.google.com</a>
                    にアクセスし、プロジェクト <code>trampoline-dd-test</code> を選択します。
                </div>
            </li>
            <li>
                <span class="step-num">2</span>
                <div class="step-body">
                    <strong>Firestore Database → admins コレクションを開く</strong><br>
                    左メニューの <strong>Firestore Database</strong> → データタブを開き、
                    <code>admins</code> コレクションを選択します。
                    <div class="path-block">Firestore Database → データ → admins</div>
                </div>
            </li>
            <li>
                <span class="step-num">3</span>
                <div class="step-body">
                    <strong>ドキュメントを追加する</strong><br>
                    <strong>「＋ ドキュメントを追加」</strong> をクリックし、
                    <strong>ドキュメントID</strong> の欄に受け取った UID を貼り付けます。<br>
                    管理のため <code>name</code>（文字列型）フィールドに氏名を入れておくと便利です。
                    <div class="path-block">ドキュメントID: &lt;受け取ったUID&gt;<br>フィールド（任意）: name = "氏名"</div>
                    最後に <strong>「保存」</strong> をクリックします。
                </div>
            </li>
        </ol>
        <div class="callout callout-warn">
            ⚠️　Firestore Console の操作には Firebase プロジェクトの編集権限が必要です。
        </div>

        <h3 id="google-account-done">③ 相手に完了を伝える</h3>
        <p>
            Firestore への追加が完了したら、相手に連絡してください。<br>
            以下のURLからログインできるようになります。
        </p>
        <div class="path-block">https://travaults-system.web.app/admin_login.html</div>

        <h3 id="google-account-remove">権限の削除</h3>
        <p>
            管理者権限を失効させるには、Firestore の <code>admins</code> コレクションから
            対象の UID のドキュメントを削除します。
            次回ログイン時から管理者として認可されなくなります。
        </p>
        <div class="callout callout-ok">
            ✅　Firebase Authentication 側のアカウント（Users）を削除しなくても、
            <code>admins</code> コレクションからドキュメントを消すだけで権限は失効します。
        </div>
    </section>

</main>

<script>
    // スクロールに合わせてサイドバーのアクティブリンクを更新
    const headings = document.querySelectorAll('section[id], h3[id]');
    const sideLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar a[href^="#"]');

    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(e => {
            if (!e.isIntersecting) return;
            sideLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
            const link = document.querySelector(`.sidebar a[href="#${e.target.id}"]`);
            if (link) link.classList.add('active');
        });
    }, { rootMargin: '-10% 0px -80% 0px', threshold: 0 });

    headings.forEach(h => observer.observe(h));

    // 共有URLコピー
    function copyShareUrl() {
        const url = document.getElementById('share-url').textContent.trim();
        const btn = document.querySelector('.copy-btn');
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(() => {
            btn.textContent = '✓ コピーしました';
            btn.classList.add('copied');
            setTimeout(() => {
                btn.textContent = '📋 URLをコピー';
                btn.classList.remove('copied');
            }, 2200);
        });
    }
    window.copyShareUrl = copyShareUrl;
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="sys-label">Trampoline Scoring System</div>

    <!-- ===== LINE通知（常時表示） ===== -->

<script type="module">
    import { auth } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import {
        GoogleAuthProvider,
        signInWithPopup,
        signOut,
        onAuthStateChanged
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-auth.js";

    // LINE / Instagram 等の内蔵ブラウザ（WebView）検出 → ボタンを無効化
    (function checkWebView() {
        const ua = navigator.userAgent;
        const isWebView =
            /Line\/|FBAN|FBAV|Instagram|GSA\/|Twitter\/|Snapchat/.test(ua) ||
            (/wv/.test(ua) && /Android/.test(ua)) ||
            (/iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(ua) && !/Safari\//.test(ua) && !/CriOS\//.test(ua) && /AppleWebKit/.test(ua));
        if (isWebView) {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
            if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.style.opacity = '0.4'; btn.title = 'ブラウザで開いてからご利用ください'; }
        }
    })();

    function showInfo(user) {
        document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = user.email || '—';
        document.getElementById('user-uid').textContent   = user.uid   || '—';
        document.getElementById('phase-login').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('phase-info').style.display  = 'block';
        // このページではサインイン状態を保持しない
        signOut(auth).catch(() => {});
    }

    // すでにログイン済みなら即表示
    onAuthStateChanged(auth, user => {
        if (user) showInfo(user);
    });

    window.doLogin = async function () {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
        const errEl = document.getElementById('err-msg');
        btn.disabled = true;
        errEl.style.display = 'none';
        try {
            const result = await signInWithPopup(auth, new GoogleAuthProvider());
            showInfo(result.user);
        } catch (e) {
            if (e.code === 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' || e.code === 'auth/cancelled-popup-request') {
                btn.disabled = false;
                return;
            }
            errEl.textContent = 'エラーが発生しました: ' + e.message;
            errEl.style.display = '';
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    window.copyText = function (elId, btn) {
        const text = document.getElementById(elId).textContent;
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
            btn.textContent = '✓ コピー済';
            btn.classList.add('copied');
            setTimeout(() => {
                btn.textContent = 'コピー';
                btn.classList.remove('copied');
            }, 2000);
        });
    };
</script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    const monitor = document.getElementById('monitor');
    const btnNoAnim = document.getElementById('btn-noanim');
    let noAnimMode = false;

    function replayAnim() {
      if (noAnimMode) return;
      monitor.style.transition = 'opacity 150ms';
      monitor.style.opacity = '0';
      const targets = monitor.querySelectorAll(
        '.convention-header,.athlete-section,.ribbon-wrapper,.breakdown-row,.result-section,.footer-credits'
      );
      targets.forEach(el => {
        el.style.animation = 'none';
        void el.offsetWidth;
      });
      setTimeout(() => {
        targets.forEach(el => { el.style.animation = ''; });
        monitor.style.opacity = '';
        setTimeout(() => { monitor.style.transition = ''; }, 200);
      }, 80);
    }

    function toggleNoAnim() {
      noAnimMode = !noAnimMode;
      if (noAnimMode) {
        monitor.classList.add('no-anim');
        btnNoAnim.textContent = 'アニメON';
        btnNoAnim.classList.add('active');
      } else {
        monitor.classList.remove('no-anim');
        btnNoAnim.textContent = 'アニメOFF';
        btnNoAnim.classList.remove('active');
      }
    }
  </script>
