        .athlete-row { padding: 7px 14px 7px 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #f0f4f8; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; cursor: pointer; transition: background 0.1s; border-left: 3px solid transparent; }
        .athlete-row:hover { background: #f5f8ff; }
        .active-athlete { background: #eef4ff !important; border-left-color: var(--accent) !important; }
        .ath-name-text { font-size: 0.82rem; font-weight: 700; flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; color: #1e293b; }
        .ath-order { color: var(--accent); margin-right: 4px; font-family: monospace; font-size: 0.75rem; font-weight: 900; }
        .active-athlete .ath-name-text { color: var(--accent); }
        .round-box { display: flex; gap: 3px; flex-shrink: 0; margin-left: 6px; }
        .r-dot { min-width: 28px; height: 22px; border-radius: 5px; font-size: 0.6rem; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; background: #f1f5f9; color: #94a3b8; text-decoration: none; font-weight: 800; border: 1.5px solid transparent; transition: all 0.1s; cursor: pointer; letter-spacing: -0.02em; }
        .r-dot.has-sub { background: #eff6ff; color: #3b82f6; border-color: #bfdbfe; }
        .r-dot.has-res { background: #f0fdf4; color: #16a34a; border-color: #bbf7d0; }
        .r-dot.current { background: var(--accent) !important; color: #fff !important; border-color: var(--accent) !important; }

        /* ===== メインエリア ===== */
        .console-wrapper { flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; }
        .top-bar { background: var(--primary); padding: 8px 20px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 1px solid #0d2440; flex-shrink: 0; }
        .readonly-badge { background: #43a047; color: #fff; font-size: 0.72rem; font-weight: 800; padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 20px; letter-spacing: 0.1em; }
        .athlete-banner { background: var(--card-bg); padding: 12px 22px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 20px; border-bottom: 2px solid var(--border); box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.06); flex-shrink: 0; }
        .main-content { flex: 1; min-height: 0; padding: 16px 20px; overflow-y: auto; -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; }

        /* テーブル */
        .monitor-table { width: 100%; min-width: 680px; border-collapse: separate; border-spacing: 2px; table-layout: fixed; }
        .monitor-table th, .monitor-table td { padding: 8px 6px; text-align: center; border-radius: 4px; background: var(--cell-bg); font-size: 1.1rem; overflow: hidden; white-space: nowrap; color: var(--text); border: 1px solid transparent; }
        .monitor-table th { background: var(--th-bg); font-size: 0.65rem; color: var(--th-text); border-color: #0d2440; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: 0.03em; }
        .monitor-table thead th { font-size: 0.82rem; }
        .val-filled { color: #2e7d32; font-weight: bold; }
        .middle-cell { background: #c8e6c9 !important; color: #1b5e20 !important; font-weight: 900; outline: 2px solid #66bb6a; }
        .low-cell-mild { background: #e8f1fb !important; color: #2c5fa8 !important; font-weight: 700; outline: 1px solid #a8c4e8; }
        .low-cell { background: #cfe0f7 !important; color: #1a3d7a !important; font-weight: 900; outline: 2px solid #7aaee0; }
        .high-cell-mild { background: #fdf0f0 !important; color: #a83232 !important; font-weight: 700; outline: 1px solid #e8a8a8; }
        .high-cell { background: #f7d5d5 !important; color: #7a1a1a !important; font-weight: 900; outline: 2px solid #e07a7a; }
        .middle-row td { background: rgba(67,160,71,0.08) !important; color: var(--success); font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.9rem; border-top: 1px solid rgba(67,160,71,0.3) !important; }
        .middle-row .ded-total { color: var(--danger) !important; font-weight: 900; font-size: 1rem; }
        .sn-th { user-select: none; }
        .sn-th.sn-valid { background: rgba(67,160,71,0.18); color: #1b5e20; }
        .sn-th.sn-cutoff { background: var(--accent) !important; color: #fff !important; font-weight: 900; outline: 2px solid #0d47a1; }
        .sn-th.sn-invalid { opacity: 0.4; }
        .d-info-row th { font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-dim); background: rgba(26,115,232,0.07); font-weight: normal; border-color: rgba(26,115,232,0.15) !important; }
        .d-info-row.d-confirmed th { background: rgba(67,160,71,0.1); color: var(--success); }
        .cell-blank { background: transparent !important; border-color: transparent !important; }
        .d-badge { font-size: 0.72rem; background: #43a047; color: #fff; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 5px; margin-left: 4px; font-weight: bold; vertical-align: middle; }
        #dd-total-cell { white-space: normal; line-height: 1.3; }
        .d-dup { background: rgba(255, 193, 7, 0.22) !important; }
        #skill-symbols-row:not(.d-confirmed) th { opacity: 0.65; filter: grayscale(0.2); }
        #skill-symbols-row.d-confirmed th { opacity: 1; filter: none; font-weight: bold; color: #003c8f !important; }
        #skill-symbols-row th.d-mismatch { color: var(--danger) !important; opacity: 1 !important; filter: none !important; }

        /* HDTS 行 (read-only) */
        .sv-hdts-head th { background: #f0fdfa !important; color: #0f766e !important; font-weight: bold; border-bottom: 2px solid #5eead4 !important; }
        .sv-hdts-row th { background: #f0fdfa !important; color: #0f766e !important; border-color: #99f6e4 !important; font-size: 0.78rem !important; font-family: monospace; }
        .sv-hdts-label { font-size: 0.63rem !important; font-family: inherit !important; white-space: normal; line-height: 1.4; }
        .sv-hdts-sum { font-weight: bold; }

        /* スコア集計 */
        .score-summary { background: var(--card-bg); padding: 16px 20px; border-radius: 12px; display: flex; justify-content: space-around; align-items: center; border: 2px solid var(--accent); box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(26,115,232,0.12); }
        .score-box { text-align: center; }
        .score-box label { font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-dim); display: block; margin-bottom: 4px; font-weight: bold; }
        .score-box div { font-size: 2rem; font-weight: 900; font-family: monospace; color: var(--text); }
        .info-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4,1fr); gap: 10px; }
        .info-card { background: var(--card-bg); padding: 12px; border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid var(--border); text-align: center; }
        .info-card label { display: block; font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-dim); margin-bottom: 6px; font-weight: bold; }
        .info-card .val { font-size: 1.5rem; font-weight: 900; font-family: monospace; }
    </style>
</head>

                    <th class="sn-th" id="sn-th-6">S6</th><th class="sn-th" id="sn-th-7">S7</th><th class="sn-th" id="sn-th-8">S8</th><th class="sn-th" id="sn-th-9">S9</th><th class="sn-th" id="sn-th-10">S10</th>

                    <th class="sv-hdts-label">HDTS<br>HD(減)</th>
                    <th id="sv-hdts-hd-1">-</th><th id="sv-hdts-hd-2">-</th><th id="sv-hdts-hd-3">-</th><th id="sv-hdts-hd-4">-</th><th id="sv-hdts-hd-5">-</th>

            <div class="info-card"><label>VALID</label><div class="val" id="info-valid">—</div></div>
            <div class="info-card"><label>ToF</label><div class="val" id="info-tof">—</div></div>
            <div class="info-card"><label>HD</label><div class="val" id="info-hd">—</div></div>

        <!-- スコア集計 -->
        <div class="score-summary">
            <div class="score-box"><label>Difficulty</label><div id="res-d">0.0</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>Execution</label><div id="res-e">0.0</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label id="res-t-label">T</label><div id="res-t">0.000</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>HD</label><div id="res-hd">0.000</div></div>
            <div class="score-box"><label>Total</label><div id="res-total" style="color:var(--success)">0.000</div></div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, onSnapshot, getDocs, collection, query, where } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { getTournament, getAthletes, getFinalOrders } from "./cache.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');
    let athleteId = params.get('athlete');
    let round     = params.get('round') || 'pre1';

    let athletes = {}, submissions = {}, results = {}, currentTour = null;
    let skillMasterMap = {};
    let latestData = {};
    let fType = 'individual', fClass = '', fGender = '', fFinalOnly = false;
    let unsubscribeScores = null, unsubscribeSub = null;

    // ---- パネル自動検出（judge データのキーから判定） ----
    function detectPanel(data) {
        const keys = Object.keys(data.execution?.judges || {});
        if (keys.some(k => k.startsWith('A_'))) return 'A';
        if (keys.some(k => k.startsWith('B_'))) return 'B';
        return 'A';
    }

    // ---- 初期化 ----
    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) { document.body.innerHTML = '<p style="padding:40px;text-align:center;">大会IDが見つかりません</p>'; return; }
        document.getElementById('menu-back').href = `judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

        const [tourData, athleteList, subSnap, resSnap, smSnap] = await Promise.all([
            getTournament(eventId),
            getAthletes(eventId),
            getDocs(query(collection(db, 'submissions'), where('eventId','==',eventId))),
            getDocs(query(collection(db, 'results'),     where('eventId','==',eventId))),
            getDocs(collection(db, 'skillMaster'))
        ]);

        smSnap.forEach(d => {
            skillMasterMap[d.id] = { id: d.id, symbol: d.data().symbol, dd: parseFloat(d.data().dd) || 0 };
        });
        if (!tourData) throw new Error('大会データが見つかりません');
        currentTour = tourData;
        document.getElementById('event-name-sidebar').textContent = currentTour?.name || '';

        athleteList.forEach(a => { athletes[a.id] = a; });
        // 決勝試技順（キャッシュ除外）をマージ
        const finalOrders = await getFinalOrders(eventId);
        Object.values(athletes).forEach(a => {
            const fo = finalOrders[a.id];
            if (fo) { a.finalOrder = fo.finalOrder; a.isInFinal = fo.isInFinal; }
        });
        subSnap.forEach(d => { submissions[d.id] = d.data(); });
        resSnap.forEach(d => { results[d.id] = d.data(); });

        renderSidebar();
        renderScores({});

        if (athleteId) {
            const ath = athletes[athleteId];
            if (ath) {
                setAthleteBanner(ath, round);
                applyHdtsVisibility();
                watchScores();
                watchSub();
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- サイドバー ----
    function renderSidebar() {
        const filtered  = Object.values(athletes).filter(a => (a.type || 'individual') === fType);
        const tourClassDefs = (currentTour?.classes?.[fType] || []).slice().sort((a,b) => (a.display_order??99)-(b.display_order??99)).map(c => c.name);
        const rawClasses = [...new Set(filtered.map(a => a.class || a.category || ''))];
        const classes = [...tourClassDefs.filter(c => rawClasses.includes(c)), ...rawClasses.filter(c => !tourClassDefs.includes(c))];
        const genders   = [...new Set(filtered.map(a => a.gender || ''))].filter(Boolean);

        // フィルターUI
        const filtersEl = document.getElementById('sidebar-filters');
        let fHtml = `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">種目</span>
            <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='individual'?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('type','individual')">個人</button>
            <button class="tab-btn ${fType==='synchro'?'active':''}"    onclick="setFilter('type','synchro')">シンクロ</button>
        </div>`;
        fHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">ラウンド</span>
            <button class="tab-btn ${!fFinalOnly?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',false)">予選</button>
            <button class="tab-btn ${fFinalOnly?'active':''}"  onclick="setFilter('finalOnly',true)">🏆 決勝</button>
        </div>`;
        if (classes.length > 1) {
            fHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">CLS</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fClass===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','')">ALL</button>
                ${classes.map(c => `<button class="tab-btn ${fClass===c?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('class','${c.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">${c}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }
        if (genders.length > 1) {
            fHtml += `<div class="filter-row"><span class="filter-label">性別</span>
                <button class="tab-btn ${fGender===''?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','')">ALL</button>
                ${genders.map(g => `<button class="tab-btn ${fGender===g?'active':''}" onclick="setFilter('gender','${g}')">${g}</button>`).join('')}
            </div>`;
        }
        filtersEl.innerHTML = fHtml;

        // 選手リスト
        let listHtml = '';
        const display = fFinalOnly
            ? filtered
                .filter(a => a.isInFinal && a.finalOrder != null)
                .filter(a => (!fClass  || (a.class||a.category||'') === fClass))
                .filter(a => (!fGender || a.gender === fGender))
                .sort((a, b) => a.finalOrder - b.finalOrder)
            : filtered
                .filter(a => (!fClass  || (a.class||a.category||'') === fClass))
                .filter(a => (!fGender || a.gender === fGender))
                .sort((a, b) => (a.order||0) - (b.order||0));

        const byClass = {};
        display.forEach(a => {
            const c = a.class || a.category || '—';
            if (!byClass[c]) byClass[c] = {};
            const g = a.gender || '—';
            if (!byClass[c][g]) byClass[c][g] = [];
            byClass[c][g].push(a);
        });

        // フィルターボタンと同じ順序でクラスグループを表示
        const orderedClasses = [
            ...classes.filter(c => byClass[c]),
            ...Object.keys(byClass).filter(c => !classes.includes(c))
        ];
        orderedClasses.forEach(cls => {
            const genderMap = byClass[cls];
            if (!genderMap) return;
            listHtml += `<div class="cat-group">${cls}</div>`;
            Object.entries(genderMap).forEach(([gnd, athl]) => {
                if (Object.keys(genderMap).length > 1) listHtml += `<div class="gender-group">${gnd}</div>`;
                athl.forEach(a => {
                    const dispName = a.type === 'synchro' && a.name2 ? `${a.name1} / ${a.name2}` : a.name1;
                    const isActive = a.id === athleteId;
                    const rounds = fFinalOnly ? [{ key:'final', label:'F' }] : getRoundsForAth(a);
                    const defaultRound = fFinalOnly ? 'final' : round;
                    const orderNum = fFinalOnly ? a.finalOrder : (a.order || '');
                    const dotsHtml = rounds.map(r => {
                        const key = `${eventId}_${a.id}_${r.key}`;
                        const cls2 = (results[key]?.published ? 'has-res' : submissions[key] ? 'has-sub' : '') +
                            (a.id === athleteId && r.key === round ? ' current' : '');
                        return `<span class="r-dot ${cls2}" onclick="selectAthlete('${a.id}','${r.key}');event.stopPropagation()">${r.label}</span>`;
                    }).join('');
                    listHtml += `<div class="athlete-row ${isActive?'active-athlete':''}" onclick="selectAthlete('${a.id}','${defaultRound}')">
                        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
                            <div class="ath-name-text"><span class="ath-order">${orderNum}</span>${dispName}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="round-box">${dotsHtml}</div>
                    </div>`;
                });
            });
        });
        document.getElementById('sidebar-list').innerHTML = listHtml || '<div style="padding:20px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:0.8rem;text-align:center;">選手なし</div>';
    }

    function getRoundsForAth(a) {
        const rule = currentTour?.classRules?.[a.type||'individual']?.[a.class||a.category||''] || {};
        const n = rule.qualifyingRoutines || 2;
        const rounds = Array.from({length:n}, (_, i) => ({ key:`pre${i+1}`, label:`P${i+1}` }));
if (rule.hasFinal !== false) rounds.push({ key:'final', label:'F' });
        return rounds;
    }

    window.setFilter = function(key, val) {
        if (key === 'type')      { fType = val; fClass = ''; fGender = ''; fFinalOnly = false; }
        if (key === 'class')     fClass = val;
        if (key === 'gender')    fGender = val;
        if (key === 'finalOnly') fFinalOnly = val;
        renderSidebar();
    };

    window.selectAthlete = function(aid, rid) {
        athleteId = aid;
        round = rid;
        const ath = athletes[aid];
        if (ath) {
            setAthleteBanner(ath, rid);
            applyHdtsVisibility();
            watchScores();
            watchSub();
        }
        renderSidebar();
        history.replaceState({}, '', `score_viewer.html?event=${eventId}&athlete=${aid}&round=${rid}`);
    };

    function setAthleteBanner(ath, rid) {
        const dispName = ath.type === 'synchro' && ath.name2 ? `${ath.name1} / ${ath.name2}` : ath.name1;
        document.getElementById('ath-name').textContent = `No.${ath.order} ${dispName}`;
        document.getElementById('ath-meta').textContent = `${ath.class || ath.category || '-'} / ${rid.toUpperCase()}`;
    }

    // ---- Firestore監視 ----
    function watchScores() {
        if (unsubscribeScores) { unsubscribeScores(); unsubscribeScores = null; }
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        unsubscribeScores = onSnapshot(doc(db, 'results', resId), snap => {
            latestData = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : {};
            results[resId] = latestData;
            renderScores(latestData);
        });
    }
    function watchSub() {
        if (unsubscribeSub) { unsubscribeSub(); unsubscribeSub = null; }
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        unsubscribeSub = onSnapshot(doc(db, 'submissions', resId), snap => {
            submissions[resId] = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
            renderScores(latestData);
        });
    }

    // ---- スコア描画 ----
    function renderScores(data) {
        const vLimit = data.validCount ?? 10;

        // valid count ヘッダー色
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            const th = document.getElementById(`sn-th-${i}`);
            if (!th) continue;
            th.classList.toggle('sn-valid',   i < vLimit);
            th.classList.toggle('sn-cutoff',  i === vLimit);
            th.classList.toggle('sn-invalid', i > vLimit);
        }
        document.getElementById('info-valid').textContent = vLimit;

        // ---- D情報 ----
        const resId = `${eventId}_${athleteId}_${round}`;
        const subSkills  = submissions[resId]?.skills || [];
        const execSkills = data.officialSkills || [];

        // 申請行
        const declaredCells = document.querySelectorAll('.d-declared');
        const declaredSymbols = [];
        const seenSub = new Set();
        declaredCells.forEach((el, i) => {
            const s = subSkills[i];
            el.style.color = ''; el.classList.remove('d-dup');
            if (!s) { el.textContent = '-'; declaredSymbols.push(null); return; }
            // オブジェクト形式（entry.htmlが保存する{id,skill,dd}）はidでマスター照合
            let sym;
            if (typeof s === 'object' && s !== null) {
                const found = s.id ? skillMasterMap[String(s.id)] : null;
                sym = found ? found.symbol : (s.skill || '');
            } else {
                const raw = String(s);
                sym = (!isNaN(raw) && raw !== '' && skillMasterMap[raw])
                    ? skillMasterMap[raw].symbol : raw;
            }
            el.textContent = sym || '-';
            declaredSymbols.push(sym || null);
            const dupKey = (typeof s === 'object' && s.id) ? String(s.id) : sym;
            if (dupKey && seenSub.has(dupKey)) el.classList.add('d-dup');
            else if (dupKey) seenSub.add(dupKey);
        });

        // 実施行
        const skillHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.skill-header');
        const seenExec = new Set();
        skillHeaders.forEach((el, i) => {
            el.style.color = ''; el.classList.remove('d-dup','d-mismatch');
            const id = execSkills[i];
            if (i >= vLimit || !id) { el.textContent = '-'; return; }
            const skill = skillMasterMap[id] || Object.values(skillMasterMap).find(v => v.symbol === id);
            const sym = skill?.symbol || String(id);
            el.textContent = sym;
            if (seenExec.has(sym)) el.classList.add('d-dup');
            else seenExec.add(sym);
            if (i < vLimit && declaredSymbols[i] && sym !== declaredSymbols[i]) el.classList.add('d-mismatch');
        });

        // D確定バッジ
        const badge = document.getElementById('d-confirmed-badge');
        const skillsRow = document.getElementById('skill-symbols-row');
        if (data.dConfirmedAt) {
            badge.textContent = '確定'; badge.className = 'd-badge';
            skillsRow.classList.add('d-confirmed');
        } else {
            badge.textContent = '未確定'; badge.style.cssText = 'font-size:0.68rem;color:#90a4ae;font-weight:normal;margin-left:4px;';
            badge.className = '';
            skillsRow.classList.remove('d-confirmed');
        }

        // DD行
        const totalDD = data.officialTotalDD || 0;
        const bonus   = data.officialDDBonus || 0;
        const ddCells = document.querySelectorAll('.d-dd');
        if (data.officialDDValues?.length > 0) {
            ddCells.forEach((el, i) => { el.textContent = data.officialDDValues[i] != null ? Number(data.officialDDValues[i]).toFixed(1) : '-'; });
        } else {
            const seen = new Set();
            ddCells.forEach((el, i) => {
                const id = execSkills[i];
                if (i >= vLimit || !id) { el.textContent = '-'; return; }
                // ID直接参照、なければSymbolでフォールバック（manual保存の旧データ互換）
                const skill = skillMasterMap[id] || Object.values(skillMasterMap).find(v => v.symbol === id);
                if (!skill) { el.textContent = '-'; return; }
                const key = skill.id; // 重複判定はIDで統一
                el.textContent = seen.has(key) ? '0.0' : skill.dd.toFixed(1);
                seen.add(key);
            });
        }
        document.getElementById('dd-bonus-cell').textContent = bonus > 0 ? `+${bonus.toFixed(1)}` : '-';
        document.getElementById('dd-total-cell').textContent = totalDD > 0 ? totalDD.toFixed(1) : '-';
        document.getElementById('dd-label').textContent = `DD: ${totalDD.toFixed(1)}`;
        document.getElementById('res-d').textContent = totalDD.toFixed(1);
        const bannerBadge = document.getElementById('confirmed-badge-banner');
        bannerBadge.textContent = data.published ? '✓ 確定済み' : '';
        bannerBadge.style.color = data.published ? '#43a047' : '';

        // ---- E審判テーブル ----
        // クラスルール優先、なければ大会設定、なければ 6（judge_cjp の getNumE() と同ロジック）
        const _ath  = athletes[athleteId];
        const _type = _ath?.type || 'individual';
        const _cls  = _ath?.class || _ath?.category || '';
        const _n    = parseInt(currentTour?.classRules?.[_type]?.[_cls]?.eJudgeCount);
        const numE  = (!isNaN(_n) && _n > 0) ? _n : (currentTour?.numExecJudges || 6);
        const jKeys = Array.from({length: numE}, (_, i) => `E${i+1}`);
        const allJudges   = data.execution?.judges || {};
        const activePanel = detectPanel(data);
        const panelPrefix = `${activePanel}_`;
        const judges = Object.fromEntries(
            Object.entries(allJudges)
                .filter(([k]) => k.startsWith(panelPrefix))
                .map(([k, v]) => [k.slice(panelPrefix.length), v])
        );

        const isSynchro = (athletes[athleteId]?.type || fType) === 'synchro';
        const { skillResults, landingResult, execution } = calcExecution(data, judges, numE, jKeys, vLimit, isSynchro);

        const tbody = document.getElementById('judge-rows');
        let rows = '';
        jKeys.forEach((jKey, jIdx) => {
            const jData = judges[jKey] || { deductions: Array(10).fill(null), landing: null };
            rows += `<tr><td>${jKey}</td>`;
            for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
                const v = jData.deductions?.[i] ?? null;
                const dimStyle = i >= vLimit ? 'opacity:0.15;' : '';
                const sr = skillResults[i];
                const colorCls = cellColorCls(v, sr, jIdx, i, vLimit, isSynchro);
                rows += `<td class="${colorCls}" style="${dimStyle}">${v !== null ? Math.round(v * 10) : '-'}</td>`;
            }
            const lv = jData.landing ?? null;
            const lnCls = landingColorCls(lv, landingResult, jIdx, vLimit, isSynchro);
            rows += `<td class="${lnCls}" style="${vLimit < 10 ? 'opacity:0.15;' : ''}">${lv !== null ? Math.round(lv * 10) : '-'}</td>`;
            rows += `<td style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.05)">${(vLimit - (jData.totalDeduction || 0)).toFixed(1)}</td>`;
            rows += `</tr>`;
        });

        // Med行
        const hasData = Object.keys(judges).length > 0;
        const _medFmt = (v) => isSynchro ? (v * 10).toFixed(2) : Math.round(v * 10);
        rows += `<tr class="middle-row"><td>Med</td>`;
        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const hasVal = hasData && (skillResults[i].hasData ?? skillResults[i].indices.size > 0);
            rows += i < vLimit
                ? `<td>${hasVal ? _medFmt(skillResults[i].sum) : '-'}</td>`
                : `<td style="opacity:0.15">-</td>`;
        }
        const hasLn = hasData && (landingResult.hasData ?? landingResult.indices.size > 0);
        rows += `<td>${vLimit === 10 && hasLn ? _medFmt(landingResult.sum) : '-'}</td>`;
        rows += `<td class="ded-total">${hasData ? execution.toFixed(1) : '-'}</td></tr>`;
        tbody.innerHTML = rows;

        // スコア集計
        const t = data.tofByPanel?.[activePanel] ?? data.tof ?? 0;
        const h = data.hdByPanel?.[activePanel]  ?? data.hd?.total ?? 0;
        const p = data.penalty || 0;
        document.getElementById('info-tof').textContent = t ? Number(t).toFixed(3) : '—';
        document.getElementById('info-hd').textContent  = h ? Number(h).toFixed(3) : '—';
        document.getElementById('info-pen').textContent = p ? Number(p).toFixed(1) : '—';
        document.getElementById('res-t-label').textContent = isSynchro ? 'Syn' : 'T';
        document.getElementById('res-e').textContent    = hasData ? execution.toFixed(1) : '0.0';
        document.getElementById('res-t').textContent    = t ? Number(t).toFixed(3) : '0.000';
        document.getElementById('res-hd').textContent   = h ? Number(h).toFixed(3) : '0.000';
        document.getElementById('res-total').textContent = Math.max(0, totalDD + (hasData ? execution : 0) + Number(t) + Number(h) - p).toFixed(2);

        // HDTS 合計列（技別データは Firestore 未保存のため合計値のみ）
        document.getElementById('sv-hdts-tof-sum').textContent = t ? Number(t).toFixed(3) : '-';
        document.getElementById('sv-hdts-hd-sum').textContent  = h ? Number(h).toFixed(3)  : '-';
    }

    // ---- HDTS 行 表示切替 ----
    function applyHdtsVisibility() {
        const ath   = athletes[athleteId];
        const show  = ath
            ? (currentTour?.classRules?.[ath.type||'individual']?.[ath.class||ath.category||'']?.useHDTS) !== false
            : true;
        ['sv-hdts-head','sv-hdts-tof-row','sv-hdts-hd-row'].forEach(id => {
            const el = document.getElementById(id);
            if (el) el.style.display = show ? '' : 'none';
        });
    }

    // ---- 演技点計算 ----
    function calcExecution(data, judges, numE, jKeys, vLimit, isSynchro) {
        if (!isSynchro) {
            const skillResults = Array.from({length:10}, (_, si) => {
                const vals = jKeys.map(k => (judges[k]?.deductions || [])[si] ?? null);
                return middle2(vals);
            });
            const landingResult = vLimit === 10 ? middle2(jKeys.map(k => judges[k]?.landing ?? null)) : { sum:0, indices:new Set() };
            const totalDed = skillResults.slice(0, vLimit).reduce((a, r) => a + r.sum, 0) + landingResult.sum;
            return { skillResults, landingResult, execution: (vLimit * 2.0) - totalDed };
        } else {
            const _group = numE >= 6
                ? (vals) => { const odd = middle2Synchro(vals,[0,2,4]); const even = middle2Synchro(vals,[1,3,5]); return { groupData:{oddResult:odd,evenResult:even}, indices:new Set([...odd.indices,...even.indices]) }; }
                : () => ({ groupData:null, indices:new Set() });
            const skillResults = Array.from({length:10}, (_, si) => {
                const vals = jKeys.map(k => (judges[k]?.deductions || [])[si] ?? null);
                const r = synchroEDed(vals, numE); const g = _group(vals);
                return { sum:r.avg, indices:g.indices, midMin:null, midMax:null, hasData:r.hasData, groupData:g.groupData };
            });
            const landingResult = vLimit === 10 ? (() => {
                const lv = jKeys.map(k => judges[k]?.landing ?? null);
                const r = synchroEDed(lv, numE); const g = _group(lv);
                return { sum:r.avg, indices:g.indices, midMin:null, midMax:null, hasData:r.hasData, groupData:g.groupData };
            })() : { sum:0, indices:new Set(), hasData:false, groupData:null };
            const totalDed = skillResults.slice(0, vLimit).reduce((a, r) => a + r.sum, 0) + landingResult.sum;
            return { skillResults, landingResult, execution: (vLimit * 1.0) - totalDed };
        }
    }

    function middle2(values) {
        const filled = values.map((v,i) => ({v,i})).filter(x => x.v !== null && x.v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length < 2) return { sum:0, indices:new Set(), midMin:null, midMax:null };
        const sorted = [...filled].sort((a,b) => a.v - b.v);
        const n = sorted.length, mid = Math.floor((n-2)/2);
        const v0 = sorted[mid].v, v1 = sorted[mid+1].v;
        return { sum:v0+v1, indices:new Set([sorted[mid].i, sorted[mid+1].i]), midMin:Math.min(v0,v1), midMax:Math.max(v0,v1) };
    }
    function middle2Synchro(allVals, groupIdxs) {
        const filled = groupIdxs.map(gi => ({v:allVals[gi],gi})).filter(x => x.v !== null && x.v !== undefined);
        if (filled.length < 2) return { sum:0, indices:new Set(), midMin:null, midMax:null };
        const sorted = [...filled].sort((a,b) => a.v - b.v);
        const n = sorted.length, mid = Math.floor((n-2)/2);
        const v0 = sorted[mid].v, v1 = sorted[mid+1].v;
        return { sum:v0+v1, indices:new Set([sorted[mid].gi, sorted[mid+1].gi]), midMin:Math.min(v0,v1), midMax:Math.max(v0,v1) };
    }
    function median1(values) {
        const f = values.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined).sort((a,b) => a-b);
        if (!f.length) return null;
        return f.length < 3 ? (f[0]+f[f.length-1])/2 : f[Math.floor(f.length/2)];
    }
    function synchroEDed(values, numE) {
        const filled = values.filter(v => v !== null && v !== undefined);
        if (!filled.length) return { avg:0, hasData:false };
        if (numE <= 4) return { avg: filled.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0)/filled.length, hasData:true };
        const m1 = median1([values[0],values[2],values[4]]);
        const m2 = median1([values[1],values[3],values[5]]);
        if (m1 === null && m2 === null) return { avg:0, hasData:false };
        if (m1 === null) return { avg:m2, hasData:true };
        if (m2 === null) return { avg:m1, hasData:true };
        return { avg:(m1+m2)/2, hasData:true };
    }

    function _resolveRange(sr, jIdx) {
        if (sr.groupData) {
            const gr = jIdx % 2 === 0 ? sr.groupData.oddResult : sr.groupData.evenResult;
            return { midMin:gr.midMin, midMax:gr.midMax, isMiddle:gr.indices.has(jIdx) };
        }
        return { midMin:sr.midMin, midMax:sr.midMax, isMiddle:sr.indices.has(jIdx) };
    }
    function cellColorCls(v, sr, jIdx, i, vLimit) {
        if (i >= vLimit || v === null) return v !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        const { midMin, midMax, isMiddle } = _resolveRange(sr, jIdx);
        if (midMin === null) return v !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        if (isMiddle) return 'val-filled';
        if (v < midMin - 1e-9) { const d = Math.round((midMin-v)*10); return d >= 2 ? 'low-cell' : 'low-cell-mild'; }
        if (v > midMax + 1e-9) { const d = Math.round((v-midMax)*10); return d >= 2 ? 'high-cell' : 'high-cell-mild'; }
        return 'val-filled';
    }
    function landingColorCls(lv, lr, jIdx, vLimit) {
        if (vLimit < 10 || lv === null) return lv !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        const { midMin, midMax, isMiddle } = _resolveRange(lr, jIdx);
        if (midMin === null) return lv !== null ? 'val-filled' : '';
        if (isMiddle) return 'val-filled';
        if (lv < midMin - 1e-9) { const d = Math.round((midMin-lv)*10); return d >= 2 ? 'low-cell' : 'low-cell-mild'; }
        if (lv > midMax + 1e-9) { const d = Math.round((lv-midMax)*10); return d >= 2 ? 'high-cell' : 'high-cell-mild'; }
        return 'val-filled';
    }

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

    <div class="card">
        <h2>クラス管理</h2>

        <div class="type-section">
            <div class="type-header">
                <span class="type-badge individual">個人</span>
            </div>
            <div id="individual-list" class="class-list"></div>
            <button class="btn-add" onclick="addToType('individual')">＋ 個人クラスを追加</button>
        </div>

        <div class="type-section" style="margin-top:24px;">
            <div class="type-header">
                <span class="type-badge synchro">シンクロ</span>
            </div>
            <div id="synchro-list" class="class-list"></div>
            <button class="btn-add" onclick="addToType('synchro')">＋ シンクロクラスを追加</button>
        </div>

        <button class="btn-save" id="btn-save-classes" style="margin-top:20px;" onclick="saveClasses()">クラスを保存</button>
    </div>

    <div class="danger-zone">
        <h2>⚠️ 危険な操作</h2>
        <p>大会を削除すると、すべてのデータが失われます。この操作は取り消せません。</p>
        <button class="btn-danger" onclick="deleteTournament()">この大会を削除する</button>
    </div>
</div>

<div id="toast" class="toast"></div>

<script type="module">
    import { doc, getDoc, setDoc, collection, getDocs, writeBatch, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { ensureAdmin, logout } from './auth.js';
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { invalidateCache } from "./cache.js";

    const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');

    // クラスのローカル状態（個人／シンクロ別）
    let individual = [];
    let synchro    = [];

    ensureAdmin(user => {
        document.getElementById('auth-info').style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = user.displayName || user.email;
        document.getElementById('logout-btn').onclick = logout;
        initPage();
    });

    async function initPage() {
        if (!eventId) { location.href = 'admin_index.html'; return; }
        document.getElementById('back-link').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

        const tDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId));
        if (!tDoc.exists()) { showToast('大会データが見つかりません', true); return; }

        const d = tDoc.data();
        document.getElementById('name').value = d.name || '';
        document.getElementById('start_date').value = d.start_date || d.startDate || '';
        document.getElementById('end_date').value = d.end_date || d.endDate || '';
        document.getElementById('venue').value = d.venue || '';

        const normalize = arr => (arr || [])
            .map((c, i) => ({ name: c.name || '', display_order: c.display_order ?? i, originalName: c.name || '' }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.display_order - b.display_order);

        const rulesKeys = obj => Object.keys(obj || {}).map((name, i) => ({ name, display_order: i, originalName: name }));

        if (d.classes && !Array.isArray(d.classes) && (d.classes.individual || d.classes.synchro)) {
            // 新形式: { individual: [...], synchro: [...] }
            individual = normalize(d.classes.individual);
            synchro    = normalize(d.classes.synchro);
            // シンクロが未保存でも classRules にデータがあれば補完
            if (synchro.length === 0 && d.classRules?.synchro) {
                synchro = rulesKeys(d.classRules.synchro);
            }
        } else if (Array.isArray(d.classes) && d.classes.length > 0) {
            // 旧フラット配列: すべて個人として移行し、classRules からシンクロも補完
            individual = normalize(d.classes);
            synchro    = d.classRules?.synchro ? rulesKeys(d.classRules.synchro) : [];
            showToast('既存クラスを個人クラスとして読み込みました');
        } else if (d.classRules) {
            // 最旧形式: classRules のキーから抽出
            individual = rulesKeys(d.classRules.individual);
            synchro    = rulesKeys(d.classRules.synchro);
            const total = individual.length + synchro.length;
            if (total > 0) showToast(`classRulesから${total}件のクラスを読み込みました`);
        }

        renderAll();
    }

    function renderAll() {
        renderList('individual-list', individual, 'individual');
        renderList('synchro-list',    synchro,    'synchro');
    }

    function renderList(listId, arr, type) {
        const list = document.getElementById(listId);
        list.innerHTML = '';
        arr.forEach((cls, i) => {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'class-item';

            const input = document.createElement('input');
            input.type = 'text';
            input.value = cls.name;
            input.placeholder = 'クラス名（例：Aクラス）';
            input.addEventListener('input', e => { arr[i].name = e.target.value; });

            const btnUp   = mkBtn('▲', '上へ',  i === 0,            () => { moveItem(arr, i, -1); renderList(listId, arr, type); });
            const btnDown = mkBtn('▼', '下へ',  i === arr.length-1, () => { moveItem(arr, i,  1); renderList(listId, arr, type); });
            const btnDel  = mkBtn('✕', '削除', false, () => {
                if (!confirm(`クラス「${arr[i].name || '（名前なし）'}」を削除しますか？`)) return;
                arr.splice(i, 1);
                renderList(listId, arr, type);
            }, true);

            div.append(input, btnUp, btnDown, btnDel);
            list.appendChild(div);
        });
    }

    function mkBtn(text, title, disabled, onclick, isDanger = false) {
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.className = 'btn-icon' + (isDanger ? ' danger' : '');
        btn.title = title;
        btn.textContent = text;
        btn.disabled = disabled;
        btn.onclick = onclick;
        return btn;
    }

    function moveItem(arr, i, dir) {
        const t = i + dir;
        if (t < 0 || t >= arr.length) return;
        [arr[i], arr[t]] = [arr[t], arr[i]];
    }

    window.addToType = (type) => {
        const arr = type === 'individual' ? individual : synchro;
        const listId = type === 'individual' ? 'individual-list' : 'synchro-list';
        arr.push({ name: '', display_order: arr.length, originalName: null });
        renderList(listId, arr, type);
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll(`#${listId} .class-item input`);
        if (inputs.length) inputs[inputs.length - 1].focus();
    };

    window.saveBasicInfo = async () => {
        const name = document.getElementById('name').value.trim();
        if (!name) { showToast('大会名を入力してください', true); return; }

        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-basic');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = '保存中…';
        try {
            await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                name,
                start_date: document.getElementById('start_date').value,
                end_date: document.getElementById('end_date').value,
                venue: document.getElementById('venue').value.trim(),
                updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
            }, { merge: true });
            showToast('基本情報を保存しました');
        } catch (e) {
            showToast('保存に失敗しました: ' + e.message, true);
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.textContent = '基本情報を保存';
        }
    };

    window.saveClasses = async () => {
        const allClasses = [...individual, ...synchro];
        if (allClasses.some(c => !c.name.trim())) {
            showToast('クラス名を入力してください', true);
            return;
        }

        // ── リネーム検出 ──────────────────────────────────────────
        // originalName（読込時の名称）と現在の名称を比較
        const renames = [];
        [{ arr: individual, type: 'individual' }, { arr: synchro, type: 'synchro' }]
            .forEach(({ arr, type }) => {
                arr.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.originalName && c.originalName !== c.name.trim()) {
                        renames.push({ type, oldName: c.originalName, newName: c.name.trim() });
                    }
                });
            });

        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-save-classes');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = '保存中…';
        try {
            const toPayload = arr => arr.map((c, i) => ({ name: c.name.trim(), display_order: i }));
            const payload = { individual: toPayload(individual), synchro: toPayload(synchro) };

            // 既存 classRules を取得
            const tDoc = await getDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId));
            const rawRules = tDoc.data()?.classRules || {};
            const rulesHasNewFormat = 'individual' in rawRules || 'synchro' in rawRules;
            const existingRules = rulesHasNewFormat ? rawRules :
                                  Object.keys(rawRules).length > 0 ? { individual: rawRules, synchro: {} } :
                                  { individual: {}, synchro: {} };
            const defaultRule = {
                qualifyingRoutines: 2, scoreMode: 'best',
                finalCount: 8, eJudgeCount: 4, hasFinal: true
            };
            const mergedIndividual = { ...existingRules.individual };
            const mergedSynchro    = { ...existingRules.synchro };

            // ── classRules キーのリネーム移行 ─────────────────────
            // スワップ等に対応するため「旧キーを先に全削除 → 新キーを旧スナップから追加」の2ステップ
            if (renames.length > 0) {
                const origInd = { ...existingRules.individual };
                const origSyn = { ...existingRules.synchro };
                // Step1: 旧名称キーを削除
                renames.forEach(({ type, oldName }) => {
                    if (type === 'individual') delete mergedIndividual[oldName];
                    else                       delete mergedSynchro[oldName];
                });
                // Step2: 新名称キーへルール設定をコピー
                renames.forEach(({ type, oldName, newName }) => {
                    const src  = type === 'individual' ? origInd : origSyn;
                    const dest = type === 'individual' ? mergedIndividual : mergedSynchro;
                    if (src[oldName] !== undefined) dest[newName] = src[oldName];
                });
            }

            // 新規クラスにデフォルトを設定
            payload.individual.forEach(c => { if (!mergedIndividual[c.name]) mergedIndividual[c.name] = defaultRule; });
            payload.synchro.forEach(c =>    { if (!mergedSynchro[c.name])    mergedSynchro[c.name]    = defaultRule; });

            // ── 選手の class フィールドを一括更新 ─────────────────
            let migratedCount = 0;
            if (renames.length > 0) {
                const athSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'athletes'));
                const batch = writeBatch(db);
                athSnap.docs.forEach(d => {
                    const ath = d.data();
                    const rename = renames.find(r => r.oldName === (ath.class || ath.category || ''));
                    if (rename) {
                        batch.update(d.ref, { class: rename.newName });
                        migratedCount++;
                    }
                });
                if (migratedCount > 0) await batch.commit();
            }

            // ── Firestore 保存 ────────────────────────────────────
            await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId), {
                classes: payload,
                classRules: { individual: mergedIndividual, synchro: mergedSynchro }
            }, { merge: true });

            // 保存後に originalName を新名称へ更新
            individual = payload.individual.map(c => ({ ...c, originalName: c.name }));
            synchro    = payload.synchro.map(c =>    ({ ...c, originalName: c.name }));

            invalidateCache(eventId);

            const msg = migratedCount > 0
                ? `クラスを保存しました（選手 ${migratedCount} 名のクラス名を自動更新）`
                : 'クラスを保存しました';
            showToast(msg);
            renderAll();
        } catch (e) {
            showToast('保存に失敗しました: ' + e.message, true);
        } finally {
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.textContent = 'クラスを保存';
        }
    };

    window.deleteTournament = async () => {
        const name = document.getElementById('name').value.trim() || '（名前未設定）';
        if (!confirm(`大会「${name}」を削除しますか？\nすべてのデータが失われます。`)) return;
        if (!confirm('本当に削除しますか？この操作は取り消せません。')) return;
        try {
            // athletes サブコレクションも削除
            const athSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'athletes'));
            const batch = writeBatch(db);
            athSnap.docs.forEach(d => batch.delete(d.ref));
            batch.delete(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId));
            await batch.commit();
            showToast('大会を削除しました');
            setTimeout(() => { location.href = 'admin_index.html'; }, 1500);
        } catch (e) {
            showToast('削除に失敗しました: ' + e.message, true);
        }
    };

    function showToast(msg, isError = false) {
        const t = document.getElementById('toast');
        t.textContent = msg;
        t.className = 'toast' + (isError ? ' error' : '');
        t.classList.add('show');
        setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2500);
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>



========================================
File: shared.js
========================================
/**
 * 共通ユーティリティ
 * 複数ページで使う関数をここに集約する
 */

/**
 * classRules を新旧フォーマットに統一して返す
 *   旧形式: { クラス名: { ... } }
 *   新形式: { individual: { クラス名: {...} }, synchro: { クラス名: {...} } }
 */
export function normalizeClassRules(raw) {
  if (!raw) return { individual: {}, synchro: {} };
  if ('individual' in raw || 'synchro' in raw) return raw;
  if (Object.keys(raw).length > 0) return { individual: raw, synchro: {} };
  return { individual: {}, synchro: {} };
}

// ============================================================
//  順位計算ユーティリティ（ranking.html / display.html / admin_final_order.html 共用）
// ============================================================

/**
 * 結果オブジェクトからタイブレーク用スコアを抽出
 * @param {object|null} result  Firestore result document data
 * @returns {{ tof: number|null, hd: number|null, diff: number|null }}
 */
export function extractTbScores(result) {
  if (!result) return { tof: null, hd: null, diff: null };
  return {
    tof:  result.tof                    ?? null,
    hd:   result.hd?.total              ?? null,
    diff: result.officialD ?? result.dd ?? null   // dd は旧フィールド名フォールバック
  };
}

/**
 * 予選スコア計算（numPre / scoreMode に応じて）
 * @param {number|null} pre1
 * @param {number|null} pre2
 * @param {number} numPre     予選本数
 * @param {'best'|'total'} scoreMode
 * @returns {number|null}
 */
export function calcQualifyingScore(pre1, pre2, numPre, scoreMode) {
  if (numPre === 1) return pre1 ?? null;
  if (pre1 != null && pre2 != null)
    return scoreMode === 'total' ? pre1 + pre2 : Math.max(pre1, pre2);
  return pre1 ?? pre2 ?? null;
}

/**
 * 予選タイブレーク用データ計算
 *   best採用: tbSum = 2本合計、tb = ベスト演技の T/H/D
 *   total採用: tbSum = null（qualScore が既に合計）、tb = 2本合計の T/H/D
 * @param {number|null} pre1
 * @param {number|null} pre2
 * @param {object|null} pre1r  pre1 の Firestore result document data
 * @param {object|null} pre2r  pre2 の Firestore result document data
 * @param {number} numPre
 * @param {'best'|'total'} scoreMode
 * @returns {{ tbSum: number|null, tb: { tof, hd, diff } }}
 */
export function calcQualifyingTb(pre1, pre2, pre1r, pre2r, numPre, scoreMode) {
  if (numPre < 2 || pre1 == null || pre2 == null) {
    return { tbSum: null, tb: extractTbScores(pre1 != null ? pre1r : pre2r) };
  }
  if (scoreMode === 'best') {
    return {
      tbSum: pre1 + pre2,
      tb:    extractTbScores(pre1 >= pre2 ? pre1r : pre2r)
    };
  } else {
    const s1 = extractTbScores(pre1r), s2 = extractTbScores(pre2r);
    return {
      tbSum: null,
      tb: {
        tof:  (s1.tof  ?? 0) + (s2.tof  ?? 0),
        hd:   (s1.hd   ?? 0) + (s2.hd   ?? 0),
        diff: (s1.diff ?? 0) + (s2.diff ?? 0)
      }
    };
  }
}

/**
 * 決勝スコア計算（finalScoreMode に応じて）
 * @param {number|null} qualPre       予選スコア（combined モード時に使用）
 * @param {number|null} final         決勝スコア
 * @param {'zero'|'combined'} finalScoreMode
 * @returns {number|null}
 */
export function calcFinalScore(qualPre, final, finalScoreMode) {
  if (finalScoreMode === 'combined') {
    if (qualPre != null && final != null) return qualPre + final;
    return final ?? qualPre ?? null;
  }
  // zero: 決勝スコア単体（未確定時は null → 末尾へ）
  return final ?? null;
}

/**
 * ランキング用ソート比較関数（降順、null 末尾）
 * TR2025 §4.4.4: 主スコア → 2本合計（best採用時のみ）→ T → H → D
 *
 * 各エントリは以下を持つこと:
 *   { qualScore: number|null, tbSum: number|null, tb: { tof, hd, diff } }
 *
 * @returns {number}  負 = a が上位
 */
export function compareRankEntries(a, b) {
  if (a.qualScore == null && b.qualScore == null) return 0;
  if (a.qualScore == null) return  1;
  if (b.qualScore == null) return -1;
  // ① 主スコア（小数第 3 位で比較）
  const as2 = Math.round(a.qualScore * 1000) / 1000;
  const bs2 = Math.round(b.qualScore * 1000) / 1000;
  if (bs2 !== as2) return bs2 - as2;
  // ② 2本合計（best採用時のみ; total採用時は tbSum = null なので skip）
  if (a.tbSum != null && b.tbSum != null && Math.abs(b.tbSum - a.tbSum) > 1e-9) return b.tbSum - a.tbSum;
  // ③ T（個人=ToF / シンクロ=Syn）→ H → D
  for (const k of ['tof', 'hd', 'diff']) {
    const va = a.tb?.[k], vb = b.tb?.[k];
    if (va != null && vb != null && Math.abs(vb - va) > 1e-9) return vb - va;
  }
  return 0;
}

/**
 * ランク番号割り当て（同点タイ対応）
 * compareRankEntries でソート済みの配列を受け取り、各エントリに .rank を付与する
 * @param {Array} entries  mutable, ソート済み
 * @returns {Array}  同一参照（チェーン用）
 */
export function assignRanks(entries) {
  entries.forEach((r, idx) => {
    if (idx === 0) { r.rank = 1; return; }
    const prev = entries[idx - 1];
    if (r.qualScore == null || prev.qualScore == null) { r.rank = idx + 1; return; }
    // タイ判定はソート基準 (compareRankEntries) と完全一致させる
    // （独自の === 比較だと浮動小数誤差でソートと食い違い、同着の順位がずれることがある）
    r.rank = compareRankEntries(r, prev) === 0 ? prev.rank : idx + 1;
  });
  return entries;
}

// ============================================================

/**
 * トランポリン競技ボーナス点計算 (FIG規則)
 *   男子: 5本超のトリプル 1本につき +0.3（6本目以降）
 *   女子・混合: 2本超のトリプル 1本につき +0.3（3本目以降）
 */
export function calcBonus(gender, tripleCount) {
  if (gender === '男子' && tripleCount > 5) return (tripleCount - 5) * 0.3;
  if ((gender === '女子' || gender === '混合') && tripleCount >= 3) return (tripleCount - 2) * 0.3;
  return 0;
}
