        .col-order   { width: 60px; text-align: center; }
        .col-id      { width: 40px; text-align: center; color: #aaa; font-size: 0.78rem; }
        .col-sym     { width: 130px; font-family: monospace; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: bold; color: #202124; }
        .col-disp    { width: 150px; font-size: 0.9rem; color: #202124; font-weight: bold; }
        .col-dd      { width: 60px; text-align: center; }
        .col-triple  { width: 52px; text-align: center; }
        .col-dir     { width: 70px; }
        .col-soma    { width: 70px; }
        .col-note    { min-width: 140px; color: #777; font-size: 0.85rem; }
        .col-act     { width: 110px; text-align: right; white-space: nowrap; }

        /* 順序ボタン */
        .order-btns { display: flex; gap: 2px; justify-content: center; }
        .order-btn { width: 24px; height: 24px; border: 1px solid #ccc; border-radius: 4px; background: white; color: #555; font-size: 0.7rem; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 0; line-height: 1; }
        .order-btn:hover { background: #e3f2fd; border-color: var(--primary); }
        .order-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.3; cursor: default; background: white; border-color: #eee; }
        .order-num { font-size: 0.75rem; color: #999; margin-bottom: 2px; }

        /* 一括保存バー */
        .order-save-bar { position: sticky; bottom: 0; background: #fff3e0; border-top: 2px solid var(--warn); padding: 10px 20px; display: none; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 12px; z-index: 10; }
        .order-save-bar.visible { display: flex; }
        .btn-save-order { padding: 8px 24px; border-radius: 8px; border: none; background: var(--warn); color: white; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; }
        .btn-save-order:hover { background: #e65100; }
        .btn-reset-order { padding: 8px 18px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #ccc; background: white; color: #555; font-size: 0.9rem; cursor: pointer; }

        /* ドラッグ中 */
        .skill-table tbody tr.dragging { opacity: 0.4; }
        .skill-table tbody tr.drag-over { border-top: 3px solid var(--primary); }
        .drag-handle { cursor: grab; color: #bbb; font-size: 1rem; user-select: none; }
        .drag-handle:active { cursor: grabbing; }

        /* 表示モードのテキスト */
        .display-text { color: #555; }
        .display-empty { color: #bbb; font-style: italic; }
        .triple-check { color: var(--primary); font-size: 1rem; }

        /* 編集モードのinput */
        .edit-input { width: 100%; padding: 5px 7px; border: 1px solid #90caf9; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 0.88rem; box-sizing: border-box; background: white; }
        .edit-input:focus { border-color: var(--primary); outline: none; box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(26,115,232,0.15); }
        input[type="number"].edit-input { text-align: center; }
        input[type="checkbox"].edit-triple-cb { width: 18px; height: 18px; cursor: pointer; }
        select.edit-input { padding: 5px 4px; }

        /* ボタン */
        .btn-edit { padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid #ccc; background: white; color: #555; font-size: 0.8rem; cursor: pointer; transition: 0.15s; }
        .btn-edit:hover { background: #f0f0f0; border-color: #aaa; }
        .btn-save-row { padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: 6px; border: none; background: var(--primary); color: white; font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: bold; cursor: pointer; }
        .btn-save-row:hover { background: #1558b0; }
        .btn-save-row:disabled { background: #ccc; cursor: not-allowed; }
        .btn-cancel-row { padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid #ccc; background: white; color: #777; font-size: 0.8rem; cursor: pointer; margin-left: 4px; }
        .btn-cancel-row:hover { background: #f5f5f5; }
        .btn-delete { padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1px solid #ffcdd2; background: white; color: #e53935; font-size: 0.8rem; cursor: pointer; margin-left: 4px; transition: 0.15s; }
        .btn-delete:hover { background: #ffebee; border-color: #e53935; }
        .save-ok { color: var(--ok); font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: bold; }

        .empty { text-align: center; padding: 60px; color: var(--gray); }
        .loading { text-align: center; padding: 60px; color: var(--gray); font-size: 1.1rem; }

        /* トースト通知 */
        .toast { position: fixed; bottom: 80px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%);
            background: #323232; color: white; padding: 10px 22px; border-radius: 8px;
            font-size: 0.9rem; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.25s; pointer-events: none; z-index: 200; white-space: nowrap; }
        .toast.show { opacity: 1; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="container">
    <div class="header">
        <a href="javascript:history.back()" class="back-link">← 戻る</a>
        <h1>📖 技マスター</h1>
        <button class="btn-add" onclick="toggleAddPanel()">＋ 技を追加</button>
    </div>

    <!-- 新規追加パネル -->
    <div class="add-panel" id="add-panel" style="display:none;">
        <h3>新規技を追加</h3>
        <div class="add-fields">
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">Symbol <span style="color:red">*</span></div>
                <input type="text" class="add-input" id="new-symbol" placeholder="例: o, 841<" oninput="syncDisplaySymbol()">
            </div>
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">表示用 <span style="color:#aaa; font-weight:normal;">(会場表示)</span></div>
                <input type="text" class="add-input" id="new-display-symbol" placeholder="例: 841&lt;">
            </div>
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">DD</div>
                <input type="number" class="add-input" id="new-dd" step="0.1" min="0" max="9.9" value="0.0">
            </div>
            <div class="add-triple-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">3回転</div>
                <input type="checkbox" id="new-triple">
            </div>
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">系統</div>
                <select class="add-input" id="new-dir">
                    <option value="">-- 選択 --</option>
                    <option value="前方系">前方系</option>
                    <option value="後方系">後方系</option>
                    <option value="基礎技">基礎技</option>
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">宙系</div>
                <select class="add-input" id="new-soma">
                    <option value="">-- なし --</option>
                    <option value="1回宙">1回宙</option>
                    <option value="2回宙">2回宙</option>
                    <option value="3回宙">3回宙</option>
                    <option value="4回宙">4回宙</option>
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="add-field-wrap">
                <div class="add-field-label">注釈</div>
                <input type="text" class="add-input" id="new-note" placeholder="メモ...">
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="add-actions">
            <button class="btn-confirm" onclick="addSkill()">追加する</button>
            <button class="btn-cancel-add" onclick="toggleAddPanel()">キャンセル</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="controls">
        <input type="text" class="search-box" id="search" placeholder="Symbolで検索..." oninput="render()">
        <button class="filter-btn active" data-dir="" onclick="setDir(this)">すべて</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-dir="前方系" onclick="setDir(this)">前方系</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-dir="後方系" onclick="setDir(this)">後方系</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-dir="基礎技" onclick="setDir(this)">基礎技</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" id="triple-btn" onclick="setTriple(this)">3回転のみ</button>
        <span style="width:1px; background:#ddd; align-self:stretch; margin:0 4px;"></span>
        <button class="filter-btn active" data-soma="" onclick="setSoma(this)">宙系: 全て</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-soma="1回宙" onclick="setSoma(this)">1回宙</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-soma="2回宙" onclick="setSoma(this)">2回宙</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-soma="3回宙" onclick="setSoma(this)">3回宙</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" data-soma="4回宙" onclick="setSoma(this)">4回宙</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" onclick="autoAssignSoma()" title="Symbolの先頭数字から宙系を自動割り当て">🔄 宙系自動割当</button>
        <button class="filter-btn" onclick="bulkCopySymbolToDisplay()" title="未設定の表示用にSymbolを一括コピー">📋 表示用一括コピー</button>
        <span style="width:1px; background:#ddd; align-self:stretch; margin:0 4px;"></span>
        <button class="filter-btn" onclick="sortByDD()" title="難度点の低い順に並べ替え（保存が必要）" style="border-color:#7e57c2; color:#7e57c2;">📊 難度順に並替</button>
        <span class="count-label" id="count-label"></span>
    </div>

    <div id="loading" class="loading">読み込み中...</div>
    <table class="skill-table" id="skill-table" style="display:none;">
        <thead>
            <tr>
                <th class="col-order">順序</th>
                <th class="col-id">#</th>
                <th class="col-sym">Symbol</th>
                <th class="col-disp">表示用</th>
                <th class="col-dd">DD</th>
                <th class="col-triple">3回転</th>
                <th class="col-dir">系統</th>
                <th class="col-soma">宙系</th>
                <th class="col-note">注釈</th>
                <th class="col-act"></th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="skill-body"></tbody>
    </table>
    <div id="empty-msg" class="empty" style="display:none;">該当する技がありません</div>

    <div class="order-save-bar" id="order-save-bar">
        <span>⚠️ 表示順を変更しました</span>
        <button class="btn-save-order" onclick="saveOrder()">順序を保存</button>
        <button class="btn-reset-order" onclick="resetOrder()">元に戻す</button>
    </div>
</div>
<div class="toast" id="toast"></div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { collection, getDocs, setDoc, deleteDoc, doc, serverTimestamp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    let skills = [];
    let editingId = null;   // 現在編集中の行ID
    let currentDir = '';
    let tripleOnly = false;
    let currentSoma = '';
    let orderDirty = false; // 順序変更フラグ
    let savedOrder = [];    // 保存前の順序（元に戻す用）

    function detectSomaType(symbol) {
        const s = (symbol || '').trim();
        if (s.startsWith('16')) return '4回宙';
        if (s.startsWith('12')) return '3回宙';
        if (s.startsWith('8'))  return '2回宙';
        if (s.startsWith('4'))  return '1回宙';
        return '';
    }

    function skillSortKey(s) {
        // displayOrder があればそれ、なければ id の数値
        return s.displayOrder ?? Number(s.id);
    }

    async function init() {
        const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, 'skillMaster'));
        skills = snap.docs
            .map(d => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }))
            .sort((a, b) => skillSortKey(a) - skillSortKey(b));

        // displayOrder が未設定のものに初期値を振る
        skills.forEach((s, i) => {
            if (s.displayOrder === undefined || s.displayOrder === null) {
                s.displayOrder = i;
            }
        });

        savedOrder = skills.map(s => s.id);
        document.getElementById('loading').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('skill-table').style.display = '';
        render();
    }

    window.toggleAddPanel = function() {
        const panel = document.getElementById('add-panel');
        const visible = panel.style.display !== 'none';
        panel.style.display = visible ? 'none' : '';
        if (!visible) {
            document.getElementById('new-symbol').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-display-symbol').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-dd').value = '0.0';
            document.getElementById('new-triple').checked = false;
            document.getElementById('new-dir').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-soma').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-note').value = '';
            document.getElementById('new-symbol').focus();
        }
    };

    window.addSkill = async function() {
        const symbol = document.getElementById('new-symbol').value.trim();
        if (!symbol) {
            alert('Symbolは必須です。');
            document.getElementById('new-symbol').focus();
            return;
        }

        const nextId = String(skills.length > 0 ? Math.max(...skills.map(s => Number(s.id))) + 1 : 0);
        const nextOrder = skills.length > 0 ? Math.max(...skills.map(s => s.displayOrder ?? 0)) + 1 : 0;
        const newDir = document.getElementById('new-dir').value;
        let newSoma = document.getElementById('new-soma').value;
        // 宙系未選択の場合はSymbolから自動検出
        if (!newSoma && newDir !== '基礎技') newSoma = detectSomaType(symbol);
        const newDisplaySymbol = document.getElementById('new-display-symbol').value.trim() || symbol;
        const data = {
            symbol,
            displaySymbol: newDisplaySymbol,
            dd: parseFloat(document.getElementById('new-dd').value) || 0,
            isTriple: document.getElementById('new-triple').checked,
            direction: newDir,
            somersaultType: newSoma,
            note: document.getElementById('new-note').value.trim(),
            displayOrder: nextOrder,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        };

        try {
            await setDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', nextId), data);
            skills.push({ id: nextId, ...data });
            skills.sort((a, b) => skillSortKey(a) - skillSortKey(b));
            document.getElementById('add-panel').style.display = 'none';
            render();
            setTimeout(() => {
                const row = document.getElementById(`row-${nextId}`);
                if (row) row.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
            }, 100);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Add error:', e);
            alert('追加に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    window.setDir = function(btn) {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-dir]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        currentDir = btn.dataset.dir;
        render();
    };

    window.setTriple = function(btn) {
        tripleOnly = !tripleOnly;
        btn.classList.toggle('active', tripleOnly);
        render();
    };

    window.setSoma = function(btn) {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-soma]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        currentSoma = btn.dataset.soma;
        render();
    };

    window.startEdit = function(id) {
        editingId = id;
        render();
        setTimeout(() => {
            const el = document.querySelector(`input.edit-input[data-id="${id}"]`);
            if (el) el.focus();
        }, 30);
    };

    window.cancelEdit = function() {
        editingId = null;
        render();
    };

    function passesFilter(s) {
        if (currentDir && s.direction !== currentDir) return false;
        if (currentSoma && s.somersaultType !== currentSoma) return false;
        if (tripleOnly && !s.isTriple) return false;
        const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (q && !s.symbol.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
        return true;
    }

    window.render = function() {
        const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
        const filtered = skills.filter(passesFilter);

        const dirLabel = { '前方系': '前方', '後方系': '後方', '基礎技': '基礎' };

        const tbody = document.getElementById('skill-body');
        if (filtered.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            document.getElementById('empty-msg').style.display = '';
        } else {
            document.getElementById('empty-msg').style.display = 'none';
            const isFiltered = !!(currentDir || currentSoma || tripleOnly || q);
            tbody.innerHTML = filtered.map((s, fi) => {
                // skills配列内のインデックス（フィルタなしの全体順序）
                const globalIdx = skills.indexOf(s);
                // フィルタ中は表示リスト内での先頭/末尾、未フィルタは全体での先頭/末尾
                const isFirst = fi === 0;
                const isLast  = fi === filtered.length - 1;

                if (editingId === s.id) {
                    // 編集モード行
                    return `<tr id="row-${s.id}" class="editing-row">
                        <td class="col-order"><span class="order-num">${globalIdx + 1}</span></td>
                        <td class="col-id">${s.id}</td>
                        <td class="col-sym">
                            <input type="text" class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="symbol"
                                value="${s.symbol.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" style="font-family:monospace; font-weight:bold;">
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-disp">
                            <input type="text" class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="displaySymbol"
                                value="${(s.displaySymbol || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" placeholder="(Symbolと同じ)">
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-dd">
                            <input type="number" class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="dd"
                                step="0.1" min="0" max="9.9" value="${Number(s.dd).toFixed(1)}" style="width:58px;">
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-triple">
                            <input type="checkbox" class="edit-triple-cb" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="isTriple"
                                ${s.isTriple ? 'checked' : ''}>
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-dir">
                            <select class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="direction" style="width:80px;">
                                <option value="前方系" ${s.direction === '前方系' ? 'selected' : ''}>前方</option>
                                <option value="後方系" ${s.direction === '後方系' ? 'selected' : ''}>後方</option>
                                <option value="基礎技" ${s.direction === '基礎技' ? 'selected' : ''}>基礎</option>
                            </select>
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-soma">
                            <select class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="somersaultType" style="width:78px;">
                                <option value="" ${!s.somersaultType ? 'selected' : ''}>--</option>
                                <option value="1回宙" ${s.somersaultType === '1回宙' ? 'selected' : ''}>1回宙</option>
                                <option value="2回宙" ${s.somersaultType === '2回宙' ? 'selected' : ''}>2回宙</option>
                                <option value="3回宙" ${s.somersaultType === '3回宙' ? 'selected' : ''}>3回宙</option>
                                <option value="4回宙" ${s.somersaultType === '4回宙' ? 'selected' : ''}>4回宙</option>
                            </select>
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-note">
                            <input type="text" class="edit-input" data-id="${s.id}" data-field="note"
                                value="${(s.note || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" placeholder="メモ...">
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-act">
                            <button class="btn-save-row" id="save-${s.id}" onclick="saveRow('${s.id}')">保存</button>
                            <button class="btn-cancel-row" onclick="cancelEdit()">✕</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                } else {
                    // 表示モード行
                    const noteText = s.note
                        ? s.note
                        : `<span class="display-empty">-</span>`;
                    return `<tr id="row-${s.id}" draggable="${isFiltered ? 'false' : 'true'}" data-skill-id="${s.id}"
                                ondragstart="onDragStart(event)" ondragover="onDragOver(event)" ondragleave="onDragLeave(event)" ondrop="onDrop(event)" ondragend="onDragEnd(event)">
                        <td class="col-order">
                            <span class="order-num">${globalIdx + 1}</span>
                            <div class="order-btns">
                                <button class="order-btn" onclick="moveSkill('${s.id}',-1)" ${isFirst ? 'disabled' : ''} title="上へ">▲</button>
                                <button class="order-btn" onclick="moveSkill('${s.id}',1)" ${isLast ? 'disabled' : ''} title="下へ">▼</button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="col-id">${s.id}</td>
                        <td class="col-sym">${isFiltered ? '' : '<span class="drag-handle">☰ </span>'}${s.symbol}</td>
                        <td class="col-disp">${s.displaySymbol || '<span class="display-empty">—</span>'}</td>
                        <td class="col-dd" style="text-align:center;">${Number(s.dd).toFixed(1)}</td>
                        <td class="col-triple">${s.isTriple ? '<span class="triple-check">✓</span>' : ''}</td>
                        <td class="col-dir">${dirLabel[s.direction] || s.direction || ''}</td>
                        <td class="col-soma">${s.somersaultType || ''}</td>
                        <td class="col-note">${noteText}</td>
                        <td class="col-act">
                            <button class="btn-edit" onclick="startEdit('${s.id}')">編集</button>
                            <button class="btn-delete" onclick="deleteSkill('${s.id}')">削除</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>`;
                }
            }).join('');
        }
        document.getElementById('count-label').textContent = `${filtered.length} / ${skills.length} 件`;
    };

    // ---- 順序変更 ----
    window.moveSkill = function(id, direction) {
        const idx = skills.findIndex(s => s.id === id);
        if (idx < 0) return;
        const isFiltered = !!(currentDir || currentSoma || tripleOnly || document.getElementById('search').value.trim());
        if (isFiltered) {
            // フィルタ中は表示されている隣のアイテムと入れ替え
            const visible = skills.filter(passesFilter);
            const vi = visible.findIndex(s => s.id === id);
            const neighbor = visible[vi + direction];
            if (!neighbor) return;
            const neighborIdx = skills.findIndex(s => s.id === neighbor.id);
            [skills[idx], skills[neighborIdx]] = [skills[neighborIdx], skills[idx]];
        } else {
            const newIdx = idx + direction;
            if (newIdx < 0 || newIdx >= skills.length) return;
            [skills[idx], skills[newIdx]] = [skills[newIdx], skills[idx]];
        }
        reindexOrder();
        markOrderDirty();
        render();
    };

    function reindexOrder() {
        skills.forEach((s, i) => { s.displayOrder = i; });
    }

    function markOrderDirty() {
        const current = skills.map(s => s.id);
        orderDirty = JSON.stringify(current) !== JSON.stringify(savedOrder);
        document.getElementById('order-save-bar').classList.toggle('visible', orderDirty);
    }

    function showToast(msg) {
        const t = document.getElementById('toast');
        t.textContent = msg;
        t.classList.add('show');
        clearTimeout(t._timer);
        t._timer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
    }

    window.saveOrder = async function() {
        try {
            const batch = [];
            for (const s of skills) {
                batch.push(setDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', s.id), { displayOrder: s.displayOrder }, { merge: true }));
            }
            await Promise.all(batch);
            savedOrder = skills.map(s => s.id);
            orderDirty = false;
            document.getElementById('order-save-bar').classList.remove('visible');
            showToast('✅ 表示順を保存しました');
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Order save error:', e);
            showToast('❌ 保存に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    window.resetOrder = function() {
        // savedOrder の順番に戻す
        const map = {};
        skills.forEach(s => { map[s.id] = s; });
        skills = savedOrder.map(id => map[id]).filter(Boolean);
        reindexOrder();
        orderDirty = false;
        document.getElementById('order-save-bar').classList.remove('visible');
        render();
    };

    // ---- ドラッグ & ドロップ ----
    let draggedId = null;

    window.onDragStart = function(e) {
        draggedId = e.currentTarget.dataset.skillId;
        e.currentTarget.classList.add('dragging');
        e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
    };

    window.onDragOver = function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
        e.currentTarget.classList.add('drag-over');
    };

    window.onDragLeave = function(e) {
        e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over');
    };

    window.onDrop = function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over');
        const targetId = e.currentTarget.dataset.skillId;
        if (!draggedId || draggedId === targetId) return;
        const fromIdx = skills.findIndex(s => s.id === draggedId);
        const toIdx   = skills.findIndex(s => s.id === targetId);
        if (fromIdx < 0 || toIdx < 0) return;
        const [moved] = skills.splice(fromIdx, 1);
        skills.splice(toIdx, 0, moved);
        reindexOrder();
        markOrderDirty();
        render();
    };

    window.onDragEnd = function(e) {
        draggedId = null;
        document.querySelectorAll('.dragging, .drag-over').forEach(el => {
            el.classList.remove('dragging', 'drag-over');
        });
    };

    window.saveRow = async function(id) {
        const skill = skills.find(s => s.id === id);
        if (!skill) return;

        const saveBtn = document.getElementById(`save-${id}`);
        if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = true;

        const symbolEl      = document.querySelector(`input.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="symbol"]`);
        const dispSymbolEl  = document.querySelector(`input.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="displaySymbol"]`);
        const ddEl          = document.querySelector(`input.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="dd"]`);
        const tripleEl      = document.querySelector(`input.edit-triple-cb[data-id="${id}"]`);
        const dirEl         = document.querySelector(`select.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="direction"]`);
        const somaEl        = document.querySelector(`select.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="somersaultType"]`);
        const noteEl        = document.querySelector(`input.edit-input[data-id="${id}"][data-field="note"]`);

        const newSymbol         = symbolEl?.value.trim() || skill.symbol;
        const newDisplaySymbol  = dispSymbolEl?.value.trim() || newSymbol;
        const newDd             = parseFloat(ddEl?.value) || 0;
        const newIsTriple       = tripleEl?.checked || false;
        const newDirection      = dirEl?.value || skill.direction;
        const newSomersault     = somaEl?.value || '';
        const newNote           = noteEl?.value.trim() || '';

        if (!newSymbol) {
            alert('Symbolは必須です。');
            if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = false;
            return;
        }

        const data = {
            symbol: newSymbol,
            displaySymbol: newDisplaySymbol,
            dd: newDd,
            isTriple: newIsTriple,
            direction: newDirection,
            somersaultType: newSomersault,
            note: newNote,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        };

        try {
            await setDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', id), data, { merge: true });

            const idx = skills.findIndex(s => s.id === id);
            if (idx >= 0) {
                skills[idx] = { ...skills[idx], symbol: newSymbol, displaySymbol: newDisplaySymbol, dd: newDd, isTriple: newIsTriple, direction: newDirection, somersaultType: newSomersault, note: newNote };
            }
            editingId = null;
            render();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Save error:', e);
            alert('保存に失敗しました。');
            if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    // Symbol入力時に表示用フィールドが空なら自動追従
    window.syncDisplaySymbol = function() {
        const sym = document.getElementById('new-symbol').value;
        const disp = document.getElementById('new-display-symbol');
        // 一度も手編集していない場合だけ追従（空の場合）
        if (!disp.dataset.edited) disp.value = sym;
    };
    document.getElementById('new-display-symbol')?.addEventListener('input', function() {
        this.dataset.edited = '1';
    });

    // 全技にSymbolを一括コピー
    window.bulkCopySymbolToDisplay = async function() {
        const targets = skills.filter(s => !s.displaySymbol || s.displaySymbol === s.symbol);
        if (!confirm(`表示用が未設定の ${targets.length} 件にSymbolをコピーします。\nよろしいですか？`)) return;
        const updates = skills.map(s => {
            const val = s.displaySymbol || s.symbol;
            s.displaySymbol = val;
            return setDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', s.id), { displaySymbol: val }, { merge: true });
        });
        await Promise.all(updates);
        alert(`${skills.length}件を更新しました`);
        render();
    };

    // ---- 難度順ソート ----
    window.sortByDD = function() {
        if (!confirm('難度点の低い順（同点はSymbol名順）に並べ替えます。\n※保存バーから「順序を保存」してください。\nよろしいですか？')) return;
        skills.sort((a, b) => {
            const da = Number(a.dd) || 0, db = Number(b.dd) || 0;
            if (Math.abs(da - db) > 1e-9) return da - db;
            return (a.symbol || '').localeCompare(b.symbol || '', 'ja');
        });
        reindexOrder();
        markOrderDirty();
        render();
        showToast('📊 難度順に並べ替えました（保存してください）');
    };

    window.deleteSkill = async function(id) {
        const skill = skills.find(s => s.id === id);
        if (!skill) return;
        if (!confirm(`「${skill.symbol}」を削除しますか？\n選手の申告データには影響しません。`)) return;
        try {
            await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', id));
            skills = skills.filter(s => s.id !== id);
            render();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('Delete error:', e);
            alert('削除に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    window.autoAssignSoma = async function() {
        if (!confirm('Symbolの先頭数字から宙系を自動判定して一括更新します。\n4始まり→1回宙、8始まり→2回宙、12始まり→3回宙、16始まり→4回宙\nよろしいですか？')) return;
        const updates = [];
        for (const s of skills) {
            const soma = s.direction === '基礎技' ? '' : detectSomaType(s.symbol);
            if (soma !== (s.somersaultType || '')) {
                s.somersaultType = soma;
                updates.push(setDoc(doc(db, 'skillMaster', s.id), { somersaultType: soma }, { merge: true }));
            }
        }
        if (updates.length === 0) { alert('変更対象なし'); return; }
        await Promise.all(updates);
        alert(`${updates.length}件を更新しました`);
        render();
    };

    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

        .editing-notice { font-size:0.8rem; font-weight:700; color:var(--accent); padding:6px 12px; background:#e3f2fd; border-radius:8px; margin-bottom:10px; display:none; }

        /* チーム一覧 */

        .team-card.editing { border-color:var(--accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(26,115,232,0.12); }

        .toast.show { opacity:1; }
    </style>

        <div id="auth-info" class="auth-info">
            <span id="user-name"></span>としてログイン中
            <button id="logout-btn" class="logout-btn">ログアウト</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="header">
        <h1>🏆 団体戦設定</h1>
        <div id="event-name" class="event-badge">読み込み中...</div>
    </div>

    <!-- ① フィルター -->
    <div class="section-title">🔍 クラス・性別の選択</div>
    <div class="card">
        <div class="filter-row">
            <div class="filter-group">
                <label>クラス</label>
                <select id="sel-class" class="filter-select">
                    <option value="">（選択してください）</option>
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="filter-group">
                <label>性別</label>
                <select id="sel-gender" class="filter-select">
                    <option value="">（選択してください）</option>
                    <option value="男子">男子</option>
                    <option value="女子">女子</option>
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="filter-group" style="flex:1;">
                <label>所属で絞り込み</label>
                <select id="sel-club-filter" class="filter-select" style="width:100%;">
                    <option value="">（全員表示）</option>
                </select>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ② 新規チーム作成 -->
    <div class="section-title">➕ チーム作成</div>
    <div class="card" id="new-team-section">
        <div id="editing-notice" class="editing-notice">✏️ 編集中のチームがあります</div>

        <div class="team-name-row">
            <span class="team-name-label">チーム名</span>
            <input type="text" id="inp-team-name" class="team-name-input" placeholder="例: 鈴木道場 A">
        </div>

        <div id="athlete-list" class="athlete-list-wrap">
            <div class="empty-msg">クラスと性別を選択してください</div>
        </div>

        <div class="save-row">
            <span id="sel-counter" class="sel-counter">選手を3〜4人選択してください</span>
            <button id="btn-cancel-edit" class="btn-cancel-edit">キャンセル</button>
            <button id="btn-save-team" class="btn-save-team" disabled>チームを保存</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ③ 登録済みチーム一覧 -->
    <div class="section-title">📋 登録済みチーム一覧</div>
    <div id="team-list" class="loading-msg">読み込み中...</div>
</div>

<div id="toast" class="toast"></div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import {
        doc, getDocs, addDoc, setDoc, deleteDoc,
        collection, serverTimestamp
    } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";
    import { ensureAdmin, logout } from "./auth.js";
    import { getTournament, getAthletes } from "./cache.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');
    document.getElementById('back-link').href = `admin_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

    // ---- State ----
    let allAthletes      = [];   // individual athletes only
    let allTeams         = [];   // teams from Firestore
    let individualClasses = [];

    let selectedClass  = '';
    let selectedGender = '';
    let clubFilter     = '';
    let checkedIds     = new Set();
    let editingTeamId  = null;   // null = new, string = editing existing

    // ---- Toast ----
    function showToast(msg) {
        const t = document.getElementById('toast');
        t.textContent = msg;
        t.classList.add('show');
        setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2800);
    }

    // ---- フィルタリング後の選手一覧 ----
    function getFilteredAthletes() {
        return allAthletes
            .filter(a => {
                if (selectedClass  && a.class  !== selectedClass)  return false;
                if (selectedGender && a.gender !== selectedGender) return false;
                if (clubFilter     && !(a.club1 || '').includes(clubFilter)) return false;
                return true;
            })
            .sort((a, b) => {
                const c = (a.club1 || '').localeCompare(b.club1 || '', 'ja');
                return c !== 0 ? c : (a.order ?? 999) - (b.order ?? 999);
            });
    }

    // 選手が所属しているチームを返す（editingTeamId は除外）
    function getAssignedTeam(athleteId) {
        return allTeams.find(t =>
            t.memberIds.includes(athleteId) && t.id !== editingTeamId
        ) || null;
    }

    // ---- 所属プルダウンの選択肢を再構築 ----
    function buildClubFilterOptions() {
        const sel = document.getElementById('sel-club-filter');
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">（全員表示）</option>';

        if (!selectedClass || !selectedGender) return;

        const clubs = [...new Set(
            allAthletes
                .filter(a => a.class === selectedClass && a.gender === selectedGender)
                .map(a => a.club1 || '')
                .filter(Boolean)
        )].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'ja'));

        clubs.forEach(club => {
            const o = document.createElement('option');
            o.value = club; o.textContent = club;
            sel.appendChild(o);
        });

        // clubFilter が選択肢にあれば維持、なければリセット
        if (clubFilter && clubs.includes(clubFilter)) {
            sel.value = clubFilter;
        } else {
            sel.value = '';
            clubFilter = '';
        }
    }

    // ---- 選手リスト描画 ----
    function renderAthleteList() {
        const wrap = document.getElementById('athlete-list');

        if (!selectedClass || !selectedGender) {
            wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-msg">クラスと性別を選択してください</div>';
            return;
        }

        const filtered = getFilteredAthletes();
        if (filtered.length === 0) {
            wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-msg">該当する選手がいません</div>';
            return;
        }

        wrap.innerHTML = '';
        let lastClub = null;

        filtered.forEach(a => {
            const assignedTeam = getAssignedTeam(a.id);
            const disabled = !!assignedTeam;
            const checked  = checkedIds.has(a.id);
            const clubName = a.club1 || '（所属なし）';

            // 所属ごとのヘッダー行
            if (clubName !== lastClub) {
                const hdr = document.createElement('div');
                hdr.className = 'club-header';
                hdr.textContent = `── ${clubName}`;
                wrap.appendChild(hdr);
                lastClub = clubName;
            }

            const item = document.createElement('label');
            item.className = 'athlete-item' +
                (disabled ? ' disabled' : '') +
                (checked  ? ' checked'  : '');

            const badge = assignedTeam
                ? `<span class="assigned-badge">登録済: ${assignedTeam.teamName}</span>`
                : '';

            item.innerHTML = `
                <input type="checkbox" data-id="${a.id}"
                    ${checked   ? 'checked'  : ''}
                    ${disabled  ? 'disabled' : ''}>
                <span class="ath-order">${a.order != null ? 'No.' + a.order : '-'}</span>
                <span class="ath-name">${a.name1 || ''}</span>
                <span class="ath-club">${a.club1 || ''}</span>
                ${badge}
            `;

            item.querySelector('input').addEventListener('change', e => {
                if (e.target.checked) checkedIds.add(a.id);
                else                  checkedIds.delete(a.id);
                item.classList.toggle('checked', e.target.checked);
                updateSaveBtn();
            });

            wrap.appendChild(item);
        });
    }

    // ---- 保存ボタン更新 ----
    function updateSaveBtn() {
        const teamName = document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value.trim();
        const count    = checkedIds.size;
        const btn      = document.getElementById('btn-save-team');
        const counter  = document.getElementById('sel-counter');

        const ready = count >= 3 && count <= 4 && teamName.length > 0
            && selectedClass && selectedGender;
        btn.disabled = !ready;

        if (!selectedClass || !selectedGender) {
            counter.textContent = 'クラスと性別を選択してください';
            counter.className = 'sel-counter';
        } else if (count === 0) {
            counter.textContent = '選手を3〜4人選択してください';
            counter.className = 'sel-counter';
        } else if (count < 3) {
            counter.textContent = `${count}人選択中（あと${3 - count}人必要）`;
            counter.className = 'sel-counter warning';
        } else if (count > 4) {
            counter.textContent = `${count}人選択中（4人まで）`;
            counter.className = 'sel-counter error';
        } else {
            counter.textContent = `${count}人選択中 ✓`;
            counter.className = 'sel-counter ok';
        }
    }

    // ---- チーム保存 ----
    document.getElementById('btn-save-team').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const teamName = document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value.trim();
        if (!teamName || checkedIds.size < 3 || checkedIds.size > 4
            || !selectedClass || !selectedGender) return;

        const memberIds = [...checkedIds];
        const data = {
            teamName,
            class:     selectedClass,
            gender:    selectedGender,
            memberIds,
            updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        };

        try {
            if (editingTeamId) {
                await setDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'teams', editingTeamId), data);
                showToast('✅ チームを更新しました');
            } else {
                await addDoc(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'teams'), data);
                showToast('✅ チームを保存しました');
            }
            cancelEdit();
            await reloadTeams();
        } catch (e) {
            alert('保存に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    });

    // ---- 編集キャンセル ----
    function cancelEdit() {
        editingTeamId = null;
        checkedIds.clear();
        document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value = '';
        document.getElementById('editing-notice').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('btn-cancel-edit').style.display = 'none';
        renderAthleteList();
        updateSaveBtn();
        renderTeamList();
    }
    document.getElementById('btn-cancel-edit').addEventListener('click', cancelEdit);

    // ---- チーム編集 ----
    window.editTeam = function(teamId) {
        const team = allTeams.find(t => t.id === teamId);
        if (!team) return;

        editingTeamId = teamId;
        selectedClass  = team.class;
        selectedGender = team.gender;
        checkedIds     = new Set(team.memberIds);

        document.getElementById('sel-class').value    = team.class;
        document.getElementById('sel-gender').value   = team.gender;
        document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value = team.teamName;
        clubFilter = '';
        buildClubFilterOptions();   // クラス・性別に合わせて選択肢を更新
        document.getElementById('sel-club-filter').value = '';

        document.getElementById('editing-notice').style.display = '';
        document.getElementById('btn-cancel-edit').style.display = '';

        renderAthleteList();
        updateSaveBtn();
        renderTeamList();
        document.getElementById('new-team-section').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    };

    // ---- チーム削除 ----
    window.deleteTeam = async function(teamId) {
        const team = allTeams.find(t => t.id === teamId);
        if (!team) return;
        if (!confirm(`「${team.teamName}」を削除しますか？`)) return;
        try {
            await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'teams', teamId));
            if (editingTeamId === teamId) cancelEdit();
            allTeams = allTeams.filter(t => t.id !== teamId);
            renderAthleteList();
            renderTeamList();
            showToast('🗑️ チームを削除しました');
        } catch (e) {
            alert('削除に失敗しました: ' + e.message);
        }
    };

    // ---- チーム一覧描画 ----
    function renderTeamList() {
        const container = document.getElementById('team-list');

        // 現在のフィルターに合わせて絞り込み（クラス・性別が未選択なら全件表示）
        const visible = allTeams.filter(t =>
            (!selectedClass  || t.class  === selectedClass) &&
            (!selectedGender || t.gender === selectedGender)
        );

        if (visible.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-msg">登録済みのチームはありません</div>';
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = '';
        visible.forEach(team => {
            const members = team.memberIds.map(id => allAthletes.find(a => a.id === id));
            const card = document.createElement('div');
            card.className = 'team-card' + (editingTeamId === team.id ? ' editing' : '');

            const memberHtml = members.map(m =>
                m ? `<span class="member-chip">No.${m.order ?? '-'} ${m.name1 || ''}（${m.club1 || ''}）</span>`
                  : `<span class="member-chip member-missing">（データなし）</span>`
            ).join('');

            card.innerHTML = `
                <div class="team-card-header">
                    <span class="team-name-disp">🏆 ${team.teamName}</span>
                    <span class="team-meta-badge">${team.class} ${team.gender}（${members.length}名）</span>
                    <div class="team-card-btns">
                        <button class="btn-team-edit" onclick="editTeam('${team.id}')">✏️ 編集</button>
                        <button class="btn-team-del"  onclick="deleteTeam('${team.id}')">🗑️</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="team-members">${memberHtml || '<span class="member-missing">メンバーなし</span>'}</div>
            `;
            container.appendChild(card);
        });
    }

    // ---- チーム再読み込み ----
    async function reloadTeams() {
        const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, 'tournaments', eventId, 'teams'));
        allTeams = snap.docs.map(d => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }));
        renderAthleteList();
        renderTeamList();
    }

    // ---- フィルター操作 ----
    document.getElementById('sel-class').addEventListener('change', e => {
        selectedClass = e.target.value;
        clubFilter = '';
        checkedIds.clear();
        editingTeamId = null;
        document.getElementById('editing-notice').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('btn-cancel-edit').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value = '';
        buildClubFilterOptions();
        renderAthleteList();
        renderTeamList();
        updateSaveBtn();
    });
    document.getElementById('sel-gender').addEventListener('change', e => {
        selectedGender = e.target.value;
        clubFilter = '';
        checkedIds.clear();
        editingTeamId = null;
        document.getElementById('editing-notice').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('btn-cancel-edit').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('inp-team-name').value = '';
        buildClubFilterOptions();
        renderAthleteList();
        renderTeamList();
        updateSaveBtn();
    });
    document.getElementById('sel-club-filter').addEventListener('change', e => {
        clubFilter = e.target.value;
        // 選択した所属をチーム名のデフォルト値として入力（未入力時のみ）
        const nameInput = document.getElementById('inp-team-name');
        if (clubFilter && !nameInput.value.trim()) {
            nameInput.value = clubFilter;
            updateSaveBtn();
        }
        renderAthleteList();
    });
    document.getElementById('inp-team-name').addEventListener('input', updateSaveBtn);

    // ---- 認証 & 初期ロード ----
    ensureAdmin(async user => {
        document.getElementById('auth-info').style.display = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('user-name').textContent = user.displayName || user.email;
        document.getElementById('logout-btn').onclick = logout;

        if (!eventId) {
            document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = 'URLにeventパラメータがありません';
            return;
        }

        // 大会情報（cache.js 経由でキャッシュ）
        const tourData = await getTournament(eventId);
        if (!tourData) {
            document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = '大会が見つかりません';
            return;
        }
        document.getElementById('event-name').textContent = tourData.name || eventId;

        // クラス一覧を構築（settings.html / athlete_management.html と同じロジック）
        const normalize = arr => (arr || [])
            .map((c, i) => ({ name: (typeof c === 'string' ? c : (c.name || '')), display_order: c.display_order ?? i }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.display_order - b.display_order);
        const ruleKeys = obj => Object.keys(obj || {}).map((name, i) => ({ name, display_order: i }));
        const toNames  = arr => arr.map(c => c.name);

        if (tourData.classes && !Array.isArray(tourData.classes) && tourData.classes.individual) {
            individualClasses = toNames(normalize(tourData.classes.individual));
        } else if (Array.isArray(tourData.classes) && tourData.classes.length > 0) {
            individualClasses = toNames(normalize(tourData.classes));
        } else if (tourData.classRules?.individual) {
            individualClasses = toNames(ruleKeys(tourData.classRules.individual));
        }

        // クラス selector 構築
        const selClass = document.getElementById('sel-class');
        individualClasses.forEach(cls => {
            const o = document.createElement('option');
            o.value = cls; o.textContent = cls;
            selClass.appendChild(o);
        });

        // 選手一覧（cache.js 経由でキャッシュ、個人のみ）
        const cachedAthletes = await getAthletes(eventId);
        cachedAthletes.forEach(a => {
            if ((a.type || 'individual') !== 'individual') return;
            if (!a.name1) return;
            allAthletes.push({
                id:        a.id,
                name1:     a.name1     || '',
                kana1:     a.kana1     || '',
                club1:     a.club1     || '',
                clubKana1: a.clubKana1 || '',
                class:     a.class     || '',
                gender:    a.gender    || '',
                order:     a.order     ?? null,
            });
        });

        // チーム一覧
        await reloadTeams();
    });
</script>
</body>
</html>



========================================
File: tournament_info.html

<html lang="ja">

    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>大会情報 - Trampoline System</title>
    <style>
        :root { --accent: #1a73e8; --bg: #f0f2f5; --card: #fff; --text-main: #263238; --text-sub: #546e7a; }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; background: var(--bg); margin: 0; color: var(--text-main); }
        .header { background: #1e3a5f; padding: 16px 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; }
        .btn-back { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); text-decoration: none; font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: 700; }
        .btn-back:hover { color: #fff; }
        .header-title { color: #fff; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 900; margin: 0; flex: 1; text-align: center; }
        .container { max-width: 560px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 24px 16px 48px; }

        .card { background: var(--card); border-radius: 14px; padding: 20px; box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.06); margin-bottom: 16px; }
        .card-title { font-size: 0.72rem; font-weight: 800; color: var(--text-sub); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin: 0 0 14px; padding-bottom: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eef0f3; }

        .field { margin-bottom: 14px; }
        .field:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
        .field-label { font-size: 0.7rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-sub); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.3px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .field-value { font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-main); }
        .field-value.large { font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 900; }
        .field-value.empty { color: #b0bec5; font-weight: 400; font-size: 0.9rem; }

        .date-row { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; }

        .cls-section { margin-bottom: 12px; }
        .cls-badge { display: inline-block; font-size: 0.68rem; font-weight: 800; padding: 2px 9px; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
        .cls-badge.individual { background: #e3f2fd; color: #1565c0; }
        .cls-badge.synchro    { background: #e8f5e9; color: #2e7d32; }
        .cls-list { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; }
        .cls-chip { background: #f4f6f8; border-radius: 8px; padding: 5px 12px; font-size: 0.88rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-main); }
    </style>

<body>
<div class="header">
    <a href="#" id="btn-back" class="btn-back">← 戻る</a>
    <h1 class="header-title">大会情報</h1>
    <div style="width:40px"></div>
</div>

<div class="container">
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-title">基本情報</div>
        <div class="field">
            <div class="field-label">大会名</div>
            <div class="field-value large" id="info-name">—</div>
        </div>
        <div class="date-row">
            <div class="field">
                <div class="field-label">開始日</div>
                <div class="field-value" id="info-start">—</div>
            </div>
            <div class="field">
                <div class="field-label">終了日</div>
                <div class="field-value" id="info-end">—</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="field" style="margin-bottom:0;">
            <div class="field-label">会場</div>
            <div class="field-value" id="info-venue">—</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-title">クラス</div>
        <div id="classes-container"><div class="field-value empty">クラス未設定</div></div>
    </div>
</div>

<script type="module">
    import { db } from "./firebase-config.js";
    import { doc, getDoc } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.7.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    const params  = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const eventId = params.get('event');
    document.getElementById('btn-back').href = `judge_menu.html?event=${eventId}`;

    async function init() {
        if (!eventId) return;
        const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'tournaments', eventId));
        if (!snap.exists()) return;
        const d = snap.data();

        document.title = `${d.name || '大会'} — 大会情報`;
        document.getElementById('info-name').textContent  = d.name  || '—';
        document.getElementById('info-start').textContent = d.start_date || d.startDate || '—';
        document.getElementById('info-end').textContent   = d.end_date   || d.endDate   || '—';
        document.getElementById('info-venue').textContent = d.venue || '—';

        const cls = d.classes || {};
        const individual = (cls.individual || []).map(c => c.name);
        const synchro    = (cls.synchro    || []).map(c => c.name);
        if (individual.length === 0 && synchro.length === 0) return;

        const container = document.getElementById('classes-container');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        if (individual.length > 0) {
            container.innerHTML += `
                <div class="cls-section">
                    <span class="cls-badge individual">個人</span>
                    <div class="cls-list">${individual.map(n => `<span class="cls-chip">${n}</span>`).join('')}</div>
                </div>`;
        }
        if (synchro.length > 0) {
            container.innerHTML += `
                <div class="cls-section">
                    <span class="cls-badge synchro">シンクロ</span>
                    <div class="cls-list">${synchro.map(n => `<span class="cls-chip">${n}</span>`).join('')}</div>
                </div>`;
        }
    }
    init();
</script>
</body>
</html>
